Variables: // MSG_SHEET_ID = 1REAKPmTx8CtOmz5hqU_hv2dgmEkPBc7EZcYtbaJuJEw // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // PREVIOUS (v13 — Pack & Ship): // • Added Pack & Ship routes under /ship/* // • SHIP_SHEET_ID = 1NqMqrzagU5cZLPbD0CPk4aRcBnXxF0qUxcYu_snQQp4 // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── const SCOPES = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets'; const TOKEN_URL = 'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token'; const SHEETS_URL = 'https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets'; const ALLOWED_TABS = new Set([ 'Master_List','Products','AWD_Master','UPC','Out_of_Stock', 'JQ_Vendor','EFF_Vendor','COGS', 'Part#_Sales','SKU_Sales', 'Amz30','Amz90','Ebay30','Ebay90','XF30','XF90', 'FBA_Inventory','FBA_Live','AWD_Inventory','FBA_Reserve','AWD_Shipment', 'UPC_Shipment','Discontinued_SKUs', 'eBay_Orders','eBay_Inventory','Amazon_Orders','Amazon_AWD_Live','Shopify_Orders', v7 additions (repricer sheet tabs) ── 'SKU_MASTER','MARKET_RESEARCH','PRICE_DECISION', 'Velocity_Dashboard','Velocity_DashboardP2','Velocity_DashboardP3','Price_Research_Log', 'Ebay_30sales','2025 H1 vs H2 Comparison', ]); const WRITABLE_TABS = new Set([ 'Amz30','Amz90','Ebay30','Ebay90','XF30','XF90', 'FBA_Inventory','FBA_Live','AWD_Inventory','FBA_Reserve','AWD_Shipment', 'UPC_Shipment','Discontinued_SKUs', 'eBay_Orders','eBay_Inventory','Amazon_Orders','Amazon_AWD_Live','Shopify_Orders', 'Price_History','Price_Research_Log','eBay_Listings','eBay_Sales_30d','eBay_Sales_90d','eBay_Sales_6mo','eBay_Sales_1yr','eBay_Sales_History', ]); // ─── SESSION TOKEN ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function hmacSign(data, secret) { const enc = new TextEncoder(); const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey( 'raw', enc.encode(secret), { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign'] ); const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, enc.encode(data)); return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(sig))) .replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=/g, ''); } async function createToken(pinLevel, secret) { const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000); const payload = btoa(JSON.stringify({ pin_level: pinLevel, iat: now, exp: now + SESSION_TTL })) .replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=/g, ''); const sig = await hmacSign(payload, secret); return payload + '.' + sig; } async function verifyToken(token, secret) { if (!token) return null; const parts = token.split('.'); if (parts.length !== 2) return null; const [payload, sig] = parts; const expectedSig = await hmacSign(payload, secret); if (sig !== expectedSig) return null; try { const data = JSON.parse(atob(payload.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'))); if (Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) > data.exp) return null; return data; } catch { return null; } } const SESSION_TTL = 2592000; // 30 days // ─── AUTH HANDLER ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function handleAuth(request, env) { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { pin, pin_type } = body; if (!pin || !pin_type) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing pin or pin_type' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const pinMap = { ops: env.PIN_OPS, mgmt: env.PIN_MGMT, mobile: env.PIN_MOBILE, price: env.PRICE_PIN, }; const correctPin = pinMap[pin_type]; if (!correctPin || pin !== correctPin) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid PIN' }), { status: 401, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // price PIN grants mgmt-level access (repricer operators) const tokenLevel = pin_type === 'price' ? 'mgmt' : pin_type; const token = await createToken(tokenLevel, env.SESSION_SECRET); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ token, pin_level: pin_type }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ─── MAIN ROUTER ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // INVENTORY APP HANDLERS // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // GET /inventory/lookup?code= // Looks up a scanned barcode in the UPC sheet (col D = outside UPC) // then finds all Master_List rows for that part number async function buildPendingMap(env) { // Phase 2: read from D1 — ~5ms vs ~800ms from Sheets // Falls back to Sheets if D1 not available if (env.DB) { try { const rows = await d1All(env, "SELECT part_num, location, cases FROM inventory_log WHERE status='Pending' AND (type='OUT' OR type='TRANSFER_OUT') AND cases>0" ); const pendingMap = {}; for (const r of rows) { const pk = (r.part_num||'').toUpperCase() + '|' + (r.location||'').toUpperCase(); pendingMap[pk] = (pendingMap[pk] || 0) + (r.cases || 0); } return pendingMap; } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] buildPendingMap fallback:', e.message); } } // Sheets fallback try { const countData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Inventory_Log!H2:H60000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const totalRows = (countData.values || []).length; const startRow = Math.max(2, totalRows - 1999 + 2); const logData = await invSheetGet(env, `Inventory_Log!B${startRow}:H${totalRows + 2}`).catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const pendingMap = {}; for (const lr of (logData.values || [])) { const lType = String(lr[0] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const lPart = String(lr[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const lLoc = String(lr[2] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const lCases = parseFloat(lr[3]) || 0; const lStatus = String(lr[6] || '').trim(); if ((lType === 'OUT' || lType === 'TRANSFER_OUT') && lStatus === 'Pending' && lPart && lLoc && lCases > 0) { const pk = lPart + '|' + lLoc; pendingMap[pk] = (pendingMap[pk] || 0) + lCases; } } return pendingMap; } catch(e) { return {}; } } async function inventoryLookup(url, env) { const code = (url.searchParams.get('code') || '').trim().toUpperCase(); if (!code) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'No code provided' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); // 1. Look up in UPC sheet: col A=SKU, col B=PartNum/variant, col C=inside UPC, col D=outside UPC const upcData = await invSheetGet(env, 'UPC!A2:D5000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const upcRows = upcData.values || []; let partNum = null; let sku = null; // Normalize to digits-only for UPC comparison (handles spaces, dashes, leading zeros) const normalize = s => String(s || '').replace(/\D/g, ''); const codeNorm = normalize(code); let insideUpc = null; let outsideUpc = null; for (const row of upcRows) { const outside = String(row[3] || '').trim(); const inside = String(row[2] || '').trim(); const variant = String(row[1] || '').trim(); // col B = the real SKU/part number string const skuCol = String(row[0] || '').trim(); // col A = variant_id — a numeric ID, NOT a SKU // (importUPC's own comment confirms this: "A=variant_id B=variant(sku)") const outsideNorm = normalize(outside); const insideNorm = normalize(inside); // Match by normalized UPC (outside or inside) OR exact part number const upcMatch = codeNorm && (outsideNorm === codeNorm || insideNorm === codeNorm); const partMatch = variant && variant.toUpperCase() === code; if (upcMatch || partMatch) { // BUGFIX: sku previously prioritized skuCol (col A, the numeric // variant_id) over variant (col B, the real SKU string) — backwards // from partNum's correct priority just below. Since col A is // populated on nearly every row, sku almost always ended up as a // bare number like "936" instead of the real part number. This // number then flowed into base_sku (sku.split('=')[0], which just // returns the number unchanged since it has no '=' in it) on any // row created via a transfer's new-location path — exactly the // "sku/base_sku shows 936 instead of a real base sku" symptom. // Now matches partNum's priority: real SKU string first, numeric // ID only as a last-resort fallback. partNum = variant || skuCol || null; sku = variant || skuCol || null; insideUpc = inside || null; outsideUpc = outside || null; break; } } // If not found in UPC sheet, treat the code itself as a part number if (!partNum) partNum = code; // 2. Read Master_List from D1 (fast) + pending map in parallel let mlRows = [], pendingMapLookup = {}; if (env.DB) { try { const [d1Rows, pMap] = await Promise.all([ d1All(env, 'SELECT id,sku,name,part_num,location,cases,units_per_case FROM master_list'), buildPendingMap(env) ]); mlRows = [['hdr']]; for (const r of d1Rows) { const row = new Array(17).fill(''); row[3]=r.sku||''; row[4]=r.name||''; row[6]=r.part_num||''; row[7]=r.location||''; row[8]=String(r.cases??0); row[9]=String(r.units_per_case??0); row[15]=r.id||''; // real D1 master_list id — index 15 was unused in the Sheets column layout mlRows.push(row); } pendingMapLookup = pMap; } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] lookup ML fallback:',e.message); mlRows=[]; } } if (mlRows.length <= 1) { const [mlData, pMap] = await Promise.all([ invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(()=>({values:[]})), buildPendingMap(env) ]); mlRows = mlData.values || []; pendingMapLookup = pMap; } // Derive base SKU from the resolved part number // Use sku col A first if available, else partNum col B, strip after '=' const baseSku = ((sku || partNum || '').split('=')[0]).trim().toUpperCase(); const locations = []; const partNums = new Set(); let name = ''; for (let i = 1; i < mlRows.length; i++) { const r = mlRows[i]; const rowPart = String(r[6] || '').trim(); const rowSku = String(r[3] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const rowBase = rowSku.split('=')[0].trim().toUpperCase(); const rowPartUp = rowPart.toUpperCase(); const rowPartBase = rowPartUp.split('=')[0].trim(); // Match: exact part number, OR same base SKU (from either SKU col or PartNum col) const matchExact = rowPartUp === partNum.toUpperCase(); const matchBase = rowBase === baseSku && baseSku.length > 2; const matchPartBase = rowPartBase === baseSku && baseSku.length > 2; if (!matchExact && !matchBase && !matchPartBase) continue; if (!name && r[4]) name = String(r[4]).trim(); if (rowPart) partNums.add(rowPart); const loc = String(r[7] || '').trim(); const cases = String(r[8] || '').trim(); const eachQty = String(r[9] || '').trim(); if (loc) { locations.push({ partNum: rowPart, location: loc, cases: cases, eachQty: eachQty, rowIndex: i + 1, masterId: r[15] || null, // real D1 master_list id, when sourced from D1 isPlaceholder: /will be here/i.test(loc) }); } } // If typed/scanned code was a base SKU with multiple variants, flag it const variants = Array.from(partNums).sort(); const isMultiVariant = variants.length > 1 && partNum.toUpperCase() === baseSku; for (const loc of locations) { const pk = (loc.partNum || '').toUpperCase() + '|' + (loc.location || '').toUpperCase(); const pending = pendingMapLookup[pk] || 0; loc.pendingCases = pending; loc.adjustedCases = Math.max(0, (parseFloat(loc.cases) || 0) - pending); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ partNum, sku: sku || partNum, name, baseSku, insideUpc, outsideUpc, locations, variants, isMultiVariant }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // GET /inventory/search?name= // Searches Master_List col E (name) AND Products sheet col C (name) + col G (keywords). // Returns up to 20 distinct base SKUs with name, totalCases, locCount, lastLocation. // Items with no current stock are still returned with totalCases=0 + lastLocation. async function inventoryNameSearch(url, env) { const query = (url.searchParams.get('name') || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!query || query.length < 2) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Query too short' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Fetch Master_List and Products in parallel const [mlData, prodData] = await Promise.all([ invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })), invSheetGet(env, 'Products!B2:G5000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })) ]); const mlRows = mlData.values || []; const prodRows = prodData.values || []; // skuMap: baseSku -> { baseSku, name, sku, totalCases, locCount, lastLocation, partNums } const skuMap = {}; // ── Step 1: scan Master_List col E (name) ── for (let i = 1; i < mlRows.length; i++) { const r = mlRows[i]; const name = String(r[4] || '').trim(); if (!name.toLowerCase().includes(query)) continue; const sku = String(r[3] || '').trim(); const partNum = String(r[6] || '').trim(); const loc = String(r[7] || '').trim(); const cases = parseFloat(r[8] || '') || 0; const baseSku = sku.split('=')[0].trim().toUpperCase() || partNum.split('=')[0].trim().toUpperCase(); if (!baseSku) continue; if (!skuMap[baseSku]) skuMap[baseSku] = { baseSku, name, sku, totalCases: 0, locCount: 0, lastLocation: '', partNums: new Set() }; if (partNum) skuMap[baseSku].partNums.add(partNum); if (loc) { if (cases > 0) { skuMap[baseSku].totalCases += cases; skuMap[baseSku].locCount++; } skuMap[baseSku].lastLocation = loc; // keep updating — last row wins (most recent location) } } // ── Step 2: scan Products sheet col C (name) and col G (keywords) ── // Products rows offset: col B=r[0], C=r[1], D=r[2], E=r[3], F=r[4], G=r[5] for (const row of prodRows) { const prodSku = String(row[0] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); // col B = SKU (may be blank) const prodName = String(row[1] || '').trim(); // col C = product name const prodKeywords = String(row[5] || '').trim(); // col G = keywords // Must have at least a name or keywords to be useful if (!prodName && !prodKeywords) continue; const nameMatch = prodName.toLowerCase().includes(query); // Keywords: check full string AND each token split by '/' or ',' const kwTokens = prodKeywords.toLowerCase().split(/[\/,]/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean); const kwMatch = prodKeywords.toLowerCase().includes(query) || kwTokens.some(tok => tok.includes(query)); if (!nameMatch && !kwMatch) continue; // Determine baseSku — use col B if present, else find by matching prodName in Master_List let baseSku = prodSku ? prodSku.split('=')[0].trim() : ''; if (!baseSku && prodName) { // No SKU in Products col B — find by looking up the product name in Master_List col E for (let i = 1; i < mlRows.length; i++) { const mlName = String(mlRows[i][4] || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (mlName && prodName.toLowerCase().includes(mlName) || mlName.includes(prodName.toLowerCase())) { const mlSku = String(mlRows[i][3] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const mlPart = String(mlRows[i][6] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); baseSku = (mlSku || mlPart).split('=')[0].trim(); if (baseSku) break; } } } if (!baseSku) continue; // If already in skuMap from Master_List step, just enrich name if blank if (skuMap[baseSku]) { if (!skuMap[baseSku].name && prodName) skuMap[baseSku].name = prodName; continue; } // Not yet in skuMap — add it, then scan Master_List for stock + last location skuMap[baseSku] = { baseSku, name: prodName, sku: prodSku || baseSku, totalCases: 0, locCount: 0, lastLocation: '', partNums: new Set() }; for (let i = 1; i < mlRows.length; i++) { const r = mlRows[i]; const rowSku = String(r[3] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const rowPart = String(r[6] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const rowBase = (rowSku.split('=')[0].trim() || rowPart.split('=')[0].trim()); if (rowBase !== baseSku) continue; const loc = String(r[7] || '').trim(); const cases = parseFloat(r[8] || '') || 0; if (!skuMap[baseSku].name && r[4]) skuMap[baseSku].name = String(r[4]).trim(); if (r[6]) skuMap[baseSku].partNums.add(String(r[6]).trim()); if (loc) { if (cases > 0) { skuMap[baseSku].totalCases += cases; skuMap[baseSku].locCount++; } skuMap[baseSku].lastLocation = loc; } } } const results = Object.values(skuMap) .map(s => ({ baseSku: s.baseSku, name: s.name, sku: s.sku, totalCases: s.totalCases, locCount: s.locCount, lastLocation: s.lastLocation, variantCount: s.partNums.size })) .sort((a, b) => b.totalCases - a.totalCases) .slice(0, 20); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ results }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // GET /inventory/pending // GET /inventory/pending-count — any valid session // Returns just the count for the badge — no item details exposed async function inventoryPendingCount(env) { try { // D1 fast path — single COUNT query if (env.DB) { const row = await d1First(env, "SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM inventory_log WHERE status='Pending' AND type != 'TRANSFER_IN'" ); const n = row?.cnt || 0; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, count: n, source: 'd1' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Sheets fallback — read just col H (status) of recent rows const countData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Inventory_Log!H2:H60000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const totalRows = (countData.values || []).length; const startRow = Math.max(2, totalRows - 4000 + 2); const data = await invSheetGet(env, `Inventory_Log!B${startRow}:H${totalRows + 2}`).catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const n = (data.values || []).filter(r => { const type = String(r[0]||'').trim().toUpperCase(); const status = String(r[6]||'').trim(); return status === 'Pending' && type !== 'TRANSFER_IN'; }).length; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, count: n, source: 'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, count: 0 }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // Auto gap-fill: if D1 log count is behind Sheets by more than 10 rows, // quietly syncs the missing rows in the background. // Runs fire-and-forget on /inventory/pending calls so it self-heals. async function d1GapFill(env) { if (!env.DB) return; try { const d1Count = await d1First(env, 'SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM inventory_log'); const d1Rows = d1Count?.cnt || 0; // Get sheet row count quickly (just col H) const countData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Inventory_Log!H2:H60000').catch(()=>({values:[]})); const sheetRows = (countData.values||[]).length; const gap = sheetRows - d1Rows; if (gap <= 10) return; // within tolerance — no action needed console.log(`[D1] Gap detected: ${gap} rows behind Sheets — syncing...`); // Fetch only the missing rows (from where D1 left off) const startRow = d1Rows + 2; // +2 for header + 1-based const logData = await invSheetGet(env, `Inventory_Log!A${startRow}:R${sheetRows+2}`).catch(()=>({values:[]})); const rows = logData.values || []; if (!rows.length) return; const BATCH = 50; for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += BATCH) { const batch = rows.slice(i, i+BATCH); const stmts = batch.map((r, bi) => { if (!r[0]) return null; return env.DB.prepare( `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO inventory_log (sheet_row,timestamp,type,part_num,location,cases,initials,notes,status, verified_by,verified_at,overwrite_loc,is_new,is_placeholder, master_row_index,sku,transfer_id,paired_location,name) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind( i+startRow+bi, String(r[0]||''), String(r[1]||'').toUpperCase(), String(r[2]||'').toUpperCase(), String(r[3]||''), parseFloat(r[4])||0, String(r[5]||''), String(r[6]||''), String(r[7]||'Pending'), String(r[8]||''), String(r[9]||''), String(r[10]||''), r[11]==='TRUE'?1:0, r[12]==='TRUE'?1:0, String(r[13]||''), String(r[14]||''), String(r[15]||''), String(r[16]||''), String(r[17]||'') ); }).filter(Boolean); if (stmts.length) await env.DB.batch(stmts); } console.log(`[D1] Gap-fill complete: added ${rows.length} rows`); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] Gap-fill error:', e.message); } } async function inventoryPending(env) { // Phase 3: Read pending items from D1 — Sheets is now a mirror only if (!env.DB) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ items: [], error: 'D1 unavailable' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } try { const d1Items = await d1All(env, `SELECT l.id, l.sheet_row, l.timestamp, l.type, l.part_num, l.location, l.cases, l.initials, l.notes, l.status, l.overwrite_loc, l.is_new, l.is_placeholder, l.master_row_index, l.master_id, l.sku, l.transfer_id, l.paired_location, COALESCE(NULLIF(l.name,''), p.name, '') as name FROM inventory_log l LEFT JOIN products p ON UPPER(SUBSTR(l.part_num, 1, INSTR(l.part_num||'=','=')-1)) = UPPER(p.sku) WHERE l.status='Pending' ORDER BY l.timestamp ASC` ); const items = d1Items.map(r => ({ d1Id: r.id, // Use sheet_row as rowIndex if available, otherwise fall back to d1 id // This ensures every item has a unique rowIndex for the frontend invItemMap rowIndex: r.sheet_row || r.id, timestamp: r.timestamp || '', type: r.type || '', partNum: r.part_num || '', location: r.location || '', cases: String(r.cases ?? ''), initials: r.initials || '', notes: r.notes || '', status: r.status || '', overwriteLocation: r.overwrite_loc || '', isNew: r.is_new ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE', isPlaceholder: r.is_placeholder ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE', masterRowIndex: r.master_row_index || '', masterId: r.master_id || null, sku: r.sku || '', transferId: r.transfer_id || '', pairedLocation: r.paired_location || '', name: r.name || '', })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ items, debug: { source: 'd1', count: items.length } }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] inventoryPending failed:', e.message); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ items: [], error: e.message }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/log // Appends a new entry to Inventory_Log as Pending async function inventoryLog(request, env) { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { type, partNum, sku, name, location, overwriteLocation, isPlaceholder, isNew, rowIndex, cases, initials, notes, masterId, // real D1 master_list.id, captured at scan-pick time when // available — lets approve target the EXACT row even when // two rows share identical part_num+location text (e.g. a // 13-case and 41-case entry at the same shelf), instead of // resolving ambiguously by that text alone. _requestId // present only on requests replayed from the offline // queue (sw.js v4+) — used to detect and safely no-op a // duplicate replay of a request that actually already // succeeded, just lost its response on the way back. } = body; if (_requestId) { const claim = await claimRequestId(env, _requestId, 'inventory/log'); if (claim.isDuplicate) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify(claim.cachedResponse), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } if (!type || !partNum || !location || cases === undefined || cases === null || cases === '') { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing required fields' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const ts = new Date().toISOString(); // Auto-generate stock note for IN/OUT — shows current cases and expected result let autoStockNote = notes || ''; if ((type === 'IN' || type === 'OUT') && !isNew && !isPlaceholder && env.DB) { try { const currentRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT cases FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(part_num)=? AND UPPER(location)=? LIMIT 1', [partNum.toUpperCase(), (overwriteLocation || location).toUpperCase()] ); if (currentRow !== null && currentRow.cases !== undefined) { const currentCases = parseFloat(currentRow.cases) || 0; const changeCases = parseFloat(cases) || 0; const afterCases = type === 'IN' ? currentCases + changeCases : Math.max(0, currentCases - changeCases); const sysNote = type === 'IN' ? `[STOCK IN] Had ${currentCases} — adding ${changeCases} — should be ${afterCases}` : `[STOCK OUT] Had ${currentCases} — removing ${changeCases} — should leave ${afterCases}`; autoStockNote = autoStockNote ? autoStockNote + ' | ' + sysNote : sysNote; } } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] stock note lookup:', e.message); } } // Cols: A=Timestamp, B=Type, C=PartNum, D=Location, E=Cases, F=Initials, // G=Notes, H=Status, I=VerifiedBy, J=VerifiedAt, K=OverwriteLocation, L=IsNew, M=IsPlaceholder, N=MasterRowIndex, O=SKU, P=(TransferId blank), Q=(PairedLocation blank), R=Name const row = [ ts, type.toUpperCase(), partNum, location, cases, initials, autoStockNote, 'Pending', '', '', overwriteLocation || '', isNew ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE', isPlaceholder ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE', rowIndex || '', sku || '', '', '', name || '' ]; // Phase 3: D1 is primary write — Sheets is fire-and-forget mirror let d1Id = null; let appendedRow = null; // 1. Write to D1 first — this is the source of truth if (env.DB) { try { const d1Result = await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO inventory_log (sheet_row,timestamp,type,part_num,location,cases,initials,notes,status, verified_by,verified_at,overwrite_loc,is_new,is_placeholder, master_row_index,sku,transfer_id,paired_location,name,master_id) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind( 0, ts, type.toUpperCase(), partNum, location, parseFloat(cases)||0, initials||'', autoStockNote||'', 'Pending', '', '', overwriteLocation||'', (isNew===true||isNew==='TRUE')?1:0, (isPlaceholder===true||isPlaceholder==='TRUE')?1:0, rowIndex||'', sku||'', '', '', name||'', masterId || null ).run(); d1Id = d1Result.meta?.last_row_id || null; } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] log insert failed:', e.message); } } // 2. Mirror to Sheets fire-and-forget — don't block response on this invSheetAppend(env, 'Inventory_Log!A:R', [row]) .then(appendData => { const updatedRange = (appendData.updates&&appendData.updates.updatedRange)||appendData.updatedRange||''; const rowMatch = updatedRange.match(/(\d+):/); appendedRow = rowMatch ? parseInt(rowMatch[1]) : null; // Update D1 sheet_row with the real Sheets row number for reference if (appendedRow && d1Id && env.DB) { env.DB.prepare('UPDATE inventory_log SET sheet_row=? WHERE id=?') .bind(appendedRow, d1Id).run().catch(()=>{}); } }).catch(e => console.error('[Sheets] log mirror failed:', e.message)); const inventoryLogResponseBody = { ok:true, rowIndex:d1Id, d1Id }; if (_requestId) await recordRequestResult(env, _requestId, inventoryLogResponseBody); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify(inventoryLogResponseBody), { headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } // POST /inventory/verify // Approves or rejects a pending entry and updates Master_List accordingly. // Uses live row lookup by partNum+location — never trusts the stored masterRowIndex, // which may be stale if rows were inserted/deleted in Master_List since scan time. async function inventoryVerify(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); let { rowIndex, action, item } = body; if (!rowIndex || !action) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing rowIndex or action', received: JSON.stringify(body).slice(0,200) }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Cast rowIndex to integer — frontend may send string or number const rowIndexInt = parseInt(rowIndex) || 0; // Phase 3: rowIndex may be 0 for items where sheet_row=0 — allow if item payload exists if (!item) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Item not found — refresh the Review tab and try again', reload: true, received: JSON.stringify(body).slice(0,200) }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const verifiedBy = 'MGMT'; if (action === 'reject' || action === 'Rejected') { // Phase 3: Sheets log no longer updated on reject — D1 is source of truth // D1 dual-write — fire-and-forget, best-effort (matches by sheet_row if present) // Pass item fields for composite key fallback const rPartNum = (item.partNum||'').trim().toUpperCase(); const rLoc = (item.location||'').trim(); const rType = (item.type||'').toUpperCase(); const rD1Id = item.d1Id ? parseInt(item.d1Id) : null; try { const d1RR = await d1UpdateLogStatus(env, rowIndexInt, 'Rejected', verifiedBy, ts, rPartNum, rLoc, rType, rD1Id); console.log('[D1] reject — sheetRow:', rowIndexInt, 'd1Id:', rD1Id, 'part:', rPartNum, 'loc:', rLoc, 'changes:', d1RR?.changes ?? 'null'); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] reject FAILED:', e.message, 'sheetRow:', rowIndexInt, 'd1Id:', rD1Id); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── APPROVE ── const type = (item.type || '').toUpperCase(); // Sanitize partNum — fix common worker entry errors: // "23-2-6 OLD" → "23-2-6=OLD" (space before suffix → =) // "28-2-6-2W.1C" → "28-2-6=2W.1C" (dash before letter variant → =) let partNum = (item.partNum || '').trim().toUpperCase(); if (!partNum.includes('=')) { // space before suffix: "23-2-6 OLD" → "23-2-6=OLD" partNum = partNum.replace(/\s+([A-Z0-9].*)$/, '=$1'); // trailing dash+letters: "28-2-6-2W" → "28-2-6=2W" only if looks like variant partNum = partNum.replace(/^(\d[\d\-]+[\d])-([A-Z].*)$/, '$1=$2'); } const location = (item.location || '').trim(); const overwriteLocation = (item.overwriteLocation || '').trim(); const isPlaceholder = item.isPlaceholder === 'TRUE' || item.isPlaceholder === true; const isNew = item.isNew === 'TRUE' || item.isNew === true; const cases = parseFloat(item.cases) || 0; const sku = (item.sku || '').trim(); // Use D1 to find sheet_row instead of reading 15k rows from Sheets // Falls back to full Sheets read if D1 unavailable or row not found let mlRows = []; let liveRowFromD1 = null; if (env.DB) { try { // ORDER BY cases DESC, id ASC — if a duplicate row exists for this exact // part_num+location (e.g. from an earlier Receive PO insert or manual // edit), always deterministically pick the SAME row every time this // query runs. Without explicit ordering, a write to one duplicate // between two approve calls could shift SQLite's unordered scan // order, causing back-to-back bulk approvals to silently decrement // TWO DIFFERENT rows instead of the same row twice — the exact bug // this fixes (4 cases, two OUT-2 approvals, ends at 2 instead of 0). const d1Row = await d1First(env, `SELECT sheet_row FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(part_num)=? AND UPPER(location)=? ORDER BY cases DESC, id ASC LIMIT 1`, [partNum.toUpperCase(), (overwriteLocation || location).toUpperCase()] ); if (d1Row && d1Row.sheet_row) liveRowFromD1 = d1Row.sheet_row; } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] verify lookup:', e.message); } } // For isNew path we still need mlRows from Sheets — lazy load only if needed // Always derive baseSku from the sanitized partNum — sku field may contain unsanitized value const baseSku = partNum.includes('=') ? partNum.split('=')[0].trim().toUpperCase() : ((sku||'').split('=')[0] || partNum.split(/[= ]/)[0]).trim().toUpperCase(); const liveRowIndex = liveRowFromD1 || 0; if (isNew && type === 'IN') { // Phase 3: Use D1 to find last row for base_sku — no Sheets 15k row read // Get the highest sheet_row across the ENTIRE table — must be GLOBAL, // not scoped to this base_sku. BUGFIX: this used to be // "WHERE UPPER(base_sku)=?", which only guaranteed the new row number // was higher than THIS part's own rows — not higher than every row in // the whole table. That let two completely unrelated parts end up // assigned the SAME sheet_row. Once that happens, every atomic // UPDATE/read-back/location-clear that resolves by sheet_row (the // fallback path below, used whenever masterId isn't available) matches // BOTH colliding rows — so a stock-out on one part could read the // OTHER part's cases value (possibly 0) and clear the location on // both, even though the intended part still had real stock remaining. // Reproduced and confirmed this exact scenario with a live test // before applying this fix — it is the root cause of locations // disappearing on approvals that still leave cases in stock. let newRowNum = 0; if (env.DB) { try { const lastRow = await d1First(env, `SELECT MAX(sheet_row) as max_row FROM master_list WHERE sheet_row > 0` ); if (lastRow && lastRow.max_row) newRowNum = lastRow.max_row + 1; } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] isNew row lookup:', e.message); } } // Fallback: append to end of Sheets (no row insertion needed — just append) if (!newRowNum) { try { const countData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!G1:G15000').catch(()=>({values:[]})); newRowNum = (countData.values||[]).length + 1; } catch(e) { newRowNum = 5900; } // safe fallback } // No insertDimension needed — write directly to newRowNum row // D1 has live data; Sheets append is fire-and-forget mirror const finalLoc = overwriteLocation || location; // Inherit J=UnitsPerCase, M=Vendor, N=Price from D1 (fast) — no Sheets read needed let inheritJ = '', inheritM = '', inheritN = ''; if (env.DB) { try { console.log('[D1] isNew inherit lookup for baseSku:', baseSku); // Get units_per_case and vendor from existing master_list row for same base_sku const existingRow = await d1First(env, `SELECT units_per_case, vendor FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(base_sku)=? AND (units_per_case > 0 OR vendor != '') ORDER BY units_per_case DESC, sheet_row LIMIT 1`, [baseSku] ); if (existingRow) { inheritJ = String(existingRow.units_per_case || ''); inheritM = String(existingRow.vendor || ''); } // Get price from cogs table const cogsRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT unit_price FROM cogs WHERE UPPER(base_sku)=? LIMIT 1', [baseSku] ); if (cogsRow && cogsRow.unit_price) inheritN = String(cogsRow.unit_price); // Fall back to master_list price if cogs missing if (!inheritN) { const priceRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT price FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(base_sku)=? AND price > 0 LIMIT 1', [baseSku] ); if (priceRow) inheritN = String(priceRow.price || ''); } } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] isNew inherit lookup:', e.message); } } // D1 lookups above are sufficient — no Sheets fallback needed // Write G:O — same structure as transfer verify new rows // G=PartNum, H=Location, I=Cases, J=UnitsPerCase, K=Note, L=TotalQty, M=Vendor, N=Price, O=TotalValue const autoNote = '[SYSTEM] Auto-created ' + new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US',{timeZone:'America/New_York'}) + ' by ' + (verifiedBy||'MGMT'); // Phase 3: Sheets new row write is fire-and-forget — D1 is primary, sync via d1-to-sheets invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!G${newRowNum}:O${newRowNum}`, [[ partNum, finalLoc, cases, inheritJ, autoNote + (item.notes ? ' — ' + String(item.notes).slice(0,80) : ''), inheritJ ? `=I${newRowNum}*J${newRowNum}` : '', inheritM, inheritN, inheritN ? `=L${newRowNum}*N${newRowNum}` : '' ]]).catch(()=>{}); const newInIsAudit = String(item.notes || '').includes('[AUDIT]') || String(item.notes || '').includes('[FOUND ON SHELF]'); (newInIsAudit ? invSetTealCell(env, newRowNum) : invSetPurpleCell(env, newRowNum)).catch(() => {}); // Insert new row into D1 master_list // BUGFIX: this was never awaited — the function kept running straight // through to the final `return {ok:true}` with no guarantee this // insert (the actual row creation) had completed. In a Cloudflare // Worker, once a Response is returned, the execution context can be // frozen/reused at any point after — an un-awaited write has no // guarantee of ever finishing. This is exactly why "Found on Shelf" // approvals could show as Verified in the log while the location // never actually got created in master_list: the log status update // (correctly awaited elsewhere) succeeded, but this insert may never // have run. Same anti-pattern already found and fixed twice this // session (transfer reject, SKU Manager save) — now awaited here too, // with a real error surfaced if it fails instead of a silent no-op. const insertResult = await d1InsertMasterRow(env, { sku: baseSku, base_sku: baseSku, name: item.name || '', part_num: partNum, location: finalLoc, cases, units_per_case: parseFloat(inheritJ) || 0, vendor: inheritM || '', price: parseFloat(inheritN) || 0, prev_notes: autoNote || '', sheet_row: newRowNum }).catch(e => { console.error('[D1] isNew insert THREW:', e.message); return null; }); if (!insertResult) { console.error('[D1] isNew insert FAILED — partNum:', partNum, 'location:', finalLoc, 'sheetRow:', newRowNum); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Failed to create the new inventory row — please try approving again. If this keeps happening, the row may already exist at a conflicting position.' }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Auto-register location in locations table if (finalLoc && env.DB) { const locPrefix = finalLoc.includes('=') ? finalLoc.split('=')[0].trim() : ''; env.DB.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO locations (location,prefix,active,created_at) VALUES (?,?,1,?)') .bind(finalLoc.toUpperCase(), locPrefix, new Date().toISOString()) .run().catch(()=>{}); } } else if (isPlaceholder && overwriteLocation && type === 'IN') { // isPlaceholder needs full ML scan — load Sheets only here if (!mlRows.length) { const mlData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); mlRows = mlData.values || []; } // Find placeholder row fresh by partNum + "will be here" in location const phRowIndex = findMasterRowPlaceholder(mlRows, partNum); if (!phRowIndex) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Placeholder row not found in Master_List — may have been edited' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const currentCases = parseFloat(mlRows[phRowIndex - 1]?.[8] || 0) || 0; const newCases = currentCases + cases; // Phase 3: fire-and-forget — D1 is primary invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!H${phRowIndex}:I${phRowIndex}`, [[overwriteLocation, newCases]]).catch(()=>{}); invSetPurpleCell(env, phRowIndex).catch(() => {}); // Update D1 for placeholder resolve — update location and cases if (env.DB) { env.DB.prepare('UPDATE master_list SET location=?,cases=?,updated_at=? WHERE sheet_row=?') .bind(overwriteLocation, newCases, new Date().toISOString(), phRowIndex) .run().catch(e => console.error('[D1] placeholder update failed:', e.message)); } } else { // ── Resolve which exact master_list row to target ────────────────── // Priority 1: masterId (the real D1 primary key), captured at // scan-pick time when available. This is the ONLY way to // unambiguously target one specific row when two rows share // identical part_num+location text — e.g. a 13-case entry and a // 41-case entry at the exact same shelf. Text-based matching // literally cannot distinguish them; picking "highest cases" as a // tiebreak (the legacy fallback below) can silently update the // WRONG row if the worker intentionally chose the smaller one. let targetId = (item.masterId && env.DB) ? (parseInt(item.masterId) || null) : null; let resolvedRowIndex = null; if (!targetId) { // Legacy path — no masterId captured (older pending item created // before this fix, or a Sheets-sourced fallback). Resolve by // sheet_row via text matching, unchanged from before. resolvedRowIndex = liveRowIndex; if (!resolvedRowIndex && env.DB) { try { const fuzzyRow = await d1First(env, `SELECT sheet_row FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(part_num)=? AND sheet_row > 0 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1`, [partNum.toUpperCase()] ); if (fuzzyRow && fuzzyRow.sheet_row) resolvedRowIndex = fuzzyRow.sheet_row; } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] fuzzy row lookup:', e.message); } } if (!resolvedRowIndex) { if (!mlRows.length) { const mlData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); mlRows = mlData.values || []; } resolvedRowIndex = findMasterRow(mlRows, partNum, overwriteLocation || location); } if (!resolvedRowIndex) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: `Row not found in Master_List for ${partNum} @ ${location} — sheet may have changed` }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Everything below targets whichever key was resolved above — id // when we have it (unambiguous), sheet_row otherwise (legacy path). // Defense in depth: even if a sheet_row collision somehow occurs // again in the future (see the isNew-branch fix above for the root // cause we found and fixed), scoping the fallback match to part_num // too means it can never cross-contaminate a different, unrelated // part — the update/read-back/clear can only ever touch rows that // are BOTH this sheet_row AND this exact part number. const whereClause = targetId ? 'id = ?' : 'sheet_row = ? AND UPPER(part_num) = ?'; const whereValue = targetId ? [targetId] : [resolvedRowIndex, partNum.toUpperCase()]; // Phase 3: Atomic SQL update — prevents race condition on bulk approve // Instead of read-then-write (which loses updates when concurrent), use SQL arithmetic const noneFound = String(item.notes || '').includes('[NONE FOUND ON SHELF]'); let newCases; if (env.DB) { try { if (type === 'IN') { await env.DB.prepare( `UPDATE master_list SET cases = cases + ?, updated_at = ? WHERE ${whereClause}` ).bind(cases, new Date().toISOString(), ...whereValue).run(); } else if (type === 'OUT') { if (noneFound) { await env.DB.prepare( `UPDATE master_list SET cases = 0, updated_at = ? WHERE ${whereClause}` ).bind(new Date().toISOString(), ...whereValue).run(); } else { await env.DB.prepare( `UPDATE master_list SET cases = MAX(0, cases - ?), updated_at = ? WHERE ${whereClause}` ).bind(cases, new Date().toISOString(), ...whereValue).run(); } } // Read back the new value for zero-case location clear const updated = await d1First(env, `SELECT cases FROM master_list WHERE ${whereClause}`, whereValue).catch(()=>null); newCases = updated ? (parseFloat(updated.cases) || 0) : null; // null = read-back failed } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] atomic cases update failed:', e.message, whereClause, JSON.stringify(whereValue)); newCases = null; // unknown — never clear location on an uncertain read } } // Clear location in D1 ONLY when cases is confirmed exactly 0. // newCases === null means the read-back failed/was uncertain — in that // case we must NOT clear, otherwise a partial change (e.g. 3 -> 1) could // wipe a location it still occupies. Require a real numeric 0. if (typeof newCases === 'number' && newCases === 0 && env.DB) { env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE master_list SET location=? WHERE ${whereClause}`) .bind('', ...whereValue).run().catch(()=>{}); } // Phase 3: No Sheets cases write — D1 is source of truth // Cell color is cosmetic only, and needs an actual Sheets row number — // look one up if we only resolved by id. let colorRowIndex = resolvedRowIndex; if (targetId && !colorRowIndex && env.DB) { try { const srRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT sheet_row FROM master_list WHERE id=?', [targetId]); if (srRow && srRow.sheet_row) colorRowIndex = srRow.sheet_row; } catch(e) {} } if (colorRowIndex) { const noteStr = String(item.notes || ''); const isAuditOp = noteStr.includes('[AUDIT]') || noteStr.includes('[FOUND ON SHELF]'); (isAuditOp ? invSetTealCell(env, colorRowIndex) : invSetPurpleCell(env, colorRowIndex)).catch(() => {}); } } // Phase 3: Sheets log no longer updated on approve — D1 is source of truth // D1 status update — primary write const d1Id = item.d1Id ? parseInt(item.d1Id) : null; try { const d1SR = await d1UpdateLogStatus(env, rowIndexInt, 'Verified', verifiedBy, ts, partNum, location, type, d1Id); console.log('[D1] verify — sheetRow:', rowIndexInt, 'd1Id:', d1Id, 'part:', partNum, 'loc:', location, 'changes:', d1SR?.changes ?? 'null'); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] verify FAILED:', e.message, 'sheetRow:', rowIndexInt, 'd1Id:', d1Id); } // D1 cases update already handled in the approve block above await invalidateMasterListCache(env); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (err) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: err.message || 'verify failed' }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/transfer // Logs a transfer as two linked Inventory_Log rows (TRANSFER_OUT + TRANSFER_IN), both Pending. // Col P = TransferId links the pair. Col Q = PairedLocation (destination on OUT row, source on IN row). async function inventoryTransferLog(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { partNum, sku, name, fromLocation, fromRowIndex, fromMasterId, toLocation, toRowIndex, isNewLocation, cases, initials, notes, _requestId } = body; if (_requestId) { const claim = await claimRequestId(env, _requestId, 'inventory/transfer'); if (claim.isDuplicate) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify(claim.cachedResponse), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } if (!partNum || !fromLocation || !toLocation || !cases) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing required fields' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const transferId = 'XFR-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6).toUpperCase(); // BUGFIX: transfers never had an auto-generated note, unlike regular // stock IN/OUT (which get a "[STOCK IN] Had X — adding Y — should be Z" // note automatically). Reported as "transfer isn't making any notes in // the inventory log... like it should" — this was a missing feature, // not a broken one. Now generates a matching auto-note for both sides // of the pair, with the user's own notes (if any) appended after it. const casesNum = parseFloat(cases) || 0; const autoOutNote = `[TRANSFER OUT] ${casesNum} case(s) moved from ${fromLocation} to ${toLocation}`; const autoInNote = `[TRANSFER IN] ${casesNum} case(s) received at ${toLocation} from ${fromLocation}`; const outNotes = notes ? autoOutNote + ' — ' + notes : autoOutNote; const inNotes = notes ? autoInNote + ' — ' + notes : autoInNote; // Cols A-O same as inventory log; P=TransferId, Q=PairedLocation, R=Name const outRow = [ ts, 'TRANSFER_OUT', partNum, fromLocation, cases, initials, outNotes, 'Pending', '', '', '', 'FALSE', 'FALSE', fromRowIndex || '', sku || '', transferId, toLocation, name || '' ]; const inRow = [ ts, 'TRANSFER_IN', partNum, toLocation, cases, initials, inNotes, 'Pending', '', '', '', isNewLocation ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE', 'FALSE', toRowIndex || '', sku || '', transferId, fromLocation, name || '' ]; // Phase 3: D1 first, Sheets fire-and-forget let d1Out = null, d1In = null; if (env.DB) { d1Out = await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO inventory_log (sheet_row,timestamp,type,part_num,location,cases,initials,notes,status, verified_by,verified_at,overwrite_loc,is_new,is_placeholder,master_row_index,sku,transfer_id,paired_location,name,master_id) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind(0, ts, 'TRANSFER_OUT', partNum, fromLocation, parseFloat(cases)||0, initials||'', outNotes, 'Pending', '', '', '', 0, 0, fromRowIndex||'', sku||'', transferId, toLocation, name||'', fromMasterId || null) .run().catch(e=>{console.error('[D1] transfer out insert:',e.message);return null;}); d1In = await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO inventory_log (sheet_row,timestamp,type,part_num,location,cases,initials,notes,status, verified_by,verified_at,overwrite_loc,is_new,is_placeholder,master_row_index,sku,transfer_id,paired_location,name) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind(0, ts, 'TRANSFER_IN', partNum, toLocation, parseFloat(cases)||0, initials||'', inNotes, 'Pending', '', '', '', isNewLocation?1:0, 0, toRowIndex||'', sku||'', transferId, fromLocation, name||'') .run().catch(e=>{console.error('[D1] transfer in insert:',e.message);return null;}); } // Sheets fire-and-forget mirror invSheetAppend(env, 'Inventory_Log!A:R', [outRow]).catch(()=>{}); invSheetAppend(env, 'Inventory_Log!A:R', [inRow]).catch(()=>{}); const outD1Id = d1Out?.meta?.last_row_id || null; const inD1Id = d1In?.meta?.last_row_id || null; // BUGFIX: this previously always returned ok:true regardless of whether // the D1 inserts actually succeeded — both are wrapped in .catch(()=>null) // above, so a failed insert was silently swallowed and the caller had no // way to know. Since D1 inventory_log is what /inventory/pending reads // from, a failed insert meant the transfer looked successful to the // worker who submitted it but simply never appeared in Review — exactly // the reported symptom. Now surfaces a real error if either write failed. if (!env.DB) { // No D1 at all — Sheets-only fire-and-forget mirrors above are the // only record; can't confirm success either way, but don't claim a // false positive. console.error('[D1] transfer log: env.DB unavailable, only Sheets mirror attempted'); } else if (!d1Out || !d1In) { console.error('[D1] transfer log FAILED — outOk:', !!d1Out, 'inOk:', !!d1In, 'transferId:', transferId); // Release the claim on genuine failure — a retry with this SAME // requestId should be free to try again fresh, not get wrongly // blocked as "already in progress" forever. Dedup protection is // only meant to catch a request that actually SUCCEEDED but lost // its response, not to permanently lock out a real failure. if (_requestId && env.DB) { env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM processed_requests WHERE request_id=?').bind(_requestId).run().catch(()=>{}); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Transfer partially or fully failed to save — please retry. ' + (!d1Out ? 'OUT entry failed. ' : '') + (!d1In ? 'IN entry failed.' : ''), transferId, outD1Id, inD1Id }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const transferLogResponseBody = { ok: true, transferId, outD1Id, inD1Id }; if (_requestId) await recordRequestResult(env, _requestId, transferLogResponseBody); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify(transferLogResponseBody), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (err) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: err.message || 'transfer log failed' }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/transfer/verify // Approves or rejects a pending transfer pair. // Approve: subtract from FROM row, add to TO row (or insert new row), mark both Verified. // Reject: mark both Rejected. No Master_List changes. // Uses live row lookup — never trusts stored row indices. async function inventoryTransferVerify(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { action, outItem, inItem } = body; if (!action || !outItem) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing fields' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const verifiedBy = 'MGMT'; if (action === 'Rejected' || action === 'reject') { // BUGFIX: neither D1 status update below was ever awaited — both were // fire-and-forget with .catch(()=>{}) silently swallowing errors, and // the function returned {ok:true} immediately without waiting for // either write to actually happen. In a Cloudflare Worker, once a // Response is returned, the execution context can be frozen/reused at // any point after — an un-awaited promise with no ctx.waitUntil() has // no guarantee of ever completing. This is exactly why reject could // report "successful" while the log entries stayed Pending forever // and the card never left Review: the actual database write may // never have run at all. Now awaited, with real failure surfaced. const outResult = await d1UpdateLogStatus(env, outItem.rowIndex||0, 'Rejected', verifiedBy, ts, outItem.partNum, outItem.location, 'TRANSFER_OUT', outItem.d1Id||null); const inResult = inItem ? await d1UpdateLogStatus(env, inItem.rowIndex||0, 'Rejected', verifiedBy, ts, inItem.partNum, inItem.location, 'TRANSFER_IN', inItem.d1Id||null) : true; // Sheets is a fire-and-forget mirror, not the source of truth // /inventory/pending reads from — fine to leave un-awaited. invSheetUpdate(env, `Inventory_Log!H${outItem.rowIndex}:J${outItem.rowIndex}`, [['Rejected', verifiedBy, ts]]).catch(()=>{}); if (inItem && inItem.rowIndex) invSheetUpdate(env, `Inventory_Log!H${inItem.rowIndex}:J${inItem.rowIndex}`, [['Rejected', verifiedBy, ts]]).catch(()=>{}); if (!outResult || !inResult) { console.error('[Transfer] Reject FAILED — outOk:', !!outResult, 'inOk:', !!inResult, 'outItem:', outItem.d1Id, 'inItem:', inItem?.d1Id); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Reject failed to save — please retry. ' + (!outResult ? 'OUT entry failed. ' : '') + (!inResult ? 'IN entry failed.' : '') }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── APPROVE ── Phase 3: D1 primary throughout ── const partNum = (outItem.partNum || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const fromLocation = (outItem.location || '').trim(); const toLocation = inItem ? (inItem.location || '').trim() : (outItem.pairedLocation || '').trim(); const cases = parseFloat(outItem.cases) || 0; const sku = (outItem.sku || '').trim(); const isNewLoc = inItem ? (inItem.isNew === 'TRUE' || inItem.isNew === true) : false; const baseSku = sku.split('=')[0].trim().toUpperCase() || partNum.split('=')[0].trim(); // 1. Find FROM row in D1 — no Sheets read needed // BUGFIX: this previously ALWAYS resolved by text-matching // part_num+location, which breaks if that location's text changes // between when the transfer is queued and when it's approved — e.g. // a receiving pallet gets received again, or partially shelved, // renaming the row's location out from under an already-queued // transfer. This is confirmed to have caused repeated "FROM row not // found in D1" failures on exactly this kind of pallet-to-shelf // transfer. Now prefers resolving by outItem.masterId (the real D1 // row ID, captured client-side at pick time) when available — the // row can be found correctly regardless of what its location text // says by approval time. Falls back to the original text-match only // for older pending items logged before this fix existed. const fromMasterId = outItem.masterId ? (parseInt(outItem.masterId) || null) : null; let d1FromRow = null; if (fromMasterId) { d1FromRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT id, sheet_row, cases, units_per_case, vendor, price, prev_notes, name FROM master_list WHERE id=? LIMIT 1', [fromMasterId] ).catch(()=>null); } if (!d1FromRow) { d1FromRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT id, sheet_row, cases, units_per_case, vendor, price, prev_notes, name FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(part_num)=? AND UPPER(location)=? LIMIT 1', [partNum, fromLocation.toUpperCase()] ).catch(()=>null); } if (!d1FromRow) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: `FROM row not found in D1 for ${partNum} @ ${fromLocation}` }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const liveFromRow = d1FromRow.sheet_row; const fromWhereClause = 'id = ?'; // always resolve the actual UPDATE by id now that we have it, unambiguous either way this row was found const fromWhereValue = d1FromRow.id; const fromJ = String(d1FromRow.units_per_case || ''); const fromM = String(d1FromRow.vendor || ''); const fromN = String(d1FromRow.price || ''); const fromName = String(d1FromRow.name || outItem.name || ''); // 1. Subtract cases from FROM — atomic SQL, resolved by id (see above) await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE master_list SET cases = MAX(0, cases - ?), updated_at = ? WHERE ${fromWhereClause}`) .bind(cases, ts, fromWhereValue).run(); if (liveFromRow) { invSetPurpleCell(env, liveFromRow).catch(()=>{}); invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!I${liveFromRow}`, [[(parseFloat(d1FromRow.cases)||0) - cases]]).catch(()=>{}); } // BUGFIX: regular stock-out approvals (inventoryVerify) have always // cleared the location once cases confirmedly hit exactly 0 — transfer // approvals never had the equivalent logic, so a FROM location that // gets fully emptied by a transfer stayed listed with 0 cases instead // of being cleared. Same safety rule as the regular flow: read back // the ACTUAL value after the update (never trust a client-computed // number), and only clear when it's confirmed exactly 0 — a failed or // uncertain read-back must never clear a location that might still // hold stock. const fromReadback = await d1First(env, `SELECT cases FROM master_list WHERE ${fromWhereClause}`, [fromWhereValue]).catch(()=>null); const fromNewCases = fromReadback ? (parseFloat(fromReadback.cases) || 0) : null; if (typeof fromNewCases === 'number' && fromNewCases === 0) { await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE master_list SET location=? WHERE ${fromWhereClause}`).bind('', fromWhereValue).run().catch(()=>{}); if (liveFromRow) invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!H${liveFromRow}`, [['']]).catch(()=>{}); } // 2. Find TO row in D1 const d1ToRow = isNewLoc ? null : await d1First(env, 'SELECT sheet_row, cases FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(part_num)=? AND UPPER(location)=? LIMIT 1', [partNum, toLocation.toUpperCase()] ).catch(()=>null); if (isNewLoc || !d1ToRow) { // New location — find max sheet_row, insert after it. // BUGFIX: was scoped to WHERE base_sku=? — same cross-part sheet_row // collision risk as inventoryVerify's isNew-IN path (see that fix's // comment for the full explanation and a reproduced example). Fixed // to use the same global max across the whole table. const lastRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT MAX(sheet_row) as max_row FROM master_list WHERE sheet_row > 0' ).catch(()=>null); const newRowNum = (lastRow?.max_row || liveFromRow) + 1; const newK = [fromLocation].filter(Boolean).join(' '); // Insert into D1 await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO master_list (sku,base_sku,name,part_num,location,cases,units_per_case,vendor,price,prev_notes,sheet_row,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind(sku, baseSku, fromName, partNum, toLocation, cases, parseFloat(fromJ)||0, fromM, parseFloat(fromN)||0, newK, newRowNum, ts).run(); // Auto-register new TO location if (toLocation && env.DB) { const toPrefix = toLocation.includes('=') ? toLocation.split('=')[0].trim() : ''; env.DB.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO locations (location,prefix,active,created_at) VALUES (?,?,1,?)') .bind(toLocation.toUpperCase(), toPrefix, ts).run().catch(()=>{}); } // Fire-and-forget Sheets new row invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!G${newRowNum}:O${newRowNum}`, [[ partNum, toLocation, cases, fromJ||'', newK, fromJ ? `=I${newRowNum}*J${newRowNum}` : '', fromM, fromN, fromN ? `=L${newRowNum}*N${newRowNum}` : '' ]]).catch(()=>{}); invSetPurpleCell(env, newRowNum).catch(()=>{}); } else { // Existing TO row — add cases atomically await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE master_list SET cases = cases + ?, updated_at = ? WHERE sheet_row = ?') .bind(cases, ts, d1ToRow.sheet_row).run(); invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!I${d1ToRow.sheet_row}`, [[(parseFloat(d1ToRow.cases)||0) + cases]]).catch(()=>{}); invSetPurpleCell(env, d1ToRow.sheet_row).catch(()=>{}); } // 3. Mark both log rows Verified in D1 — fire-and-forget Sheets await d1UpdateLogStatus(env, outItem.rowIndex||0, 'Verified', verifiedBy, ts, outItem.partNum, outItem.location, 'TRANSFER_OUT', outItem.d1Id||null); if (inItem) await d1UpdateLogStatus(env, inItem.rowIndex||0, 'Verified', verifiedBy, ts, inItem.partNum, inItem.location, 'TRANSFER_IN', inItem.d1Id||null); invSheetUpdate(env, `Inventory_Log!H${outItem.rowIndex}:J${outItem.rowIndex}`, [['Verified', verifiedBy, ts]]).catch(()=>{}); if (inItem && inItem.rowIndex) invSheetUpdate(env, `Inventory_Log!H${inItem.rowIndex}:J${inItem.rowIndex}`, [['Verified', verifiedBy, ts]]).catch(()=>{}); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (err) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: err.message || 'transfer verify failed' }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/po-log // Writes a PO note to Master_List col H for all rows matching the base SKU // where col I (cases) is blank/zero — these are the "on order" placeholder rows // Note format: "M/D/YY order QTY" — matches existing management convention async function inventoryPoLog(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { sku, note } = body; if (!sku || !note) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'sku and note required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const baseSku = sku.split('=')[0].trim().toUpperCase(); const mlData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:I15000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const mlRows = mlData.values || []; // Find all rows for this base SKU where cases (col I) is blank or 0 const updates = []; for (let i = 1; i < mlRows.length; i++) { const r = mlRows[i]; const rowSku = String(r[3] || '').trim().toUpperCase().split('=')[0]; const rowPart = String(r[6] || '').trim().toUpperCase().split('=')[0]; if (rowSku !== baseSku && rowPart !== baseSku) continue; const cases = String(r[8] || '').trim(); // Only update rows where cases is blank or 0 (on-order rows) if (cases !== '' && parseFloat(cases) > 0) continue; const rowNum = i + 1; const existing = String(r[7] || '').trim(); // current col H value // Append note to existing (may already have a date note) const newNote = existing && existing !== note ? existing + '\n' + note : note; updates.push({ range: `Master_List!H${rowNum}`, majorDimension: 'ROWS', values: [[newNote]] }); } if (!updates.length) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'No matching on-order rows found for ' + baseSku }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const token = await getToken(env); const resp = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHEET_ID}/values:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ valueInputOption: 'RAW', data: updates }) }); const result = await resp.json(); if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(result.error?.message || 'batchUpdate failed'); await invalidateMasterListCache(env); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, updated: updates.length, note }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/location-search?loc=BARN=5-1 async function inventoryLocationSearch(url, env) { try { const q = (url.searchParams.get('loc') || '').trim().toUpperCase(); if (!q || q.length < 2) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Query too short' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); let mlRows = [], pendingMap = {}; if (env.DB) { try { // masterId (real D1 primary key) added — sheet_row alone can't // disambiguate two genuinely different rows sharing the same // part_num+location (e.g. two separate 13-case/41-case entries at // the same physical shelf): sheet_row is commonly 0 for rows // created directly in D1 (Receive PO inserts), so multiple such // rows would collide on rowIndex too. masterId is always unique. const [d1Rows, pMap] = await Promise.all([ d1All(env, `SELECT id,sku,name,part_num,location,cases,units_per_case,sheet_row FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(location) = ? OR UPPER(location) LIKE ? OR UPPER(location) LIKE ?`, [q, q + '-%', q + '=%']), buildPendingMap(env) ]); for (const r of d1Rows) { const pending = pMap[(r.part_num||'').toUpperCase()+'|'+(r.location||'').toUpperCase()] || 0; const casesNum = parseFloat(r.cases) || 0; mlRows.push({ rowIndex: r.sheet_row||0, masterId: r.id, partNum: r.part_num||'', location: r.location||'', cases: String(r.cases??0), casesNum, name: r.name||'', adjustedCases: Math.max(0, casesNum - pending), pendingCases: pending }); } pendingMap = pMap; mlRows.sort((a,b) => a.location.localeCompare(b.location)||a.partNum.localeCompare(b.partNum)); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, results:mlRows, count:mlRows.length, source:'d1' }), { headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] locSearch fallback:',e.message); mlRows=[]; } } // Sheets fallback const [mlData, pMapFb] = await Promise.all([ invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(()=>({values:[]})), buildPendingMap(env) ]); mlRows = mlData.values || []; pendingMap = pMapFb; const results = []; for (let i = 1; i < mlRows.length; i++) { const r = mlRows[i]; const loc = String(r[7] || '').trim(); const partNum = String(r[6] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); if (!loc || !partNum) continue; if (!loc.toUpperCase().includes(q)) continue; const cases = String(r[8] || '').trim(); const casesNum = parseFloat(cases) || 0; const name = String(r[4] || '').trim(); const pk = partNum + '|' + loc.toUpperCase(); const pending = pendingMap[pk] || 0; results.push({ rowIndex: i+1, partNum, location: loc, cases, casesNum, adjustedCases: Math.max(0, casesNum-pending), pendingCases: pending, name }); } results.sort((a,b) => a.location.localeCompare(b.location) || a.partNum.localeCompare(b.partNum)); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, results, count: results.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/suggest-location?partNum=26-2-1=2&fromLoc=BARN=1-1-1 // GET /inventory/suggest-location?partNum=X&fromLoc=Y // Rebuilt on D1 — was reading Master_List!G2:I15000 + a separate LocationID // Sheets tab directly, which is slow (up to 15k-row Sheets reads) and, // worse, blind to any row with sheet_row=0 (rows created directly in D1 by // Receive PO for a brand-new SKU/location — never synced to Sheets, so // invisible to a Sheets-only query). D1 is the actual source of truth for // current cases/location, so this now matches inventoryLocations' proven // pattern instead. Response shape (existing/emptyNearby/emptyOther) is // UNCHANGED — this is a pure backend swap, no frontend changes needed. async function inventorySuggestLocation(url, env) { if (!env.DB) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } try { const partNum = String(url.searchParams.get('partNum') || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const fromLoc = String(url.searchParams.get('fromLoc') || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const baseSku = partNum.split('=')[0].trim(); // Existing locations already holding stock of this exact part_num OR // this base_sku (any variant) — mirrors the original's matching logic. // BUGFIX: exclude fromLoc — suggesting the location the worker is // transferring OUT OF as a destination is never useful (it trivially // always "has stock," since that's the source of the transfer). const existingRows = await d1All(env, `SELECT location, SUM(cases) as cases FROM master_list WHERE (UPPER(part_num)=? OR UPPER(base_sku)=?) AND cases > 0 AND location != '' AND UPPER(location) != ? GROUP BY location ORDER BY cases DESC`, [partNum, baseSku, fromLoc] ); const existing = existingRows.map(r => ({ location: r.location, cases: r.cases })); // Empty locations — registered in D1's locations table (auto-registered // whenever a new location is used elsewhere in the app) with either no // master_list rows or all-zero cases. Same LEFT JOIN pattern already // used by inventoryLocations. Also excludes fromLoc for the same reason. const emptyRows = await d1All(env, `SELECT l.location FROM locations l LEFT JOIN master_list m ON UPPER(m.location) = UPPER(l.location) WHERE l.active = 1 AND UPPER(l.location) != ? GROUP BY l.location HAVING COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN m.cases > 0 THEN m.cases ELSE 0 END), 0) = 0 ORDER BY l.prefix, l.location`, [fromLoc] ); const emptyLocs = emptyRows.map(r => r.location.toUpperCase()); let nearby = [], other = []; if (fromLoc) { const fromPrefix = fromLoc.indexOf('=') >= 0 ? fromLoc.slice(0, fromLoc.indexOf('=')+1) : ''; emptyLocs.forEach(l => { const lPrefix = l.indexOf('=') >= 0 ? l.slice(0, l.indexOf('=')+1) : ''; if (lPrefix === fromPrefix) nearby.push(l); else other.push(l); }); } else { other = emptyLocs; } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, existing, emptyNearby: nearby.slice(0,10), emptyOther: other.slice(0,5) }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/location-list async function inventoryLocationList(env) { try { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'LocationID!A2:A5000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const locations = (data.values || []).map(r => String(r[0]||'').trim()).filter(Boolean).map(loc => ({ location: loc, barcodeId: loc })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, locations, count: locations.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/location-prefixes // Rebuilt on D1 — was reading Master_List!H2:I15000 directly from Sheets, // which is a fire-and-forget mirror now, not the source of truth. Blind to // any row with sheet_row=0 (Receive PO inserts for brand-new SKU/location // combos that never got synced to Sheets), so counts/prefixes found here // could be incomplete. Response shape unchanged — no frontend changes needed. async function inventoryLocationPrefixes(env) { if (!env.DB) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } try { const rows = await d1All(env, "SELECT location FROM master_list WHERE cases > 0 AND location != ''"); const prefixMap = {}; for (const r of rows) { const loc = String(r.location||'').trim(); if (!loc) continue; const eqIdx = loc.indexOf('='); const prefix = eqIdx >= 0 ? loc.slice(0, eqIdx+1) : loc.split(/[\s\-]/)[0]; if (!prefix) continue; if (!prefixMap[prefix]) prefixMap[prefix] = { count: 0, sample: loc }; prefixMap[prefix].count++; } const prefixes = Object.entries(prefixMap).map(([prefix, v]) => ({ prefix, count: v.count, sample: v.sample })).sort((a,b) => b.count - a.count); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, prefixes }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } const DEFAULT_PREFIXES = ['2FL=','BARN=','BASEMENT=','BH=','C1=','C2=','C3=','C4=','C5=','FRONT=']; // GET /inventory/prefixes — reads from the Prefixes Google Sheets tab. // Reverted from a D1-backed version after two rounds of D1 fixes didn't // resolve reported cross-device sync issues that couldn't be reproduced // or diagnosed further without live error data. Sheets is easier to // verify by eye (open the tab directly), and the actual most-likely root // cause of cross-device staleness — missing Cache-Control headers on API // responses — is already fixed globally in cors() regardless of which // backend stores the data, so that fix stays in effect either way. async function inventoryGetPrefixes(env) { try { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Prefixes!A2:A100'); if (data.error) { console.error('[Prefixes] GET Sheets error:', data.error.message || JSON.stringify(data.error)); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Sheets read failed: ' + (data.error.message || 'unknown') }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const prefixes = (data.values || []).map(r => String(r[0]||'').trim()).filter(Boolean); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, prefixes: prefixes.length ? prefixes : DEFAULT_PREFIXES, source: 'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { console.error('[Prefixes] GET failed:', e.message); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/prefixes — full replace of the Prefixes Sheets tab. // BUGFIX vs the original Sheets implementation: that version never // checked whether the Sheets API calls actually succeeded before // reporting ok:true — the exact same "always claim success" pattern // found and fixed elsewhere this session (SKU Manager save, transfer // log). Now checks both the clear and the write for real errors. async function inventorySetPrefixes(request, env) { try { const { prefixes } = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!prefixes || !Array.isArray(prefixes) || !prefixes.length) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'prefixes array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const token = await getToken(env); const clearRes = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent('Prefixes!A:A')}:clear`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }); const clearData = await clearRes.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!clearRes.ok || clearData.error) { console.error('[Prefixes] POST clear failed:', clearRes.status, JSON.stringify(clearData)); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Failed to clear Prefixes sheet: ' + (clearData.error?.message || clearRes.status) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const values = [['LOCATIONPREFIX'], ...prefixes.map(p => [String(p).trim()])]; const writeRes = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent('Prefixes!A1')}?valueInputOption=RAW`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values }) }); const writeData = await writeRes.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!writeRes.ok || writeData.error) { console.error('[Prefixes] POST write failed:', writeRes.status, JSON.stringify(writeData)); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Failed to write Prefixes sheet: ' + (writeData.error?.message || writeRes.status) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } console.log('[Prefixes] Saved', prefixes.length, 'prefixes:', prefixes.join(',')); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, count: prefixes.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { console.error('[Prefixes] POST failed:', e.message); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/rename-locations // Rebuilt on D1 — this was the more serious gap: it only ever wrote to the // Sheets mirror (Master_List!H column), never touching master_list.location // in D1 at all. Since D1 is the actual source of truth the rest of the app // reads from (lookups, location search, stock in/out, suggestions), a // rename here would show the new prefix in Sheets while every real // operation in the app kept silently using the OLD prefix underneath — // the rename would appear to succeed with zero actual effect. // Now updates D1 first (master_list AND the locations registry table, so // suggestions/empty-shelf lookups stay consistent too), Sheets mirrored // fire-and-forget after. async function inventoryRenameLocations(request, env) { if (!env.DB) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } try { const { oldPrefix, newPrefix } = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!oldPrefix || !newPrefix) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'oldPrefix and newPrefix required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const oldP = oldPrefix.trim(); const newP = newPrefix.trim(); const oldLen = oldP.length; // Find every master_list row whose location starts with oldPrefix — // compare the exact-length prefix substring rather than LIKE, avoiding // any wildcard-character edge cases in the prefix text. const mlRows = await d1All(env, `SELECT id, location FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(SUBSTR(location, 1, ?)) = UPPER(?) AND location != ''`, [oldLen, oldP] ); const ts = new Date().toISOString(); let updated = 0; const CHUNK = 50; for (let i = 0; i < mlRows.length; i += CHUNK) { const chunk = mlRows.slice(i, i + CHUNK); const stmts = chunk.map(r => { const newLoc = newP + r.location.slice(oldLen); return env.DB.prepare('UPDATE master_list SET location=?, updated_at=? WHERE id=?') .bind(newLoc, ts, r.id); }); await env.DB.batch(stmts); updated += chunk.length; } // Also rename matching entries in the locations registry table, so // empty-shelf suggestions and the Locations admin view stay consistent // with what master_list now says. const locRows = await d1All(env, `SELECT location FROM locations WHERE UPPER(SUBSTR(location, 1, ?)) = UPPER(?)`, [oldLen, oldP] ); for (let i = 0; i < locRows.length; i += CHUNK) { const chunk = locRows.slice(i, i + CHUNK); const stmts = chunk.map(r => { const newLoc = newP + r.location.slice(oldLen); return env.DB.prepare('UPDATE locations SET location=?, prefix=? WHERE UPPER(location)=?') .bind(newLoc, newP, r.location.toUpperCase()); }); await env.DB.batch(stmts); } // Sheets fire-and-forget mirror — best-effort, D1 is already correct // regardless of whether this succeeds. (async () => { try { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!H2:H15000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const rows = data.values || []; const sheetUpdates = []; for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { const loc = String(rows[i][0]||'').trim(); if (loc.toUpperCase().startsWith(oldP.toUpperCase())) { sheetUpdates.push({ range: `Master_List!H${i+2}`, values: [[newP + loc.slice(oldLen)]] }); } } if (sheetUpdates.length) { const token = await getToken(env); await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHEET_ID}/values:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ valueInputOption: 'RAW', data: sheetUpdates.map(u => ({ range: u.range, majorDimension: 'ROWS', values: u.values })) }) }); } } catch(e) { console.error('[Rename] Sheets mirror failed:', e.message); } })(); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, updated }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/audit-mode async function inventoryAuditModeGet(env) { try { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Config!A2:B50').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const rows = data.values || []; let mode = 'manual'; for (const r of rows) { if (String(r[0]||'').trim().toLowerCase() === 'audit_mode') { mode = String(r[1]||'manual').trim().toLowerCase(); break; } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, mode }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, mode: 'manual' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/audit-mode async function inventoryAuditModeSet(request, env) { try { const { mode } = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); if (mode !== 'auto' && mode !== 'manual') return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Invalid mode' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Config!A2:B50').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const rows = data.values || []; let rowIdx = -1; for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { if (String(rows[i][0]||'').trim().toLowerCase() === 'audit_mode') { rowIdx = i+2; break; } } if (rowIdx > 0) { await invSheetUpdate(env, `Config!B${rowIdx}`, [[mode]]);
