} else { try { await invSheetAppend(env, 'Config!A:B', [['audit_mode', mode]]); } catch(e) { const token = await getToken(env); await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHEET_ID}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests: [{ addSheet: { properties: { title: 'Config' } } }] }) }); await invSheetAppend(env, 'Config!A:B', [['audit_mode', mode]]); } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, mode }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/receive-preview // Returns all D1 master_list rows + cogs prices for the frontend to match against pallet sheet. // Frontend finds the first row per partNum where cases is blank/null. async function inventoryReceivePreview(env) { try { // Read master_list from D1 const d1Rows = await d1All(env, `SELECT id, sheet_row, part_num, base_sku, location, cases, prev_notes, updated_at FROM master_list WHERE part_num != '' ORDER BY sheet_row ASC`, [] ); // Read cogs prices from D1 const cogsRows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT base_sku, unit_price FROM cogs', [] ); const cogsMap = {}; (cogsRows || []).forEach(r => { cogsMap[r.base_sku] = parseFloat(r.unit_price) || 0; }); const rows = (d1Rows || []).map(r => ({ rowIndex: r.sheet_row || 0, d1Id: r.id, partNum: (r.part_num || '').trim().toUpperCase(), baseSku: (r.base_sku || '').trim().toUpperCase(), location: (r.location || '').trim(), cases: (r.cases != null && r.cases !== '' && parseFloat(r.cases) !== 0) ? String(r.cases) : '', prevNotes: (r.prev_notes || '').trim(), })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ rows, cogsMap }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (err) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: err.message || 'preview failed' }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/receive-apply // D1-only rewrite — no Sheets writes. // Updates master_list cases + location, writes inventory_log IN entry, // updates cogs + cogs_history if price provided. // Accepts both EFF/EFF1 and JQ/JQ1 sheet names from frontend. async function inventoryReceiveApply(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { d1Id, rowIndex, partNum, baseSku, pallet, vendor, cases, upc, pricePerUnit, colKValue, logPrice, initials } = body; if (!partNum || !pallet) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing required fields' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const casesNum = parseFloat(cases) || 0; const priceNum = pricePerUnit != null ? parseFloat(pricePerUnit) : null; const today = new Date(); const palletDate = (today.getMonth()+1) + '/' + today.getDate() + '/' + String(today.getFullYear()).slice(-2); // BUGFIX: pallet was appended with today's date unconditionally, with // no check for whether it already HAD a date on it. If the same // pallet/PO gets received again (a split shipment, a re-scan, or the // frontend pre-filling this field from the row's current location on // a second pass) before ever being moved to a permanent shelf, the // date kept accumulating — "JQ2512A-16 3/17/26" received again became // "JQ2512A-16 3/17/26 3/17/26". That doubled string is what breaks // transfer approval later: a transfer queued against the single-date // location fails with "row not found" once a second receive silently // renamed the row's location out from under it. Strip any existing // trailing date(s) first (a loop, not a single strip, since data // already corrupted by this bug in the past may have more than one) // so the final location always carries exactly one, current date. let palletBase = String(pallet || '').trim(); const trailingDatePattern = /\s+\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{2,4}\s*$/; while (trailingDatePattern.test(palletBase)) { palletBase = palletBase.replace(trailingDatePattern, ''); } const palletWithDate = palletBase + ' ' + palletDate; const bs = (baseSku || partNum.split('=')[0]).trim().toUpperCase(); // ── 1. Update D1 master_list ───────────────────────────────────────── // Find row by d1Id if available, fallback to sheet_row let d1Row = null; if (d1Id) { d1Row = await d1First(env, 'SELECT * FROM master_list WHERE id = ?', [d1Id]); } if (!d1Row && rowIndex) { d1Row = await d1First(env, 'SELECT * FROM master_list WHERE sheet_row = ? AND UPPER(part_num) = ?', [rowIndex, partNum.toUpperCase()]); } if (!d1Row) { // ── INSERT path — no existing blank-cases row found ───────────────── // Insert new row into D1 master_list directly const upcNumNew = parseFloat(body.unitsPerCase) || 0; const insertResult = await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO master_list (part_num, base_sku, location, cases, price, units_per_case, prev_notes, sheet_row, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?)` ).bind( partNum.toUpperCase(), bs, palletWithDate, casesNum, priceNum != null ? priceNum : 0, upcNumNew, colKValue || '', ts ).run(); const newId = insertResult?.meta?.last_row_id || null; // Write inventory_log for new insert const logNoteNew = `[RECEIVE PO NEW] Pallet: ${palletWithDate}${vendor ? ' · Vendor: ' + vendor : ''}${priceNum != null ? ' · Price: $' + priceNum.toFixed(3) : ''}`; await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO inventory_log (sheet_row, timestamp, type, part_num, location, cases, initials, notes, status, verified_by, verified_at, is_new, is_placeholder, master_row_index, sku) VALUES (0,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,0,0,?)` ).bind(ts, 'IN', partNum.toUpperCase(), palletWithDate, casesNum, initials || 'MGMT', logNoteNew, 'Verified', initials || 'MGMT', ts, bs ).run(); // Update cogs if price provided if (logPrice && priceNum != null) { try { const oldCogs = await d1First(env, 'SELECT unit_price FROM cogs WHERE base_sku = ?', [bs]); const oldPrice = oldCogs ? parseFloat(oldCogs.unit_price) : null; if (oldPrice === null || Math.abs(oldPrice - priceNum) > 0.00001) { await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO cogs (base_sku, unit_price, updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(base_sku) DO UPDATE SET unit_price=excluded.unit_price, updated_at=excluded.updated_at` ).bind(bs, priceNum, ts).run(); await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO cogs_history (base_sku, unit_price, note, source, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind(bs, priceNum, `${vendor || 'PO'} ${palletDate}`, 'receive_po', ts).run(); } } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] cogs insert error:', e.message); } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, inserted: true, d1Id: newId }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Move old location to prev_notes, set new pallet location + cases const oldLocation = (d1Row.location || '').trim(); const existingNotes = (d1Row.prev_notes || '').trim(); const newPrevNotes = oldLocation ? (existingNotes ? existingNotes + ' | ' + oldLocation : oldLocation) : existingNotes; const upcNum = parseFloat(body.unitsPerCase) || 0; await env.DB.prepare( 'UPDATE master_list SET cases=?, location=?, prev_notes=?, price=?, units_per_case=CASE WHEN units_per_case=0 OR units_per_case IS NULL THEN ? ELSE units_per_case END, updated_at=? WHERE id=?' ).bind(casesNum, palletWithDate, newPrevNotes, priceNum != null ? priceNum : (d1Row.price || 0), upcNum, ts, d1Row.id).run(); // ── 2. Write inventory_log entry (IN, auto-verified) ──────────────── const logNote = `[RECEIVE PO] Pallet: ${palletWithDate}${vendor ? ' · Vendor: ' + vendor : ''}${priceNum != null ? ' · Price: $' + priceNum.toFixed(3) : ''}`; await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO inventory_log (sheet_row, timestamp, type, part_num, location, cases, initials, notes, status, verified_by, verified_at, is_new, is_placeholder, master_row_index, sku) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,0,0,?,?)` ).bind( d1Row.sheet_row || 0, ts, 'IN', partNum, palletWithDate, casesNum, initials || 'MGMT', logNote, 'Verified', initials || 'MGMT', ts, d1Row.sheet_row || 0, bs ).run(); // ── 3. Update cogs + cogs_history if price provided ────────────────── if (logPrice && priceNum != null) { try { // Get old price from D1 cogs const oldCogs = await d1First(env, 'SELECT unit_price FROM cogs WHERE base_sku = ?', [bs]); const oldPrice = oldCogs ? parseFloat(oldCogs.unit_price) : null; const priceChanged = oldPrice === null || Math.abs(oldPrice - priceNum) > 0.00001; if (priceChanged) { // Upsert cogs await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO cogs (base_sku, unit_price, updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(base_sku) DO UPDATE SET unit_price=excluded.unit_price, updated_at=excluded.updated_at` ).bind(bs, priceNum, ts).run(); // Insert cogs_history const noteStr = `${vendor || 'PO'} ${palletDate}`; await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO cogs_history (base_sku, unit_price, note, source, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind(bs, priceNum, noteStr, 'receive_po', ts).run(); } } catch (priceErr) { console.error('[D1] cogs update error:', priceErr.message); // non-fatal — don't fail the whole apply } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, d1Id: d1Row.id }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (err) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: err.message || 'apply failed' }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── Row lookup helpers — always find Master_List rows fresh at approve time ── // Matches by partNum (col G, index 6) + exact location (col H, index 7). // Immune to row insertions/deletions that would make a stored rowIndex stale. function findMasterRow(mlRows, partNum, location) { const pn = (partNum || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const loc = (location || '').trim().toLowerCase(); // When multiple rows match same partNum+location, prefer highest cases // prevents subtracting from a stale 0-case duplicate row let bestIdx = 0, bestCases = -Infinity; for (let i = 1; i < mlRows.length; i++) { const rowPart = String(mlRows[i][6] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const rowLoc = String(mlRows[i][7] || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (rowPart === pn && rowLoc === loc) { const rowCases = parseFloat(mlRows[i][8]) || 0; if (rowCases > bestCases) { bestCases = rowCases; bestIdx = i + 1; } } } return bestIdx; } // Find a placeholder row for a part number (location text contains "will be here") function findMasterRowPlaceholder(mlRows, partNum) { const pn = (partNum || '').trim().toUpperCase(); for (let i = 1; i < mlRows.length; i++) { const rowPart = String(mlRows[i][6] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const rowLoc = String(mlRows[i][7] || '').trim(); if (rowPart === pn && /will be here/i.test(rowLoc)) return i + 1; } return 0; } // Helper: get Master_List sheetId (gid) for batchUpdate // Helper: set background color on a cell range in Master_List async function invSetPurpleCell(env, rowNum) { try { const sheetId = await getMasterListSheetId(env); // Col I = index 8 (0-based), rowNum is 1-based so startRowIndex = rowNum-1 await batchUpdateSheets(env, env.SHEET_ID, [{ repeatCell: { range: { sheetId, startRowIndex: rowNum - 1, endRowIndex: rowNum, startColumnIndex: 8, // col I endColumnIndex: 9 }, cell: { userEnteredFormat: { backgroundColor: { red: 0.6, green: 0.2, blue: 0.8 } } }, fields: 'userEnteredFormat.backgroundColor' } }]); } catch(e) { // Non-fatal — don't fail the whole verify console.error('Purple cell error:', e); } } async function invSetTealCell(env, rowNum) { try { const sheetId = await getMasterListSheetId(env); // Cyan on cols H+I (location + cases) for audit/found-on-shelf operations await batchUpdateSheets(env, env.SHEET_ID, [{ repeatCell: { range: { sheetId, startRowIndex: rowNum-1, endRowIndex: rowNum, startColumnIndex: 7, endColumnIndex: 9 }, cell: { userEnteredFormat: { backgroundColor: { red: 0.698, green: 0.922, blue: 0.949 } } }, fields: 'userEnteredFormat.backgroundColor' } }]); } catch(e) { console.error('Teal cell error:', e); } } async function invalidateMasterListCache(env) { // no-op — KV cache not yet configured; here for API compatibility } let _masterListSheetIdCache = null; async function getMasterListSheetId(env) { if (_masterListSheetIdCache !== null) return _masterListSheetIdCache; const token = await getToken(env); const r = await fetch(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${env.SHEET_ID}?fields=sheets.properties`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }); const d = await r.json(); const sheet = (d.sheets || []).find(s => s.properties?.title === 'Master_List'); _masterListSheetIdCache = sheet?.properties?.sheetId ?? 0; return _masterListSheetIdCache; } // Helper: Sheets batchUpdate (for insertDimension) async function batchUpdateSheets(env, sheetId, requests) { const token = await getToken(env); const r = await fetch(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${sheetId}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests }) }); if (!r.ok) { const e = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); throw new Error(e.error?.message || `batchUpdate failed ${r.status}`); } return r.json(); } let _currentOrigin = "*"; export default { async fetch(request, env) { const url = new URL(request.url); const method = request.method; const origin = request.headers.get('Origin') || '*'; _currentOrigin = origin; // Top-level safety net — ensures CORS headers are always present even on crashes try { if (method === 'OPTIONS') { return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: { 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': origin, 'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET,POST,PUT,OPTIONS', 'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type,X-API-Key,X-Session-Token,X-Session', 'Access-Control-Max-Age': '86400', } }); } // /auth — no token needed if (url.pathname === '/auth' && method === 'POST') { return handleAuth(request, env); } // Webhooks — no token needed const isWebhook = url.pathname.startsWith('/webhook/'); if (isWebhook) { return handleWebhook(request, env, url); } // /msg/send and /msg/list — fully open, no session required // reply and status auth is enforced inside handleMsgRoute if (url.pathname === '/msg/send' && method === 'POST') { try { return await msgSend(request, env); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } if (url.pathname === '/msg/list' && method === 'GET') { try { return await msgList(url, env, null); const sheetId = getSheetId(tab, env); const range = encodeURIComponent(`${tab}`); const res = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${sheetId}/values/${range}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }); const data = await res.json(); if (data.error) throw new Error(data.error.message); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values: data.values || [] }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ─── EXISTING PUT VALIDATION (unchanged) ────────────────────────────────────── const PUT_RULES = { Amz30: { maxRows: 50000, maxCols: 30, requiredCols: ['order-id','sku','quantity'] }, Amz90: { maxRows: 50000, maxCols: 30, requiredCols: ['order-id','sku','quantity'] }, Ebay30: { maxRows: 50000, maxCols: 30, requiredCols: ['sales record number','item number','sold for'] }, Ebay90: { maxRows: 50000, maxCols: 30, requiredCols: ['sales record number','item number','sold for'] }, XF30: { maxRows: 50000, maxCols: 30, requiredCols: ['sku','quantity'] }, XF90: { maxRows: 50000, maxCols: 30, requiredCols: ['sku','quantity'] }, }; function validatePutPayload(tab, rows) { const rule = PUT_RULES[tab]; if (!rule) return null; if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) return 'Empty payload'; if (rows.length > rule.maxRows) return 'Too many rows: ' + rows.length + ' (max ' + rule.maxRows + ')'; const headers = (rows[0] || []).map(h => String(h).toLowerCase().trim()); const missing = rule.requiredCols.filter(c => !headers.includes(c.toLowerCase())); if (missing.length > 0) return 'Missing required columns: ' + missing.join(', '); const widest = Math.max(...rows.map(r => r.length)); if (widest > rule.maxCols) return 'Row too wide: ' + widest + ' columns (max ' + rule.maxCols + ')'; return null; } // ─── EXISTING handlePut (unchanged) ────────────────────────────────────────── async function handlePut(tab, request, env) { try { const body = await request.json(); } const token = await getToken(env); const sheetId = getSheetId(tab, env); const tabRange = encodeURIComponent(`${tab}!A1`); const tabClear = encodeURIComponent(`${tab}`); // ── Clear the sheet first so stale rows from a longer previous report don't persist ── await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${sheetId}/values/${tabClear}:clear`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, } ); } } // ─── EXISTING handleAI (unchanged) ─────────────────────────────────────────── async function handleAI(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json(); const res = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-api-key': env.ANTHROPIC_KEY || env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' }, body: JSON.stringify({ model: 'claude-sonnet-4-20250514', max_tokens: 4096, system: body.system || '', messages: body.messages || [] }) }); } } // ─── EXISTING handleWebhook (unchanged) ────────────────────────────────────── async function handleWebhook(request, env, url) { const platform = url.pathname.split('/')[2]; if (platform === 'ebay' && request.method === 'GET') { const challenge = url.searchParams.get('challenge_code'); if (challenge) { const endpoint = url.origin + url.pathname; const token = env.EBAY_VERIFICATION_TOKEN || ''; const raw = challenge + token + endpoint; const hash = await sha256(raw); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ challengeResponse: hash }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } let payload; try { payload = await request.json(); } catch(e) { payload = {}; } try { const token = await getToken(env); const sheetId = env.SHEET_ID; const tabMap = { ebay: 'eBay_Orders', shopify: 'Shopify_Orders', amazon: 'Amazon_Orders' }; const tab = tabMap[platform]; if (!tab) return new Response('Unknown platform', { status: 400 }); const row = [new Date().toISOString(), platform, JSON.stringify(payload)]; await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${sheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(tab)}:append?valueInputOption=RAW&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: [row] }) } ); async function getToken(env) { const now = Date.now() / 1000; if (_token && now < _tokenExp - 60) return _token; const iat = Math.floor(now); const exp = iat + 3600; const b64url = obj => btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(obj)))).replace(/=/g,'').replace(/\+/g,'-').replace(/\//g,'_'); const header = b64url({ alg: 'RS256', typ: 'JWT' }); const payload = b64url({ iss: env.CLIENT_EMAIL, scope: SCOPES, aud: TOKEN_URL, iat, exp }); const sig = await sign(`${header}.${payload}`, env.PRIVATE_KEY); const jwt = `${header}.${payload}.${sig}`; const res = await fetch(TOKEN_URL, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: `grant_type=urn%3Aietf%3Aparams%3Aoauth%3Agrant-type%3Ajwt-bearer&assertion=${jwt}` }); const data = await res.json(); if (!data.access_token) throw new Error('Token error: ' + JSON.stringify(data)); _token = data.access_token; _tokenExp = exp; return _token; } // ─── EXISTING sign / sha256 / cors (unchanged) ─────────────────────────────── async function sign(data, pem) { const rawKey = pem.replace(/\\n/g, '\n'); const pemBody = rawKey.replace(/-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----|-----END PRIVATE KEY-----|\\n|\n/g, '').replace(/\s/g,''); const der = Uint8Array.from(atob(pemBody), c => c.charCodeAt(0)); const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('pkcs8', der, { name:'RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5', hash:'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']); const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign('RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5', key, new TextEncoder().encode(data)); const sigBytes = new Uint8Array(sig); let sigStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < sigBytes.length; i++) sigStr += String.fromCharCode(sigBytes[i]); return btoa(sigStr).replace(/=/g,'').replace(/\+/g,'-').replace(/\//g,'_'); } async function sha256(str) { const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(str)); return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join(''); } function cors(res) { const h = new Headers(res.headers); h.set('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', _currentOrigin || '*'); h.set('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET,POST,PUT,OPTIONS'); h.set('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Content-Type,X-API-Key,X-Session-Token,X-Session'); h.set('Content-Security-Policy', "default-src 'self'; " + "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; " + "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' https://fonts.googleapis.com; " + "font-src 'self' https://fonts.gstatic.com; " + "img-src 'self' data:; " + "connect-src 'self' https://xfitting-lookup.alfixedinc88.workers.dev https://api.anthropic.com;" ); h.set('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff'); h.set('X-Frame-Options', 'DENY'); h.set('Referrer-Policy', 'no-referrer'); // No API response was ever explicitly marked non-cacheable. Without this, // browsers (mobile Safari especially) are free to use heuristic caching // on GET requests — a device that loaded, say, /inventory/prefixes once // could keep serving that same stale response on later visits even after // another device successfully saved new values. This affects every GET // route in the app (prefixes was the one reported, but the same gap // applies everywhere), so it's fixed centrally here rather than one // route at a time. h.set('Cache-Control', 'no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate'); h.set('Pragma', 'no-cache'); return new Response(res.body, { status: res.status, headers: h }); } // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ▼▼▼ v7 ADDITIONS — REPRICER ONLY — nothing above this line was changed ▼▼▼ // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // // NEW env vars to add in Cloudflare → Workers → Settings → Variables: // // PRICE_PIN Your chosen PIN for price change authorisation [Encrypt] // SHEET_ID_REPRICER The repricer sheet ID (leave blank to reuse SHEET_ID) // // Everything else (CLIENT_EMAIL, PRIVATE_KEY, EBAY_CLIENT_ID, etc.) // is already set and is reused as-is — no changes needed. // // ─── HOW THE REPRICER APP AUTHENTICATES ────────────────────────────────────── // The repricer app sends X-Session-Token exactly like the existing apps. // It calls POST /auth with { pin: "6523", pin_type: "mgmt" } to get a token, // then sends that token on every subsequent request. No new auth system. // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── eBay token cache (separate variable — doesn't touch _token above) ───────── let _ebayToken = null, _ebayTokenExp = 0; async function getEbayToken(env) { if (_ebayToken && Date.now() < _ebayTokenExp - 60000) return _ebayToken; const creds = btoa(`${env.EBAY_CLIENT_ID}:${env.EBAY_CLIENT_SECRET}`); const r = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/identity/v1/oauth2/token', { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Basic ${creds}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: `grant_type=refresh_token&refresh_token=${encodeURIComponent(env.EBAY_REFRESH_TOKEN)}&scope=https%3A%2F%2Fapi.ebay.com%2Foauth%2Fapi_scope%20https%3A%2F%2Fapi.ebay.com%2Foauth%2Fapi_scope%2Fsell.inventory%20https%3A%2F%2Fapi.ebay.com%2Foauth%2Fapi_scope%2Fsell.fulfillment%20https%3A%2F%2Fapi.ebay.com%2Foauth%2Fapi_scope%2Fsell.account%20https%3A%2F%2Fapi.ebay.com%2Foauth%2Fapi_scope%2Fcommerce.message` }); return _ebayToken; } // ── Repricer sheet helpers — uses same getToken() as everything else ────────── // Uses SHEET_ID_REPRICER env var if set, otherwise falls back to SHEET_ID. function repricerSheetId(env) { return env.SHEET_ID_REPRICER || env.SHEET_ID; } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // AMAZON ACTIVE LISTINGS — 3-step request/status/download, same shape as the // already-proven awdVelocityRequest/Status/Download pattern above. Report // generation isn't instant, so this is deliberately split into 3 separate // calls rather than one synchronous call that risks a Worker timeout while // waiting for Amazon to finish building the report. // // IMPORTANT — unlike everything else built this session, this could NOT be // tested against live data (no Amazon SP-API credentials available here). // Built to match SP-API's documented report shape and this codebase's own // proven pattern, but the report type, exact column headers, and price // field location should be verified against a real report the first time // this runs — logged clearly below if headers don't match what's expected, // rather than failing silently. // // Report type: GET_MERCHANT_LISTINGS_ALL_DATA — Amazon's standard "all my // active listings" flat-file report.
