const magic = new Uint8Array(rawBytes.slice(0,2)); let reportText; if (magic[0]===0x1f && magic[1]===0x8b) { // Same gzip-decompression approach already proven in awdVelocityDownload try { const ds = new DecompressionStream('gzip'); const writer = ds.writable.getWriter(); const reader = ds.readable.getReader(); writer.write(new Uint8Array(rawBytes)); writer.close(); const chunks = []; let done = false; while (!done) { const { value, done: d } = await reader.read(); if (value) chunks.push(value); done = d; } const total = chunks.reduce((a,c)=>a+c.length,0); const combined = new Uint8Array(total); let offset = 0; for (const chunk of chunks) { combined.set(chunk, offset); offset += chunk.length; } reportText = new TextDecoder().decode(combined); } catch(e) { reportText = new TextDecoder().decode(rawBytes); } } else { reportText = new TextDecoder().decode(rawBytes); } // Merchant listings reports are tab-separated, not JSON. Parse by // HEADER NAME rather than fixed column position — Amazon's exact // column set/order for this report type can vary by account/region, // same defensive approach already used elsewhere in this codebase // (see salesComputeTabs' findIdx helper). const lines = reportText.split(/\r?\n/).filter(l => l.trim()); if (!lines.length) throw new Error('Report is empty'); const headers = lines[0].split('\t').map(h => h.trim().toLowerCase()); const idx = { sku: headers.findIndex(h => h === 'seller-sku' || h === 'sku'), asin: headers.findIndex(h => h === 'asin1' || h === 'asin'), price: headers.findIndex(h => h === 'price'), qty: headers.findIndex(h => h === 'quantity'), title: headers.findIndex(h => h === 'item-name'), status:headers.findIndex(h => h === 'status'), }; if (idx.sku === -1 || idx.price === -1) { // Surfacing this clearly rather than silently returning garbage — // if Amazon's actual column names differ from what's expected here, // this is exactly what needs to be checked/fixed first. console.error('[Amazon listings] Unexpected report headers:', headers.join(', ')); throw new Error('Report headers did not match expected format (missing seller-sku or price column) — actual headers: ' + headers.join(', ')); } const rawItems = []; for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) { const cols = lines[i].split('\t'); const sku = (cols[idx.sku] || '').trim(); const status = idx.status >= 0 ? (cols[idx.status] || '').trim().toLowerCase() : ''; if (!sku) continue; if (status && status !== 'active') continue; // skip inactive/incomplete listings if the column exists rawItems.push({ sku, asin: idx.asin >= 0 ? (cols[idx.asin] || '').trim() : '', price: parseFloat(cols[idx.price]) || 0, qty: idx.qty >= 0 ? (parseInt(cols[idx.qty]) || 0) : 0, title: idx.title >= 0 ? (cols[idx.title] || '').trim() : '', }); } // Enrich with COGS + D1 price_tiers, same pattern as ebayListings — // direct SKU extraction (base_sku = everything before '='), no // dependency on the AmazonSKU sheet. Confirmed acceptable: FBA SKUs // reliably use the correct format; FBM siblings on the same ASIN that // don't just won't match COGS, same as any malformed SKU would today. if (!env.DB) throw new Error('D1 not available — cannot compute suggested pricing'); await ensurePriceTiersTables(env); const tierRows = await d1All(env, "SELECT min_qty, max_qty, multiplier FROM price_tiers WHERE marketplace='amazon' ORDER BY sort_order"); const configRows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT key, value FROM price_calc_config'); const cfg = { ...PRICE_CALC_DEFAULTS }; configRows.forEach(r => { cfg[r.key] = r.value; }); const enriched = []; for (const item of rawItems) { const baseSku = item.sku.includes('=') ? item.sku.split('=')[0].trim() : item.sku.trim(); const packQty = item.sku.includes('=') ? (parseInt(item.sku.split('=')[1]) || 1) : 1; const cogsRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT unit_price FROM cogs WHERE UPPER(base_sku)=?', [baseSku.toUpperCase()]); const unitCogs = cogsRow ? parseFloat(cogsRow.unit_price) || 0 : 0; let floor = null, target = null, max = null, cog1pc = null; if (unitCogs > 0) { cog1pc = +(unitCogs * cfg.landed_multiplier).toFixed(4); const landedTotal = +(cog1pc * packQty).toFixed(4); const matched = tierRows.find(t => packQty >= t.min_qty && packQty <= t.max_qty) || tierRows[tierRows.length-1]; const mult = matched ? matched.multiplier : 1; floor = +(landedTotal * cfg.floor_multiplier + cfg.shipping).toFixed(2); target = Math.max(+(cog1pc * mult * packQty + cfg.shipping).toFixed(2), floor); max = +(landedTotal * cfg.ceiling_multiplier + cfg.shipping).toFixed(2); } enriched.push({ marketplace: 'amazon', sku: item.sku, baseSku, asin: item.asin, title: item.title, price: item.price, packQty, qty: item.qty, cog1pc, floor, target, max, status: !cog1pc ? 'no-cog' : item.price < floor ? 'below-floor' : item.price >= target ? 'ok' : 'needs-review', }); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, listings: enriched, total: enriched.length, syncedAt: new Date().toISOString() }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } let _amazonTokenCache = null; // Shopify client_credentials grant — mirrors getAmazonToken exactly. // Confirmed working against the real store (2026-07-25): this specific // store IS permitted for client_credentials (some Shopify stores require // token exchange or authorization code grant instead — see Shopify's own // docs on this — but this one works). Token expires in ~24h // (expires_in from Shopify was 86399), same short-lived-token pattern as // Amazon, so this needs the same auto-refresh caching, not a static env // var holding a fixed token. let _shopifyTokenCache = null; async function getShopifyToken(env) { const now = Date.now(); if (_shopifyTokenCache && _shopifyTokenCache.exp > now + 60000) return _shopifyTokenCache.token; const res = await fetch(`https://${env.SHOPIFY_STORE_DOMAIN}/admin/oauth/access_token`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: new URLSearchParams({ grant_type: 'client_credentials', client_id: env.SHOPIFY_CLIENT_ID, client_secret: env.SHOPIFY_CLIENT_SECRET }), }); const data = await res.json(); if (!data.access_token) throw new Error('Shopify token error: ' + JSON.stringify(data)); _shopifyTokenCache = { token: data.access_token, exp: now + (data.expires_in * 1000) }; return data.access_token; } // Walmart Marketplace client_credentials — genuinely different auth // convention from every other platform in this project: Basic auth // (base64 client_id:client_secret) rather than a body-based exchange, // custom WM_QOS.CORRELATION_ID/WM_SVC.NAME headers required alongside // it, and the resulting token goes in a WM_SEC.ACCESS_TOKEN header on // every subsequent call, not a standard Authorization: Bearer header. // Also a much shorter-lived token (15 min / 900s) than eBay/Shopify, so // the same caching pattern refreshes noticeably more often here. let _walmartTokenCache = null; async function getWalmartToken(env) { const now = Date.now(); if (_walmartTokenCache && _walmartTokenCache.exp > now + 60000) return _walmartTokenCache.token; const basicAuth = btoa(`${env.WALMART_CLIENT_ID}:${env.WALMART_CLIENT_SECRET}`); const res = await fetch('https://marketplace.walmartapis.com/v3/token', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': `Basic ${basicAuth}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded', 'Accept': 'application/json', 'WM_SVC.NAME': 'Walmart Marketplace', 'WM_QOS.CORRELATION_ID': crypto.randomUUID(), }, body: 'grant_type=client_credentials', }); const data = await res.json(); if (!data.access_token) throw new Error('Walmart token error: ' + JSON.stringify(data)); _walmartTokenCache = { token: data.access_token, exp: now + (data.expires_in * 1000) }; return data.access_token; } async function shopifyGraphQL(env, query, variables) { const token = await getShopifyToken(env); const apiVersion = '2026-07'; const res = await fetch(`https://${env.SHOPIFY_STORE_DOMAIN}/admin/api/${apiVersion}/graphql.json`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-Shopify-Access-Token': token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables }), }); const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Shopify GraphQL HTTP ' + res.status + ': ' + JSON.stringify(data)); if (data.errors) throw new Error('Shopify GraphQL errors: ' + JSON.stringify(data.errors)); return data.data; } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SHOPIFY NEW LISTING UPLOAD // // Confirmed with the user: Shopify listings are always single-piece // (qty=1) — the tiered pack-size pricing that Amazon/eBay get as separate // listings/variants is instead handled by a volume-discount app at // checkout on Shopify. This means NO product options/variants are // needed — genuinely simpler than the multi-variant "new product model" // flow, which has real, actively-reported gotchas (default variant // replacement bugs, etc) that this design sidesteps entirely by not // using variants at all. // // Flow (2 GraphQL calls, both confirmed against Shopify's own current // docs — REST is fully deprecated for products as of 2024-04, this is // GraphQL-only): // 1. productCreate — creates the product (title, description, vendor, // productType, tags, media/images). Shopify auto-creates ONE default // variant since no options were specified. // 2. productVariantsBulkUpdate — sets price, weight, SKU, barcode on // that single default variant. Shopify has no plain single-variant // update mutation — the bulk one is the documented correct call // even for exactly one variant. // // Reads from the product_catalog D1 table (imported earlier from the // Product_Catalog sheet) — base_sku rows only, since Shopify doesn't need // the pack-size fan-out the marketplace platforms do. If multiple SKUs // share a base_sku (the =1XX/=5XX/etc pack variants), only ONE Shopify // listing gets created per base_sku — pack size doesn't apply here. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // GET /shopify/catalog-list — one row per base_sku (pack_qty=1 preferred, // same deterministic ordering as shopifyListingCreate), for the Shopify // Listings tab's table. Includes shopify_product_id/pushed_at so the // frontend can show already-pushed status without a separate call. async function shopifyCatalogList(url, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { // BUGFIX: this always grouped to one row per base_sku (the pack_qty=1 // variant), which is correct for Shopify (single-piece only, by // design) but wrong for eBay — individual eBay listings are meant to // be one PER PACK SIZE, so the eBay tab's table was silently only // showing/letting the user select the 1-pack row, even though // staging actually creates listings for every pack size. Defaults to // grouped=true (Shopify's existing behavior, unchanged) unless // ?grouped=false is passed, which the eBay tab now does. const grouped = url.searchParams.get('grouped') !== 'false'; const allRows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT * FROM product_catalog ORDER BY base_sku, pack_qty ASC'); let sourceRows; if (grouped) { const byBaseSku = {}; for (const row of allRows) { if (!byBaseSku[row.base_sku]) byBaseSku[row.base_sku] = row; // first row per base_sku wins — already ordered by pack_qty ASC } sourceRows = Object.values(byBaseSku); } else { sourceRows = allRows; // every pack-size row, ungrouped } const rows = sourceRows.map(r => ({ baseSku: r.base_sku, sku: r.sku, packQty: r.pack_qty, title: r.title, titleShort: r.title_short, manufacturer: r.manufacturer, fittingType: r.fitting_type, price: r.starting_price, status: r.status, shopifyProductId: r.shopify_product_id, shopifyPushedAt: r.shopify_pushed_at, ebayCategoryId: r.ebay_category_id, ebayOfferId: r.ebay_offer_id, ebayListingId: r.ebay_listing_id, ebayStagedAt: r.ebay_staged_at, ebayPublishedAt: r.ebay_published_at, ebayVariationGroupKey: r.ebay_variation_group_key, ebayVariationListingId: r.ebay_variation_listing_id, })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, rows }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // EBAY NEW LISTING UPLOAD — account discovery first // // eBay's Inventory API create-listing flow needs THREE things referenced // by ID that are specific to this seller's account and can't be // defaulted the way Shopify's productType could: // - A payment policy ID, fulfillment (shipping) policy ID, and return // policy ID — normally set up once in Seller Hub > Business Policies // - A merchant inventory location key // - An eBay category ID for the listing // // Rather than asking the user to hunt these down manually, the existing // eBay OAuth token already includes sell.account scope, so this reads // them directly from the account via the Account API and Inventory API. // Read-only, safe to call any time — this is the discovery step before // building the actual create-listing flow, not the create flow itself. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // POST /ebay/location-create // Creates the inventory location required before any offer can be // created — confirmed via ebayAccountDiscover that none currently exist // on this account. merchantLocationKey is a fixed, simple identifier // since there's only ever one warehouse for this business. // GET /ebay/category-suggest?q=... — finds real, valid eBay category IDs // for a search term via the Taxonomy API, rather than guessing a numeric // ID from memory (a wrong guess would silently misfile every listing // into the wrong category with no obvious error). 2-step flow per eBay's // own docs: get the marketplace's category tree ID, then query // suggestions within that tree. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // EBAY LISTING CREATE — stage, then publish separately // // eBay's own Inventory API naturally splits into: create the inventory // item record, create an offer referencing it (policies/location/ // category/price), and only THEN publish to make it live. This gives the // same "review before it's live" safety Shopify's draft status gave us, // using eBay's own staging mechanism instead of a status flag — nothing // created here is a live listing until listing-publish is called // separately with the returned offerId. // // Confirmed account specifics used as defaults: // locationKey: XFITTING_WAREHOUSE (created earlier) // paymentPolicyId: 252377429011 (eBay Managed Payments) // returnPolicyId: 132540225011 (seller-paid, 30 days) // fulfillmentPolicyId: selectable per item — defaults to 148662065011 // (charges shipping), since 1-piece listings on this account don't // bake shipping into the price the way the tiered-pack pricing does // // HONESTY NOTE: eBay categories can have REQUIRED "item specifics" // (aspects) that vary by category and aren't something I have full // visibility into without an additional Taxonomy API call // (getItemAspectsForCategory) this doesn't make yet. Mapped the // product_catalog fields that clearly correspond (material, connection // types, sizes) as a best-effort aspects payload, but if eBay's category // requires something not covered here, createOffer will fail with a // specific error naming what's missing — not a guess, a real answer from // eBay directly, which is a fast fix once we see it. Always dry-run // first, same as everywhere else in this project. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const EBAY_DEFAULTS = { merchantLocationKey: 'XFITTING_WAREHOUSE', paymentPolicyId: '252377429011', returnPolicyId: '132540225011', fulfillmentPolicyId: '148662065011', // charges shipping — 1-piece default marketplaceId: 'EBAY_US', }; // Account-level constants, confirmed with the user — same everywhere // across the catalog, not per-product, so these live here rather than as // new product_catalog columns. const WALMART_DEFAULTS = { fulfillmentCenterID: '10001604424', countryOfOrigin: 'China', quantity: 8888, // same "always in stock" convention as eBay/Shopify }; async function ebayListingStage(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const items = Array.isArray(body.items) ? body.items : []; const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; if (!items.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'items array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const results = []; for (const item of items) { const baseSku = String(item.baseSku || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const quantity = item.quantity !== undefined && item.quantity !== null ? parseInt(item.quantity) : 8888; const fulfillmentPolicyId = item.fulfillmentPolicyId || EBAY_DEFAULTS.fulfillmentPolicyId; if (!baseSku || !quantity || quantity < 1) { results.push({ baseSku, ok: false, error: 'baseSku and a positive quantity are required' }); continue; } // BUGFIX: previously picked only the single pack_qty=1 row per // base_sku, so 2X/5X/10X pack variants never got their own listing // at all. Individual listings (this function) are meant to be one // per PACK SIZE — confirmed as the account's established // convention, matching Amazon's per-ASIN pack-size listings — not // one per base product. Loops every pack-size row now. const rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT * FROM product_catalog WHERE base_sku=? ORDER BY pack_qty ASC', [baseSku]); if (!rows.length) { results.push({ baseSku, ok: false, error: 'Not found in product_catalog' }); continue; } for (const row of rows) { if (!row.title_short || !row.starting_price || !row.ebay_category_id) { results.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, ok: false, error: 'Missing required field (title_short, starting_price, or ebay_category_id)' }); continue; } if (row.title_short.length > 80) { results.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, ok: false, error: `title_short is ${row.title_short.length} chars — exceeds eBay's 80-char limit` }); continue; } // Guard against accidental duplicate offers — same pattern as // Shopify's already-pushed check. Publishing is a separate step, // so "already staged" doesn't necessarily mean "already live" — // surfaced distinctly so the frontend can tell the difference. const alreadyStaged = !!row.ebay_offer_id; const alreadyPublished = !!row.ebay_listing_id; if (alreadyStaged && !item.force && !dryRun) { results.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, ok: false, error: `Already staged (offer ${row.ebay_offer_id})${alreadyPublished ? ', already published as listing ' + row.ebay_listing_id : ' — not yet published'} — pass force:true to stage another offer anyway`, alreadyStaged, alreadyPublished, existingOfferId: row.ebay_offer_id, existingListingId: row.ebay_listing_id || null }); continue; } const images = [row.image_url_1, row.image_url_2, row.image_url_3, row.image_url_4, row.image_url_5].filter(Boolean); const bullets = [row.bullet_1, row.bullet_2, row.bullet_3, row.bullet_4, row.bullet_5].filter(Boolean); const descriptionHtml = (row.description || '') + (bullets.length ? '
    ' + bullets.map(b => `
  • ${b}
    • `).join('') + '
' : ''); // Best-effort aspects mapping — see HONESTY NOTE above const aspects = {}; // BUGFIX: confirmed via a real "item specific Type is missing" // error — this category requires a Type aspect that was never // being set at all. Maps directly to fitting_type, already in the // catalog (e.g. "Check Valve"). if (row.fitting_type) aspects['Type'] = [row.fitting_type]; if (row.material) aspects['Material'] = [row.material]; if (row.connection_type_1) aspects['Connection Type'] = [row.connection_type_1]; if (row.size_1) aspects['Size'] = [row.size_1]; if (row.manufacturer) aspects['Brand'] = [row.manufacturer]; const inventoryItemPayload = { availability: { shipToLocationAvailability: { quantity } }, condition: 'NEW', product: { title: row.title_short, description: descriptionHtml, aspects, imageUrls: images, ...(row.upc ? { upc: [row.upc] } : {}), }, }; const offerPayload = { sku: row.sku, marketplaceId: EBAY_DEFAULTS.marketplaceId, format: 'FIXED_PRICE', availableQuantity: quantity, categoryId: row.ebay_category_id, listingDescription: descriptionHtml, listingPolicies: { paymentPolicyId: EBAY_DEFAULTS.paymentPolicyId, fulfillmentPolicyId, returnPolicyId: EBAY_DEFAULTS.returnPolicyId, }, pricingSummary: { price: { value: String(row.starting_price), currency: 'USD' } }, merchantLocationKey: EBAY_DEFAULTS.merchantLocationKey, }; if (dryRun) { results.push({ baseSku, ok: true, dryRun: true, sku: row.sku, alreadyStaged, alreadyPublished, preview: { inventoryItemPayload, offerPayload } }); continue; } try { const token = await getEbayToken(env); const itemRes = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/inventory_item/${encodeURIComponent(row.sku)}`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Language': 'en-US' }, body: JSON.stringify(inventoryItemPayload), }); if (itemRes.status !== 200 && itemRes.status !== 204) { const errData = await itemRes.json().catch(() => ({})); results.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, ok: false, error: 'createOrReplaceInventoryItem failed: ' + JSON.stringify(errData) }); continue; } const offerRes = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/offer', { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Language': 'en-US' }, body: JSON.stringify(offerPayload), }); const offerData = await offerRes.json(); if (!offerRes.ok || !offerData.offerId) { // BUGFIX: confirmed via a real report — this function never // had the "offer already exists" reuse fix that the variation // flow already has. When createOffer fails with errorId 25002 // (D1's own ebay_offer_id tracking was empty/out of sync with // what eBay's system actually has — likely from an earlier // partial attempt), extracts the existing offerId from the // error and updates it with current data instead of failing // outright. const existingOfferId = offerData.errors && offerData.errors[0] && offerData.errors[0].errorId === 25002 ? (offerData.errors[0].parameters || []).find(p => p.name === 'offerId') : null; if (existingOfferId && existingOfferId.value) { const updateRes = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/offer/${encodeURIComponent(existingOfferId.value)}`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Language': 'en-US' }, body: JSON.stringify(offerPayload), }); if (updateRes.status !== 200 && updateRes.status !== 204) { const updateErrData = await updateRes.json().catch(() => ({})); results.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, ok: false, error: `updateOffer failed for existing offer ${existingOfferId.value}: ` + JSON.stringify(updateErrData) }); continue; } offerData.offerId = existingOfferId.value; } else { results.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, ok: false, error: 'createOffer failed: ' + JSON.stringify(offerData) }); continue; } } const stagedAt = new Date().toISOString(); await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE product_catalog SET ebay_offer_id=?, ebay_staged_at=? WHERE sku=?') .bind(offerData.offerId, stagedAt, row.sku).run().catch(e => console.error('[eBay] tracking write-back failed (offer still created fine):', e.message)); results.push({ baseSku, ok: true, sku: row.sku, offerId: offerData.offerId, status: 'STAGED_NOT_PUBLISHED', stagedAt }); } catch (e) { results.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, ok: false, error: e.message }); } } // end for (const row of rows) } // end for (const item of items) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, results }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /ebay/listing-publish — Body: { offerIds: [...] } // The actual "go live" step, only reachable with offerIds already // returned by a successful ebayListingStage call. async function ebayListingPublish(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const offerIds = Array.isArray(body.offerIds) ? body.offerIds : []; if (!offerIds.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'offerIds array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getEbayToken(env); const results = []; for (const offerId of offerIds) { try { const res = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/offer/${encodeURIComponent(offerId)}/publish`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, }); const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok || !data.listingId) { results.push({ offerId, ok: false, error: JSON.stringify(data) }); continue; } const publishedAt = new Date().toISOString(); if (env.DB) { await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE product_catalog SET ebay_listing_id=?, ebay_published_at=? WHERE ebay_offer_id=?') .bind(data.listingId, publishedAt, offerId).run().catch(e => console.error('[eBay] publish tracking write-back failed (listing still went live fine):', e.message)); } results.push({ offerId, ok: true, listingId: data.listingId, publishedAt }); } catch (e) { results.push({ offerId, ok: false, error: e.message }); } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, results }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /ebay/offer-delete — Body: {offerId, sku} // Deletes an offer entirely (not just unpublishes it) and clears D1's // tracking columns for that SKU, so it can be re-staged completely // fresh. Built for the "Product not found" publishOffer error (errorId // 25604) — a genuinely known, widely-reported eBay issue with no // consistent documented root cause across years of their own developer // forums. Deleting and recreating from scratch is the most reliable // path other developers report working, rather than continuing to // update a potentially stuck offer record. async function ebayOfferDelete(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const offerId = (body.offerId || '').trim(); const sku = (body.sku || '').trim(); if (!offerId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'offerId required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getEbayToken(env); const delRes = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/offer/${encodeURIComponent(offerId)}`, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, }); // eBay returns 204 with no body on success. A 404 here is treated // as success too — the offer's already gone, which is the desired // end state either way. if (delRes.status !== 204 && delRes.status !== 404) { const errData = await delRes.json().catch(() => ({})); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'deleteOffer failed: ' + JSON.stringify(errData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } if (sku) { await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE product_catalog SET ebay_offer_id=NULL, ebay_staged_at=NULL, ebay_listing_id=NULL, ebay_published_at=NULL WHERE sku=?') .bind(sku).run().catch(e => console.error('[eBay] D1 tracking clear failed (offer still deleted fine):', e.message)); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, deletedOfferId: offerId, sku: sku || null }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /ebay/return-lookup?returnId=... — full return details (reason, // state, refund amount) via eBay's Post-Order API. // HONESTY NOTE: a real developer on eBay's own community forum reported // this exact endpoint returning "prohibited" through code despite // working in the API Explorer, suggesting it may need permissions not // granted by default seller API access. Genuinely unverified whether // this account's credentials have that access — this is the actual // test of that, not a confirmed-working feature yet. async function ebayReturnLookup(url, env) { try { const returnId = url.searchParams.get('returnId'); if (!returnId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'returnId parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getEbayToken(env); const res = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/post-order/v2/return/${encodeURIComponent(returnId)}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, Accept: 'application/json' }, }); const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!res.ok) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: `Return lookup failed (HTTP ${res.status}): ` + JSON.stringify(data) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const s = data.summary || {}; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, returnId, state: s.state || null, type: s.currentType || null, reason: (s.creationInfo && s.creationInfo.reason) || null, createdAt: (s.creationInfo && s.creationInfo.creationDate && s.creationInfo.creationDate.value) || null, buyerRefund: (s.buyerTotalRefund && s.buyerTotalRefund.actualRefundAmount) || null, itemId: (s.creationInfo && s.creationInfo.item && s.creationInfo.item.itemId) || null, rawResponse: data, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // EBAY VARIATION LISTING — one listing, all pack sizes as a dropdown // // Built alongside (not instead of) the individual per-pack-size listings // above — confirmed both are wanted simultaneously. // // BUGFIX: this previously reused the same real SKU as the standalone // listings, based on an earlier confirmation that this was fine. Live // testing proved otherwise — eBay rejects createOrReplaceInventoryItemGroup // with a specific, confirmed error when a SKU is already published as a // standalone listing: "The following SKU is already listed as a single // SKU listing." Once a SKU has an active standalone listing, eBay locks // it out of group membership entirely. Reverted to a derived SKU // (suffixed "-VAR") for every variation-group member, which sidesteps // this conflict regardless of whether a given SKU happens to already be // published standalone — genuinely a separate inventory item on eBay's // side, so there's nothing for the two listing types to collide over. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function ebayVariationSku(realSku) { return realSku + '-VAR'; } async function ebayVariationStage(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const baseSku = String(body.baseSku || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; const quantity = body.quantity !== undefined && body.quantity !== null ? parseInt(body.quantity) : 8888; const fulfillmentPolicyId = body.fulfillmentPolicyId || EBAY_DEFAULTS.fulfillmentPolicyId; if (!baseSku) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'baseSku required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT * FROM product_catalog WHERE base_sku=? ORDER BY pack_qty ASC', [baseSku]); if (!rows.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Not found in product_catalog' }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); if (rows.length < 2) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Only 1 pack size found — a variation listing needs at least 2 to make sense' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); for (const row of rows) { if (!row.starting_price || !row.ebay_category_id) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: `${row.sku} is missing starting_price or ebay_category_id` }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } const alreadyStaged = rows.some(r => r.ebay_variation_group_key); if (alreadyStaged && !body.force && !dryRun) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: `Already staged as variation group ${rows[0].ebay_variation_group_key}${rows[0].ebay_variation_listing_id ? ', already published as listing ' + rows[0].ebay_variation_listing_id : ' — not yet published'} — pass force:true to stage again`, alreadyStaged: true, existingGroupKey: rows[0].ebay_variation_group_key, existingListingId: rows[0].ebay_variation_listing_id || null }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const groupKey = baseSku + '-GROUP'; const groupTitleRow = rows[0]; // any row's base product info works — title/description below are shared across all variants const images = [groupTitleRow.image_url_1, groupTitleRow.image_url_2, groupTitleRow.image_url_3, groupTitleRow.image_url_4, groupTitleRow.image_url_5].filter(Boolean); const bullets = [groupTitleRow.bullet_1, groupTitleRow.bullet_2, groupTitleRow.bullet_3, groupTitleRow.bullet_4, groupTitleRow.bullet_5].filter(Boolean); const descriptionHtml = (groupTitleRow.description || '') + (bullets.length ? '
    ' + bullets.map(b => `
  • ${b}
    • `).join('') + '
' : ''); // Group title must be <=80 chars too, same eBay-wide limit as individual listings const groupTitle = groupTitleRow.title_short ? groupTitleRow.title_short.replace(/^\d+\s*Pack\s*/i, '').trim() : ''; if (!groupTitle || groupTitle.length > 80) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: `Could not derive a valid group title (<=80 chars) from title_short` }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // BUGFIX: confirmed via real eBay errors — this previously (a) built // ONE shared aspects object reused identically for every inventory // item, so no member had its own distinguishing value at all, and // (b) used a made-up "Pack Size" aspect with values like "5 Pack". // Confirmed via the account's own manual listing convention: the // correct aspect name is "Quantity", and the value is the bare // number ("5", not "5 Pack") — this is what's actually used when // creating this exact kind of listing by hand. Each member's // inventory item needs its OWN aspects including its OWN specific // Quantity value, not just the group-level declaration of which // aspect varies. const baseAspects = {}; if (groupTitleRow.fitting_type) baseAspects['Type'] = [groupTitleRow.fitting_type]; if (groupTitleRow.material) baseAspects['Material'] = [groupTitleRow.material]; if (groupTitleRow.connection_type_1) baseAspects['Connection Type'] = [groupTitleRow.connection_type_1]; if (groupTitleRow.manufacturer) baseAspects['Brand'] = [groupTitleRow.manufacturer]; const variantSKUs = rows.map(r => ebayVariationSku(r.sku)); const quantityValues = rows.map(r => String(r.pack_qty)); const groupPayload = { title: groupTitle, description: descriptionHtml, imageUrls: images, variantSKUs, variesBy: { aspectsImageVariesBy: [], specifications: [{ name: 'Quantity', values: quantityValues }], }, }; if (dryRun) { const preview = { groupKey, groupPayload, members: rows.map((r, i) => ({ realSku: r.sku, variationSku: variantSKUs[i], packQty: r.pack_qty, price: r.starting_price, })), }; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, baseSku, alreadyStaged, preview }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const token = await getEbayToken(env); // Step 1: create an inventory item for each variant's DERIVED sku — // see the top-of-section note for why this is a separate sku from // the standalone listing again, not the same one. for (const row of rows) { const vSku = ebayVariationSku(row.sku); const itemAspects = { ...baseAspects, 'Quantity': [String(row.pack_qty)] }; const itemPayload = { availability: { shipToLocationAvailability: { quantity } }, condition: 'NEW', product: { title: row.title_short, description: descriptionHtml, aspects: itemAspects, imageUrls: images, ...(row.upc ? { upc: [row.upc] } : {}) }, }; const res = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/inventory_item/${encodeURIComponent(vSku)}`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Language': 'en-US' }, body: JSON.stringify(itemPayload), }); if (res.status !== 200 && res.status !== 204) { const errData = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: `createOrReplaceInventoryItem failed for ${vSku}: ` + JSON.stringify(errData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // Step 2: create the group, linking all the variant SKUs together const groupRes = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/inventory_item_group/${encodeURIComponent(groupKey)}`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Language': 'en-US' }, body: JSON.stringify(groupPayload), }); if (groupRes.status !== 200 && groupRes.status !== 204) { const errData = await groupRes.json().catch(() => ({})); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'createOrReplaceInventoryItemGroup failed: ' + JSON.stringify(errData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Step 3: create an offer for each variant's DERIVED SKU — // listingDescription omitted here since it's set at the group level, // per eBay's docs. const offerIds = []; for (const row of rows) { const vSku = ebayVariationSku(row.sku); const offerPayload = { sku: vSku, marketplaceId: EBAY_DEFAULTS.marketplaceId, format: 'FIXED_PRICE', availableQuantity: quantity, categoryId: row.ebay_category_id, listingPolicies: { paymentPolicyId: EBAY_DEFAULTS.paymentPolicyId, fulfillmentPolicyId, returnPolicyId: EBAY_DEFAULTS.returnPolicyId, }, pricingSummary: { price: { value: String(row.starting_price), currency: 'USD' } }, merchantLocationKey: EBAY_DEFAULTS.merchantLocationKey, }; const offerRes = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/offer', { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Language': 'en-US' }, body: JSON.stringify(offerPayload), }); const offerData = await offerRes.json(); if (!offerRes.ok || !offerData.offerId) { // BUGFIX: confirmed via a real retry attempt — if an earlier // staging attempt got partway through (e.g. failed later at // publish due to the category not supporting variations, an // unrelated failure), the offer for this sku may already exist. // createOffer isn't idempotent the way createOrReplaceInventoryItem // is, so a blind retry fails here with errorId 25002. eBay // conveniently includes the existing offerId right in the error's // parameters. // // BUGFIX 2: the first version of this fix just reused the // existing offerId as-is — confirmed via a second real failure // that this silently carries forward STALE data (e.g. the old, // wrong category from before a sheet correction), since the // offer itself was never actually updated, just reused. Now // calls updateOffer (PUT) with the CURRENT payload before // reusing the id, so a corrected category (or price, etc.) // actually takes effect on retry instead of silently persisting // whatever was there when the offer was first created. const existingOfferId = offerData.errors && offerData.errors[0] && offerData.errors[0].errorId === 25002 ? (offerData.errors[0].parameters || []).find(p => p.name === 'offerId') : null; if (existingOfferId && existingOfferId.value) { const updateRes = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/offer/${encodeURIComponent(existingOfferId.value)}`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Language': 'en-US' }, body: JSON.stringify(offerPayload), }); if (updateRes.status !== 200 && updateRes.status !== 204) { const updateErrData = await updateRes.json().catch(() => ({})); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: `updateOffer failed for existing offer ${existingOfferId.value} (${vSku}): ` + JSON.stringify(updateErrData), partialOfferIds: offerIds }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } offerIds.push(existingOfferId.value); continue; } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: `createOffer failed for ${vSku}: ` + JSON.stringify(offerData), partialOfferIds: offerIds }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } offerIds.push(offerData.offerId); } const stagedAt = new Date().toISOString(); const writeBackStmts = rows.map(r => env.DB.prepare( 'UPDATE product_catalog SET ebay_variation_group_key=?, ebay_variation_staged_at=? WHERE sku=?' ).bind(groupKey, stagedAt, r.sku)); await env.DB.batch(writeBackStmts).catch(e => console.error('[eBay] variation tracking write-back failed (group still created fine):', e.message)); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, baseSku, groupKey, offerIds, status: 'STAGED_NOT_PUBLISHED', stagedAt }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /ebay/variation-publish — Body: { groupKey } async function ebayVariationPublish(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const groupKey = String(body.groupKey || '').trim(); if (!groupKey) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'groupKey required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getEbayToken(env); const res = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/offer/publish_by_inventory_item_group', { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ inventoryItemGroupKey: groupKey, marketplaceId: EBAY_DEFAULTS.marketplaceId }), }); const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok || !data.listingId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: JSON.stringify(data) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const publishedAt = new Date().toISOString(); if (env.DB) { await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE product_catalog SET ebay_variation_listing_id=?, ebay_variation_published_at=? WHERE ebay_variation_group_key=?') .bind(data.listingId, publishedAt, groupKey).run().catch(e => console.error('[eBay] variation publish tracking write-back failed (listing still went live fine):', e.message)); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, groupKey, listingId: data.listingId, publishedAt }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function ebayCategorySuggest(url, env) { try { const q = url.searchParams.get('q'); if (!q) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'q parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getEbayToken(env); const treeRes = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/commerce/taxonomy/v1/get_default_category_tree_id?marketplace_id=EBAY_US', { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, }); const treeData = await treeRes.json(); if (!treeData.categoryTreeId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Could not get category tree: ' + JSON.stringify(treeData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const suggestRes = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/commerce/taxonomy/v1/category_tree/${treeData.categoryTreeId}/get_category_suggestions?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, }); const suggestData = await suggestRes.json(); const suggestions = (suggestData.categorySuggestions || []).map(s => ({ categoryId: s.category.categoryId, categoryName: s.category.categoryName, // ancestor path, root-to-leaf, so it's clear where in the tree this sits path: (s.categoryTreeNodeAncestors || []).map(a => a.categoryName).reverse().concat([s.category.categoryName]).join(' > '), })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, query: q, suggestions }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /ebay/category-check-leaf?categoryId=... — confirms whether a // specific category ID is currently a valid leaf category. Built after // a real publish failure ("category selected is not a leaf category") // for a categoryId that presumably was a valid leaf when originally // set — eBay's taxonomy can restructure over time, turning a former // leaf into a parent as new subcategories get added underneath it. // Uses getCategorySubtree, whose root node directly reports its own // leafCategoryTreeNode status, confirmed from eBay's own docs. async function ebayCategoryCheckLeaf(url, env) { try { const categoryId = url.searchParams.get('categoryId'); if (!categoryId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'categoryId parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getEbayToken(env); const treeRes = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/commerce/taxonomy/v1/get_default_category_tree_id?marketplace_id=EBAY_US', { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, }); const treeData = await treeRes.json(); if (!treeData.categoryTreeId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Could not get category tree: ' + JSON.stringify(treeData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const subtreeRes = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/commerce/taxonomy/v1/category_tree/${treeData.categoryTreeId}/get_category_subtree?category_id=${encodeURIComponent(categoryId)}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, }); const subtreeData = await subtreeRes.json(); if (!subtreeRes.ok) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'getCategorySubtree failed: ' + JSON.stringify(subtreeData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const node = subtreeData.categorySubtreeNode; const isLeaf = !!(node && node.leafCategoryTreeNode); const childCategories = (node && node.childCategoryTreeNodes || []).map(c => ({ categoryId: c.category.categoryId, categoryName: c.category.categoryName })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, categoryId, categoryName: node && node.category ? node.category.categoryName : null, isLeaf, childCategories: isLeaf ? [] : childCategories, // if NOT a leaf, these are candidates to use instead }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /ebay/category-check-variations?categoryId=... — confirmed via live // testing that not every category supports multi-variation listings // (createOrReplaceInventoryItemGroup can succeed while the eventual // publish still fails with "does not support multi-variation listings"). // The only reliable way to check this ahead of time is the OLDER, XML- // based Trading API's GetCategoryFeatures call — the newer REST Taxonomy // API doesn't expose this. Uses the same OAuth token via the // X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN header rather than a separate legacy auth flow. // HONESTY NOTE: the X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL value below is a // reasonably current one, not something confirmed against a live call — // if eBay rejects it as outdated, the error will say so explicitly and // the fix is just bumping this number. async function ebayCategoryVariationsCheck(url, env) { try { const categoryId = url.searchParams.get('categoryId'); if (!categoryId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'categoryId parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getEbayToken(env); const xmlBody = ` ${categoryId} ReturnAll `; const res = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN': token, 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME': 'GetCategoryFeatures', 'X-EBAY-API-SITEID': '0', 'X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL': '1157', 'Content-Type': 'text/xml', }, body: xmlBody, }); const xmlText = await res.text(); if (!res.ok) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'GetCategoryFeatures HTTP ' + res.status, rawSnippet: xmlText.slice(0, 500) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); // VariationsEnabled is returned as a present-but-empty element when // the feature applies (per eBay's own docs) — a simple substring // check is enough, no need for full XML parsing for one boolean flag. const variationsSupported = //i.test(xmlText); const ackMatch = xmlText.match(/([^<]+)<\/Ack>/i); const errorMatch = xmlText.match(/([^<]+)<\/LongMessage>/i); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, categoryId, variationsSupported, ack: ackMatch ? ackMatch[1] : null, error: errorMatch ? errorMatch[1] : null, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /ebay/category-check-aspects?categoryId=... — surfaces a // category's required/recommended item specifics upfront, via the // Taxonomy API's getItemAspectsForCategory. Built after hitting three // separate "item specific X is missing" publish failures in a row // (Type, Brand, ...) — each only discoverable one at a time by actually // attempting a real publish. This lets a category be checked once before // staging, rather than continuing to play whack-a-mole per field. async function ebayCategoryAspectsCheck(url, env) { try { const categoryId = url.searchParams.get('categoryId'); if (!categoryId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'categoryId parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getEbayToken(env); const treeRes = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/commerce/taxonomy/v1/get_default_category_tree_id?marketplace_id=EBAY_US', { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, }); const treeData = await treeRes.json(); if (!treeData.categoryTreeId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Could not get category tree: ' + JSON.stringify(treeData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const aspectsRes = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/commerce/taxonomy/v1/category_tree/${treeData.categoryTreeId}/get_item_aspects_for_category?category_id=${encodeURIComponent(categoryId)}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, }); const aspectsData = await aspectsRes.json(); if (!aspectsRes.ok) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'getItemAspectsForCategory failed: ' + JSON.stringify(aspectsData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const aspects = (aspectsData.aspects || []).map(a => ({ name: a.localizedAspectName, required: a.aspectConstraint ? a.aspectConstraint.aspectRequired === true : false, usableForVariations: a.aspectConstraint ? a.aspectConstraint.aspectEnabledForVariations === true : null, mode: a.aspectConstraint ? a.aspectConstraint.aspectMode : null, allowedValues: (a.aspectValues || []).map(v => v.localizedValue).slice(0, 20), // capped — some categories have hundreds })); const required = aspects.filter(a => a.required); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, categoryId, requiredCount: required.length, required, allAspects: aspects, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function ebayLocationCreate(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; const merchantLocationKey = 'XFITTING_WAREHOUSE'; const locationPayload = { location: { address: { addressLine1: '499 Bridgeton Pike', city: 'Mullica Hill', stateOrProvince: 'NJ', postalCode: '08062', country: 'US', }, }, locationTypes: ['WAREHOUSE'], name: 'XFitting Warehouse', merchantLocationStatus: 'ENABLED', }; if (dryRun) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, merchantLocationKey, preview: locationPayload }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const token = await getEbayToken(env); const res = await fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/location/${encodeURIComponent(merchantLocationKey)}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(locationPayload), }); if (res.status !== 204 && !res.ok) { const errData = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Location creation failed: ' + JSON.stringify(errData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, merchantLocationKey, message: 'Inventory location created' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /walmart/test-connection — read-only connectivity check, same // pattern as ebayAccountDiscover: prove the credentials actually work // before building anything that writes/creates listings. // GET /walmart/check-version?version=... — safe, read-only check of // whether this account's item spec accepts a given version, using the // taxonomy endpoint (which requires a valid feedType+version to return // anything useful). Built after repeated rounds of guessing item-field // structure blind — lets the actual version be confirmed without // risking another real feed submission each time. // GET /walmart/catalog-search?upc=... — the actual missing step: checks // whether this UPC already exists somewhere in Walmart's broader catalog // (sold by another seller), which determines whether MP_ITEM (full new // item setup — what's been submitted this whole time) or MP_ITEM_MATCH // (link an offer to an existing catalog item) is actually correct. // responseFormat=SPEC additionally returns the exact itemSpecPayload // structure to use for whichever path applies — read-only, safe to call // before submitting another real feed. // POST /walmart/spreadsheet-data — Body: { baseSkus: [...] } // Maps product_catalog rows to Walmart's exact field structure, confirmed // directly from the real spec spreadsheet (omni-marketplace-en-external- // 5_0_20260608-18_15_07.xlsx) — the frontend turns this JSON into an // actual downloadable .xlsx matching that template exactly, for upload // through Seller Center's UI (the path proven to work for this account, // unlike the API route). One row per pack size, matching the same // individual-listing convention as Amazon/eBay. async function walmartSpreadsheetData(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const baseSkus = Array.isArray(body.baseSkus) ? body.baseSkus : []; if (!baseSkus.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'baseSkus array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const rows = []; const skipped = []; for (const baseSku of baseSkus) { const catalogRows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT * FROM product_catalog WHERE base_sku=? ORDER BY pack_qty ASC', [baseSku.toUpperCase()]); if (!catalogRows.length) { skipped.push({ baseSku, reason: 'Not found in product_catalog' }); continue; } for (const row of catalogRows) { if (!row.upc) { skipped.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, reason: 'No UPC' }); continue; } // BUGFIX: confirmed via a real user report — this combined-OR // message required a follow-up round trip to find out which // specific field was actually missing. Checks each one // individually now and names exactly what's missing. const missing = []; if (!row.title_short) missing.push('title_short'); if (!row.starting_price) missing.push('starting_price'); if (!row.weight_lbs) missing.push('weight_lbs'); if (!row.walmart_product_type) missing.push('walmart_product_type'); if (missing.length) { skipped.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, reason: 'Missing: ' + missing.join(', ') }); continue; } const bullets = [row.bullet_1, row.bullet_2, row.bullet_3, row.bullet_4].filter(Boolean); // template has 4 keyFeatures columns, not 5 // MAJOR BUGFIX: confirmed via a real pasted-result cross-checked // against the actual template file — my very first reading of // this template (many messages ago) miscounted the column // offset. Data genuinely starts at column D (index 4, 1-indexed), // and previous versions only included ~21 of the real 93 // columns, silently skipping required fields (Material, Has // Written Warranty, Net Content Unit/Measure) that sat BETWEEN // fields that were included — shifting everything after them // into the wrong column when pasted. Also reverted the GTIN-14 // padding — the user confirmed this account's Product ID field // wants the plain 12-digit UPC, not a padded 14-digit GTIN. // // Uses an explicit column-number -> value map, placed into a // fixed 93-element array programmatically, rather than manually // counting blank placeholder strings — a first attempt at that // manual approach came out to 86 elements instead of 93, // exactly the kind of counting error this avoids entirely. function buildWalmartRow(fields) { const arr = new Array(93).fill(''); for (const col in fields) arr[Number(col) - 4] = fields[col]; // column 4 in the real template = array index 0 return arr; } const orderedRow = buildWalmartRow({ 4: row.sku, 5: row.walmart_product_type, 6: 'UPC', 7: row.upc, 8: row.title_short, 9: row.manufacturer || 'XFITTING', 10: row.starting_price, 11: row.weight_lbs, 12: WALMART_DEFAULTS.countryOfOrigin, 13: 'New', 14: WALMART_DEFAULTS.fulfillmentCenterID, 15: WALMART_DEFAULTS.quantity, 17: row.description || '', 18: bullets[0] || '', 19: bullets[1] || '', 20: bullets[2] || '', 21: bullets[3] || '', // BUGFIX: confirmed via a real pasted result cross-checked // against the real template — the true order is Unit (Y), // Measure (Z), Material (AA). Previous version had Material // first, then Unit, then Measure — reversed from reality, // which is exactly why "Lead-Free Brass" showed up in the // Unit column, "Count" in Measure, and "1" in Material. 22: row.image_url_1 || '', 23: 'No', 24: 'No', 25: 'Count', 26: 1, 27: row.material || '', 61: row.walmart_product_type === 'Valves' ? (row.fitting_type || '') : '', }); rows.push(orderedRow); } } if (!rows.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'No rows ready — check UPC, title_short, starting_price, weight_lbs, and walmart_product_type are all set', skipped }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, rowCount: rows.length, skipped, rows }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function walmartCatalogSearch(url, env) { try { const upc = url.searchParams.get('upc'); if (!upc) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'upc parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getWalmartToken(env); const res = await fetch(`https://marketplace.walmartapis.com/v3/items/walmart/search?upc=${encodeURIComponent(upc)}&responseFormat=SPEC`, { headers: { 'WM_SEC.ACCESS_TOKEN': token, 'WM_QOS.CORRELATION_ID': crypto.randomUUID(), 'WM_SVC.NAME': 'Walmart Marketplace', 'Accept': 'application/json', }, }); const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Catalog search failed: HTTP ' + res.status, detail: data }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const item = (data.items || [])[0] || null; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, upc, foundInWalmartCatalog: !!item, feedTypeToUse: item && item.itemSpecPayload ? item.itemSpecPayload.MPItemFeedHeader?.feedType || 'MP_ITEM_MATCH (found)' : 'MP_ITEM (not found)', rawResponse: data, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function walmartCheckVersion(url, env) { try { const version = url.searchParams.get('version') || '4.2'; const token = await getWalmartToken(env); const res = await fetch(`https://marketplace.walmartapis.com/v3/utilities/taxonomy?feedType=MP_ITEM&version=${encodeURIComponent(version)}`, { headers: { 'WM_SEC.ACCESS_TOKEN': token, 'WM_QOS.CORRELATION_ID': crypto.randomUUID(), 'WM_SVC.NAME': 'Walmart Marketplace', 'Accept': 'application/json', }, }); } } async function walmartTestConnection(env) { try { const token = await getWalmartToken(env); const res = await fetch('https://marketplace.walmartapis.com/v3/items?limit=5', { headers: { 'WM_SEC.ACCESS_TOKEN': token, 'WM_QOS.CORRELATION_ID': crypto.randomUUID(), 'WM_SVC.NAME': 'Walmart Marketplace', 'Accept': 'application/json', }, }); const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Walmart API HTTP ' + res.status, detail: data }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }));
