} catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // WALMART NEW LISTING UPLOAD // // HONESTY NOTE, same category as the Amazon PATCH schema and Shopify // productType uncertainty earlier in this project: Walmart's item-setup // feed schema is genuinely category/product-type specific with // conditional required fields (confirmed via their own docs — an actual // JSON Schema with if/then logic per product type). This is a // reasonable, well-researched FIRST attempt at the payload shape, not // something verified against a live submission yet. Expect the first // real test to come back with specific field errors — same pattern that // eventually got the eBay integration working, just applied here first // rather than guessed away. Always dry-run first. // // Starting scope, per explicit agreement: only stages items that already // have a UPC in product_catalog (matches the existing Amazon convention) // — UPC-less items are skipped, not blocked entirely, until it's // confirmed whether Walmart has a workable exemption path. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function walmartListingStage(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const baseSkus = Array.isArray(body.baseSkus) ? body.baseSkus : []; const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; if (!baseSkus.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'baseSkus array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const items = []; const skipped = []; for (const baseSku of baseSkus) { // BUGFIX: previously only checked the smallest pack size (matching // Shopify's single-piece-only logic) — but Walmart, like Amazon, // should list every pack size as its own item. Confirmed via a // real case: the =1 variant has no UPC (intentionally, not sold // on Amazon), but =2X does — only checking =1 meant a perfectly // stageable item was being skipped entirely. Loops every pack-size // row now, checking UPC per-row rather than per-base_sku. const rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT * FROM product_catalog WHERE base_sku=? ORDER BY pack_qty ASC', [baseSku.toUpperCase()]); if (!rows.length) { skipped.push({ baseSku, reason: 'Not found in product_catalog' }); continue; } for (const row of rows) { if (!row.upc) { skipped.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, reason: 'No UPC — skipped per current scope, not staged' }); continue; } if (!row.title_short || !row.starting_price || !row.weight_lbs) { skipped.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, reason: 'Missing title_short, starting_price, or weight_lbs' }); continue; } items.push({ // CONFIRMED via a real, working reference example from Walmart's // own schema docs (MP_ITEM_v5.x.json) — the actual structure // was missing this whole time: Orderable needs a // "specProductType" field naming the product type ("Valves", // matching the Visible.Valves key), which no previous round // included at all. Orderable: { sku: row.sku, productIdentifiers: { productIdType: 'UPC', productId: row.upc }, specProductType: 'Valves', price: row.starting_price, ShippingWeight: row.weight_lbs, }, Visible: { Valves: { productName: row.title_short, brand: row.manufacturer || 'XFITTING', mainImageUrl: row.image_url_1 || '', shortDescription: row.description || '', condition: 'New', }, }, }); } // end for (const row of rows) } // end for (const baseSku of baseSkus) if (!items.length) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'No items ready to stage', skipped }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // CONFIRMED via the real MP_ITEM_v5.x.json reference example — the // entire payload needed a top-level "specItemPayload" wrapper that // every previous round was missing entirely, plus several required // header fields (processMode, subset, subCategory) never included // before. This fully explains the confusing "Item required, // Orderable/Visible not valid" error — without the wrapper, // Walmart's parser likely couldn't locate the structure at all, so // nothing in the payload was recognized as valid. Version string // also needed a "-api" suffix, confirmed from the same reference. const feedPayload = { specItemPayload: { MPItemFeedHeader: { feedType: 'MP_ITEM', processMode: 'REPLACE', locale: 'en', version: '5.0.20260608-18_15_07-api', subset: 'EXTERNAL', subCategory: 'product_content_and_site_exp', }, MPItem: items, }, }; if (dryRun) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, itemCount: items.length, skipped, preview: feedPayload }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const token = await getWalmartToken(env); // TESTING: every previous submission sent a raw JSON body, but // Walmart's own API reference for this endpoint lists the request // body as "file" (required) — suggesting a multipart file upload is // actually expected, not raw JSON. This is a genuinely different, // untested hypothesis (transport format, not payload structure). // Critically, Content-Type must NOT be set manually here — fetch() // auto-generates the correct multipart/form-data header with its // own boundary when the body is a FormData object; overriding it // manually breaks the multipart parsing entirely. const formData = new FormData(); const feedBlob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(feedPayload)], { type: 'application/json' }); formData.append('file', feedBlob, 'feed.json'); const res = await fetch('https://marketplace.walmartapis.com/v3/feeds?feedType=MP_ITEM', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'WM_SEC.ACCESS_TOKEN': token, 'WM_QOS.CORRELATION_ID': crypto.randomUUID(), 'WM_SVC.NAME': 'Walmart Marketplace', 'Accept': 'application/json', }, body: formData, }); const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok || !data.feedId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Feed submission failed: ' + JSON.stringify(data) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, feedId: data.feedId, itemCount: items.length, skipped, status: 'SUBMITTED' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /walmart/feed-status?feedId=... — poll overall feed status, and // once available, per-item results (Walmart returns both in the same // call, unlike Amazon/eBay's separate status-then-report split). async function walmartFeedStatus(url, env) { try { // BUGFIX: confirmed via a real failure — Walmart feedIds can contain // '@' and other characters that don't survive some client/URL // handling unencoded. url.searchParams.get() already decodes // whatever arrived, so this just guards against a feedId that's // still double-encoded or has stray whitespace, rather than // requiring the caller to always get manual encoding exactly right. const feedId = (url.searchParams.get('feedId') || '').trim(); if (!feedId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'feedId parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getWalmartToken(env); const res = await fetch(`https://marketplace.walmartapis.com/v3/feeds/${encodeURIComponent(feedId)}?includeDetails=true`, { headers: { 'WM_SEC.ACCESS_TOKEN': token, 'WM_QOS.CORRELATION_ID': crypto.randomUUID(), 'WM_SVC.NAME': 'Walmart Marketplace', 'Accept': 'application/json', }, }); const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Feed status check failed: ' + JSON.stringify(data) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); // BUGFIX: previously only extracted a narrow, pre-guessed subset of // fields (feedStatus, item counts, itemDetails.itemIngestionStatus) // — confirmed via a real "feedStatus: ERROR, 0 items received" // response that this silently discards whatever explains a // feed-level rejection, since that reason clearly isn't in the // fields I was extracting. Returns the full raw response now // alongside the same summary fields, so nothing real gets thrown away. const itemResults = (data.itemDetails && data.itemDetails.itemIngestionStatus || []).map(i => ({ sku: i.sku, ingestionStatus: i.ingestionStatus, errors: (i.ingestionErrors && i.ingestionErrors.ingestionError || []).map(e => e.description), })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, feedId, feedStatus: data.feedStatus, itemsReceived: data.itemsReceived, itemsSucceeded: data.itemsSucceeded, itemsFailed: data.itemsFailed, itemsProcessing: data.itemsProcessing, itemResults, rawResponse: data, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function ebayAccountDiscover(env) { try { const token = await getEbayToken(env); const marketplaceId = 'EBAY_US'; const headers = { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-EBAY-C-MARKETPLACE-ID': marketplaceId }; const [paymentRes, fulfillmentRes, returnRes, locationRes] = await Promise.all([ fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/account/v1/payment_policy?marketplace_id=${marketplaceId}`, { headers }), fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/account/v1/fulfillment_policy?marketplace_id=${marketplaceId}`, { headers }), fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/account/v1/return_policy?marketplace_id=${marketplaceId}`, { headers }), fetch(`https://api.ebay.com/sell/inventory/v1/location`, { headers }), ]); const [paymentData, fulfillmentData, returnData, locationData] = await Promise.all([ paymentRes.json(), fulfillmentRes.json(), returnRes.json(), locationRes.json(), ]); const summarize = (arr, idKey, nameKey) => (arr || []).map(p => ({ id: p[idKey], name: p[nameKey] })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, paymentPolicies: summarize(paymentData.paymentPolicies, 'paymentPolicyId', 'name'), fulfillmentPolicies: summarize(fulfillmentData.fulfillmentPolicies, 'fulfillmentPolicyId', 'name'), returnPolicies: summarize(returnData.returnPolicies, 'returnPolicyId', 'name'), locations: (locationData.locations || []).map(l => ({ key: l.merchantLocationKey, name: l.name, status: l.merchantLocationStatus })), // Flags what's missing so the frontend can tell the user exactly // what needs setting up in Seller Hub before listings can be created readyToList: (paymentData.paymentPolicies||[]).length > 0 && (fulfillmentData.fulfillmentPolicies||[]).length > 0 && (returnData.returnPolicies||[]).length > 0 && (locationData.locations||[]).length > 0, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function shopifyListingCreate(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const baseSkus = Array.isArray(body.baseSkus) ? body.baseSkus : []; const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; if (!baseSkus.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'baseSkus array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); if (!env.SHOPIFY_STORE_DOMAIN || !env.SHOPIFY_CLIENT_ID || !env.SHOPIFY_CLIENT_SECRET) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'SHOPIFY_STORE_DOMAIN / SHOPIFY_CLIENT_ID / SHOPIFY_CLIENT_SECRET env vars not set' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const results = []; for (const baseSku of baseSkus) { // Prefer a row explicitly flagged Ready to List; fall back to the // first row for this base_sku if status wasn't set. // BUGFIX: previously had no ORDER BY, so which pack-size row got // used for a base_sku with multiple rows (=1, =2X, =5X, =10X) was // effectively undefined/coincidental — it happened to work in // testing because SQLite returned rows in insertion order, but // that's not guaranteed. Since Shopify listings represent a single // piece by design (confirmed with the user — volume pricing is // handled by a discount app at checkout, not separate listings), // explicitly order by pack_qty ascending so the =1 row is always // preferred deterministically, not by luck of insertion order. let row = await d1First(env, "SELECT * FROM product_catalog WHERE base_sku=? AND status='Ready to List' ORDER BY pack_qty ASC LIMIT 1", [baseSku.toUpperCase()]); if (!row) row = await d1First(env, 'SELECT * FROM product_catalog WHERE base_sku=? ORDER BY pack_qty ASC LIMIT 1', [baseSku.toUpperCase()]); if (!row) { results.push({ baseSku, ok: false, error: 'Not found in product_catalog' }); continue; } if (!row.title || !row.starting_price) { results.push({ baseSku, ok: false, error: 'Missing required field (title or starting_price)' }); continue; } // Guard against accidental duplicate listings — if this sku was // already pushed to Shopify, skip it unless force:true is explicitly // passed. Surfaced in the dry-run preview too, so the frontend can // show "already pushed" before the user even tries. const alreadyPushed = !!row.shopify_product_id; if (alreadyPushed && !body.force && !dryRun) { results.push({ baseSku, ok: false, error: `Already pushed to Shopify (${row.shopify_product_id}) on ${row.shopify_pushed_at} — pass force:true to create another listing anyway`, alreadyPushed: true, existingProductId: row.shopify_product_id }); continue; } const images = [row.image_url_1, row.image_url_2, row.image_url_3, row.image_url_4, row.image_url_5].filter(Boolean); const bullets = [row.bullet_1, row.bullet_2, row.bullet_3, row.bullet_4, row.bullet_5].filter(Boolean); const descriptionHtml = (row.description || '') + (bullets.length ? '
    ' + bullets.map(b => `
  • ${b}
    • `).join('') + '
' : ''); // BUGFIX: real catalog data uses semicolons to separate keywords, // not just commas as the template suggested — a comma-only split // was producing 1-2 giant unsplit blobs instead of proper individual // tags, confirmed against actual rows. Also strips embedded // newlines/extra whitespace within a keyword, another real thing // found in the actual data (a keyword field wrapped across two // lines in the source spreadsheet). const tags = (row.keywords || '') .replace(/\s+/g, ' ') .split(/[,;]/) .map(k => k.trim().replace(/^["']+|["']+$/g, '')) // strip stray leading/trailing quote chars — confirmed present in the actual source data (a leftover typo, not a real part of the keyword) .filter(Boolean); if (dryRun) { results.push({ baseSku, ok: true, dryRun: true, alreadyPushed, existingProductId: alreadyPushed ? row.shopify_product_id : null, preview: { title: row.title, vendor: row.manufacturer, productType: row.fitting_type, price: row.starting_price, weight: row.weight_lbs, sku: row.sku, upc: row.upc, imageCount: images.length, tags } }); continue; } try { const createResult = await shopifyGraphQL(env, ` mutation($product: ProductCreateInput!, $media: [CreateMediaInput!]!) { productCreate(product: $product, media: $media) { product { id variants(first: 1) { edges { node { id } } } } userErrors { field message } } }`, { product: { title: row.title, descriptionHtml, vendor: row.manufacturer || 'XFITTING', productType: row.fitting_type || '', tags, status: 'DRAFT', // always create as draft first — never auto-publish live, matches the caution used for every other marketplace push in this project }, media: images.map(url => ({ originalSource: url, mediaContentType: 'IMAGE' })), }); const createErrors = createResult.productCreate.userErrors; if (createErrors.length) { results.push({ baseSku, ok: false, error: createErrors.map(e => e.message).join('; ') }); continue; } const productId = createResult.productCreate.product.id; const variantId = createResult.productCreate.product.variants.edges[0].node.id; const updateResult = await shopifyGraphQL(env, ` mutation($productId: ID!, $variants: [ProductVariantsBulkInput!]!) { productVariantsBulkUpdate(productId: $productId, variants: $variants) { productVariants { id price } userErrors { field message } } }`, { productId, variants: [{ id: variantId, price: String(row.starting_price), barcode: row.upc || undefined, inventoryItem: { sku: row.sku, ...(row.weight_lbs ? { measurement: { weight: { value: row.weight_lbs, unit: 'POUNDS' } } } : {}) }, }], }); const updateErrors = updateResult.productVariantsBulkUpdate.userErrors; if (updateErrors.length) { results.push({ baseSku, ok: false, error: 'Product created but variant update failed: ' + updateErrors.map(e => e.message).join('; '), productId }); continue; } const pushedAt = new Date().toISOString(); await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE product_catalog SET shopify_product_id=?, shopify_pushed_at=? WHERE sku=?') .bind(productId, pushedAt, row.sku).run().catch(e => console.error('[Shopify] tracking write-back failed (listing still created fine):', e.message)); results.push({ baseSku, ok: true, productId, sku: row.sku, status: 'DRAFT', pushedAt }); } catch (e) { results.push({ baseSku, ok: false, error: e.message }); } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, results }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function getAmazonToken(env) { const now = Date.now(); if (_amazonTokenCache && _amazonTokenCache.exp > now+60000) return _amazonTokenCache.token; const res = await fetch('https://api.amazon.com/auth/o2/token', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: new URLSearchParams({ grant_type:'refresh_token', refresh_token:env.AMAZON_REFRESH_TOKEN, client_id:env.AMAZON_CLIENT_ID, client_secret:env.AMAZON_CLIENT_SECRET }) }); const data = await res.json(); if (!data.access_token) throw new Error('Amazon token error: '+JSON.stringify(data)); _amazonTokenCache = { token: data.access_token, exp: now+(data.expires_in*1000) }; return data.access_token; } async function spApiRetry(fn, retries=3, delayMs=5000) { for (let attempt=0; attemptsetTimeout(r,delayMs*(attempt+1))); continue; } throw e; } } } // GET /amazon/producttype-search?q=... — find candidate Amazon product // types for a genuinely new listing, before attempting creation. Same // "discover the real schema first" approach that eventually worked for // Walmart and eBay — SP-API's Product Type Definitions API confirms via // its own docs that keyword search is the intended way to find this. async function amazonProductTypeSearch(url, env) { try { const q = url.searchParams.get('q'); if (!q) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'q parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getAmazonToken(env); const marketplaceId = env.AMAZON_MARKETPLACE_ID || 'ATVPDKIKX0DER'; const res = await spApiRetry(() => fetch( `https://sellingpartnerapi-na.amazon.com/definitions/2020-09-01/productTypes?keywords=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&marketplaceIds=${marketplaceId}`, { headers: { 'x-amz-access-token': token } } )); const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Product type search failed: ' + JSON.stringify(data) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const results = (data.productTypes || []).map(pt => ({ name: pt.name, marketplaceIds: pt.marketplaceIds })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, query: q, results }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /amazon/producttype-schema?productType=... — retrieves the actual // required/optional attribute schema for a specific product type, once // found via the search above. This is what tells us the real field // requirements ahead of attempting creation, same role // getItemAspectsForCategory played for eBay. async function amazonProductTypeSchema(url, env) { try { const productType = url.searchParams.get('productType'); if (!productType) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'productType parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getAmazonToken(env); const marketplaceId = env.AMAZON_MARKETPLACE_ID || 'ATVPDKIKX0DER'; const sellerId = env.AMAZON_SELLER_ID || ''; const res = await spApiRetry(() => fetch( `https://sellingpartnerapi-na.amazon.com/definitions/2020-09-01/productTypes/${encodeURIComponent(productType)}?marketplaceIds=${marketplaceId}&requirements=LISTING${sellerId ? '&sellerId=' + sellerId : ''}`, { headers: { 'x-amz-access-token': token } } )); const data = await res.json(); if (!res.ok) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Schema fetch failed: ' + JSON.stringify(data) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); // BUGFIX: the actual schema is a pre-signed S3 download link, not // inline in this response — confirmed via a real call. That link // can't be fetched by the person directly (their own browser/tool // restrictions aside, it's an awkward multi-hundred-character signed // URL), but the worker can follow it directly with a plain fetch, no // such restriction applies here. Extracts required fields from the // real JSON Schema's top-level "required" array plus each property's // own type/enum, rather than guessing at structure. let requiredFields = []; let propertyDetails = {}; if (data.schema && data.schema.link && data.schema.link.resource) { const schemaRes = await fetch(data.schema.link.resource); if (schemaRes.ok) { const schemaJson = await schemaRes.json(); requiredFields = schemaJson.required || []; const props = schemaJson.properties || {}; for (const key in props) { const p = props[key]; // Amazon's schema often nests the real type/enum one level // down inside an "items" array (since most attributes are // themselves arrays of objects) — check both places. const inner = (p.items && p.items.properties) ? p.items.properties : p.properties; propertyDetails[key] = { required: requiredFields.includes(key), type: p.type, description: p.description || (inner && inner.value && inner.value.description) || '', // Confirmed via the real response — nested structure matches // for description, so checking the same path for enum values. allowedValues: (inner && inner.value && inner.value.enum) || null, }; } } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, productType, requiredFields, propertyDetails, rawResponse: data, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /amazon/listing-create — Body: {baseSkus, dryRun} // Creates brand-new Amazon listings via the Listings Items API (PUT), // using the VALVE product type schema confirmed via live SP-API calls — // required fields (item_name, brand, product_description, bullet_point, // country_of_origin, supplier_declared_dg_hz_regulation) and their // closed-list values (confirmed real, not guessed) mapped to // product_catalog. Reuses the same attribute-value structure already // working in amazonUpdatePrice. // HONESTY NOTE: this is a well-researched first attempt built from real, // confirmed schema data — not yet verified against a live creation call. // Always dry-run first; expect the first real submission may surface // field-level errors needing adjustment, same pattern as every other // marketplace integration in this project. // GET /inventory/d1-schema?table=... — introspects a D1 table's real // columns via SQLite's PRAGMA table_info, or lists every table if no // specific name is given. Built because the earlier /inventory/d1-query // endpoint referenced in conversation never actually existed, and // several tables (master_list, products, upc, cogs) need their real // schemas confirmed before any sync flow writes to them — guessing at // column names for an insert/update risks silently writing to the // wrong place. // POST /inventory/set-fnsku — Body: {sku, fnsku} // Adds/updates the FNSKU for a given Amazon SKU in platform_skus, // creating the fnsku column on first use if it doesn't exist yet. // Replaces the FBA_Live sheet's role — a genuinely simple two-column // mapping (part number <-> FNSKU) confirmed from the actual FBA_Live // parsing code, now living in D1 instead. async function inventorySetFnsku(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const sku = (body.sku || '').trim(); const fnsku = (body.fnsku || '').trim(); if (!sku || !fnsku) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'sku and fnsku both required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); await env.DB.prepare('ALTER TABLE platform_skus ADD COLUMN fnsku TEXT').run().catch(()=>{}); const existing = await d1First(env, "SELECT id FROM platform_skus WHERE platform='amazon' AND sku=?", [sku]); if (existing) { await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE platform_skus SET fnsku=?, updated_at=? WHERE id=?").bind(fnsku, new Date().toISOString(), existing.id).run(); } else { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: `No platform_skus row found for amazon sku "${sku}" — this only updates an existing row, doesn't create a new listing record` }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, sku, fnsku }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/fnsku-lookup?fnsku=... — replaces FBA_Live's // fnskuToPartNum map. Given a scanned FNSKU, returns the matching real // SKU/base_sku, for the AWD label scan-and-verify workflow. // GET /inventory/upc-d1-lookup?q=... — search the real D1 upc table by // SKU/part number (exact or partial) or by UPC (exact), returning // inside_upc/outside_upc. This is what's actually needed — the D1 // equivalent of the existing sheet-based inventoryLookup, for the UPC // app's "search by part number, pull up inside UPC" use case. async function inventoryUpcD1Lookup(url, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const q = (url.searchParams.get('q') || '').trim(); if (!q) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'q parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const qDigits = q.replace(/\D/g, ''); const isNumeric = qDigits.length >= 8 && qDigits === q.replace(/\s/g, ''); let rows; if (isNumeric) { // Looks like a UPC — exact match on either inside or outside rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT * FROM upc WHERE inside_upc=? OR outside_upc=? LIMIT 20', [qDigits, qDigits]); } else { // Otherwise treat as a SKU/part number — exact match first, then partial rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT * FROM upc WHERE sku=? LIMIT 20', [q]); if (!rows.length) rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT * FROM upc WHERE sku LIKE ? LIMIT 20', ['%' + q + '%']); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, query: q, count: rows.length, results: rows.map(r => ({ sku: r.sku, baseSku: r.base_sku, insideUpc: r.inside_upc, outsideUpc: r.outside_upc })), }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function inventoryFnskuLookup(url, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const fnsku = (url.searchParams.get('fnsku') || '').trim(); if (!fnsku) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'fnsku parameter required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); await env.DB.prepare('ALTER TABLE platform_skus ADD COLUMN fnsku TEXT').run().catch(()=>{}); const row = await d1First(env, "SELECT sku, base_sku, title FROM platform_skus WHERE platform='amazon' AND fnsku=?", [fnsku]); if (!row) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, found: false }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, found: true, sku: row.sku, baseSku: row.base_sku, title: row.title }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function d1SchemaInspect(url, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const table = url.searchParams.get('table'); if (!table) { const tables = await d1All(env, "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' ORDER BY name"); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, tables: tables.map(t => t.name) }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Table names can't be parameterized like values can (SQL syntax // doesn't support bound params there) — this endpoint is already // behind the same mgmt-only auth as every other sensitive route in // this worker, but a basic character whitelist is a cheap extra // safety net against malformed input regardless. if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$/.test(table)) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Invalid table name' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const cols = await d1All(env, `PRAGMA table_info(${table})`); if (!cols.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: `Table "${table}" not found or has no columns` }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const sample = await d1All(env, `SELECT * FROM ${table} LIMIT 2`); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, table, columns: cols.map(c => ({ name: c.name, type: c.type, notNull: !!c.notnull, pk: !!c.pk })), sampleRows: sample, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function amazonListingCreate(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const baseSkus = Array.isArray(body.baseSkus) ? body.baseSkus : []; const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; // Confirmed with the user: FBA is a genuinely separate listing from // FBM, not a toggle on the same one — same real SKU plus a trailing // "-" distinguishes it. FBA also has a real, confirmed-from-Amazon's- // own-docs requirement: quantity must be OMITTED entirely (Amazon // manages FBA stock itself based on what's actually received at // their warehouses, not a seller-declared number), and the channel // code is AMAZON_NA, not DEFAULT. const fulfillmentType = body.fulfillmentType === 'fba' ? 'fba' : 'fbm'; if (!baseSkus.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'baseSkus array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); if (!env.AMAZON_SELLER_ID) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'AMAZON_SELLER_ID env var not set' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const sellerId = env.AMAZON_SELLER_ID; const mid = env.AMAZON_MARKETPLACE_ID || 'ATVPDKIKX0DER'; const COUNTRY_OF_ORIGIN_ISO = 'CN'; // confirmed from the real allowedValues list — 2-letter ISO code, not the full word const payloads = []; const skipped = []; for (const baseSku of baseSkus) { const rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT * FROM product_catalog WHERE base_sku=? ORDER BY pack_qty ASC', [baseSku.toUpperCase()]); if (!rows.length) { skipped.push({ baseSku, reason: 'Not found in product_catalog' }); continue; } for (const row of rows) { if (!row.upc) { skipped.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, reason: 'No UPC' }); continue; } const missing = []; if (!row.title_short) missing.push('title_short'); if (!row.description) missing.push('description'); if (!row.starting_price) missing.push('starting_price'); if (missing.length) { skipped.push({ baseSku, sku: row.sku, reason: 'Missing: ' + missing.join(', ') }); continue; } const bulletPoints = [row.item_highlights, row.bullet_1, row.bullet_2, row.bullet_3, row.bullet_4, row.bullet_5] .filter(Boolean) .map(v => ({ value: v, marketplace_id: mid })); payloads.push({ sku: fulfillmentType === 'fba' ? row.sku + '-' : row.sku, productType: 'VALVE', requirements: 'LISTING', attributes: { item_name: [{ value: row.title_short, marketplace_id: mid }], brand: [{ value: row.manufacturer || 'XFITTING', marketplace_id: mid }], // CONFIRMED via a real submission's error response — these // fields were NOT in the schema's top-level "required" array // returned earlier, meaning Amazon's actual requirements are // context-dependent beyond what that check surfaced. manufacturer: [{ value: row.manufacturer || 'XFITTING', marketplace_id: mid }], model_name: [{ value: row.model_name || row.base_sku, marketplace_id: mid }], // CONFIRMED via real error: model_number has a stricter // 40-char limit than model_name (which uses the same source // value and did NOT get flagged) — using base_sku directly // here instead, since it's guaranteed short. model_number: [{ value: row.base_sku, marketplace_id: mid }], number_of_items: [{ value: row.pack_qty || 1, marketplace_id: mid }], list_price: [{ value: row.msrp || row.starting_price, currency: 'USD', marketplace_id: mid }], // CONFIRMED required via real error. included_components: // reasonable for a simple, single-piece item — describes // what's actually in the box. color: no dedicated column in // product_catalog, using material as the closest reasonable // stand-in for a metal fitting with no true color variant. included_components: [{ value: '1 x ' + (row.title_short || 'Item'), marketplace_id: mid }], color: [{ value: row.material || 'Brass', marketplace_id: mid }], // CONFIRMED via real errors — item_dimensions is required // (specifically height, per the error), but previously this // was all-or-nothing (all three or none). Now builds // whatever's actually available, height first since that's // what errored specifically. item_dimensions: (row.length_in || row.width_in || row.height_in) ? [Object.assign( {}, row.height_in ? { height: { value: row.height_in, unit: 'inches' } } : {}, row.length_in ? { length: { value: row.length_in, unit: 'inches' } } : {}, row.width_in ? { width: { value: row.width_in, unit: 'inches' } } : {}, { marketplace_id: mid } )] : undefined, // CONFIRMED required via real error — no batteries in this // product line. batteries_required: [{ value: false, marketplace_id: mid }], // MAJOR BUGFIX: confirmed via a real report — images weren't // showing on live listings because this field was never // being sent at all. Also confirmed field structure is // genuinely different from every other attribute here — it // uses "media_location", not "value" (verified from // multiple real developer reports/Amazon's own docs). // HONESTY NOTE: Amazon has documented, real issues rejecting // image URLs not hosted on Amazon/AWS S3 — these images are // on Shopify's CDN, a different host. This fix corrects the // confirmed missing-field bug; if images still don't show // after this, the CDN-hosting restriction is the next thing // to investigate. main_product_image_locator: row.image_url_1 ? [{ media_location: row.image_url_1, marketplace_id: mid }] : undefined, other_product_image_locator_1: row.image_url_2 ? [{ media_location: row.image_url_2, marketplace_id: mid }] : undefined, other_product_image_locator_2: row.image_url_3 ? [{ media_location: row.image_url_3, marketplace_id: mid }] : undefined, other_product_image_locator_3: row.image_url_4 ? [{ media_location: row.image_url_4, marketplace_id: mid }] : undefined, other_product_image_locator_4: row.image_url_5 ? [{ media_location: row.image_url_5, marketplace_id: mid }] : undefined, item_weight: row.weight_lbs ? [{ value: row.weight_lbs, unit: 'pounds', marketplace_id: mid }] : undefined, // CONFIRMED required via real error — a separate, // package-level pair distinct from item_dimensions/ // item_weight above (the product's own vs the shipping // package's measurements). Reusing the same catalog data // since it's the same physical package being shipped. item_package_weight: row.weight_lbs ? [{ value: row.weight_lbs, unit: 'pounds', marketplace_id: mid }] : undefined, item_package_dimensions: (row.length_in || row.width_in || row.height_in) ? [Object.assign( {}, row.height_in ? { height: { value: row.height_in, unit: 'inches' } } : {}, row.length_in ? { length: { value: row.length_in, unit: 'inches' } } : {}, row.width_in ? { width: { value: row.width_in, unit: 'inches' } } : {}, { marketplace_id: mid } )] : undefined, product_description: [{ value: row.description, marketplace_id: mid }], bullet_point: bulletPoints, country_of_origin: [{ value: COUNTRY_OF_ORIGIN_ISO, marketplace_id: mid }], supplier_declared_dg_hz_regulation: [{ value: 'not_applicable', marketplace_id: mid }], externally_assigned_product_identifier: [{ type: 'upc', value: row.upc, marketplace_id: mid }], condition_type: [{ value: 'new_new', marketplace_id: mid }], purchasable_offer: [{ marketplace_id: mid, currency: 'USD', our_price: [{ schedule: [{ value_with_tax: row.starting_price }] }], }], fulfillment_availability: fulfillmentType === 'fba' ? [{ fulfillment_channel_code: 'AMAZON_NA', marketplace_id: mid }] : [{ fulfillment_channel_code: 'DEFAULT', quantity: 8888, marketplace_id: mid }], }, }); } } if (!payloads.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'No rows ready to submit', skipped }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); if (dryRun) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, itemCount: payloads.length, skipped, preview: payloads }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getAmazonToken(env); const amzDate = () => new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:\-.]/g,'').slice(0,15)+'Z'; const results = []; for (const p of payloads) { try { const putUrl = `https://sellingpartnerapi-na.amazon.com/listings/2021-08-01/items/${encodeURIComponent(sellerId)}/${encodeURIComponent(p.sku)}` + `?marketplaceIds=${encodeURIComponent(mid)}`; const putRes = await spApiRetry(() => fetch(putUrl, { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'x-amz-access-token': token, 'x-amz-date': amzDate(), 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ productType: p.productType, requirements: p.requirements, attributes: p.attributes }), })); const putData = await putRes.json().catch(() => ({})); // BUGFIX: confirmed via a real submission — Amazon's Listings // Items API returns HTTP 200 even when status comes back // INVALID, with the actual reason in the "issues" array // regardless of HTTP status. Only checking issues when // putRes.ok was false silently discarded exactly the // information needed to see why a listing was rejected. const hasIssues = putData.issues && putData.issues.length; results.push({ sku: p.sku, ok: putRes.ok && putData.status !== 'INVALID' && !hasIssues, status: putData.status, error: (!putRes.ok || hasIssues) ? JSON.stringify(putData.issues || putData.errors || putData) : null, }); } catch(e) { results.push({ sku: p.sku, ok: false, error: e.message }); } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: results.every(r => r.ok), itemCount: payloads.length, skipped, results }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /amazon/variation-create — Body: {baseSku, dryRun} // Creates an Amazon parent-child variation family for all pack sizes of // a base SKU, varying by NUMBER_OF_ITEMS — confirmed via Amazon's own // official docs as a genuinely supported variation theme component // ("SIZE/COLOR/NUMBER_OF_ITEMS"), unlike Walmart where quantity-based // variations weren't supported for the equivalent category at all. // // Uses DERIVED skus (a "-VAR" suffix) for every member, same fix // pattern proven necessary for Walmart — confirmed via real developer // reports that converting an already-live standalone listing into a // variation parent/child typically fails outright. if(!reportDocumentId)return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,error:'reportDocumentId required'}),{status:400,headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}})); const amzToken=await getAmazonToken(env); const amzDate=new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:\-.]/g,'').slice(0,15)+'Z'; const docRes=await fetch(`https://sellingpartnerapi-na.amazon.com/reports/2021-06-30/documents/${reportDocumentId}`,{headers:{'x-amz-access-token':amzToken,'x-amz-date':amzDate}}); const docData=await docRes.json(); if(!docRes.ok||!docData.url)throw new Error('Document fetch failed'); const reportRes=await fetch(docData.url); const rawBytes=await reportRes.arrayBuffer(); const magic=new Uint8Array(rawBytes.slice(0,2)); let reportText; if(magic[0]===0x1f&&magic[1]===0x8b){ try{ const ds=new DecompressionStream('gzip');const writer=ds.writable.getWriter();const reader=ds.readable.getReader(); writer.write(new Uint8Array(rawBytes));writer.close(); const chunks=[];let done=false; while(!done){const{value,done:d}=await reader.read();if(value)chunks.push(value);done=d;} const total=chunks.reduce((a,c)=>a+c.length,0);const combined=new Uint8Array(total);let offset=0; for(const chunk of chunks){combined.set(chunk,offset);offset+=chunk.length;} reportText=new TextDecoder().decode(combined); }catch(e){reportText=new TextDecoder().decode(rawBytes);} } else { reportText=new TextDecoder().decode(rawBytes); } let reportData; try{reportData=JSON.parse(reportText);}catch(e){throw new Error('Report parse failed: '+e.message.slice(0,80));} const payload=reportData.payload||reportData; const byAsin=payload.salesAndTrafficByAsin||[]; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:true,asinsMapped:byAsin.length,completedAt:new Date().toISOString()}),{headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}})); } catch(e){return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,error:e.message}),{status:500,headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}}));} } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // D1 DATABASE LAYER — Phase 1 (dual-write) // env.DB = D1Database binding (set in wrangler.toml) // All D1 calls degrade gracefully — if DB not bound, Sheets still works. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function d1Run(env, sql, params=[]) { if (!env.DB) return null; try { return await env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...params).run(); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1]', e.message, sql.slice(0,80)); return null; } } async function d1All(env, sql, params=[]) { if (!env.DB) return []; try { const r = await env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...params).all(); return r.results||[]; } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] query', e.message); return []; } } async function d1First(env, sql, params=[]) { if (!env.DB) return null; try { return await env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...params).first(); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] first', e.message); return null; } } // Update cases for a Master_List row by sheet_row number async function d1UpdateCases(env, sheetRow, newCases) { if (!env.DB || !sheetRow) return null; try { const r = await env.DB.prepare( 'UPDATE master_list SET cases=?,updated_at=? WHERE sheet_row=?' ).bind(newCases, new Date().toISOString(), sheetRow).run(); if ((r?.meta?.changes ?? r?.changes ?? 0) === 0) { console.warn('[D1] updateCases: no row found for sheet_row=' + sheetRow + ', cases=' + newCases); } return r; } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] updateCases:', e.message); return null; } } // Insert a new Master_List row (for isNew IN operations) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // REQUEST DEDUPLICATION — for offline-queued writes (sw.js v4+) // // The offline queue in the service worker retries a POST if the fetch // throws — but that also covers the case where the request actually // reached the worker and was processed successfully, and only the // RESPONSE was lost on the way back to a flaky-signal phone. Without // this, that scenario replays the same transfer/log entry a second time, // creating a real duplicate — this is believed to be the actual cause of // the stuck/duplicated transfer entries from the mobile signal-loss // incident. sw.js v4 now attaches a client-generated _requestId to every // queued request's body; this is the server-side half that makes it // actually prevent duplicates, not just carry an inert id. // // Uses an ATOMIC CLAIM pattern, not a naive check-then-insert: the first // INSERT for a given request_id is what "claims" it (PRIMARY KEY conflict // on a second attempt is the actual duplicate signal), so two genuinely // simultaneous replays of the same id can't both slip past a check and // double-write — only one can ever successfully claim the row. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function ensureProcessedRequestsTable(env) { await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS processed_requests ( request_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, endpoint TEXT NOT NULL, response_json TEXT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL )`).run(); } // Returns { isDuplicate: false } if this is the first time this // requestId has been seen — caller should proceed normally, then call // recordRequestResult() once done. // Returns { isDuplicate: true, cachedResponse } if it's already been // processed (or is currently mid-processing by a concurrent replay) — // caller should return cachedResponse directly instead of doing the work. async function claimRequestId(env, requestId, endpoint) { if (!requestId || !env.DB) return { isDuplicate: false }; // no id sent, or D1 unavailable — dedup not possible, proceed normally try { await ensureProcessedRequestsTable(env); try { await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO processed_requests (request_id,endpoint,response_json,created_at) VALUES (?,?,NULL,?)' ).bind(requestId, endpoint, new Date().toISOString()).run(); return { isDuplicate: false }; // successfully claimed — genuinely first time } catch (insertErr) { // PRIMARY KEY conflict — someone already claimed this id const existing = await d1First(env, 'SELECT response_json, created_at FROM processed_requests WHERE request_id=?', [requestId]); if (existing && existing.response_json) { console.log('[Dedup] requestId already processed, returning cached result:', requestId); return { isDuplicate: true, cachedResponse: JSON.parse(existing.response_json) }; } // Claimed but response_json is still NULL. Could be a genuine // concurrent race (rare — a phone replays its own queue // sequentially), or it could be an ABANDONED claim from a prior // attempt that threw an unhandled exception somewhere between // claiming and recording its result (inventoryLog has no single // top-level try/catch, so this is a real possibility, not just // theoretical). A 2-minute-old unresolved claim is treated as // abandoned rather than genuinely in-progress — no real request // takes anywhere near that long, and blocking retries forever on // a truly stuck claim would be worse than the rare duplicate this // whole mechanism exists to prevent. const ageMs = existing ? (Date.now() - new Date(existing.created_at).getTime()) : Infinity; if (ageMs > 2 * 60 * 1000) { console.log('[Dedup] stale unresolved claim (>2min old), treating as abandoned and re-claiming:', requestId); await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM processed_requests WHERE request_id=?').bind(requestId).run().catch(()=>{}); try { await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO processed_requests (request_id,endpoint,response_json,created_at) VALUES (?,?,NULL,?)' ).bind(requestId, endpoint, new Date().toISOString()).run(); return { isDuplicate: false }; } catch(_) { /* another replay won the race to re-claim it — fall through to blocking below */ } } console.log('[Dedup] requestId claimed but not yet finished, treating as duplicate-in-progress:', requestId); return { isDuplicate: true, cachedResponse: { ok: true, alreadyInProgress: true, message: 'This request is already being processed — please refresh in a moment.' } }; } } catch (e) { console.error('[Dedup] claim failed, proceeding without dedup protection:', e.message); return { isDuplicate: false }; // fail open — a dedup outage shouldn't block real functionality } } async function recordRequestResult(env, requestId, responseBody) { if (!requestId || !env.DB) return; try { await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE processed_requests SET response_json=? WHERE request_id=?') .bind(JSON.stringify(responseBody), requestId).run(); } catch (e) { console.error('[Dedup] recording result failed (request still succeeded, just not cached for future dedup):', e.message); } } async function cleanProcessedRequests(env) { if (!env.DB) return; try { await ensureProcessedRequestsTable(env); const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); const result = await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM processed_requests WHERE created_at < ?').bind(cutoff).run(); console.log('[Dedup] cleaned old processed_requests rows:', result?.meta?.changes ?? result?.changes ?? 0); } catch (e) { console.error('[Dedup] cleanup failed:', e.message); } } async function d1InsertMasterRow(env, row) { return d1Run(env, `INSERT INTO master_list (sku,base_sku,name,part_num,location,cases,units_per_case,vendor,price,prev_notes,sheet_row,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`, [row.sku||'', row.base_sku||'', row.name||'', row.part_num||'', row.location||'', row.cases||0, row.units_per_case||0, row.vendor||'', row.price||0, row.prev_notes||'', row.sheet_row||0, new Date().toISOString()] ); } // Insert an Inventory_Log entry async function d1InsertLog(env, row, sheetRow) { return d1Run(env, `INSERT INTO inventory_log (sheet_row,timestamp,type,part_num,location,cases,initials,notes,status, verified_by,verified_at,overwrite_loc,is_new,is_placeholder, master_row_index,sku,transfer_id,paired_location,name) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`, [sheetRow||0, row.timestamp||new Date().toISOString(), (row.type||'').toUpperCase(), row.partNum||'', row.location||'', parseFloat(row.cases)||0, row.initials||'', row.notes||'', row.status||'Pending', '', '', row.overwriteLocation||'', (row.isNew==='TRUE'||row.isNew===true)?1:0, (row.isPlaceholder==='TRUE'||row.isPlaceholder===true)?1:0, row.rowIndex||'', row.sku||'', '', '', row.name||''] ); } // Update log row status (Verified/Rejected) // d1Id: the D1 auto-increment id returned at insert time — most reliable key // sheetRow: Sheets row number — fallback when d1Id not available (older entries) // partNum + location: last resort composite key for pre-fix entries async function d1UpdateLogStatus(env, sheetRow, status, verifiedBy, ts, partNum, location, type, d1Id) { if (!env.DB) return null; try { // Primary: update by D1 id — unambiguous, always correct if (d1Id) { try { const r = await env.DB.prepare( 'UPDATE inventory_log SET status=?,verified_by=?,verified_at=? WHERE id=?' ).bind(status, verifiedBy||'', ts||'', d1Id).run(); console.log('[D1] id update result — id:', d1Id, 'changes:', r?.changes, 'meta:', JSON.stringify(r?.meta||{})); if (r && r.changes > 0) return r; } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] id update THREW:', e.message, 'id:', d1Id, 'status:', status); } } // Fallback 1: update by sheet_row — works for entries inserted after sequential write fix if (sheetRow) { const r2 = await env.DB.prepare( 'UPDATE inventory_log SET status=?,verified_by=?,verified_at=? WHERE sheet_row=?' ).bind(status, verifiedBy||'', ts||'', sheetRow).run(); if (r2 && r2.changes > 0) return r2; } // Fallback 2: composite key — for old entries with sheet_row=0 // Two-step: find id first, then update by id if (partNum && location) { const row = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT id FROM inventory_log WHERE UPPER(part_num)=? AND UPPER(location)=? AND status='Pending' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1` ).bind(partNum.toUpperCase(), location.toUpperCase()).first(); if (row && row.id) { const r3 = await env.DB.prepare( 'UPDATE inventory_log SET status=?,verified_by=?,verified_at=? WHERE id=?' ).bind(status, verifiedBy||'', ts||'', row.id).run(); if ((r3?.meta?.changes ?? r3?.changes ?? 0) > 0) console.log('[D1] composite key matched id=' + row.id); return r3; } } return null; } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] updateLogStatus:', e.message); return null; } } // ── POST /inventory/d1-migrate ──────────────────────────────────────────────── // One-time migration: reads Master_List + recent Inventory_Log from Sheets, // writes everything to D1. Clears tables first — fully idempotent. // Call with { dryRun: true } first to preview counts without writing. async function inventoryD1Migrate(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not bound — add [[d1_databases]] to wrangler.toml' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(()=>({})); const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; // Fetch Master_List and ALL Inventory_Log rows in parallel // No row limit — always migrate everything to prevent missing pending items const [mlData, logData] = await Promise.all([ invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(()=>({values:[]})), invSheetGet(env, 'Inventory_Log!A2:R60000').catch(()=>({values:[]})) ]); const mlRows = mlData.values || []; const logRows = logData.values || []; if (dryRun) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, masterListRows: mlRows.length-1, logRows: logRows.length, message: 'Dry run — no data written. POST with dryRun:false to execute.' }), { headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); // Clear and reload await d1Run(env, 'DELETE FROM master_list'); await d1Run(env, 'DELETE FROM inventory_log'); // Insert Master_List in batches of 50 let mlInserted = 0; const BATCH = 50; for (let i = 1; i < mlRows.length; i += BATCH) { const batch = mlRows.slice(i, i+BATCH); const stmts = batch.map((r, bi) => { const partNum = String(r[6]||'').trim().toUpperCase(); const sku = String(r[3]||'').trim(); if (!partNum && !sku) return null; const baseSku = (sku||partNum).split('=')[0].trim().toUpperCase(); return env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO master_list (sku,base_sku,name,part_num,location,cases,units_per_case,vendor,price,prev_notes,sheet_row,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind(sku, baseSku, String(r[4]||'').trim(), partNum, String(r[7]||'').trim(), parseFloat(r[8])||0, parseFloat(r[9])||0, String(r[12]||'').trim(), parseFloat(r[13])||0, String(r[10]||'').trim(), i+bi+1, new Date().toISOString()); }).filter(Boolean); if (stmts.length) { await env.DB.batch(stmts); mlInserted += stmts.length; } } // Insert Inventory_Log in batches of 50 let logInserted = 0; for (let i = 0; i < logRows.length; i += BATCH) { const batch = logRows.slice(i, i+BATCH); const stmts = batch.map((r, bi) => { if (!r[0]) return null; return env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO inventory_log (sheet_row,timestamp,type,part_num,location,cases,initials,notes,status, verified_by,verified_at,overwrite_loc,is_new,is_placeholder, master_row_index,sku,transfer_id,paired_location,name) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind( i+2+bi, String(r[0]||''), String(r[1]||'').toUpperCase(), String(r[2]||'').toUpperCase(), String(r[3]||''), parseFloat(r[4])||0, String(r[5]||''), String(r[6]||''), String(r[7]||'Pending'), String(r[8]||''), String(r[9]||''), String(r[10]||''), r[11]==='TRUE'?1:0, r[12]==='TRUE'?1:0, String(r[13]||''), String(r[14]||''), String(r[15]||''), String(r[16]||''), String(r[17]||'') ); }).filter(Boolean); if (stmts.length) { await env.DB.batch(stmts); logInserted += stmts.length; } } const mlCount = await d1First(env, 'SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM master_list'); const logCount = await d1First(env, 'SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM inventory_log'); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, masterList: { sheetRows: mlRows.length-1, inserted: mlInserted, d1Count: mlCount?.cnt||0 }, inventoryLog: { sheetRows: logRows.length, inserted: logInserted, d1Count: logCount?.cnt||0 }, migratedAt: new Date().toISOString(), next: 'Now call GET /inventory/d1-verify to confirm counts match Sheets' }), { headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } } // ── GET /inventory/d1-verify ────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Spot-checks 10 random rows: compares D1 cases vs live Sheet value. // Run after migration to confirm data matches before trusting D1 reads. async function inventoryD1Verify(env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:'D1 not bound' }), { status:503, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); try { const rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT part_num,location,cases,sheet_row FROM master_list WHERE cases>0 ORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT 10' ); const checks = await Promise.all(rows.map(async row => { const s = await invSheetGet(env, `Master_List!I${row.sheet_row}:I${row.sheet_row}`).catch(()=>({values:[]})); const sheetCases = parseFloat(((s.values||[])[0]||[])[0]) || 0; return { partNum:row.part_num, location:row.location, sheetRow:row.sheet_row, d1Cases:row.cases, sheetCases, match: Math.abs(row.cases-sheetCases)<0.01 }; })); const allMatch = checks.every(c=>c.match); const mlTotal = await d1First(env,'SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM master_list'); const logTotal = await d1First(env,'SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM inventory_log'); const pending = await d1First(env,"SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM inventory_log WHERE status='Pending'"); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, allMatch, checks, d1MasterCount: mlTotal?.cnt||0, d1LogCount: logTotal?.cnt||0, d1Pending: pending?.cnt||0, status: allMatch ? '✅ D1 matches Sheets — dual-write active, ready for Phase 2' : '⚠ Mismatches found — re-run migration' }), { headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } } // POST /inventory/edit-row // MGMT-only manual correction of cases count on a pending review item. // Writes to Master_List in Sheets AND D1 simultaneously. // Also appends an audit entry to Inventory_Log. async function inventoryEditRow(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { rowIndex, partNum, location, newCases, notes, item } = body; if (!partNum || !location || newCases === undefined || newCases === null) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing required fields' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const rowIndexInt = parseInt(rowIndex) || 0; const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const verifiedBy = 'MGMT'; // 1. Find the live Master_List row for this partNum+location const mlData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:I15000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const mlRows = mlData.values || []; const liveRow = findMasterRow(mlRows, partNum, location); if (liveRow) { // 2. Update cases in Sheets await invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!I${liveRow}`, [[newCases]]); // 3. Update cases in D1 — fire-and-forget d1UpdateCases(env, liveRow, newCases).catch(() => {}); // 4. Color cell purple (cosmetic) — fire-and-forget invSetPurpleCell(env, liveRow).catch(() => {}); } // 5. Append audit log entry to Inventory_Log const auditRow = [ ts, 'EDIT', partNum.toUpperCase(), location, newCases, verifiedBy, '[MANUAL EDIT] ' + (notes || 'cases corrected'), 'Verified', verifiedBy, ts, '', 'FALSE', 'FALSE', rowIndexInt || '', '', '', '', '' ]; invSheetAppend(env, 'Inventory_Log!A:R', [auditRow]).catch(() => {}); await invalidateMasterListCache(env); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, partNum, location, newCases, liveRow: liveRow || null }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message || 'edit-row failed' }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/sku-search?q=BASE_SKU // Returns all D1 master_list rows matching the base_sku (with Sheets fallback) async function inventorySkuSearch(url, env) { const q = (url.searchParams.get('q') || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const locQ = (url.searchParams.get('location') || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const limit = parseInt(url.searchParams.get('limit') || '6000'); const isAll = q === 'ALL'; // Location search mode if (locQ && env.DB) { try { const rows = await d1All(env, `SELECT m.id, m.sku, m.base_sku, COALESCE(NULLIF(m.name,''), (SELECT p.name FROM products p WHERE UPPER(p.sku)=UPPER(m.base_sku) LIMIT 1), '') as name, m.part_num, m.location, m.cases, m.units_per_case, m.vendor, m.price, m.prev_notes, m.sheet_row FROM master_list m WHERE UPPER(m.location) = ? AND m.cases > 0 ORDER BY m.part_num`, [locQ] ); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, results: rows, count: rows.length, mode: 'location' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } if (!q) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'No query' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); if (env.DB) { try { let rows; if (isAll) { // Initial load — return first N rows joined with products for names // Filter garbage: part_num must contain '=' (variant format) or match digit-dash pattern rows = await d1All(env, `SELECT m.id, m.sku, m.base_sku, COALESCE(NULLIF(m.name,''), (SELECT p.name FROM products p WHERE UPPER(p.sku)=UPPER(m.base_sku) LIMIT 1), '') as name, m.part_num, m.location, m.cases, m.units_per_case, m.vendor, m.price, m.prev_notes, m.sheet_row FROM master_list m WHERE m.part_num != '' AND (m.name != '' OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM products p WHERE UPPER(p.sku)=UPPER(m.base_sku) AND p.name != '')) ${url.searchParams.get('instock') === '1' ? 'AND m.cases > 0' : ''} ORDER BY m.base_sku ${(url.searchParams.get('sort')||'asc').toLowerCase() === 'desc' ? 'DESC' : 'ASC'}, m.part_num LIMIT ?`, [limit] ); } else { const sortDir = (url.searchParams.get('sort') || 'asc').toLowerCase() === 'desc' ? 'DESC' : 'ASC'; rows = await d1All(env, `SELECT m.id, m.sku, m.base_sku, COALESCE(NULLIF(m.name,''), (SELECT p.name FROM products p WHERE UPPER(p.sku)=UPPER(m.base_sku) LIMIT 1), '') as name, m.part_num, m.location, m.cases, m.units_per_case, m.vendor, m.price, m.prev_notes, m.sheet_row FROM master_list m WHERE (UPPER(m.base_sku) = ? OR UPPER(m.part_num) LIKE ?) ${url.searchParams.get('instock') === '1' ? 'AND m.cases > 0' : ''} ORDER BY m.part_num ${sortDir}, m.location`, [q, q + '%'] ); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, results: rows, source: 'd1' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] sku-search:', e.message); } } // Sheets fallback const mlData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const mlRows = mlData.values || []; const results = []; for (let i = 1; i < mlRows.length; i++) { const r = mlRows[i]; const partNum = String(r[6] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const sku = String(r[3] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const base = (sku || partNum).split('=')[0].trim(); if (base === q || partNum.startsWith(q)) { results.push({ id: null, sku: String(r[3]||'').trim(), base_sku: base, name: String(r[4]||'').trim(), part_num: partNum, location: String(r[7]||'').trim(), cases: parseFloat(r[8])||0, units_per_case: parseFloat(r[9])||0, vendor: String(r[12]||'').trim(), price: parseFloat(r[13])||0, prev_notes: String(r[10]||'').trim(), sheet_row: i + 1 }); } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, results, source: 'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // POST /inventory/sku-row — update existing row OR insert new row // Body: { mode: 'update'|'insert', sheetRow, partNum, location, cases, // name, sku, unitsPerCase, vendor, price, prevNotes } async function inventorySkuRow(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { mode, sheetRow, d1Id, partNum, location, cases, name, sku, unitsPerCase, vendor, price, prevNotes } = body; if (!partNum || !location || cases === undefined || cases === null) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'partNum, location, cases required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const casesNum = parseFloat(cases) || 0; const unitsPerCaseN = parseFloat(unitsPerCase) || 0; const priceN = parseFloat(price) || 0; const ts = new Date().toISOString(); // BUGFIX: sheetRow can legitimately be 0 (rows created directly in D1 by // Receive PO for a brand-new SKU/location that was never synced to // Sheets — see inventoryReceiveApply's INSERT path). `0` is falsy in JS, // so the old check `mode === 'update' && sheetRow` silently fell through // to the INSERT branch for every such row — creating a duplicate row // instead of updating the original, which then still showed the old // value after a refresh. Use d1Id (the real, always-unique D1 primary // key) as the authoritative "this is an update" signal instead. const isUpdate = mode === 'update' && (d1Id || sheetRow); if (isUpdate) { // Only touch Sheets if this row actually has a real Sheets row number — // sheet_row=0 means this row was never written to Sheets, so there's // nothing there to update (skip, don't create a stray Sheets row). if (sheetRow) { await invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!D${sheetRow}:N${sheetRow}`, [[ sku || partNum.split('=')[0].trim(), name || '', '', // col F unused partNum.toUpperCase(), location, casesNum, unitsPerCaseN, prevNotes || '', '', // col L vendor || '', priceN ]]); } // Update D1 — prefer the unique id when available; fall back to // sheet_row only for older callers that don't send d1Id yet. // IMPORTANT: d1Run swallows errors and returns null on failure, and a // matching WHERE clause with zero actual rows still "succeeds" with // changes=0. Both cases must be surfaced as a real error to the // caller — never silently claim ok:true when nothing was written. let d1Result = null; if (env.DB) { if (d1Id) { d1Result = await d1Run(env, `UPDATE master_list SET sku=?,name=?,part_num=?,location=?,cases=?, units_per_case=?,vendor=?,price=?,prev_notes=?,updated_at=?
