WHERE sheet_row=?`, [sku||partNum.split('=')[0].trim(), name||'', partNum.toUpperCase(), location, casesNum, unitsPerCaseN, vendor||'', priceN, prevNotes||'', ts, sheetRow] ); } const changes = d1Result?.meta?.changes ?? d1Result?.changes ?? 0; if (changes === 0) { console.error('[D1] sku-row update matched ZERO rows — d1Id:', d1Id, 'sheetRow:', sheetRow); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 update matched no rows — the row may have been deleted or the id/sheetRow is stale. Refresh and try again.', d1Id, sheetRow }), { status: 409, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } await invalidateMasterListCache(env); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, mode: 'update', sheetRow, d1Id, d1Changes: d1Result?.meta?.changes ?? d1Result?.changes ?? 0 }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } else { // Insert new row — append to Master_List const newRow = [ '', '', '', // A B C sku || partNum.split('=')[0].trim(), // D = SKU name || '', // E = Name '', // F partNum.toUpperCase(), // G = PartNum location, // H = Location casesNum, // I = Cases unitsPerCaseN, // J = UnitsPerCase prevNotes || '', // K = PrevNotes '', // L vendor || '', // M = Vendor priceN // N = Price ]; const appendData = await invSheetAppend(env, 'Master_List!A:N', [newRow]); const updatedRange = (appendData.updates&&appendData.updates.updatedRange)||appendData.updatedRange||''; const rowMatch = updatedRange.match(/(\d+):/); const newSheetRow = rowMatch ? parseInt(rowMatch[1]) : null; // Insert into D1 if (env.DB && newSheetRow) { const baseSku = (sku||partNum).split('=')[0].trim().toUpperCase(); await d1Run(env, `INSERT INTO master_list (sku,base_sku,name,part_num,location,cases, units_per_case,vendor,price,prev_notes,sheet_row,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`, [sku||baseSku, baseSku, name||'', partNum.toUpperCase(), location, casesNum, unitsPerCaseN, vendor||'', priceN, prevNotes||'', newSheetRow, ts] ); } await invalidateMasterListCache(env); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, mode: 'insert', sheetRow: newSheetRow }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message || 'sku-row failed' }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/history // Returns Inventory_Log entries filtered by date range, type, partNum, initials // MGMT only — reads from D1, falls back to Sheets async function inventoryHistory(url, env) { try { const days = parseInt(url.searchParams.get('days') || '7'); const typeF = (url.searchParams.get('type') || '').toUpperCase(); const partF = (url.searchParams.get('part') || '').toUpperCase(); const initF = (url.searchParams.get('initials') || '').toUpperCase(); const locF = (url.searchParams.get('location') || '').toUpperCase(); const statusF = (url.searchParams.get('status') || '').toLowerCase(); // pending|verified|rejected|all const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); if (env.DB) { try { let sql = `SELECT sheet_row,timestamp,type,part_num,location,cases,initials, notes,status,verified_by,verified_at,name FROM inventory_log WHERE timestamp >= ?`; const params = [cutoff]; if (typeF && typeF !== 'ALL') { sql += ' AND type = ?'; params.push(typeF); } if (partF) { sql += ' AND UPPER(part_num) LIKE ?'; params.push('%' + partF + '%'); } if (initF) { sql += ' AND UPPER(initials) = ?'; params.push(initF); } if (locF) { sql += ' AND UPPER(location) = ?'; params.push(locF); } if (statusF && statusF !== 'all') { sql += ' AND LOWER(status) = ?'; params.push(statusF); } sql += ' ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 500'; const rows = await d1All(env, sql, params); // Total active cases in stock const totalRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT SUM(cases) as total FROM master_list WHERE cases > 0'); const totalCases = totalRow?.total || 0; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, rows, count: rows.length, source: 'd1', cutoff, days, totalCases }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] history:', e.message); } } // Sheets fallback — read recent log rows const countData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Inventory_Log!H2:H60000').catch(()=>({values:[]})); const totalRows = (countData.values||[]).length; const startRow = Math.max(2, totalRows - 1999 + 2); const data = await invSheetGet(env, `Inventory_Log!A${startRow}:R${totalRows+2}`).catch(()=>({values:[]})); const rows = (data.values||[]) .filter(r => r[0] >= cutoff) .filter(r => !typeF || typeF === 'ALL' || String(r[1]||'').toUpperCase() === typeF) .filter(r => !partF || String(r[2]||'').toUpperCase().includes(partF)) .filter(r => !initF || String(r[5]||'').toUpperCase() === initF) .filter(r => !statusF || statusF === 'all' || String(r[7]||'').toLowerCase() === statusF) .slice(0, 500) .map(r => ({ sheet_row: 0, timestamp: r[0]||'', type: r[1]||'', part_num: r[2]||'', location: r[3]||'', cases: r[4]||0, initials: r[5]||'', notes: r[6]||'', status: r[7]||'', verified_by: r[8]||'', verified_at: r[9]||'', name: r[17]||'' })) .reverse(); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, rows, count: rows.length, source: 'sheets', cutoff, days }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/d1-to-sheets // Reverse sync: reads D1 master_list and updates Master_List cases column in Sheets // Processes in chunks to avoid Sheets API rate limits // MGMT only — run after heavy approve sessions to keep Sheets mirror current async function inventoryD1ToSheets(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(()=>({})); const dryRun = body.dryRun !== false; // default to dryRun=true for safety // Read all rows from D1 master_list that have a valid sheet_row const d1Rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT sheet_row, part_num, location, cases FROM master_list WHERE sheet_row > 0 ORDER BY sheet_row' ); if (dryRun) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, rowCount: d1Rows.length, message: 'Dry run — pass dryRun:false to execute', sample: d1Rows.slice(0, 5).map(r => ({ sheet_row: r.sheet_row, part_num: r.part_num, location: r.location, cases: r.cases })) }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Build batch update requests for Sheets — update only col I (cases) per row // Process in chunks of 100 to avoid API limits const CHUNK = 100; let updated = 0; let errors = 0; for (let i = 0; i < d1Rows.length; i += CHUNK) { const chunk = d1Rows.slice(i, i + CHUNK); const requests = chunk.map(r => ({ range: `Master_List!I${r.sheet_row}`, values: [[r.cases ?? 0]] })); try { const token = await getToken(env); const res = await fetch( `https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${env.SHEET_ID}/values:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ valueInputOption: 'RAW', data: requests }) } ); const data = await res.json(); if (data.error) { console.error('[d1-to-sheets] chunk error:', data.error.message); errors += chunk.length; } else { updated += chunk.length; } } catch(e) { console.error('[d1-to-sheets] chunk failed:', e.message); errors += chunk.length; } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: false, total: d1Rows.length, updated, errors, message: `Synced ${updated} rows from D1 to Sheets. ${errors} errors.` }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/master-list // Returns D1 master_list in same column format as Sheets Master_List // Cols match parseMasterListRows: r[3]=sku r[4]=name r[6]=partNum r[7]=location r[8]=cases r[9]=unitsPerCase r[12]=vendor r[13]=price async function inventoryMasterList(env) { if (!env.DB) { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values: data.values||[], source:'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } try { const rows = await d1All(env, `SELECT sku,name,part_num,location,cases,units_per_case,vendor,price,prev_notes FROM master_list WHERE part_num != '' ORDER BY sheet_row` ); // Build array matching Sheets column structure expected by parseMasterListRows // A=0 B=1 C=2 D=3(sku) E=4(name) F=5 G=6(partNum) H=7(location) I=8(cases) J=9(unitsPerCase) K=10(prevNotes) L=11 M=12(vendor) N=13(price) const values = [['','','','SKU','Name','','Part#','Location','Cases','Each Case Qty','Notes','','Vendor','Price','Total']]; rows.forEach(r => { values.push([ '','','', r.sku||'', r.name||'', '', r.part_num||'', r.location||'', r.cases??0, r.units_per_case||0, r.prev_notes||'', '', r.vendor||'', r.price||0, (r.cases||0)*(r.price||0) ]); }); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values, source:'d1', count:rows.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] inventoryMasterList:', e.message); const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values: data.values||[], source:'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/rebuild-sheets-from-d1 // Full Sheets rebuild — matches each Sheets row to D1 by part_num+location, writes correct cases async function rebuildSheetsFromD1(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(()=>({})); const dryRun = body.dryRun !== false; const sheetData = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!G1:I15000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); const sheetRows = (sheetData.values || []).slice(1); const d1Rows = await d1All(env, `SELECT part_num, location, cases FROM master_list WHERE part_num != ''`); const d1Map = {}; d1Rows.forEach(r => { const key = (r.part_num||'').toUpperCase() + '|' + (r.location||'').toUpperCase(); d1Map[key] = r.cases ?? 0; }); const updates = []; let matched = 0, notFound = 0; sheetRows.forEach((r, i) => { const partNum = String(r[0]||'').trim(); const location = String(r[1]||'').trim(); if (!partNum) return; const key = partNum.toUpperCase() + '|' + location.toUpperCase(); if (d1Map.hasOwnProperty(key)) { updates.push({ row: i+2, cases: d1Map[key] }); matched++; } else notFound++; }); if (dryRun) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, dryRun:true, sheetRows:sheetRows.length, matched, notFound, sample:updates.slice(0,5), message:'Dry run — pass dryRun:false to execute' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const CHUNK = 100; let updated = 0; for (let i = 0; i < updates.length; i += CHUNK) { const chunk = updates.slice(i, i+CHUNK); const token = await getToken(env); const res = await fetch(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${env.SHEET_ID}/values:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ valueInputOption:'RAW', data: chunk.map(u=>({ range:`Master_List!I${u.row}`, values:[[u.cases]] })) }) }); const result = await res.json(); if (!result.error) updated += chunk.length; else console.error('[rebuild-sheets] chunk error:', result.error.message); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, dryRun:false, sheetRows:sheetRows.length, matched, notFound, updated, message:`Rebuilt Sheets from D1 — ${updated} rows updated by part_num+location match` }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/import-cogs async function importCOGS(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(()=>({})); if (body.dryRun === true) { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'COGS!A1:B1000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); const rows = (data.values||[]).slice(1).filter(r => String(r[0]||'').trim()); } const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'COGS!A1:B1000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); const rows = (data.values||[]).slice(1).filter(r => String(r[0]||'').trim()); await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cogs ( base_sku TEXT PRIMARY KEY, unit_price REAL DEFAULT 0, updated_at TEXT )`).run(); await env.DB.prepare('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cogs_base_sku ON cogs(base_sku)').run(); // Last row per base_sku wins const priceMap = {}; rows.forEach(r => { const sku = String(r[0]||'').trim().toUpperCase(); const priceRaw = String(r[1]||'').trim().replace(/[$,]/g, ''); const price = parseFloat(priceRaw) || 0; // Filter garbage rows — valid base SKU: has digits, no spaces, no non-ASCII, length < 30 const isValid = sku.length > 0 && sku.length < 30 && /\d/.test(sku) && sku.indexOf(' ') === -1 && !/[^\x00-\x7F]/.test(sku); inserted += chunk.length; } // Update master_list prices where currently 0 await env.DB.prepare( `UPDATE master_list SET price=(SELECT unit_price FROM cogs WHERE UPPER(cogs.base_sku)=UPPER(master_list.base_sku)) WHERE (price IS NULL OR price=0) AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM cogs WHERE UPPER(cogs.base_sku)=UPPER(master_list.base_sku))` ).run(); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, inserted, message:`Imported ${inserted} base SKUs. master_list prices updated.` }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/update-cogs-from-master // Reads Master_List col G (partNum) + col N (price), updates cogs with latest prices async function updateCogsFromMaster(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!G1:N15000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); const rows = (data.values||[]).slice(1); const priceMap = {}; rows.forEach(r => { const partNum = String(r[0]||'').trim().toUpperCase(); const priceRaw = String(r[7]||'').trim().replace(/[$,]/g, ''); const price = parseFloat(priceRaw) || 0; // col N = index 7 in G:N range if (!partNum || !price) return; const baseSku = partNum.split('=')[0].trim(); if (baseSku) priceMap[baseSku] = price; }); await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cogs ( base_sku TEXT PRIMARY KEY, unit_price REAL DEFAULT 0, updated_at TEXT )`).run(); const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const entries = Object.entries(priceMap); const CHUNK = 50; let updated = 0; for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i += CHUNK) { const chunk = entries.slice(i, i + CHUNK); await env.DB.batch(chunk.map(([sku, price]) => env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO cogs (base_sku,unit_price,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(base_sku) DO UPDATE SET unit_price=?,updated_at=?' ).bind(sku, price, ts, price, ts) )); updated += chunk.length; } await env.DB.prepare( `UPDATE master_list SET price=(SELECT unit_price FROM cogs WHERE UPPER(cogs.base_sku)=UPPER(master_list.base_sku)) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM cogs WHERE UPPER(cogs.base_sku)=UPPER(master_list.base_sku))` ).run(); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, updated, message:`Updated ${updated} base SKU prices from Master_List col N.` }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // REFERENCE TABLE IMPORTS — products, upc, base_sku_master // POST /inventory/import-products — imports Products sheet into D1 // POST /inventory/import-upc — imports UPC sheet into D1 // POST /inventory/rebuild-sku-master — rebuilds base_sku_master from master_list + products // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // POST /inventory/import-products async function importProducts(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(()=>({})); const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; // Read Products sheet — A=product_id B=sku C=name D=warehouse_name E=weight const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Products!A1:E2000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); const rows = (data.values||[]).slice(1).filter(r => String(r[1]||'').trim()); if (dryRun) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, dryRun:true, rowCount:rows.length, sample: rows.slice(0,3).map(r=>({ sku:r[1], name:r[2], warehouse_name:r[3] })) }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); // Create table if not exists await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS products ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, product_id TEXT, sku TEXT NOT NULL, base_sku TEXT NOT NULL, name TEXT, warehouse_name TEXT, weight REAL DEFAULT 0, updated_at TEXT )`).run(); await env.DB.prepare('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_sku ON products(sku)').run(); await env.DB.prepare('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_base_sku ON products(base_sku)').run(); // Clear and reinsert await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM products').run(); let inserted = 0; const CHUNK = 50; for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += CHUNK) { const chunk = rows.slice(i, i + CHUNK); const stmts = chunk.map(r => { const sku = String(r[1]||'').trim(); const baseSku = sku.split('=')[0].trim().toUpperCase(); return env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO products (product_id,sku,base_sku,name,warehouse_name,weight,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)' ).bind(String(r[0]||'').trim(), sku, baseSku, String(r[2]||'').trim(), String(r[3]||'').trim(), parseFloat(r[4])||0, new Date().toISOString()); }); await env.DB.batch(stmts); inserted += chunk.length; } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, inserted, source:'Products sheet' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/import-upc async function importUPC(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(()=>({})); const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; // Read UPC sheet — A=variant_id B=variant(sku) C=inside_upc D=outside_upc const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'UPC!A1:D5000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); const rows = (data.values||[]).slice(1).filter(r => String(r[1]||'').trim()); if (dryRun) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, dryRun:true, rowCount:rows.length, sample: rows.slice(0,3).map(r=>({ sku:r[1], inside_upc:r[2], outside_upc:r[3] })) }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); // Create table if not exists await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS upc ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, variant_id TEXT, sku TEXT NOT NULL, base_sku TEXT NOT NULL, inside_upc TEXT, outside_upc TEXT, updated_at TEXT )`).run(); await env.DB.prepare('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_upc_sku ON upc(sku)').run(); await env.DB.prepare('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_upc_outside ON upc(outside_upc)').run(); await env.DB.prepare('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_upc_inside ON upc(inside_upc)').run(); await env.DB.prepare('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_upc_base_sku ON upc(base_sku)').run(); // Clear and reinsert await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM upc').run(); let inserted = 0; const CHUNK = 50; for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += CHUNK) { const chunk = rows.slice(i, i + CHUNK); const stmts = chunk.map(r => { const sku = String(r[1]||'').trim(); const baseSku = sku.split('=')[0].trim().toUpperCase(); return env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO upc (variant_id,sku,base_sku,inside_upc,outside_upc,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)' ).bind(String(r[0]||'').trim(), sku, baseSku, String(r[2]||'').trim(), String(r[3]||'').trim(), new Date().toISOString()); }); await env.DB.batch(stmts); inserted += chunk.length; } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, inserted, source:'UPC sheet' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/rebuild-sku-master // Builds base_sku_master from D1 master_list + products // One row per base_sku — total cases, name, out_of_stock flag // POST /inventory/sync-new-product — Body: {items: [{baseSku, warehouseName}], dryRun} // Populates master_list, products, upc, and cogs from product_catalog // for a genuinely new product, then rebuilds base_sku_master from the // result. Confirmed field mappings from the user directly: // - part_num = the sku itself (base_sku=pack_qty is the existing // naming convention, not a separate manufacturer part number) // - cases/vendor/location start blank until stock is physically // received/placed // - upc.inside_upc = product_catalog.upc (case/carton UPC); // outside_upc left blank, not yet tracked per-product // - cogs.landed_cogs = unit_price * 1.5 (50% markup, confirmed) // - warehouse_name is a manually-provided shipping-label abbreviation // (M/F/X/L/Press/etc.) — NOT auto-generated here, since the full // abbreviation rule set isn't something this worker has complete, // reliable visibility into, and getting it wrong risks real // packing/shipping mistakes on physical labels. async function inventorySyncNewProduct(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const items = Array.isArray(body.items) ? body.items : []; const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; if (!items.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'items array required — [{baseSku, warehouseName}]' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const preview = { master_list: [], products: [], upc: [], cogs: [] }; const skipped = []; for (const item of items) { const baseSku = (item.baseSku || '').toUpperCase(); const warehouseName = item.warehouseName || ''; if (!baseSku) { skipped.push({ baseSku: item.baseSku, reason: 'baseSku required' }); continue; } if (!warehouseName) { skipped.push({ baseSku, reason: 'warehouseName required — the shipping-label abbreviation, provided manually' }); continue; } const rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT * FROM product_catalog WHERE base_sku=? ORDER BY pack_qty ASC', [baseSku]); if (!rows.length) { skipped.push({ baseSku, reason: 'Not found in product_catalog' }); continue; } for (const row of rows) { preview.master_list.push({ sku: row.sku, base_sku: row.base_sku, name: row.title || row.title_short || '', part_num: row.sku, location: '', cases: 0, units_per_case: row.pack_qty || 1, vendor: '', price: row.starting_price || 0 }); preview.products.push({ sku: row.sku, base_sku: row.base_sku, name: (row.title || row.title_short || '').toLowerCase(), warehouse_name: warehouseName, weight: row.weight_lbs || 0 }); if (row.upc) preview.upc.push({ sku: row.sku, base_sku: row.base_sku, inside_upc: row.upc, outside_upc: '' }); } const firstRow = rows[0]; if (firstRow.cost_per_unit) { preview.cogs.push({ base_sku: baseSku, unit_price: firstRow.cost_per_unit, landed_cogs: Math.round(firstRow.cost_per_unit * 1.5 * 100) / 100 }); } } if (!preview.master_list.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Nothing to sync', skipped }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); if (dryRun) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, skipped, preview }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const ts = new Date().toISOString(); for (const r of preview.master_list) { await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO master_list (sku, base_sku, name, part_num, location, cases, units_per_case, vendor, price, sheet_row, updated_at, prev_notes) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,0,?,'')` ).bind(r.sku, r.base_sku, r.name, r.part_num, r.location, r.cases, r.units_per_case, r.vendor, r.price, ts).run(); } for (const r of preview.products) { await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO products (sku, base_sku, name, warehouse_name, weight, updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind(r.sku, r.base_sku, r.name, r.warehouse_name, r.weight, ts).run(); } for (const r of preview.upc) { await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO upc (sku, base_sku, inside_upc, outside_upc, updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind(r.sku, r.base_sku, r.inside_upc, r.outside_upc, ts).run(); } for (const r of preview.cogs) { await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO cogs (base_sku, unit_price, landed_cogs, updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?)` ).bind(r.base_sku, r.unit_price, r.landed_cogs, ts).run(); } // Reuses the existing aggregation function rather than duplicating // its logic — base_sku_master is derived data, not something // inserted into directly. await rebuildSkuMaster(env); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, skipped, inserted: { master_list: preview.master_list.length, products: preview.products.length, upc: preview.upc.length, cogs: preview.cogs.length }, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function rebuildSkuMaster(env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { // Create table if not exists await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS base_sku_master ( base_sku TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT, warehouse_name TEXT, total_cases REAL DEFAULT 0, variant_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0, out_of_stock INTEGER DEFAULT 0, updated_at TEXT )`).run(); // Aggregate from master_list const agg = await d1All(env, `SELECT base_sku, SUM(CASE WHEN cases > 0 THEN cases ELSE 0 END) as total_cases, COUNT(*) as variant_count FROM master_list WHERE base_sku != '' AND part_num != '' GROUP BY base_sku` ); // Load products for names let productMap = {}; try { const prods = await d1All(env, 'SELECT sku, name, warehouse_name FROM products'); prods.forEach(p => { productMap[p.sku.toUpperCase()] = p; }); } catch(e) { /* products table may not exist yet */ } // Clear and rebuild await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM base_sku_master').run(); const CHUNK = 50; let inserted = 0; const ts = new Date().toISOString(); for (let i = 0; i < agg.length; i += CHUNK) { const chunk = agg.slice(i, i + CHUNK); const stmts = chunk.map(r => { const prod = productMap[r.base_sku.toUpperCase()] || {}; const outOfStock = (r.total_cases || 0) <= 0 ? 1 : 0; return env.DB.prepare( `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO base_sku_master (base_sku,name,warehouse_name,total_cases,variant_count,out_of_stock,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)` ).bind(r.base_sku, prod.name||'', prod.warehouse_name||'', r.total_cases||0, r.variant_count||0, outOfStock, ts); }); await env.DB.batch(stmts); inserted += chunk.length; } const outOfStockCount = agg.filter(r => (r.total_cases||0) <= 0).length; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, total:inserted, outOfStock: outOfStockCount, inStock: inserted - outOfStockCount, message: `Built base_sku_master with ${inserted} base SKUs (${outOfStockCount} out of stock)` }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/stock-levels // Returns stock levels from D1 base_sku_master + master_list // Replaces ?sheet=Master_List for the Stock Levels tab in warehouse lookup // GET /inventory/out-of-stock // Live SQL translation of the Out_of_Stock sheet formula — SUM(cases) GROUP BY sku, // computed fresh from master_list on every request. No stored table, no staleness. async function inventoryOutOfStock(env) { if (!env.DB) { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Out_of_Stock!A1:D5000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values: data.values||[], source:'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } try { const rows = await d1All(env, `SELECT sku, MAX(name) as name, SUM(cases) as cases FROM master_list WHERE sku != '' GROUP BY sku` ); const withStatus = rows.map(r => { const cases = r.cases || 0; const status = cases === 0 ? 'Out of Stock' : cases < 5 ? 'Low Stock' : 'In Stock'; const sortOrder = cases === 0 ? 1 : cases < 5 ? 2 : 3; return { sku: r.sku, name: r.name || '', cases, status, sortOrder }; }).sort((a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder || a.sku.localeCompare(b.sku)); const values = [['SKU', 'Name', 'Cases', 'Status']]; withStatus.forEach(r => values.push([r.sku, r.name, r.cases, r.status])); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values, source:'d1', count:withStatus.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] inventoryOutOfStock:', e.message); const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Out_of_Stock!A1:D5000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values: data.values||[], source:'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /inventory/awd-lookup // Live SQL translation of the AWD_Master sheet formula. Unions UPC + master_list // part numbers (so items with a UPC but zero current stock still appear), sums // cases per part number, joins products for warehouse name/weight. // Location/units-per-case use an arbitrary single match per part (MAX) — the // frontend already cross-references live master_list separately (mlByPN) to // build the full multi-location list, so a single "primary" value here is // sufficient and matches existing app behavior. async function inventoryAwdLookup(env) { if (!env.DB) { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'AWD_Master!A1:H5000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values: data.values||[], source:'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } try { const rows = await d1All(env, `WITH all_parts AS ( SELECT DISTINCT sku as part_num FROM upc WHERE sku != '' UNION SELECT DISTINCT part_num FROM master_list WHERE part_num != '' ) SELECT ap.part_num, COALESCE(SUM(m.cases), 0) as cases, CASE WHEN instr(ap.part_num,'=') > 0 THEN substr(ap.part_num, 1, instr(ap.part_num,'=')-1) ELSE ap.part_num END as base_sku, CASE WHEN instr(ap.part_num,'=') > 0 THEN substr(ap.part_num, instr(ap.part_num,'=')+1) ELSE '' END as suffix, MAX(p.warehouse_name) as warehouse_name, MAX(p.weight) as weight, MAX(m.units_per_case) as units_per_case, MAX(m.location) as location, MAX(u.inside_upc) as upc FROM all_parts ap LEFT JOIN master_list m ON m.part_num = ap.part_num LEFT JOIN products p ON p.base_sku = ( CASE WHEN instr(ap.part_num,'=') > 0 THEN substr(ap.part_num, 1, instr(ap.part_num,'=')-1) ELSE ap.part_num END) LEFT JOIN upc u ON u.sku = ap.part_num GROUP BY ap.part_num` ); const values = [['Cases', 'Label', 'UPC', 'Location', 'Weight', 'UnitsPerCase', 'PartNum', 'SKU']]; rows.forEach(r => { const label = r.suffix + '=' + (r.warehouse_name || ''); values.push([ r.cases, label, r.upc || '', r.location || '', r.weight || '', r.units_per_case || 0, r.part_num, r.base_sku ]); }); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values, source:'d1', count:rows.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] inventoryAwdLookup:', e.message); const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'AWD_Master!A1:H5000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values: data.values||[], source:'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function inventoryStockLevels(env) { if (!env.DB) { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values: data.values||[], source:'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } try { // Drive from products (737 clean base SKUs) LEFT JOIN master_list for variants const rows = await d1All(env, `SELECT m.sheet_row, m.sku, m.base_sku, m.part_num, m.location, m.cases, m.units_per_case, m.vendor, m.price, m.prev_notes, COALESCE(p.name, m.name, '') as name, COALESCE(p.warehouse_name, '') as warehouse_name FROM products p LEFT JOIN master_list m ON UPPER(m.base_sku) = UPPER(p.sku) WHERE p.sku != '' ORDER BY p.sku, m.part_num` ); const values = [['','','','SKU','Name','','Part#','Location','Cases','Each Case Qty','Notes','','Vendor','Price','Total']]; rows.forEach(r => { values.push([ '','','', r.sku || r.base_sku || '', r.name || '', '', r.part_num || '', r.location || '', r.cases ?? 0, r.units_per_case || 0, r.prev_notes || '', (r.cases||0) * (r.units_per_case||0), // col L = totalQty (cases × units/case) r.vendor || '', r.price || 0, (r.cases||0) * (r.price||0) ]); }); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values, source:'d1', count:rows.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] inventoryStockLevels:', e.message); const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:O15000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ values: data.values||[], source:'sheets' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // PRICE TIERS — single D1 source of truth for volume-discount markup tiers. // // Previously this same data existed in THREE separate places that could // silently drift apart: // 1. ebayListings()'s hardcoded TIERS array (used for live eBay repricing) // 2. The repricer app's Price Calculator tab — its own separate hardcoded // MULTS/TIER_BREAKS JS constants, with DIFFERENT numbers than #1 // (Amazon 80%/eBay 77% here vs whatever ebayListings actually used) // 3. A landed-COGS spreadsheet built separately, with yet another copy // This is exactly the kind of drift that already happened — the calculator // tab's numbers had gone stale relative to an actual pricing policy update // before this fix. One D1 table now backs all three; edit it in one place. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const PRICE_CALC_DEFAULTS = { shipping: 5.25, landed_multiplier: 1.50, floor_multiplier: 1.40, ceiling_multiplier: 2.00 }; async function ensurePriceTiersTables(env) { await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS price_tiers ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, marketplace TEXT NOT NULL, label TEXT NOT NULL, min_qty INTEGER NOT NULL, max_qty INTEGER NOT NULL, multiplier REAL NOT NULL, sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT )`).run(); await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS price_calc_config ( key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value REAL NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT )`).run(); const count = await d1First(env, 'SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM price_tiers'); if (count && count.cnt > 0) return; // already seeded // Seed: confirmed 2026-07-20 — Amazon 1pc starts at 83%, eBay/Walmart at // 79%, Shopify at 75%. Every marketplace steps down by the same // 7.5 / 2.5 / 5 / 5 / 5 percentage-point sequence at higher quantities — // only the starting point differs per marketplace. const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const seedRows = [ // marketplace, label, min, max, multiplier, sort_order ['amazon', 'Amazon', 1, 1, 1.830, 0], ['amazon', 'Amazon', 2, 4, 1.755, 1], ['amazon', 'Amazon', 5, 9, 1.730, 2], ['amazon', 'Amazon', 10, 24, 1.680, 3], ['amazon', 'Amazon', 25, 49, 1.630, 4], ['amazon', 'Amazon', 50, 9999, 1.580, 5], ['ebay', 'eBay', 1, 1, 1.790, 0], ['ebay', 'eBay', 2, 4, 1.715, 1], ['ebay', 'eBay', 5, 9, 1.690, 2], ['ebay', 'eBay', 10, 24, 1.640, 3], ['ebay', 'eBay', 25, 49, 1.590, 4], ['ebay', 'eBay', 50, 9999, 1.540, 5], ['walmart', 'Walmart', 1, 1, 1.790, 0], ['walmart', 'Walmart', 2, 4, 1.715, 1], ['walmart', 'Walmart', 5, 9, 1.690, 2], ['walmart', 'Walmart', 10, 24, 1.640, 3], ['walmart', 'Walmart', 25, 49, 1.590, 4], ['walmart', 'Walmart', 50, 9999, 1.540, 5], ['shopify', 'Shopify / XFITTING', 1, 1, 1.750, 0], ['shopify', 'Shopify / XFITTING', 2, 4, 1.675, 1], ['shopify', 'Shopify / XFITTING', 5, 9, 1.650, 2], ['shopify', 'Shopify / XFITTING', 10, 24, 1.600, 3], ['shopify', 'Shopify / XFITTING', 25, 49, 1.550, 4], ['shopify', 'Shopify / XFITTING', 50, 9999, 1.500, 5], ]; const stmts = seedRows.map(r => env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO price_tiers (marketplace,label,min_qty,max_qty,multiplier,sort_order,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)' ).bind(r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3], r[4], r[5], ts)); await env.DB.batch(stmts); const cfgStmts = Object.entries(PRICE_CALC_DEFAULTS).map(([k, v]) => env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO price_calc_config (key,value,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?)').bind(k, v, ts) ); await env.DB.batch(cfgStmts); } // GET /repricer/price-tiers — full tier table + config, for the Edit Tiers UI async function repricerPriceTiersGet(env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { await ensurePriceTiersTables(env); const tiers = await d1All(env, 'SELECT id, marketplace, label, min_qty, max_qty, multiplier, sort_order FROM price_tiers ORDER BY marketplace, sort_order'); const config = await d1All(env, 'SELECT key, value FROM price_calc_config'); const configMap = {}; config.forEach(r => { configMap[r.key] = r.value; }); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, tiers, config: configMap }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /repricer/price-tiers — full replace, for the Edit Tiers UI // Body: { tiers: [{marketplace,label,min_qty,max_qty,multiplier,sort_order}, ...], config: {shipping, landed_multiplier, floor_multiplier, ceiling_multiplier} } async function repricerPriceTiersSave(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const tiers = Array.isArray(body.tiers) ? body.tiers : null; const config = body.config && typeof body.config === 'object' ? body.config : null; if (!tiers || !tiers.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'tiers array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); await ensurePriceTiersTables(env); const ts = new Date().toISOString(); await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM price_tiers').run(); const stmts = tiers.map(t => env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO price_tiers (marketplace,label,min_qty,max_qty,multiplier,sort_order,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)' ).bind( String(t.marketplace||'').toLowerCase(), String(t.label||''), parseInt(t.min_qty)||0, parseInt(t.max_qty)||0, parseFloat(t.multiplier)||0, parseInt(t.sort_order)||0, ts )); await env.DB.batch(stmts); if (config) { const cfgStmts = Object.entries(config).map(([k, v]) => env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO price_calc_config (key,value,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value, updated_at=excluded.updated_at') .bind(k, parseFloat(v)||0, ts) ); if (cfgStmts.length) await env.DB.batch(cfgStmts); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, tiersUpdated: tiers.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /repricer/suggest-price?sku=X&qty=Y — the live lookup screen's endpoint. // Replaces: the app's client-side COGS-sheet parsing + hardcoded MULTS/ // TIER_BREAKS arithmetic. All of that duplicated logic now lives here once, // reading the same price_tiers table that the Edit Tiers UI edits and that // ebayListings() (once updated to match) will also read from. async function repricerSuggestPrice(url, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { await ensurePriceTiersTables(env); const sku = (url.searchParams.get('sku') || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const qty = parseInt(url.searchParams.get('qty')) || 0; if (!sku) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'sku required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); if (qty < 1) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'qty must be at least 1' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const cogsRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT unit_price FROM cogs WHERE UPPER(base_sku)=?', [sku]); if (!cogsRow || !cogsRow.unit_price) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: sku + ' not found in COGS table', notFound: true }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const unitCogs = parseFloat(cogsRow.unit_price) || 0; const configRows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT key, value FROM price_calc_config'); const cfg = { ...PRICE_CALC_DEFAULTS }; configRows.forEach(r => { cfg[r.key] = r.value; }); const landedCogs1pc = +(unitCogs * cfg.landed_multiplier).toFixed(4); const landedCogsTotal = +(landedCogs1pc * qty).toFixed(4); const allTiers = await d1All(env, 'SELECT marketplace, label, min_qty, max_qty, multiplier FROM price_tiers ORDER BY marketplace, sort_order'); const byMarket = {}; allTiers.forEach(t => { (byMarket[t.marketplace] = byMarket[t.marketplace] || []).push(t); }); const floor = +(landedCogsTotal * cfg.floor_multiplier + cfg.shipping).toFixed(2); const ceiling = +(landedCogsTotal * cfg.ceiling_multiplier + cfg.shipping).toFixed(2); let tierLabel = null; const results = Object.entries(byMarket).map(([mkt, tiers]) => { const matched = tiers.find(t => qty >= t.min_qty && qty <= t.max_qty) || tiers[tiers.length - 1]; if (matched && !tierLabel) { tierLabel = (matched.max_qty >= 9999 ? matched.min_qty + '+' : matched.min_qty + '-' + matched.max_qty) + ' pc'; } const mult = matched ? matched.multiplier : 1; const suggested = Math.max(+(landedCogs1pc * mult * qty + cfg.shipping).toFixed(2), floor); return { marketplace: mkt, label: matched ? matched.label : mkt, perUnit: +(suggested / qty).toFixed(2), floor, suggested, max: ceiling, }; }); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, sku, qty, unitCogs, landedCogs1pc, landedCogsTotal, tier: tierLabel, results }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function repricerSkuMaster(url, env) { const sku = url.searchParams.get('sku'); const data = await rSheetGet(env, 'SKU_MASTER!A3:S5000'); let rows = (data.values || []).filter(r => r[0]); if (sku) rows = rows.filter(r => r[0] === sku); const parsed = rows.map(r => ({ sku: r[0], cog1pc: parseFloat(r[1]) || 0, cog2pc: parseFloat(r[2]) || 0, cog4pc: parseFloat(r[3]) || 0, cog5pc: parseFloat(r[4]) || 0, cog10pc: parseFloat(r[5]) || 0, cog25pc: parseFloat(r[6]) || 0, cog50pc: parseFloat(r[7]) || 0, cog100pc: parseFloat(r[8]) || 0, price1pc: parseFloat(r[13]) || 0, price2_4: parseFloat(r[14]) || 0, price5_9: parseFloat(r[15]) || 0, price10_24: parseFloat(r[16]) || 0, price25_49: parseFloat(r[17]) || 0, price50plus: parseFloat(r[18]) || 0, })); if (sku) { if (!parsed.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'SKU not found' }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify(parsed[0]), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ skus: parsed, total: parsed.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // GET /inventory/locations — all locations with inventory status async function inventoryLocations(url, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const prefix = (url.searchParams.get('prefix') || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const status = (url.searchParams.get('status') || 'all').toLowerCase(); let sql = 'SELECT l.location, l.prefix, l.notes, COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN m.cases > 0 THEN m.cases ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_cases, COUNT(CASE WHEN m.cases > 0 THEN 1 END) as variant_count FROM locations l LEFT JOIN master_list m ON UPPER(m.location) = UPPER(l.location) WHERE l.active = 1'; const params = []; if (prefix) { sql += ' AND UPPER(l.prefix) = ?'; params.push(prefix); } sql += ' GROUP BY l.location ORDER BY l.prefix, l.location'; const rows = await d1All(env, sql, params); let filtered = rows; if (status === 'empty') filtered = rows.filter(r => !r.total_cases || r.total_cases === 0); if (status === 'inuse') filtered = rows.filter(r => r.total_cases > 0); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, count:filtered.length, total:rows.length, locations:filtered }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/import-locations // One-time (or occasional) bulk import: reads the LocationID Sheets tab — // a comprehensive physical registry of every labeled shelf position, // whether it's ever held stock or not — and registers ALL of them into // D1's locations table. // // This exists because D1's locations table is otherwise only populated // by "register on first use" (see inventoryVerify/transfer-verify/register // above) — a genuinely empty shelf that has NEVER been used is invisible // to D1 until this import runs, which meant suggest-location could only // ever recommend locations that had been touched before, never truly // fresh/never-used empty shelves. Safe to re-run any time — INSERT OR // IGNORE means it only adds what's missing, never overwrites/duplicates. async function importLocationIDs(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(()=>({})); const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'LocationID!A2:A5000').catch(()=>({ values:[] })); const rows = (data.values||[]).map(r => String(r[0]||'').trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean); if (dryRun) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, dryRun:true, rowCount:rows.length, sample: rows.slice(0,5) }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const ts = new Date().toISOString(); let inserted = 0; const CHUNK = 50; for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += CHUNK) { const chunk = rows.slice(i, i + CHUNK); const stmts = chunk.map(loc => { const prefix = loc.includes('=') ? loc.split('=')[0].trim() : ''; return env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO locations (location,prefix,active,notes,created_at) VALUES (?,?,1,?,?)' ).bind(loc, prefix, 'imported from LocationID sheet', ts); }); await env.DB.batch(stmts); inserted += chunk.length; } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, processed: rows.length, message: `Registered ${rows.length} physical locations from LocationID sheet (existing ones skipped via INSERT OR IGNORE)` }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/import-platform-skus // Imports the 4 per-marketplace SKU reference sheets (EbaySKU, AmazonSKU, // WalmartSKU, ShopifySKU — the same tabs repricerSkuLookup already reads // live from Sheets on every search) into one combined D1 table. Same // benefit as every other import here: instant local lookups instead of a // live Sheets API round-trip per search, and this data becomes queryable // for other features (e.g. matching a listing's SKU to a base_sku). // // All 4 sheets share the same 3-column shape (id, title, sku), just with // different header names and column positions per platform: // EbaySKU: Item number | Title | Custom label (SKU) — id=A, sku=C // AmazonSKU: ASIN | Title | SKU — id=A, sku=C // WalmartSKU: Item Number | Title | SKU — id=A, sku=C // ShopifySKU: Product ID | Title | SKU — id=A (often // blank — many Shopify rows have no Product ID filled in), // sku=C // Safe to re-run any time to refresh — clears and reloads per platform, // not additive, so no duplicate buildup from repeated imports. async function importPlatformSkus(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; const token = await getToken(env); const ranges = ['EbaySKU!A2:C5000', 'AmazonSKU!A2:C5000', 'WalmartSKU!A2:C5000', 'ShopifySKU!A2:C5000']; const batchUrl = SHEETS_URL + '/' + env.SHEET_ID + '/values:batchGet?' + ranges.map(r => 'ranges=' + encodeURIComponent(r)).join('&'); const batchResp = await fetch(batchUrl, { headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } }); const batchData = await batchResp.json(); const vrs = batchData.valueRanges || []; const platforms = [ { key: 'ebay', rows: (vrs[0] && vrs[0].values) || [] }, { key: 'amazon', rows: (vrs[1] && vrs[1].values) || [] }, { key: 'walmart', rows: (vrs[2] && vrs[2].values) || [] }, { key: 'shopify', rows: (vrs[3] && vrs[3].values) || [] }, ]; if (dryRun) { const counts = {}; platforms.forEach(p => { counts[p.key] = p.rows.length; }); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, counts, sample: platforms.map(p => ({ platform: p.key, sample: p.rows.slice(0, 2) })) }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS platform_skus ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, platform TEXT NOT NULL, listing_id TEXT, title TEXT, sku TEXT NOT NULL, base_sku TEXT, updated_at TEXT )`).run(); const values = [ sku, // sku (primary key) String(r[0] || '').trim().toUpperCase(),// base_sku parseInt(r[2]) || null, // pack_qty String(r[3] || '').trim(), // manufacturer String(r[4] || '').trim(), // model_name String(r[5] || '').trim(), // fitting_type String(r[6] || '').trim(), // ebay_category_id String(r[7] || '').trim(), // material String(r[8] || '').trim(), // connection_type_1 String(r[9] || '').trim(), // connection_type_2 String(r[10] || '').trim(), // size_1 String(r[11] || '').trim(), // size_2 String(r[12] || '').trim(), // title String(r[13] || '').trim(), // title_short String(r[14] || '').trim(), // item_highlights String(r[15] || '').trim(), // description String(r[16] || '').trim(), // bullet_1 String(r[17] || '').trim(), // bullet_2 String(r[18] || '').trim(), // bullet_3 String(r[19] || '').trim(), // bullet_4 String(r[20] || '').trim(), // bullet_5 String(r[21] || '').trim(), // keywords parseFloat(r[22]) || null, // weight_lbs parseFloat(r[23]) || null, // length_in parseFloat(r[24]) || null, // width_in parseFloat(r[25]) || null, // height_in String(r[26] || '').trim(), // upc String(r[27] || '').trim(), // image_url_1 String(r[28] || '').trim(), // image_url_2 String(r[29] || '').trim(), // image_url_3 String(r[30] || '').trim(), // image_url_4 String(r[31] || '').trim(), // image_url_5 parseFloat(r[32]) || null, // cost_per_unit parseFloat(r[33]) || null, // msrp parseFloat(r[34]) || null, // starting_price String(r[35] || '').trim(), // status String(r[36] || '').trim(), // notes String(r[37] || '').trim(), // walmart_product_type (new column, appended — sheet column AL) ts, ]; } // Restore any preserved Shopify/eBay tracking for skus that still // exist after the refresh — this is the actual fix for the wipe bug above. const restoreSkus = Object.keys(trackingBySku).filter(sku => validRows.some(r => String(r[1]||'').trim().toUpperCase() === sku)); WHERE sku=?` ).bind( trackingBySku[sku].productId, trackingBySku[sku].pushedAt, trackingBySku[sku].ebayOfferId, trackingBySku[sku].ebayListingId, trackingBySku[sku].ebayStagedAt, trackingBySku[sku].ebayPublishedAt, trackingBySku[sku].ebayVariationGroupKey, trackingBySku[sku].ebayVariationListingId, trackingBySku[sku].ebayVariationStagedAt, trackingBySku[sku].ebayVariationPublishedAt, sku )); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /inventory/locations/register — register new location async function inventoryLocationsRegister(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { const body = await request.json().catch(()=>({})); const location = (body.location || '').trim().toUpperCase(); if (!location) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Location required' }), { status:400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }));
