} } // ── /repricer/research ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Headers row 2, data from row 3. // A=Date, B=ItemNum, C=SKU, D=OurPack, E=OurPrice, F=OurUnit, // G=CompPack, H=CompPrice, I=CompUnit, J=Rank, K=Trend, // L=30d, M=90d, N=6mo, O=1yr, P=2yr, Q=3yr, R=CompLink, S=Notes, T=Initials async function repricerResearch(url, env) { const sku = url.searchParams.get('sku'); const itemId = url.searchParams.get('itemId'); const data = await rSheetGet(env, 'MARKET_RESEARCH!A3:T1000'); let rows = (data.values || []).filter(r => r[1]); if (sku) rows = rows.filter(r => r[2] === sku); if (itemId) rows = rows.filter(r => String(r[1]).includes(itemId)); const parsed = rows.map(r => ({ date: r[0], itemId: r[1], sku: r[2], ourPack: r[3], ourPrice: r[4], ourUnit: parseFloat(r[5]) || 0, compPack: r[6], compPrice: r[7], compUnit: parseFloat(r[8]) || 0, rank: r[9], trend: r[10], s30: parseInt(r[11]) || 0, s90: parseInt(r[12]) || 0, s6m: parseInt(r[13]) || 0, s1y: parseInt(r[14]) || 0, s2y: parseInt(r[15]) || 0, s3y: parseInt(r[16]) || 0, compLink: r[17], notes: r[18], initials: r[19], })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ research: parsed, total: parsed.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── /repricer/decisions ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Headers row 2, data from row 3. // A=SKU, B=ItemNum, C=Pack, D=MinPrice, E=MaxPrice, F=Target, // G=Current, H=LowestComp, I=SuggestNew, J=%Change, // K=ApprovalRequired, L=Reason, M=FinalApplied, N=Notes, O=Date, P=CheckAlert async function repricerDecisions(url, env) { const sku = url.searchParams.get('sku'); const itemId = url.searchParams.get('itemId'); const data = await rSheetGet(env, 'PRICE_DECISION!A3:P2000'); let rows = (data.values || []).filter(r => r[0]); if (sku) rows = rows.filter(r => r[0] === sku); if (itemId) rows = rows.filter(r => String(r[1]).includes(itemId)); const parsed = rows.map((r, i) => ({ rowIndex: i + 3, sku: r[0], itemId: r[1], pack: parseInt(r[2]) || 1, minPrice: parseFloat(r[3]) || 0, maxPrice: parseFloat(r[4]) || 0, target: parseFloat(r[5]) || 0, current: r[6] || '—', lowestComp: r[7] || '—', suggest: parseFloat(r[8]) || 0, pctChange: parseFloat(r[9]) || 0, approval: r[10] || '', reason: r[11] || '', finalApplied: parseFloat(r[12]) || 0, notes: r[13] || '', date: r[14] || '', checkAlert: r[15] || '', })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ decisions: parsed, total: parsed.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── /repricer/velocity ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Pulls 3 years of eBay order history directly from Fulfillment API. // Fetches in 90-day chunks (12 chunks = 3 years). // If itemId provided: finds its base SKU, then aggregates ALL listings sharing that base SKU. // Returns per-item stats AND base SKU rollup stats + month-over-month rates. async function repricerVelocity(url, env) { const itemId = (url.searchParams.get('itemId') || '').trim(); const skuParam = (url.searchParams.get('sku') || '').trim(); if (!itemId && !skuParam) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'itemId or sku required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Resolve base SKU from param or eBay_Listings cache let targetBase = skuParam.includes('=') ? skuParam.split('=')[0].trim() : skuParam || null; if (!targetBase && itemId) { try { const cache = await rSheetGet(env, 'eBay_Listings!A2:B2000'); const row = (cache.values || []).find(r => String(r[0]).trim() === itemId); if (row?.[1]) { const s = row[1]; targetBase = s.includes('=') ? s.split('=')[0].trim() : s.trim(); } } catch(_) {} } // Fallback: look up targetBase from sales sheets directly (col A=ItemId, col B=BaseSKU) if (!targetBase && itemId) { try { const cache = await rSheetGet(env, 'eBay_Sales_30d!A2:B2000'); const row = (cache.values || []).find(r => String(r[0]).trim() === itemId); if (row?.[1]) targetBase = String(row[1]).trim(); } catch(_) {} } // Read all 5 sales sheets in parallel (5 subrequests total — safe on free tier) const windows = [ { key: 's30', tab: 'eBay_Sales_30d' }, { key: 's90', tab: 'eBay_Sales_90d' }, { key: 's6m', tab: 'eBay_Sales_6mo' }, { key: 's1y', tab: 'eBay_Sales_1yr' }, { key: 's2y', tab: 'eBay_Sales_2yr' }, ]; const [r30, r90, r6m, r1y, r2y] = await Promise.all( windows.map(w => rSheetGet(env, `${w.tab}!A2:G3000`).catch(() => ({ values: [] }))) ); const sheetData = { s30: r30, s90: r90, s6m: r6m, s1y: r1y, s2y: r2y }; // For each window: find units for this itemId AND this baseSku function lookupWindow(sheetResult, lookItemId, lookBase) { const rows = sheetResult?.values || []; let itemUnits = 0, skuUnits = 0; for (const row of rows) { const rowItem = String(row[0] || '').trim(); const rowBase = String(row[1] || '').trim(); // Col D=ListingUnitsSold — what we display in app const units = parseInt(row[3]) || 0; if (lookItemId && rowItem === lookItemId) itemUnits += units; if (lookBase && rowBase === lookBase) skuUnits += units; } return { itemUnits, skuUnits }; } const results = {}; for (const w of windows) { results[w.key] = lookupWindow(sheetData[w.key], itemId, targetBase); } // Build item-level and sku-level stats const itemCounts = { s30: results.s30.itemUnits, s90: results.s90.itemUnits, s6m: results.s6m.itemUnits, s1y: results.s1y.itemUnits, s2y: results.s2y.itemUnits }; const skuCounts = { s30: results.s30.skuUnits, s90: results.s90.skuUnits, s6m: results.s6m.skuUnits, s1y: results.s1y.skuUnits, s2y: results.s2y.skuUnits }; function enrich(counts) { const avg6m = counts.s6m / 6; return { ...counts, m30: +( counts.s30 ).toFixed(1), m90: +( counts.s90 / 3 ).toFixed(1), m6m: +( counts.s6m / 6 ).toFixed(1), m1y: +( counts.s1y / 12 ).toFixed(1), m2y: +( counts.s2y / 24 ).toFixed(1), trend: counts.s30 > avg6m * 1.1 ? 'Increasing' : counts.s30 < avg6m * 0.9 ? 'Declining' : 'Stable', }; } const hasItemData = Object.values(itemCounts).some(v => v > 0); const hasSkuData = Object.values(skuCounts).some(v => v > 0); // Collect related listings from all windows for relatedItems table const relatedMap = {}; for (const w of windows) { for (const row of (sheetData[w.key]?.values || [])) { const rowItem = String(row[0] || '').trim(); const rowBase = String(row[1] || '').trim(); const rowTitle = String(row[2] || ''); const units = parseInt(row[3]) || 0; // col D = ListingUnitsSold if (targetBase && rowBase === targetBase && rowItem) { if (!relatedMap[rowItem]) relatedMap[rowItem] = { sku: row[1] || '', title: rowTitle, s30:0,s90:0,s6m:0,s1y:0,s2y:0 }; relatedMap[rowItem][w.key] += units; } } } const relatedItems = Object.entries(relatedMap) .map(([id, v]) => ({ itemId: id, sku: v.sku, title: v.title, ...enrich(v) })) .sort((a, b) => b.s90 - a.s90); const debug = { source: 'Google Sheets (cron-populated)', itemId, targetBase, hasItemData, hasSkuData, rowCounts: { s30: (sheetData.s30?.values||[]).length, s90: (sheetData.s90?.values||[]).length, s6m: (sheetData.s6m?.values||[]).length, s1y: (sheetData.s1y?.values||[]).length, }, hint: (!hasItemData && !hasSkuData) ? 'Sheets are empty — run /cron/sales-30d manually first, or wait for 6am cron' : null, }; if (!hasItemData && !hasSkuData) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'No sales data yet — sheets are empty', ...debug, }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ item: hasItemData ? { itemId, ...enrich(itemCounts) } : null, sku: hasSkuData ? { baseSku: targetBase, relatedItems, ...enrich(skuCounts) } : null, debug, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } async function repricerSalesHistory(url, env) { const sku = url.searchParams.get('sku'); const itemId = url.searchParams.get('itemId'); // Read from whichever sales tabs exist — missing tabs return empty gracefully const tabs = ['eBay_Sales_30d','eBay_Sales_90d','eBay_Sales_6mo','eBay_Sales_1yr','eBay_Sales_2yr']; const keys = ['s30','s90','s6m','s1y','s2y']; const results = await Promise.all( tabs.map(t => rSheetGet(env, `${t}!A2:F3000`).catch(() => ({ values: [] }))) ); // Merge: itemId → { baseSku, title, s30, s90, s6m, s1y, s2y, updated } // Col layout: A=ItemId B=BaseSKU C=Title D=PiecesSold E=Orders F=Date const map = {}; results.forEach((res, i) => { const key = keys[i]; for (const row of res.values || []) { const id = String(row[0] || '').trim(); const base = String(row[1] || '').trim(); const title = String(row[2] || ''); const pieces = parseInt(row[3]) || 0; const orders = parseInt(row[4]) || 0; const date = row[5] || ''; if (!id) continue; if (!map[id]) map[id] = { itemId: id, sku: row[1]||'', baseSku: base, title, s30:0, s90:0, s6m:0, s1y:0, s2y:0, updated: date }; map[id][key] = pieces; if (date) map[id].updated = date; } }); let rows = Object.values(map); // Filter if (itemId) rows = rows.filter(r => r.itemId === String(itemId).trim()); if (sku) rows = rows.filter(r => r.baseSku === sku.trim() || r.sku.startsWith(sku.trim())); // Sort by 30d desc rows.sort((a, b) => b.s30 - a.s30); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ rows, total: rows.length, tabsPopulated: results.map((r, i) => ({ tab: tabs[i], rows: (r.values||[]).length })), }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── /repricer/build-sales-history (POST) ───────────────────────────────────── // Fetches 1 year of Fulfillment API orders, aggregates per item ID, // writes full table to eBay_Sales_History sheet. // Each row: ItemId | SKU | Title | 30d | 90d | 6mo | 1yr | UpdatedAt async function repricerBuildSalesHistory(env) { const now = Date.now(); const oneYearMs = 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; const chunkMs = 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; const chunks = []; for (let start = now - oneYearMs; start < now; start += chunkMs) { chunks.push({ from: start, to: Math.min(start + chunkMs, now) }); } // Fetch all chunks (4 at a time) let allOrders = []; for (let i = 0; i < chunks.length; i += 4) { const batch = chunks.slice(i, i + 4); const results = await Promise.allSettled(batch.map(async chunk => { const fromStr = new Date(chunk.from).toISOString(); const toStr = new Date(chunk.to).toISOString(); const filterVal = `creationdate:[${fromStr}..${toStr}]`; let orders = [], offset = 0; while (true) { const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); const r = await fetch( `https://api.ebay.com/sell/fulfillment/v1/order?filter=${encodeURIComponent(filterVal)}&limit=50&offset=${offset}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${ebayToken}` } } ); const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); const batch = data.orders || []; orders = orders.concat(batch); if (batch.length < 50 || orders.length >= 5000) break; offset += 50; } return orders; })); for (const r of results) { if (r.status === 'fulfilled') allOrders = allOrders.concat(r.value); } } // Aggregate per item ID const periods = [ { key: 's30', ms: 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 }, { key: 's90', ms: 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 }, { key: 's6m', ms: 180 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 }, { key: 's1y', ms: 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 }, ]; const stats = {}; // itemId -> { sku, title, s30, s90, s6m, s1y } for (const order of allOrders) { const ts = new Date(order.creationDate).getTime(); const age = now - ts; for (const line of order.lineItems || []) { const id = line.legacyItemId || ''; const sku = line.sku || ''; const ttl = line.title || ''; const qty = parseInt(line.quantity) || 1; if (!id) continue; if (!stats[id]) stats[id] = { sku, title: ttl, s30: 0, s90: 0, s6m: 0, s1y: 0 }; for (const p of periods) { if (age <= p.ms) stats[id][p.key] += qty; } } } const updated = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); const rows = Object.entries(stats) .sort((a, b) => b[1].s30 - a[1].s30) // sort by 30d desc .map(([id, v]) => [id, v.sku, v.title.slice(0, 80), v.s30, v.s90, v.s6m, v.s1y, updated]); // Write header row (always, so tab is self-documenting) const headers = [['ItemId','SKU','Title','30d_Units','90d_Units','6mo_Units','1yr_Units','Updated']]; await rSheetUpdate(env, 'eBay_Sales_History!A1:H1', headers); if (rows.length) { // Clear a wide range first so stale rows from a previous (larger) build don't linger const clearRange = `eBay_Sales_History!A2:H3000`; try { const token = await getToken(env); await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${repricerSheetId(env)}/values/${encodeURIComponent(clearRange)}:clear`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } } ); } catch(_) {} await rSheetUpdate(env, `eBay_Sales_History!A2:H${rows.length + 1}`, rows); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, itemsWritten: rows.length, ordersScanned: allOrders.length, updated, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── Sales cron helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Each window is ONE API call (max 50 orders/page, 1-2 pages for most accounts). // On free tier this stays well under the 50 subrequest ceiling. // // Sheet layout (all 4 tabs same structure): // A=ItemId B=BaseSKU C=Title D=UnitsSold E=Orders F=Date // // Cron schedule (set all 4 in Cloudflare Dashboard → Workers → Triggers): // 0 11 * * * daily-sales / 30d (runs every day) // 0 11 * * 2 sales-90d (Tuesdays) // 0 11 * * 3 sales-6mo (Wednesdays) // 0 11 * * 4 sales-1yr (Thursdays) const SALES_WINDOWS = { '30d': { days: 30, tab: 'eBay_Sales_30d' }, '90d': { days: 90, tab: 'eBay_Sales_90d' }, '6mo': { days: 180, tab: 'eBay_Sales_6mo' }, '1yr': { days: 365, tab: 'eBay_Sales_1yr' }, '2yr': { days: 730, tab: 'eBay_Sales_2yr' }, // eBay max history is ~2 years }; // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SALES COMPUTE TABS — POST /sales/compute-tabs?step=ebay|amz|xf|write // Split into 4 separate worker calls — each reads 2-3 tabs within 30s budget. // Browser accumulates results and sends merged data on the write step. // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function salesComputeTabs(request, env) { const url = new URL(request.url); const step = url.searchParams.get('step') || 'ebay'; const token = await getToken(env); const salesSheetId = env.SALES_SHEET_ID || env.SHEET_ID; async function readTab(tab, fromRow) { const r = await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(tab + '!A' + (fromRow||1) + ':BZ50000')}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } } ); const d = await r.json(); return d.values || []; } function findIdx(headers, keys) { for (const k of keys) { const i = headers.findIndex(h => (h||'').toLowerCase().includes(k)); if (i >= 0) return i; } return -1; } function fnum(v) { return parseFloat((v||'').toString().replace(/[$,]/g,'')) || 0; } try { // ── STEP: upc — read UPC tab to get master canonical part# list ────────────── // Also reads Master_List to flag out-of-stock parts. // Returns { upcParts: Set as array, oosParts: Set as array } if (step === 'upc') { const warehouseSheetId = env.SHEET_ID; async function readWarehouseTab(tab) { const r = await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${warehouseSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(tab + '!A1:Z50000')}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } } ); const d = await r.json(); return d.values || []; } const upcRaw = await readWarehouseTab('UPC'); const mlRaw = await readWarehouseTab('Master_List'); // UPC tab: col B (idx 1) = variant part# (e.g. 27-1-1=10XX) const upcParts = new Set(); for (const row of upcRaw.slice(1)) { const pn = (row[1] || '').trim(); if (pn) upcParts.add(pn.toUpperCase()); } // Master_List: col G (idx 6) = part#, col I (idx 8) = cases // Flag parts where cases = 0 or blank across all locations const mlCases = {}; // partNum.upper → total cases const SKIP = new Set(['push fit','name','location','case','each case quantity','date arrive','total quantity','brand','uint price','total','type','sku','']); for (const row of mlRaw.slice(1)) { const pn = (row[6] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const cases = parseFloat(row[8]) || 0; if (!pn || SKIP.has(pn.toLowerCase())) continue; mlCases[pn] = (mlCases[pn] || 0) + cases; } const oosParts = new Set( [...upcParts].filter(pn => (mlCases[pn] || 0) === 0) ); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, step: 'upc', upcParts: [...upcParts], oosParts: [...oosParts], totalParts: upcParts.size, oosCount: oosParts.size, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── STEP: ebay — read EbaySKU + SKU_Map + Ebay30 + Ebay90 ───────────────── if (step === 'ebay') { const ebaySkuRaw = await readTab('EbaySKU', 1); const skuMapRaw = await readTab('SKU_Map', 1).catch(() => []); const eb30 = await readTab('Ebay30', 11); const eb90 = await readTab('Ebay90', 11); // Build itemId → SKU lookup from EbaySKU tab // Headers: col A=Item number, col B=Title, col C=Custom label (SKU) const esh = ebaySkuRaw[0] || []; const iItemNum = findIdx(esh, ['item number','item_number']); const iSkuCol = findIdx(esh, ['custom label','sku']); const itemToSku = {}; for (const row of ebaySkuRaw.slice(1)) { const id = (row[iItemNum >= 0 ? iItemNum : 0] || '').trim(); const sku = (row[iSkuCol >= 0 ? iSkuCol : 2] || '').trim(); if (id && sku) itemToSku[id] = sku; } // Build SKU_Map: dirty eBay SKU → canonical part# that has a UPC const skuMap = {}; for (const row of skuMapRaw.slice(1)) { const ch = (row[0]||'').trim().toUpperCase(); const mp = (row[1]||'').trim(); if (ch && mp) skuMap[ch] = mp; } // Resolve eBay SKU → canonical part# // Only returns part#s that have a UPC (checked at write step against upcParts) function resolveEbaySku(rawSku) { if (!rawSku) return null; const upper = rawSku.toUpperCase(); // 1. Exact SKU_Map match if (skuMap[upper]) return skuMap[upper]; // 2. Strip trailing _/- and try SKU_Map again const stripped = rawSku.replace(/[_\-]+$/, '').toUpperCase(); if (skuMap[stripped]) return skuMap[stripped]; // 3. Use stripped SKU as-is — will be filtered at write step if not in UPC return rawSku.replace(/[_\-]+$/, ''); } // Aggregate eBay sales by resolved part# const acc = {}; function processEbay(rows, is30) { const h = rows[0] || []; const iItem = findIdx(h, ['item number','itemid']); const iQty = findIdx(h, ['quantity','qty']); const iSku = findIdx(h, ['sku','custom label']); const iPrice = findIdx(h, ['sold for','price','amount']); for (const row of rows.slice(1)) { const itemId = (row[iItem >= 0 ? iItem : 1] || '').trim(); const qty = fnum(row[iQty >= 0 ? iQty : 2]); const price = fnum(row[iPrice >= 0 ? iPrice: 4]); const rawSku = itemId && itemToSku[itemId] ? itemToSku[itemId] : (row[iSku >= 0 ? iSku : 3] || '').trim(); const pn = resolveEbaySku(rawSku); if (!pn || qty <= 0) continue; if (!acc[pn]) acc[pn] = {ebayU30:0,ebayU90:0,ebayS30:0,ebayS90:0}; if (is30) { acc[pn].ebayU30 += qty; acc[pn].ebayS30 += price; } else { acc[pn].ebayU90 += qty; acc[pn].ebayS90 += price; } } } processEbay(eb30, true); processEbay(eb90, false); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, step:'ebay', acc, rows30:eb30.length-1, rows90:eb90.length-1 }), { headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } // ── STEP: amz — read Amz30 + Amz90 ──────────────────────────────────────── // Amazon SKU is col D (idx 3) — has trailing '-' and spaces e.g. '28-3-7=1-', '4-4-1= 1' // Units Ordered = col AE (idx 30), Ordered Product Sales = col AI (idx 34) if (step === 'amz') { const amz30 = await readTab('Amz30', 1); const amz90 = await readTab('Amz90', 1); const acc = {}; function processAmz(rows, is30) { const h = rows[0] || []; const iPn = findIdx(h, ['sku']); const iQty = findIdx(h, ['units ordered']); const iPrice = findIdx(h, ['ordered product sales']); for (const row of rows.slice(1)) { const rawPn = (row[iPn >= 0 ? iPn : 3] || '').toString().trim(); // Strip trailing - _ and spaces (Amazon appends trailing - to all SKUs) const pn = rawPn.replace(/[-_\s]+$/, '').trim(); const qty = fnum(row[iQty >= 0 ? iQty : 30]); const price = fnum(row[iPrice >= 0 ? iPrice : 34]); if (!pn || qty <= 0) continue; if (!acc[pn]) acc[pn] = {amzU30:0,amzU90:0,amzS30:0,amzS90:0}; if (is30) { acc[pn].amzU30 += qty; acc[pn].amzS30 += price; } else { acc[pn].amzU90 += qty; acc[pn].amzS90 += price; } } } processAmz(amz30, true); processAmz(amz90, false); // Debug: count non-zero entries const nonZero = Object.values(acc).filter(v => v.amzU30 > 0 || v.amzU90 > 0).length; const sample = Object.entries(acc).filter(([,v]) => v.amzU30 > 0).slice(0,3).map(([k,v]) => k+'='+v.amzU30+'u'); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, step:'amz', acc, rows30:amz30.length-1, rows90:amz90.length-1, nonZero, sample }), { headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } // ── STEP: xf — read XF30 + XF90 ─────────────────────────────────────────── // Shopify order-level export — each line item is a separate row. // Col Q (idx 16) = Lineitem quantity, Col S (idx 18) = Lineitem price // Col U (idx 20) = Lineitem sku, Col BH (idx 59) = Lineitem discount // Revenue per row = (price × qty) - discount — sum all rows by SKU if (step === 'xf') { const xf30 = await readTab('XF30', 1); const xf90 = await readTab('XF90', 1); const acc = {}; function processXF(rows, is30) { const h = rows[0] || []; const iPn = findIdx(h, ['lineitem sku']); const iQty = findIdx(h, ['lineitem quantity']); const iPrice = findIdx(h, ['lineitem price']); const iDiscount = findIdx(h, ['lineitem discount']); for (const row of rows.slice(1)) { const pn = (row[iPn >= 0 ? iPn : 20] || '').toString().trim(); const qty = fnum(row[iQty >= 0 ? iQty : 16]); const price = fnum(row[iPrice >= 0 ? iPrice : 18]); const disc = fnum(row[iDiscount >= 0 ? iDiscount : 59]); // Revenue = (unit price × qty) - line discount const revenue = Math.max(0, (price * qty) - disc); if (!pn || qty <= 0) continue; if (!acc[pn]) acc[pn] = {xfU30:0,xfU90:0,xfS30:0,xfS90:0}; if (is30) { acc[pn].xfU30 += qty; acc[pn].xfS30 += revenue; } else { acc[pn].xfU90 += qty; acc[pn].xfS90 += revenue; } } } processXF(xf30, true); processXF(xf90, false); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, step:'xf', acc, rows30:xf30.length-1, rows90:xf90.length-1 }), { headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } // ── STEP: vendors — receive merged acc, update JQ_Vendor + EFF_Vendor ───── // JQ cols: A=X Part #, B=UPC CODE, C=30 Units Total, D=30 QTY total, E=90 Units Total, F=90 QTY Total // EFF cols: A=Part Number, B=INSIDE PLASTIC UPC CODE, C=30 Units Total, D=30 QTY total, E=90 Units Total, F=90 QTY Total // Units = packs sold (listing units), QTY = individual pieces (units × pack size from SKU suffix) if (step === 'vendors') { const body = await request.json(); const acc = body.acc || {}; // merged accumulator from browser // Build lookup: partNum.upper → { u30total, u90total } summing all platforms const salesByPn = {}; for (const [pn, v] of Object.entries(acc)) { const totalU30 = (v.amzU30||0) + (v.ebayU30||0) + (v.xfU30||0); const totalU90 = (v.amzU90||0) + (v.ebayU90||0) + (v.xfU90||0); salesByPn[pn.toUpperCase()] = { u30: totalU30, u90: totalU90 }; } function packSize(partNum) { // Extract pack size from SKU suffix e.g. '27-1-1=10XX' → 10, '27-1-1=1' → 1 const m = (partNum || '').match(/=([0-9]+)/); return m ? (parseInt(m[1]) || 1) : 1; } async function updateVendorTab(tabName, partCol) { const rows = await readTab(tabName, 1); if (!rows.length) return 0; const headers = rows[0]; // Find column indices by exact header name const iPartNum = headers.findIndex(h => (h||'').toString().trim() === partCol); const iU30 = headers.findIndex(h => (h||'').toString().trim().toLowerCase().includes('30 units total')); const iQ30 = headers.findIndex(h => (h||'').toString().trim().toLowerCase().includes('30 qty') || (h||'').toString().trim().toLowerCase().includes('30 qty')); const iU90 = headers.findIndex(h => (h||'').toString().trim().toLowerCase().includes('90 units total')); const iQ90 = headers.findIndex(h => (h||'').toString().trim().toLowerCase().includes('90 qty') || (h||'').toString().trim().toLowerCase().includes('90 qty')); // Build updated rows: preserve all existing data, update only the 4 sales cols const updated = [headers]; let changed = 0; for (const row of rows.slice(1)) { const pn = (row[iPartNum >= 0 ? iPartNum : 0] || '').toString().trim().toUpperCase(); if (!pn) { updated.push(row); continue; } const s = salesByPn[pn] || { u30: 0, u90: 0 }; const ps = packSize(pn); const newRow = [...row]; // Pad row if needed while (newRow.length <= Math.max(iU30, iQ30, iU90, iQ90, 5)) newRow.push(''); newRow[iU30 >= 0 ? iU30 : 2] = s.u30; newRow[iQ30 >= 0 ? iQ30 : 3] = s.u30 * ps; newRow[iU90 >= 0 ? iU90 : 4] = s.u90; newRow[iQ90 >= 0 ? iQ90 : 5] = s.u90 * ps; updated.push(newRow); if (s.u30 > 0 || s.u90 > 0) changed++; } // Write back — clear and rewrite entire tab await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(tabName+'!A1:F50000')}:clear`, { method:'POST', headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`} } ); await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(tabName+'!A1')}?valueInputOption=RAW`, { method:'PUT', headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({values:updated}) } ); return changed; } const jqChanged = await updateVendorTab('JQ_Vendor', 'X Part #'); const effChanged = await updateVendorTab('EFF_Vendor', 'Part Number'); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, step: 'vendors', jqUpdated: jqChanged, effUpdated: effChanged, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── STEP: write — receive merged acc + upcParts from browser, write tabs ────── if (step === 'write') { const body = await request.json(); const acc = body.acc || {}; // upcParts = canonical part#s from UPC tab (uppercase) — used as master list // oosParts = subset of upcParts with 0 warehouse stock const upcParts = new Set((body.upcParts || []).map(p => p.toUpperCase())); const oosParts = new Set((body.oosParts || []).map(p => p.toUpperCase())); // Build master part# list: // 1. All UPC parts (even with 0 sales — shows OOS parts in planner) // 2. Any sales-data parts that are in UPC (already covered by #1) // 3. Skip sales-data parts NOT in UPC (unmapped eBay SKUs etc.) const allParts = new Set([...upcParts]); // Also include any acc parts that ARE in UPC (handles case sensitivity) for (const pn of Object.keys(acc)) { if (upcParts.has(pn.toUpperCase())) allParts.add(pn.toUpperCase()); } // Build a lookup: pn.upper → acc entry (from sales data) const accUpper = {}; for (const [pn, v] of Object.entries(acc)) { accUpper[pn.toUpperCase()] = v; } const partHeaders = [ 'Part#', 'AMZ 30 units sold','AMZ 90 units sold','AMZ daily units sold','AMZ 30 sales','AMZ 90 sales', 'eBay 30 units sold','eBay 90 units sold','eBay daily units sold','eBay 30 sales','eBay 90 sales', 'XF 30 units sold','XF 90 units sold','XF daily units sold','XF 30 sales','XF 90 sales', 'OOS', ]; const partRows = [partHeaders]; // Helper: extract pack size from part# suffix e.g. '27-1-1=10XX' -> 10, '27-1-1=1' -> 1 function getPackSize(pn) { const m = (pn || '').match(/=([0-9]+)/); return m ? (parseInt(m[1]) || 1) : 1; } const skuAcc = {}; // baseSku -> { pieces30, pieces90, s30, s90 } for (const pnUpper of [...allParts].sort()) { const v = accUpper[pnUpper] || {}; const ps = getPackSize(pnUpper); const amzDaily = parseFloat(((v.amzU30||0)+(v.amzU90||0))/90).toFixed(4)*1; const ebayDaily = parseFloat(((v.ebayU30||0)+(v.ebayU90||0))/90).toFixed(4)*1; const xfDaily = parseFloat(((v.xfU30||0)+(v.xfU90||0))/90).toFixed(4)*1; const oos = oosParts.has(pnUpper) ? 'YES' : ''; partRows.push([ pnUpper, v.amzU30||0, v.amzU90||0, amzDaily, v.amzS30||0, v.amzS90||0, v.ebayU30||0, v.ebayU90||0, ebayDaily, v.ebayS30||0, v.ebayS90||0, v.xfU30||0, v.xfU90||0, xfDaily, v.xfS30||0, v.xfS90||0, oos, ]); // SKU_Sales: accumulate INDIVIDUAL PIECES (packs × pack size) const bsku = pnUpper.split('=')[0].trim(); if (!skuAcc[bsku]) skuAcc[bsku] = {pieces30:0, pieces90:0, s30:0, s90:0}; const totalU30 = (v.amzU30||0)+(v.ebayU30||0)+(v.xfU30||0); const totalU90 = (v.amzU90||0)+(v.ebayU90||0)+(v.xfU90||0); skuAcc[bsku].pieces30 += totalU30 * ps; skuAcc[bsku].pieces90 += totalU90 * ps; skuAcc[bsku].s30 += (v.amzS30||0)+(v.ebayS30||0)+(v.xfS30||0); skuAcc[bsku].s90 += (v.amzS90||0)+(v.ebayS90||0)+(v.xfS90||0); } const skuHeaders = ['BASE SKU','30 units sold','90 units sold','30 sales','90 sales','daily units sold']; const skuRows = [skuHeaders]; for (const [bsku, v] of Object.entries(skuAcc)) { // daily = average individual pieces per day over 90d window const daily = parseFloat(((v.pieces30 + v.pieces90) / 90).toFixed(4)); skuRows.push([bsku, v.pieces30, v.pieces90, v.s30.toFixed(2), v.s90.toFixed(2), daily]); } // Write both tabs async function writeTab(tabName, rows) { await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(tabName+'!A1:Z50000')}:clear`, { method:'POST', headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`} } ); await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(tabName+'!A1')}?valueInputOption=RAW`, { method:'PUT', headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({values:rows}) } ); } await writeTab('Part#_Sales', partRows); await writeTab('SKU_Sales', skuRows); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, step:'write', parts:partRows.length-1, skus:skuRows.length-1, date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10) }), { headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false, error:'Unknown step: '+step}), {status:400, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}})); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false, step, error:e.message}), {status:500, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}})); } } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SALES SKU MAPPING — /sales/unmapped-skus, /sales/sku-map GET/POST // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Normalize a SKU for matching — strips trailing underscores, dashes, spaces // Does NOT strip X/XX/W1C etc — those are meaningful variant suffixes function normalizeSkuForMatch(sku) { return (sku || '').trim().replace(/[_\-\s]+$/, '').toUpperCase(); } // ── GET /sales/unmapped-skus ────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Reads Ebay30+Ebay90 col D (SKU) and compares against UPC tab col B (variant part#) // Returns: { unmapped: [{sku, units, suggestion}], mapped: N, total: N } async function salesUnmappedSkus(env) { try { const token = await getToken(env); const salesSheetId = env.SALES_SHEET_ID || env.SHEET_ID; const warehouseSheetId = env.SHEET_ID; // Read Ebay30 col B (itemId), C (qty), D (sku) from row 12 down // Read Ebay90 same cols // Read UPC tab col B (variant/partNum) // Read SKU_Map tab if it exists (Channel SKU → Master Part#) const [eb30res, eb90res, upcRes, mapRes] = await Promise.all([ fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent('Ebay30!B12:D')}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }).then(r => r.json()), fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent('Ebay90!B12:D')}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }).then(r => r.json()), fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${warehouseSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent('UPC!B:B')}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }).then(r => r.json()), fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent('SKU_Map!A:B')}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({ values: [] })), ]); // Build set of known canonical part numbers from UPC tab (col B = variant) const knownParts = new Set(); const knownNormalized = new Map(); // normalizedKey → original partNum for (const row of (upcRes.values || []).slice(1)) { const pn = (row[0] || '').trim(); if (!pn) continue; knownParts.add(pn.toUpperCase()); knownNormalized.set(normalizeSkuForMatch(pn), pn); } // Build existing SKU_Map: channelSku.toUpperCase() → masterPart const existingMap = {}; for (const row of (mapRes.values || []).slice(1)) { const ch = (row[0] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const mp = (row[1] || '').trim(); if (ch && mp) existingMap[ch] = mp; } // Aggregate units per SKU across both windows const skuUnits = {}; // normalizedSku → { raw, units } for (const row of [...(eb30res.values || []), ...(eb90res.values || [])]) { // row: [itemId, qty, sku] (cols B,C,D starting col B = index 0) const rawSku = (row[2] || '').trim(); if (!rawSku) continue; const qty = parseInt(row[1]) || 0; const norm = normalizeSkuForMatch(rawSku); if (!skuUnits[norm]) skuUnits[norm] = { raw: rawSku, units: 0 }; skuUnits[norm].units += qty; } // Classify each SKU as mapped, matched, or unmapped const unmapped = []; let mappedCount = 0, matchedCount = 0; for (const [norm, { raw, units }] of Object.entries(skuUnits)) { const upper = raw.toUpperCase(); // 1. Already in SKU_Map — fully mapped if (existingMap[upper]) { mappedCount++; continue; } // 2. Exact match in UPC after normalization if (knownParts.has(upper) || knownNormalized.has(norm)) { matchedCount++; continue; } // 3. No match — find best suggestion // Strategy: strip everything after = and find UPC parts with same base prefix const basePrefix = norm.split('=')[0]; // e.g. "5-5-1" from "5-5-1=1" const packNum = (norm.split('=')[1] || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''); // digits after = const candidates = [...knownNormalized.entries()] .filter(([k]) => k.startsWith(basePrefix + '=')) .map(([k, original]) => { // Score: exact pack number match scores highest const kPack = (k.split('=')[1] || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''); const score = kPack === packNum ? 2 : 1; return { original, score }; }) .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score); const suggestion = candidates.length ? candidates[0].original : null; unmapped.push({ sku: raw, normalizedSku: norm, units, suggestion }); } // Sort unmapped by units desc so highest-volume misses are at top unmapped.sort((a, b) => b.units - a.units); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, unmapped, mappedCount, matchedCount, totalSkus: Object.keys(skuUnits).length, knownPartsCount: knownParts.size, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── GET /sales/sku-map — read existing SKU_Map tab ──────────────────────────── async function salesSkuMapGet(env) { try { const token = await getToken(env); const salesSheetId = env.SALES_SHEET_ID || env.SHEET_ID; const res = await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent('SKU_Map!A:B')}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } } ); const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, values: data.values || [] }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── POST /sales/sku-map — save SKU mapping rows to SKU_Map tab ──────────────── // Body: { rows: [["channelSku","masterPart#"], ...] } // Clears and rewrites the entire tab so duplicates don't accumulate async function salesSkuMapSave(request, env) { try { const { rows } = await request.json(); if (!rows || !rows.length) throw new Error('No rows provided'); const token = await getToken(env); const salesSheetId = env.SALES_SHEET_ID || env.SHEET_ID; // Ensure SKU_Map tab exists const spreadsheet = await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}?fields=sheets.properties.title`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } } ).then(r => r.json()); const exists = (spreadsheet.sheets || []).some(s => s.properties?.title === 'SKU_Map'); if (!exists) { await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests: [{ addSheet: { properties: { title: 'SKU_Map' } } }] }) }); } // Clear and rewrite await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent('SKU_Map!A:B')}:clear`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } } ); const writeRows = [['Channel SKU', 'Master Part#'], ...rows]; await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent('SKU_Map!A1')}?valueInputOption=RAW`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: writeRows }) } ); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, saved: rows.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── /ebay/sales-fetch — Manual trigger from Reorder Planner / Sales Dashboard ── // Fetches 30d then 90d SEQUENTIALLY to stay under 1000 subrequest limit. // Parallel would double the subrequest count and hit the 524 timeout. async function ebayFetchSalesManual(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const windows = (body.window === 'both' || !body.window) ? ['30d', '90d'] : [body.window]; // SEQUENTIAL — not parallel. Each window uses ~20-55 subrequests; parallel would exceed 1000 limit. const out = { ok: true, windows: {} }; let totalOrders = 0, totalSkus = 0; for (const w of windows) { const r = await ebaySalesWindowToSalesSheet(env, w); out.windows[w] = r; totalOrders += r.ordersProcessed || 0; totalSkus += r.itemsWritten || 0; if (r.error && !r.ordersProcessed) out.ok = false; } out.orders = totalOrders; out.skus = totalSkus; if (!out.ok) out.error = Object.values(out.windows).find(r => r.error)?.error || 'Fetch error'; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify(out), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── Fetch eBay orders for a window and write to Ebay30 or Ebay90 tab in SALES_SHEET ── // This writes the same CSV-compatible format the frontend expects for Upload + Process flow. // Cols: sales record number, item number, sold for, sku, quantity (+ extras) async function ebaySalesWindowToSalesSheet(env, window = '30d') { const tabName = window === '90d' ? 'Ebay90' : 'Ebay30'; const now = Date.now(); // 90d fetches only days 31-90 (the older slice) — days 1-30 are already in Ebay30. // This cuts the 90d fetch from ~8,000 orders to ~5,300 and avoids hitting the page cap. const fromMs = window === '90d' ? now - 90 * 86400000 : now - 30 * 86400000; const toMs = window === '90d' ? now - 30 * 86400000 : now; const from = new Date(fromMs).toISOString(); const to = new Date(toMs).toISOString(); const filterVal = `creationdate:[${from}..${to}]`; // Page caps per window — each call gets its own 1000-subrequest budget. // 30d: 15 pages × 200 = 3,000 orders max // 90d (60-day slice): 30 pages × 200 = 6,000 orders max const PAGE_CAP = window === '90d' ? 30 : 15; const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); let orders = [], offset = 0, page = 0, hitCap = false, lastError = null; while (page < PAGE_CAP) { const r = await fetch( `https://api.ebay.com/sell/fulfillment/v1/order?filter=${encodeURIComponent(filterVal)}&limit=200&offset=${offset}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${ebayToken}` } } ); if (!r.ok) { lastError = `eBay API ${r.status} on page ${page + 1}`; break; } const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); if (data.errors?.length) { lastError = data.errors[0]?.message || 'eBay error'; break; } const batch = data.orders || []; orders = orders.concat(batch); page++; if (batch.length < 200) break; offset += 200; if (orders.length >= 9400) { hitCap = true; break; } } if (lastError && !orders.length) { return { error: lastError, ordersProcessed: 0, itemsWritten: 0, window, tab: tabName }; } // Build rows in format matching salesParseCSV expectations for Ebay30/Ebay90: // Headers: sales record number | item number | sold for | sku | quantity | buyer | date const CANCELLED = new Set(['CANCEL_COMPLETE']); const rowMap = {}; // lineItemId → row for (const order of orders) { const cancelState = (order.cancelStatus?.cancelState || '').toUpperCase(); if (CANCELLED.has(cancelState)) continue; const orderDate = (order.creationDate || '').slice(0, 10); const orderId = order.orderId || ''; for (const line of (order.lineItems || [])) { const itemId = String(line.legacyItemId || line.lineItemId || '').trim(); const sku = (line.sku || '').trim(); const qty = parseInt(line.quantity) || 1; const price = parseFloat(line.lineItemCost?.value || 0) * qty; const buyer = order.buyer?.username || ''; const key = orderId + '_' + (line.lineItemId || itemId); rowMap[key] = [ orderId, // A: sales record number itemId, // B: item number ← formula matches on this qty, // C: quantity ← H2 (eBay 30 units sold) pulls Ebay30!C sku, // D: sku price.toFixed(2), // E: sold for ← K2 (eBay 30 sales) pulls Ebay30!E buyer, // F: buyer username orderDate // G: date ]; } } // Col layout matches Part#_Sales formula references: // B12:B = item number (matched against EbaySKU) // C12:C = quantity (units sold → H/I cols) // E12:E = sold for (revenue → K/L cols) const headerRow = ['sales record number', 'item number', 'quantity', 'sku', 'sold for', 'buyer', 'date']; const dataRows = Object.values(rowMap); // Write to SALES_SHEET Ebay30 or Ebay90 tab const salesSheetId = env.SALES_SHEET_ID || env.SHEET_ID; try { const token = await getToken(env); // Clear from row 11 down only — preserve any metadata in rows 1-10 await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(tabName + '!A11:Z50000')}:clear`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } } ); // Write header on row 11, data from row 12 — matches formula refs B12:B, C12:C, E12:E const writeValues = [headerRow, ...dataRows]; await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${salesSheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(tabName + '!A11')}?valueInputOption=RAW`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: writeValues }) }); } catch (e) { return { error: 'Sheet write failed: ' + e.message, ordersProcessed: orders.length, itemsWritten: 0, window, tab: tabName }; } return { ok: true, window, tab: tabName, ordersProcessed: orders.length, itemsWritten: dataRows.length, pages: page, hitCap, error: lastError || null }; } // ── Chunked sales sync: app sends explicit from/to dates, worker fetches just that slice ── // Stays well under 50 subrequest limit by fetching max ~15 pages (3000 orders) per call async function handleCronChunk(request, env) { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { from, to, window: win } = body; if (!from || !to) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing from/to dates' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const result = await cronSalesSlice(env, from, to, win || 'chunk'); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify(result), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } async function cronSalesSlice(env, from, to, windowLabel) { const filterVal = `creationdate:[${from}..${to}]`; const PAGE_CAP = 15; // 15 pages × 200 orders = 3000 max per chunk, ~17 subrequests total const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); let orders = [], offset = 0, page = 0, hitCap = false, lastError = null; while (page < PAGE_CAP) { const r = await fetch( `https://api.ebay.com/sell/fulfillment/v1/order?filter=${encodeURIComponent(filterVal)}&limit=200&offset=${offset}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${ebayToken}` } } ); if (!r.ok) { lastError = `eBay API ${r.status} on page ${page+1}`; break; } const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); if (data.errors?.length) { lastError = data.errors[0]?.message || 'eBay error'; break; } const batch = data.orders || []; orders = orders.concat(batch); page++; if (batch.length < 200) break; offset += 200; if (orders.length >= 2800) { hitCap = true; break; } } if (lastError && !orders.length) return { error: lastError, fromDate: from, toDate: to }; // Aggregate SKU sales from orders const skuMap = {}; const CANCELLED = new Set(['CANCELLED','CHECKOUT_ERROR','FAILED']); const statusCounts = {}; for (const order of orders) { const status = order.orderFulfillmentStatus || order.orderPaymentStatus || 'UNKNOWN'; statusCounts[status] = (statusCounts[status]||0)+1; if (CANCELLED.has(order.cancelStatus?.cancelState) || CANCELLED.has(status)) continue; for (const line of (order.lineItems||[])) { const sku = (line.sku||line.lineItemId||'UNKNOWN').trim(); const qty = parseInt(line.quantity)||1; if (!skuMap[sku]) skuMap[sku] = { itemId: line.legacyItemId||'', baseSku: sku, title: line.title||'', units:0, pieces:0, orders:0 }; skuMap[sku].units += qty; skuMap[sku].pieces += qty; skuMap[sku].orders += 1; } } // Read existing data for this window and merge const tabName = `eBay_Sales_${windowLabel.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'')}`; // Read existing data (7-col format matching cronSalesWindow) // Cols: ItemId, BaseSKU, Title, ListingUnitsSold, PiecesSold, Orders, Date let existing = {}; try { const ex = await rSheetGet(env, `${encodeURIComponent(tabName)}!A2:G2000`); for (const row of (ex.values||[])) { const id = (row[0]||'').trim(); if (id) existing[id] = { itemId: id, baseSku: row[1]||'', title: row[2]||'', units: parseInt(row[3])||0, pieces: parseInt(row[4])||0, orders: parseInt(row[5])||0 }; } } catch(_) {} // Merge new chunk data into existing const stamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); for (const [sku, d] of Object.entries(skuMap)) { const key = d.itemId || sku; if (!existing[key]) existing[key] = { itemId: d.itemId, baseSku: d.baseSku, title: d.title, units:0, pieces:0, orders:0 }; existing[key].units += d.units; existing[key].pieces += d.pieces; existing[key].orders += d.orders; if (d.title) existing[key].title = d.title; if (d.baseSku) existing[key].baseSku = d.baseSku; } // Write headers + sorted rows — same 7-col format as cronSalesWindow const headers = [['ItemId','BaseSKU','Title','ListingUnitsSold','PiecesSold','Orders','Date']]; await rSheetUpdate(env, `${tabName}!A1:G1`, headers); const rows = Object.values(existing) .sort((a,b) => b.units - a.units) .map(d => [d.itemId, d.baseSku, d.title, d.units, d.pieces, d.orders, stamp]); if (rows.length) { try { const token = await getToken(env); await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${repricerSheetId(env)}/values/${encodeURIComponent(tabName + '!A2:G5000')}:clear`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } } ); } catch(_) {} await rSheetUpdate(env, `${tabName}!A2:G${rows.length+1}`, rows); } return { fromDate: from, toDate: to, window: windowLabel, ordersProcessed: orders.length, itemsWritten: Object.keys(skuMap).length, pages: page, hitCap, statusCounts, error: lastError||null, date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10) }; } async function handleCronManual(env, window = '30d') { const result = await cronSalesWindow(env, window); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify(result), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } async function cronSalesWindow(env, window = '30d') { const cfg = SALES_WINDOWS[window] || SALES_WINDOWS['30d']; const now = Date.now(); // eBay Fulfillment API hard limit: orders older than ~27 months are not returned. // For 2yr we clamp the from-date to 27 months ago to avoid empty responses. const maxLookbackMs = 27 * 30 * 86400000; // ~27 months const fromMs = Math.max(now - cfg.days * 86400000, now - maxLookbackMs); const from = new Date(fromMs).toISOString(); const to = new Date(now).toISOString(); const filterVal = `creationdate:[${from}..${to}]`; // Per-window page caps to stay under free tier 50 subrequest limit. // Overhead: 1 token + 1 clear + 1 sheet write = 3. Leaves 47 for fetches. // Short windows won't hit cap naturally; only 1yr/2yr need the hard limit. const PAGE_CAPS = { '30d': 5, '90d': 10, '6mo': 20, '1yr': 47, '2yr': 47 }; const pageCap = PAGE_CAPS[window] || 47; const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); let orders = [], offset = 0, page = 0; let hitCap = false; let lastError = null; while (page < pageCap) { const r = await fetch( `https://api.ebay.com/sell/fulfillment/v1/order?filter=${encodeURIComponent(filterVal)}&limit=200&offset=${offset}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${ebayToken}` } } ); if (!r.ok) { lastError = `eBay API ${r.status} on page ${page + 1}`; break; } const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); if (data.errors?.length) { lastError = data.errors[0]?.message || 'eBay API error'; break; } const batch = data.orders || []; orders = orders.concat(batch); page++; if (batch.length < 200) break; // reached last page naturally offset += 200; if (orders.length >= 9400) { hitCap = true; break; } } // Aggregate: itemId → { baseSku, title, units, pieces, orders } // Only count FULFILLED, PAID, IN_PROGRESS orders — skip CANCELLED const SKIP_STATUSES = new Set(['CANCELLED']); const stats = {}; const statusCounts = {}; for (const order of orders) { const orderStatus = (order.orderFulfillmentStatus || order.orderPaymentStatus || '').toUpperCase(); const cancelStatus = (order.cancelStatus?.cancelState || '').toUpperCase(); statusCounts[orderStatus] = (statusCounts[orderStatus] || 0) + 1; // Skip fully cancelled orders if (cancelStatus === 'CANCEL_COMPLETE') continue; for (const line of order.lineItems || []) { const itemId = String(line.legacyItemId || '').trim(); const rawSku = (line.sku || '').trim(); const baseSku = rawSku.includes('=') ? rawSku.split('=')[0].trim() : rawSku; const title = (line.title || '').slice(0, 80); // qty = how many of this listing were ordered in this line item const qty = parseInt(line.quantity) || 1; // packSize = pieces per unit from SKU (e.g. "31-1-1=10" → 10) let packSize = 1; if (rawSku.includes('=')) { const packStr = rawSku.split('=')[1].replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''); packSize = parseInt(packStr) || 1; } // totalPieces = actual individual pieces that shipped const totalPieces = qty * packSize; if (!itemId) continue; if (!stats[itemId]) stats[itemId] = { baseSku, title, units: 0, pieces: 0, orders: 0 }; stats[itemId].units += qty; // listing units sold (orders × qty) stats[itemId].pieces += totalPieces; // actual individual pieces stats[itemId].orders += 1; } } const stamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); // Sheet cols: ItemId | BaseSKU | Title | PiecesSold | Orders | Date const rows = Object.entries(stats) .sort((a, b) => b[1].units - a[1].units) .map(([id, v]) => [id, v.baseSku, v.title, v.units, v.pieces, v.orders, stamp]); // Write headers + data (clear first to remove stale rows) const headers = [['ItemId', 'BaseSKU', 'Title', 'ListingUnitsSold', 'PiecesSold', 'Orders', 'Date']]; await rSheetUpdate(env, `${cfg.tab}!A1:G1`, headers); if (rows.length) { try { const token = await getToken(env); await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${repricerSheetId(env)}/values/${encodeURIComponent(cfg.tab + '!A2:G5000')}:clear`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } } ); } catch(_) {} await rSheetUpdate(env, `${cfg.tab}!A2:G${rows.length + 1}`, rows); } return { success: !lastError, window, tab: cfg.tab, date: stamp, fromDate: from, ordersProcessed: orders.length, itemsWritten: rows.length, pages: page, hitCap, statusCounts, error: lastError || null, note: hitCap ? '⚠️ Hit 9400 order cap (free tier max) — upgrade to Cloudflare paid plan to remove limit' : null, }; } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // eBay FUNCTIONS // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function ebayListings(env) { const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); // Fetch all active listings via Trading API GetMyeBaySelling let allItems = [], page = 1, totalPages = 1; while (page <= totalPages && page <= 50) { const xmlBody = ` ${ebayToken} true 200${page} ReturnAll `; const r = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME': 'GetMyeBaySelling', 'X-EBAY-API-SITEID': '0', 'X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL': '967', 'X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN': ebayToken, 'Content-Type': 'text/xml', }, body: xmlBody }); const xml = await r.text(); // Parse total pages from ActiveList section only const activeMatch = xml.match(/[\s\S]*?<\/ActiveList>/); if (activeMatch && page === 1) { const pgMatch = activeMatch[0].match(/(\d+)<\/TotalNumberOfPages>/); if (pgMatch) totalPages = Math.min(parseInt(pgMatch[1]), 50); } // Parse items const itemMatches = xml.match(/([\s\S]*?)<\/Item>/g) || []; for (const item of itemMatches) { const itemId = (item.match(/([^<]+)<\/ItemID>/) || [])[1] || ''; const title = (item.match(/([^<]+)<\/Title>/) || [])[1] || ''; const sku = (item.match(/<SKU>([^<]+)<\/SKU>/) || [])[1] || ''; const priceM = item.match(/<CurrentPrice[^>]*>([\d.]+)<\/CurrentPrice>/); const price = priceM ? parseFloat(priceM[1]) : 0; const qty = parseInt((item.match(/<Quantity>(\d+)<\/Quantity>/) || [])[1]) || 0; if (!itemId) continue; // Parse pack qty from SKU const packQty = sku.includes('=') ? (parseInt(sku.split('=')[1]) || 1) : 1; const baseSku = sku.includes('=') ? sku.split('=')[0].trim() : sku.trim(); allItems.push({ itemId, title, sku, baseSku, price, qty, packQty }); } page++; } // Deduplicate by itemId const seen = new Set(); allItems = allItems.filter(item => { if (seen.has(item.itemId)) return false; seen.add(item.itemId); return true; }); // COGS lookup — D1 cogs table first (same source Calculator/Amazon // listings already use), falling back to the SKU_MASTER sheet's cog1 // column for any base_sku not yet in D1. This is a safety fallback, not // a permanent second source — keeps existing eBay coverage intact while // getting consistency with the other channels for anything overlapping. let skuMasterCogMap = {}; try { const skuData = await rSheetGet(env, 'SKU_MASTER!A3:I2000'); for (const row of skuData.values || []) { if (row[0]) skuMasterCogMap[row[0].trim().toUpperCase()] = parseFloat(row[1]) || 0; // col B = cog1 } } catch(_) {} async function getCog1pc(baseSku) { if (env.DB) { try { const row = await d1First(env, 'SELECT unit_price FROM cogs WHERE UPPER(base_sku)=?', [baseSku.toUpperCase()]); if (row && row.unit_price) return parseFloat(row.unit_price) || 0; } catch(_) {} } return skuMasterCogMap[baseSku.toUpperCase()] || 0; } // BUGFIX: this was the very first hardcoded copy of the markup tiers // found this session — a THIRD copy, separate from the Price Calculator // tab's (now fixed) copy and the Active Listings tab's separate copy. // Now reads the same D1 price_tiers table (marketplace='ebay') that the // Edit Tiers screen edits — one source of truth for all three. let ebayTiers = []; if (env.DB) { try { await ensurePriceTiersTables(env); ebayTiers = await d1All(env, "SELECT min_qty, max_qty, multiplier FROM price_tiers WHERE marketplace='ebay' ORDER BY sort_order"); } catch(e) { console.error('[eBay listings] tier lookup failed:', e.message); } } function getTierMult(qty) { const matched = ebayTiers.find(t => qty >= t.min_qty && qty <= t.max_qty); return matched ? matched.multiplier : (ebayTiers[0] ? ebayTiers[0].multiplier : 1.75); } const configRows = env.DB ? await d1All(env, 'SELECT key, value FROM price_calc_config').catch(() => []) : []; const priceCfg = { ...PRICE_CALC_DEFAULTS }; configRows.forEach(r => { priceCfg[r.key] = r.value; }); const SHIPPING2 = priceCfg.shipping; const enriched = []; for (const item of allItems) { const cog1pc = await getCog1pc(item.baseSku); const mult = getTierMult(item.packQty); const floor = cog1pc > 0 ? +((cog1pc * item.packQty * priceCfg.floor_multiplier) + SHIPPING2).toFixed(2) : 0; const target = cog1pc > 0 ? +Math.max((cog1pc * item.packQty * mult) + SHIPPING2, floor).toFixed(2) : 0; const max = cog1pc > 0 ? +((cog1pc * item.packQty * priceCfg.ceiling_multiplier) + SHIPPING2).toFixed(2) : 0; const status = !cog1pc ? 'no-cog' : item.price < floor ? 'below-floor' : item.price >= target ? 'ok' : 'needs-review'; enriched.push({ ...item, cog1pc, floor, target, max, status }); } // Background cache write to eBay_Listings tab try { const rows = enriched.map(l => [l.itemId, l.sku, l.title, l.price, l.qty, l.packQty, l.cog1pc, l.status]); await rSheetUpdate(env, `eBay_Listings!A2:H${rows.length + 1}`, rows); } catch(_) {} return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ listings: enriched, total: enriched.length, synced: new Date().toISOString(), }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // POST /amazon/update-price — Body: { asin, newPrice, dryRun } // // Amazon's Listings Items API is per-SKU, not per-ASIN — there's no single // call that updates "the ASIN." Per the confirmed workflow: when you update // an ASIN's price, every SKU under it (FBA + FBM siblings) gets the same // new price. So this does two things: // 1. Fresh live lookup of every SKU currently active under this ASIN // (NOT read from already-loaded page data — re-fetched at click time // so this can't act on stale info if something changed since the // Active Listings tab was last loaded) // 2. PATCH each SKU's price individually, collecting per-SKU results — // one can succeed while another fails, so the caller needs to see // which is which, never a single generic ok/fail for the whole ASIN. // // HONESTY NOTE — read before using for real: step 1 (SKU discovery via // Listings Items Search) uses a stable, well-documented SP-API shape. // Step 2 (the actual PATCH body) is the one place in this whole feature // I could not verify against live data — the exact JSON Patch shape // Amazon expects for a price update varies by product type/category, and // I don't have access to your specific listings' schema to confirm it. // Built to Amazon's standard documented pattern below, but ALWAYS run // with dryRun:true first against a real ASIN and check the SKU list is // right before ever setting dryRun:false. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // AMAZON BULK TITLE UPDATE — JSON_LISTINGS_FEED // // Unlike amazonUpdatePrice (Listings Items API, one SKU at a time), this // uses the Feeds API — Amazon's own documented recommendation for genuine // bulk updates: "The Listings Items API accepts listings updates one at // a time. For use-cases better suited to bulk uploads, the // JSON_LISTINGS_FEED feed type can be used." One submission, one report, // instead of 300+ individual rate-limited calls. // // 5 steps (more than the report-download pattern used elsewhere in this // file, because this is an UPLOAD to Amazon, not just a download): // 1. Create a feed document — Amazon returns a pre-signed upload URL // 2. Upload the actual JSON_LISTINGS_FEED payload to that URL directly // (not through SP-API — straight PUT to the returned URL) // 3. Create the feed, referencing the uploaded document // 4. Poll feed status until DONE // 5. Download + parse the result report (gzip-aware, same pattern as // amazonListingsDownload) — per-SKU success/failure // // SKU resolution: uses the already-imported platform_skus table (from // the AmazonSKU sheet import) rather than a live per-ASIN search. This is // a deliberate difference from amazonUpdatePrice's live-lookup approach — // for a genuine bulk operation (hundreds of ASINs), a live SP-API search // per ASIN would be slow and could hit rate limits; the already-imported // mapping is the right tool for bulk specifically. Same "every SKU under // the ASIN gets updated" behavior as the price flow. // // HONESTY NOTE, same as amazonUpdatePrice: the feed submission mechanics // (create document, upload, create feed, poll, download report) follow // Amazon's stable, well-documented Feeds API shape with high confidence. // The exact JSON Patch body for item_name — like the price PATCH before // it — is the one piece that needs live confirmation, since the required // shape can vary by product type. Always dry-run first. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // POST /amazon/titles-feed-submit // Body: { titles: [{asin, newTitle}, ...], dryRun } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // AMAZON TITLE TRACKING — auto-flag + persistent progress tracking // // Reuses the listings data already pulled by the Active Listings tab's // "Sync Listings" (amazonListingsRequest/status/download) rather than // triggering a second, separate Amazon catalog pull — the frontend // already has this data in memory after a sync. // // Replaces manually cross-referencing a spreadsheet of "already done" // ASINs against the full catalog — this tracks completion permanently // in D1, so re-scanning after the catalog grows only ever surfaces // genuinely new/still-over-limit titles, never ones already fixed. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // POST /amazon/titles-scan — Body: { listings: [{asin, sku, title}, ...] } // Filters to >75-char titles not already marked done in D1, upserts new // ones as "flagged" (idempotent — re-scanning is always safe). async function amazonTitlesScan(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS amazon_title_tracking ( asin TEXT PRIMARY KEY, sku TEXT, original_title TEXT, title_length INTEGER, flagged_at TEXT, title_shortened_at TEXT, new_title TEXT )`).run(); const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const listings = Array.isArray(body.listings) ? body.listings : []; if (!listings.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'listings array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const overLimit = listings.filter(l => l.title && l.title.length > 75); const doneRows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT asin FROM amazon_title_tracking WHERE title_shortened_at IS NOT NULL'); const doneSet = new Set(doneRows.map(r => r.asin)); const needsWork = overLimit.filter(l => !doneSet.has(l.asin)); const alreadyDoneCount = overLimit.length - needsWork.length; const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const CHUNK = 50; for (let i = 0; i < needsWork.length; i += CHUNK) { const chunk = needsWork.slice(i, i + CHUNK); const stmts = chunk.map(l => env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO amazon_title_tracking (asin, sku, original_title, title_length, flagged_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(asin) DO UPDATE SET sku=excluded.sku, original_title=excluded.original_title, title_length=excluded.title_length` ).bind(l.asin, l.sku || '', l.title, l.title.length, ts)); await env.DB.batch(stmts); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, totalScanned: listings.length, overLimitCount: overLimit.length, alreadyDoneCount, needsWork, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /amazon/titles-needs-work — returns the current flagged-but-not- // done list directly from D1, without requiring a fresh scan/sync. async function amazonTitlesNeedsWork(env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { // Same safety net as amazonTitlesScan — this can legitimately be // called before any scan has ever run (e.g. clicking "Load Saved // Needs-Work List" first), so the table needs to exist here too, // not just in the scan function. await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS amazon_title_tracking ( asin TEXT PRIMARY KEY, sku TEXT, original_title TEXT, title_length INTEGER, flagged_at TEXT, title_shortened_at TEXT, new_title TEXT )`).run(); const rows = await d1All(env, 'SELECT asin, sku, original_title, title_length FROM amazon_title_tracking WHERE title_shortened_at IS NULL ORDER BY flagged_at ASC'); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, count: rows.length, needsWork: rows }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // POST /amazon/titles-mark-done — Body: { updates: [{asin, newTitle}, ...] } // Called after a successful Bulk Titles submission, so re-scanning later // never re-flags these. async function amazonTitlesMarkDone(request, env) { if (!env.DB) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'D1 not available' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); try { // In normal UI flow this table already exists by the time mark-done // runs (a scan or needs-work load always happens first) — this is // just defensive consistency in case it's ever called directly. await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS amazon_title_tracking ( asin TEXT PRIMARY KEY, sku TEXT, original_title TEXT, title_length INTEGER, flagged_at TEXT, title_shortened_at TEXT, new_title TEXT )`).run(); const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const updates = Array.isArray(body.updates) ? body.updates : []; if (!updates.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'updates array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const stmts = updates.map(u => env.DB.prepare( 'UPDATE amazon_title_tracking SET title_shortened_at=?, new_title=? WHERE asin=?' ).bind(ts, u.newTitle, u.asin)); await env.DB.batch(stmts); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, markedDone: updates.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function amazonTitlesFeedSubmit(request, env) { try { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const titles = Array.isArray(body.titles) ? body.titles : []; const dryRun = body.dryRun === true; if (!titles.length) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'titles array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); for (const t of titles) { const newTitle = String(t.newTitle || '').trim(); if (newTitle.length > 75) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: `Title for ${t.asin} is ${newTitle.length} chars — exceeds the 75-char limit. Fix before submitting.` }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } const token = await getAmazonToken(env); const mid = env.AMAZON_MARKETPLACE_ID || 'ATVPDKIKX0DER'; const sellerId = env.AMAZON_SELLER_ID; if (!sellerId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'AMAZON_SELLER_ID env var not set' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const amzDate = () => new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:\-.]/g,'').slice(0,15)+'Z'; // BUGFIX: previously resolved SKUs via the imported AmazonSKU sheet // (platform_skus table) — but that sheet only lists ONE correctly- // formatted SKU per ASIN by design, not every SKU (FBA/FBM siblings // etc). Fine for other lookups, wrong here — a title update needs // every SKU under the ASIN, same as the price-update feature. // Switched to the same live SP-API search amazonUpdatePrice already // uses. Same 20-per-call / 5-parallel cap already proven in // repricerAutoReprice, since a live per-ASIN lookup at scale risks // the worker's execution time limit if done all at once. // remainingAsins echoes back what's left so the frontend can loop. const batch = titles.slice(0, 20); const remaining = titles.slice(20).map(t => t.asin); const resolved = []; const unresolved = []; async function resolveOne(t) { const asin = String(t.asin || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const newTitle = String(t.newTitle || '').trim(); if (!asin || !newTitle) return; try { const searchUrl = `https://sellingpartnerapi-na.amazon.com/listings/2021-08-01/items/${encodeURIComponent(sellerId)}` + `?marketplaceIds=${encodeURIComponent(mid)}&identifiers=${encodeURIComponent(asin)}&identifiersType=ASIN&includedData=summaries`; const searchRes = await fetch(searchUrl, { headers: { 'x-amz-access-token': token, 'x-amz-date': amzDate() } }); const searchData = await searchRes.json(); if (!searchRes.ok) { unresolved.push(asin); return; } const skus = (searchData.items || []).map(i => i.sku).filter(Boolean); if (!skus.length) { unresolved.push(asin); return; } for (const sku of skus) resolved.push({ asin, sku, newTitle }); } catch (e) { unresolved.push(asin); } } for (let i = 0; i < batch.length; i += 5) { await Promise.all(batch.slice(i, i + 5).map(resolveOne)); } if (dryRun) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, batchAsins: batch.length, resolvedSkus: resolved.length, unresolvedAsins: unresolved, remainingAsins: remaining, sample: resolved.slice(0, 10), }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } if (!resolved.length) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'No ASINs in this batch resolved to a SKU — nothing to submit', unresolvedAsins: unresolved, remainingAsins: remaining }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── Step 1: create feed document ── const createDocRes = await fetch('https://sellingpartnerapi-na.amazon.com/feeds/2021-06-30/documents', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'x-amz-access-token': token, 'x-amz-date': amzDate(), 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ contentType: 'application/json; charset=UTF-8' }), }); const createDocData = await createDocRes.json(); if (!createDocRes.ok || !createDocData.url) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Feed document creation failed: ' + JSON.stringify(createDocData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const { feedDocumentId, url: uploadUrl } = createDocData; // ── Step 2: build the JSON_LISTINGS_FEED payload and upload it ── // messageMap is returned to the caller and is the ONLY way to later // know which SKU a given messageId in the processing report refers // to — Amazon's report doesn't repeat the SKU back, only the // messageId (see the report-parsing note further down). const messageMap = resolved.map((r, i) => ({ messageId: i + 1, sku: r.sku, asin: r.asin })); // BUGFIX: every message failed with "'#/patches: false schema always // fails'" -- confirmed against real submitted data. That error means // Amazon couldn't resolve which product-type schema to validate // `patches` against, because productType was missing from the // message entirely. amazonUpdatePrice already includes // productType:'PRODUCT' in its PATCH body for the exact same reason; // this was the one place that pattern didn't get carried over. const messages = resolved.map((r, i) => ({ messageId: i + 1, sku: r.sku, operationType: 'PATCH', productType: 'PRODUCT', patches: [{ op: 'replace', path: '/attributes/item_name', value: [{ value: r.newTitle, marketplace_id: mid }], }], })); const feedPayload = { header: { sellerId, version: '2.0' }, messages, }; const uploadRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=UTF-8' }, body: JSON.stringify(feedPayload), }); if (!uploadRes.ok) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Feed upload failed: HTTP ' + uploadRes.status }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── Step 3: create the feed, referencing the uploaded document ── const createFeedRes = await fetch('https://sellingpartnerapi-na.amazon.com/feeds/2021-06-30/feeds', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'x-amz-access-token': token, 'x-amz-date': amzDate(), 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ feedType: 'JSON_LISTINGS_FEED', marketplaceIds: [mid], inputFeedDocumentId: feedDocumentId }), }); const createFeedData = await createFeedRes.json(); if (!createFeedRes.ok || !createFeedData.feedId) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Feed creation failed: ' + JSON.stringify(createFeedData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, feedId: createFeedData.feedId, skusSubmitted: resolved.length, unresolvedAsins: unresolved, remainingAsins: remaining, messageMap: messageMap, submittedAt: new Date().toISOString(), }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /amazon/titles-feed-status?feedId=X async function amazonTitlesFeedStatus(url, env) { try { const feedId = url.searchParams.get('feedId'); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // GET /amazon/titles-feed-report?resultFeedDocumentId=X async function amazonTitlesFeedReport(url, env) { try { const resultFeedDocumentId = url.searchParams.get('resultFeedDocumentId'); if (!resultFeedDocumentId) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'resultFeedDocumentId required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getAmazonToken(env); const amzDate = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:\-.]/g,'').slice(0,15)+'Z'; const docRes = await fetch(`https://sellingpartnerapi-na.amazon.com/feeds/2021-06-30/documents/${resultFeedDocumentId}`, { headers: { 'x-amz-access-token': token, 'x-amz-date': amzDate }, }); const docData = await docRes.json(); if (!docRes.ok || !docData.url) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Report document fetch failed: ' + JSON.stringify(docData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const reportRes = await fetch(docData.url); const rawBytes = await reportRes.arrayBuffer(); const magic = new Uint8Array(rawBytes.slice(0,2)); let reportText; if (magic[0]===0x1f && magic[1]===0x8b) { // Same gzip-decompression approach already proven elsewhere in this file try { const ds = new DecompressionStream('gzip'); const writer = ds.writable.getWriter(); const reader = ds.readable.getReader(); writer.write(new Uint8Array(rawBytes)); writer.close(); const chunks = []; let done = false; while (!done) { const { value, done: d } = await reader.read(); if (value) chunks.push(value); done = d; } const total = chunks.reduce((a,c)=>a+c.length,0); const combined = new Uint8Array(total); let offset = 0; for (const chunk of chunks) { combined.set(chunk, offset); offset += chunk.length; } reportText = new TextDecoder().decode(combined); } catch(e) { reportText = new TextDecoder().decode(rawBytes); } } else { reportText = new TextDecoder().decode(rawBytes); } let reportJson; try { reportJson = JSON.parse(reportText); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Report parse failed: ' + e.message, rawSnippet: reportText.slice(0,500) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // BUGFIX: the real JSON_LISTINGS_FEED processing report schema is // { summary: { messagesProcessed, messagesAccepted, messagesInvalid, // errors, warnings }, issues: [{ messageId, severity, message }] } — // NOT { results: [...] } as originally assumed (confirmed against a // real report that came back as {"results":[],"total":0} — the key // simply didn't exist). Critically, `issues` only lists messages that // had a PROBLEM, keyed by messageId, not sku — Amazon's report never // repeats back which SKU a messageId belonged to. The caller must // have its own messageId→SKU mapping (returned by the submit // endpoint) to turn this into a per-SKU results table; this endpoint // returns the raw summary + issues, correlation happens in the // frontend where that mapping is already held. const summary = reportJson.summary || {}; const issues = (reportJson.issues || []).map(i => ({ messageId: i.messageId, severity: i.severity, message: i.message, })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, summary, issues }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch (e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function amazonUpdatePrice(request, env) { try { const { asin, newPrice, dryRun } = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!asin || !newPrice) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'asin and newPrice required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); if (!env.AMAZON_SELLER_ID) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'AMAZON_SELLER_ID env var not set — add it in Cloudflare Workers settings' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const token = await getAmazonToken(env); const sellerId = env.AMAZON_SELLER_ID; const mid = env.AMAZON_MARKETPLACE_ID || 'ATVPDKIKX0DER'; const amzDate = () => new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:\-.]/g,'').slice(0,15)+'Z'; // ── Step 1: fresh lookup of every SKU currently active under this ASIN ── const searchUrl = `https://sellingpartnerapi-na.amazon.com/listings/2021-08-01/items/${encodeURIComponent(sellerId)}` + `?marketplaceIds=${encodeURIComponent(mid)}&identifiers=${encodeURIComponent(asin)}&identifiersType=ASIN&includedData=summaries`; const searchRes = await fetch(searchUrl, { headers: { 'x-amz-access-token': token, 'x-amz-date': amzDate() } }); const searchData = await searchRes.json(); if (!searchRes.ok) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'SKU lookup failed: ' + JSON.stringify(searchData.errors || searchData) }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const skus = (searchData.items || []).map(i => i.sku).filter(Boolean); if (!skus.length) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'No active SKUs found for ASIN ' + asin }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } if (dryRun) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, asin, newPrice, skus, wouldUpdate: skus.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── Step 2: PATCH each SKU's price individually ── // Standard SP-API price-update pattern — a JSON Patch replacing the // purchasable_offer attribute. This is the part that needs live // verification (see function-level note above). const results = []; for (const sku of skus) { try { const patchUrl = `https://sellingpartnerapi-na.amazon.com/listings/2021-08-01/items/${encodeURIComponent(sellerId)}/${encodeURIComponent(sku)}` + `?marketplaceIds=${encodeURIComponent(mid)}`; const patchRes = await fetch(patchUrl, { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'x-amz-access-token': token, 'x-amz-date': amzDate(), 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ productType: 'PRODUCT', patches: [{ op: 'replace', path: '/attributes/purchasable_offer', value: [{ marketplace_id: mid, currency: 'USD', our_price: [{ schedule: [{ value_with_tax: newPrice }] }], }], }], }), }); const patchData = await patchRes.json().catch(() => ({})); results.push({ sku, ok: patchRes.ok, error: patchRes.ok ? null : JSON.stringify(patchData.errors || patchData).slice(0, 300) }); } catch(e) { results.push({ sku, ok: false, error: e.message });
