} } const allOk = results.every(r => r.ok); if (!allOk) console.error('[Amazon] price update partial/full failure — asin:', asin, 'results:', JSON.stringify(results)); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: allOk, asin, newPrice, results }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } async function ebayUpdatePrice(request, env) { const { itemId, newPrice } = await request.json(); if (!itemId || !newPrice) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'itemId and newPrice required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); const xmlBody = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?> <ReviseItemRequest xmlns="urn:ebay:apis:eBLBaseComponents"> <RequesterCredentials><eBayAuthToken>${ebayToken}</eBayAuthToken></RequesterCredentials> <Item> <ItemID>${itemId}</ItemID> <StartPrice>${parseFloat(newPrice).toFixed(2)}</StartPrice> </Item> </ReviseItemRequest>`; const r = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME': 'ReviseItem', 'X-EBAY-API-SITEID': '0', 'X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL': '967', 'X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN': ebayToken, 'Content-Type': 'text/xml', }, body: xmlBody }); const xml = await r.text(); const success = xml.includes('<Ack>Success</Ack>') || xml.includes('<Ack>Warning</Ack>'); const errMatch = xml.match(/<ShortMessage>([^<]+)<\/ShortMessage>/); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ success, itemId, newPrice: parseFloat(newPrice), error: success ? null : (errMatch?.[1] || 'eBay rejected the price update'), }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } async function ebayBrowseSearch(url, env) { const q = url.searchParams.get('q') || ''; const limit = Math.min(parseInt(url.searchParams.get('limit') || '25'), 50); if (!q) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'q required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); const searchUrl = `https://api.ebay.com/buy/browse/v1/item_summary/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&limit=${limit}&sort=bestMatch`; const r = await fetch(searchUrl, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${ebayToken}` } }); const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); const items = (data.itemSummaries || []).map((item, i) => ({ rank: i + 1, itemId: item.itemId || '', title: item.title || '', price: parseFloat(item.price?.value || 0), currency: item.price?.currency || 'USD', seller: item.seller?.username || '', condition: item.condition || '', url: item.itemWebUrl || '', image: item.image?.imageUrl || '', packQty: 1, // enriched on frontend unitPrice: parseFloat(item.price?.value || 0), })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ results: items, total: items.length, query: q }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } async function ebayDebugXml(env) { const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); const xmlBody = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?> <GetMyeBaySellingRequest xmlns="urn:ebay:apis:eBLBaseComponents"> <RequesterCredentials><eBayAuthToken>${ebayToken}</eBayAuthToken></RequesterCredentials> <ActiveList><Include>true</Include><Pagination><EntriesPerPage>2</EntriesPerPage><PageNumber>1</PageNumber></Pagination></ActiveList> <DetailLevel>ReturnAll</DetailLevel> </GetMyeBaySellingRequest>`; const r = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME': 'GetMyeBaySelling', 'X-EBAY-API-SITEID': '0', 'X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL': '967', 'X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN': ebayToken, 'Content-Type': 'text/xml', }, body: xmlBody }); const xml = await r.text(); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ xml: xml.slice(0, 5000) }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } async function ebayDebugOrders(env) { const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); const from = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 86400000).toISOString(); const to = new Date().toISOString(); const filter = `creationdate:[${from}..${to}]`; const r = await fetch( `https://api.ebay.com/sell/fulfillment/v1/order?filter=${encodeURIComponent(filter)}&limit=2`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${ebayToken}` } } ); const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); const firstOrder = data.orders?.[0]; const firstLine = firstOrder?.lineItems?.[0]; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ totalOrders: data.total || 0, sampleLine: firstLine ? { keys: Object.keys(firstLine), legacyItemId: firstLine.legacyItemId, sku: firstLine.sku, title: firstLine.title, quantity: firstLine.quantity } : null, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // REPRICER WRITE FUNCTIONS // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── AI price verdict ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function aiPriceVerdict(env, ctx) { // ctx: { sku, itemId, packQty, cog1pc, floor, target, oldPrice, newPrice, pct, // sales90, lowestComp, notes } const FLOOR_GRACE = 0.05; // allow up to 5% below floor const { cog1pc, floor, oldPrice, newPrice, pct, sales90, lowestComp } = ctx; // Hard block — more than 5% below floor const hardFloor = floor * (1 - FLOOR_GRACE); if (newPrice < hardFloor) { return { verdict: 'block', reason: `Price $${newPrice.toFixed(2)} is more than 5% below floor ($${floor.toFixed(2)}). Hard block — cannot proceed.` }; } // Auto-approve <10% changes (handled before AI is called, but safety net) if (pct <= 0.10) { return { verdict: 'approve', reason: 'Change is within 10% auto-approve threshold.' }; } // Build rules context for AI const isSlow = sales90 < 3; const belowFloor = newPrice < floor; const aboveTarget = newPrice >= ctx.target; const compJustified = lowestComp > 0 && newPrice <= lowestComp; const bigDrop = pct > 0.25; const isPriceRise = ctx.newPrice > ctx.oldPrice; // Rule-based fast paths before calling AI if (isPriceRise && pct > 0.10) { // Always queue price increases >10% — AI shouldn't auto-raise return { verdict: 'queue', reason: `Price increase of ${(pct*100).toFixed(1)}% requires management review.` }; } if (bigDrop && !isSlow) { return { verdict: 'queue', reason: `Drop of ${(pct*100).toFixed(1)}% on a fast-moving item (${sales90} sales/90d) requires management review.` }; } if (!lowestComp && belowFloor) { return { verdict: 'queue', reason: `No competitor data provided and price is below floor. Management review required.` }; } // Call Claude for nuanced cases const prompt = `You are a pricing analyst for an eBay reseller. Evaluate this price change request and respond with ONLY a JSON object. Item context: - SKU: ${ctx.sku} - Pack qty: ${ctx.packQty} - Cost per piece: $${cog1pc.toFixed(2)} - Floor price (40% margin + shipping): $${floor.toFixed(2)} - Target price (75% margin + shipping): $${ctx.target.toFixed(2)} - Current price: $${oldPrice.toFixed(2)} - Proposed price: $${newPrice.toFixed(2)} - Change: ${(pct*100).toFixed(1)}% ${isPriceRise ? 'increase' : 'decrease'} - Sales last 90 days: ${sales90} units - Lowest competitor price: ${lowestComp ? '$'+lowestComp.toFixed(2) : 'not provided'} - Below floor: ${belowFloor ? 'YES (within 5% grace)' : 'no'} - Notes from employee: ${ctx.notes || 'none'} Business rules: - Slow mover = fewer than 3 sales in 90 days. Slow movers get more pricing flexibility. - If competitor is lower than us and item is slow, dropping price is generally justified. - Never auto-approve if competitor data is missing and price drops below floor. - Never auto-approve price increases >10%. - Never auto-approve drops >25% on fast movers. - Price in floor-5% band (competitive window) is allowed but needs justification. Respond ONLY with this JSON (no markdown, no explanation outside JSON): {"verdict":"approve|queue","reason":"one sentence plain English explanation","confidence":0.0-1.0}`; try { const aiResp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-api-key': env.ANTHROPIC_KEY || env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' }, body: JSON.stringify({ model: 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001', max_tokens: 200, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }] }) }); const aiData = await aiResp.json(); const text = aiData.content?.[0]?.text || ''; const parsed = JSON.parse(text.replace(/```json|```/g, '').trim()); return { verdict: parsed.verdict, reason: parsed.reason, confidence: parsed.confidence, ai: true }; } catch(e) { // AI failed — default to queue for safety return { verdict: 'queue', reason: 'AI analysis unavailable — queued for manual review.', ai: false }; } } async function repricerPriceChange(request, env) { const SHIPPING = 5.25; const body = await request.json(); const { sku, itemId, packQty, cog1pc, floor, target, oldPrice, newPrice, lowestComp, notes, user, pricePin } = body; if (String(pricePin) !== String(env.PRICE_PIN)) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid price PIN' }), { status: 403, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const old_ = parseFloat(oldPrice) || 0; const new_ = parseFloat(newPrice) || 0; const pct = old_ > 0 ? Math.abs((new_ - old_) / old_) : 1; const stamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); const ts = new Date().toISOString().replace('T',' ').slice(0,19); let status = 'Pending', result = '', verdict = 'queue', reason = ''; // ── <10%: auto-approve, apply immediately ─────────────────────────── if (pct <= 0.10) { try { const r = await ebayUpdatePrice({ json: async () => ({ itemId, newPrice: new_ }) }, env); const res = await r.clone().json(); status = res.success ? 'Applied' : 'Failed'; result = res.error || 'OK'; verdict = 'approve'; reason = 'Within 10% auto-approve threshold.'; } catch(e) { status = 'Failed'; result = e.message; } } else { // ── >10%: get sales velocity then AI verdict ───────────────────── let sales90 = 0; try { // Inline velocity lookup — resolve SKU then read eBay_Sales_90d let targetBase = (sku||'').includes('=') ? sku.split('=')[0].trim() : (sku||'').trim(); if (!targetBase && itemId) { const cache = await rSheetGet(env, 'eBay_Listings!A2:B2000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const row = (cache.values || []).find(r => String(r[0]).trim() === String(itemId)); if (row?.[1]) { const s = row[1]; targetBase = s.includes('=') ? s.split('=')[0].trim() : s.trim(); } } if (targetBase) { const r90 = await rSheetGet(env, 'eBay_Sales_90d!A2:G3000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); for (const row of (r90.values || [])) { if (String(row[0]).trim() === String(itemId) || String(row[1]).trim() === targetBase) { sales90 = parseInt(row[3]) || parseInt(row[2]) || 0; break; } } } } catch(_) {} const floorNum = parseFloat(floor) || 0; const targetNum = parseFloat(target) || 0; const compNum = parseFloat(lowestComp) || 0; const ai = await aiPriceVerdict(env, { sku, itemId, packQty: parseInt(packQty)||1, cog1pc: parseFloat(cog1pc)||0, floor: floorNum, target: targetNum, oldPrice: old_, newPrice: new_, pct, sales90, lowestComp: compNum, notes }); verdict = ai.verdict; reason = ai.reason; if (verdict === 'approve') { // AI approved — apply to eBay try { const r = await ebayUpdatePrice({ json: async () => ({ itemId, newPrice: new_ }) }, env); const res = await r.clone().json(); status = res.success ? 'Applied' : 'Failed'; result = res.error || 'OK'; } catch(e) { status = 'Failed'; result = e.message; } } else if (verdict === 'block') { status = 'Blocked'; result = reason; } else { // Queue for management approval status = 'Queued'; result = reason; await rSheetAppend(env, 'Approval_Queue!A:L', [[ ts, itemId||'', sku||'', parseInt(packQty)||1, old_, new_, +( pct*100).toFixed(1)+'%', sales90, compNum||'', reason, user||'', 'Pending' ]]); } } // Audit log — every attempt await rSheetAppend(env, 'Price_History!A:K', [[stamp, user||'', sku||'', itemId||'', packQty||'', old_, new_, +( pct*100).toFixed(1)+'%', status, result, notes||'']]); // Clean log — applied only if (status === 'Applied') { await rSheetAppend(env, 'Price_Changes!A:E', [[ts, itemId||'', old_, new_, user||'']]); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, status, verdict, reason, pct: +(pct*100).toFixed(1), result }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── Queue GET — read pending approvals ─────────────────────────────────── async function repricerQueueGet(url, env) { const data = await rSheetGet(env, 'Approval_Queue!A2:L500'); const rows = (data.values || []).filter(r => r[0]); const items = rows.map((r, i) => ({ rowIndex: i + 2, timestamp: r[0], itemId: r[1], sku: r[2], packQty: r[3], oldPrice: parseFloat(r[4])||0, newPrice: parseFloat(r[5])||0, pct: r[6], sales90: r[7], lowestComp: r[8], reason: r[9], user: r[10], status: r[11] || 'Pending' })).filter(r => r.status === 'Pending'); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ queue: items, total: items.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── Queue action — approve or deny ─────────────────────────────────────── async function repricerQueueAction(request, env) { const body = await request.json(); const { rowIndex, action, itemId, newPrice, user } = body; // action: 'approve'|'deny' if (!['approve','deny'].includes(action)) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid action' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } let applied = false, errorMsg = ''; if (action === 'approve') { try { const r = await ebayUpdatePrice({ json: async () => ({ itemId, newPrice: parseFloat(newPrice) }) }, env); const res = await r.clone().json(); applied = res.success; errorMsg = res.error || ''; } catch(e) { errorMsg = e.message; } } // Update status in sheet — column L (index 12) = status, col M = actioned by const newStatus = action === 'approve' ? (applied ? 'Approved+Applied' : 'Approve-Failed') : 'Denied'; const ts = new Date().toISOString().replace('T',' ').slice(0,19); await rSheetUpdate(env, 'Approval_Queue!L' + rowIndex + ':N' + rowIndex, [[newStatus, user||'mgmt', ts]]); // Log to Price_Changes if applied if (applied) { await rSheetAppend(env, 'Price_Changes!A:E', [[ts, itemId||'', '', parseFloat(newPrice), user||'mgmt']]); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, status: newStatus, applied }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── Auto-reprice: correct a chunk of listings to floor × 1.02 ──────────── // Caller passes an explicit list of {itemId, offerId, sku, price, packQty, cog1pc} // Worker just processes that list — no sheet scan needed (app already filtered) async function repricerAutoReprice(request, env) { const body = await request.json(); const { pricePin, user, listings } = body; if (String(pricePin) !== String(env.PRICE_PIN)) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid price PIN' }), { status: 403, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } if (!Array.isArray(listings) || !listings.length) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'No listings provided' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const SHIPPING = 5.25; const ABOVE_FLOOR = 1.02; const results = { applied: [], skipped: [], failed: [] }; const ts = new Date().toISOString().replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 19); const stamp = ts.slice(0, 10); // Process up to 20 listings — run 5 in parallel for speed const chunk = listings.slice(0, 20); async function processOne(l) { const { itemId, offerId, sku, price, packQty, cog1pc } = l; if (!cog1pc || !price) { results.skipped.push({ itemId, sku, reason: 'Missing COGS or price' }); return; } const floor = (cog1pc * packQty * 1.40) + SHIPPING; const newPrice = +(floor * ABOVE_FLOOR).toFixed(2); const pct = Math.abs((newPrice - price) / price); if (price >= floor) { results.skipped.push({ itemId, sku, reason: 'Already at or above floor' }); return; } if (pct > 0.10) { results.skipped.push({ itemId, sku, price, newPrice, reason: `Correction ${(pct*100).toFixed(1)}% exceeds 10% auto limit` }); return; } try { const r = await ebayUpdatePrice({ json: async () => ({ itemId: offerId || itemId, newPrice }) }, env); const res = await r.clone().json(); if (res.success) { results.applied.push({ itemId, sku, oldPrice: price, newPrice, pct: +(pct*100).toFixed(1) }); await rSheetAppend(env, 'Price_History!A:K', [[stamp, user||'AUTO', sku, itemId, packQty, price, newPrice, +(pct*100).toFixed(1)+'%', 'Applied', 'Auto-reprice', '']]); await rSheetAppend(env, 'Price_Changes!A:E', [[ts, itemId, price, newPrice, user||'AUTO']]); } else { results.failed.push({ itemId, sku, reason: res.error || 'eBay update failed' }); } } catch(e) { results.failed.push({ itemId, sku, reason: e.message }); } } // Process in batches of 5 concurrent for (let i = 0; i < chunk.length; i += 5) { await Promise.all(chunk.slice(i, i + 5).map(processOne)); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, applied: results.applied.length, skipped: results.skipped.length, failed: results.failed.length, details: results }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } async function repricerPriceHistory(url, env) { const sku = url.searchParams.get('sku'); const itemId = url.searchParams.get('itemId'); const limit = parseInt(url.searchParams.get('limit') || '100'); const data = await rSheetGet(env, 'Price_Changes!A2:E500'); let rows = (data.values || []).filter(r => r[0]); if (sku) rows = rows.filter(r => r[2] === sku); if (itemId) rows = rows.filter(r => String(r[3]).includes(itemId)); const parsed = rows.slice(-limit).reverse().map(r => ({ date: r[0], itemId: r[1], oldPrice: parseFloat(r[2])||0, newPrice: parseFloat(r[3])||0, user: r[4], })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ history: parsed, total: parsed.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } async function repricerSaveResearch(request, env) { const body = await request.json(); const { itemId, sku, packQty, ourPrice, ourRank, lowestComp, competitors=[], trend, s30, s90, s6m, s1y, notes, initials } = body; const stamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); const baseSku = (sku||'').includes('=') ? sku.split('=')[0].trim() : (sku||'').trim(); const ourUnit = packQty > 1 ? +(ourPrice / packQty).toFixed(2) : ourPrice; const compPrice = lowestComp || (competitors[0]?.price) || 0; const compPack = competitors[0]?.packQty || packQty; const compUnit = compPack > 1 ? +(compPrice / compPack).toFixed(2) : compPrice; const compStr = competitors.map(c => `${c.title||''}: $${c.price} [${c.url||''}]`).join(' | '); // Write summary row to MARKET_RESEARCH const summaryRow = [stamp, itemId||'', baseSku, packQty||1, ourPrice||0, ourUnit, compPack, compPrice, compUnit, ourRank||'', trend||'', s30||0, s90||0, s6m||0, s1y||0, '', '', compStr, notes||'', initials||'']; await rSheetAppend(env, 'MARKET_RESEARCH!A:T', [summaryRow]); // Write detail rows to Price_Research_Log (one row per competitor) if (competitors.length) { const detailRows = competitors.map(c => [stamp, itemId||'', baseSku, packQty||1, ourPrice||0, ourRank||'', c.rank||'', c.title||'', c.price||0, c.packQty||1, c.unitPrice||0, c.seller||'', c.url||'', notes||'', initials||'']); await rSheetAppend(env, 'Price_Research_Log!A:O', detailRows); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // INVENTORY MANAGEMENT ROUTES /inventory/* // Staff (ops/mgmt): log, lookup // Management only (mgmt): verify // Sheet: env.SHEET_ID (warehouse sheet) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function invSheetGet(env, range) { const token = await getToken(env); const [tab, cells] = range.split('!'); const enc = cells ? encodeURIComponent(tab) + '!' + cells : encodeURIComponent(tab); const r = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHEET_ID}/values/${enc}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }); return r.json(); } async function invSheetUpdate(env, range, values) { const token = await getToken(env); const [tab, cells] = range.split('!'); const enc = cells ? encodeURIComponent(tab) + '!' + cells : encodeURIComponent(tab); const r = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHEET_ID}/values/${enc}?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values }) }); return r.json(); } async function invSheetAppend(env, range, values) { const token = await getToken(env); const [tab, cells] = range.split('!'); const enc = cells ? encodeURIComponent(tab) + '!' + cells : encodeURIComponent(tab); const r = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHEET_ID}/values/${enc}:append?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values }) }); return r.json(); } // Lookup part number from D1 upc table (Phase 3 — was Sheets UPC!A2:D2000) async function invLookupUPC(env, barcode) { const scanned = barcode.trim().replace(/\D/g, ''); if (!scanned) return null; // D1 primary lookup if (env.DB) { try { const row = await d1First(env, `SELECT sku, inside_upc, outside_upc FROM upc WHERE REPLACE(outside_upc, ' ', '') = ? OR REPLACE(inside_upc, ' ', '') = ? LIMIT 1`, [scanned, scanned] ); if (row) { return { partNumber: row.sku || '', sku: row.sku || '', description: '' }; } } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] invLookupUPC:', e.message); } } // Sheets fallback const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'UPC!A2:D2000'); const rows = data.values || []; for (const r of rows) { const outsideUpc = (r[3] || '').trim().replace(/\D/g, ''); if (outsideUpc && outsideUpc === scanned) { return { partNumber: (r[0] || '').trim(), sku: (r[1] || '').trim(), description: (r[2] || '').trim() }; } } return null; } // Read Master_List rows — reads from D1 (Phase 3), falls back to Sheets // Returns array of { rowIndex (1-based), partNumber, location, cases, qtyInCase, baseSku } async function invReadMasterList(env) { if (env.DB) { try { const rows = await d1All(env, `SELECT sheet_row, part_num, location, cases, units_per_case FROM master_list WHERE part_num != '' ORDER BY sheet_row` ); return rows.map(r => { const partNumber = (r.part_num || '').trim(); const baseSku = partNumber.split('=')[0].split('-')[0].trim(); return { rowIndex: r.sheet_row || 0, partNumber, baseSku, location: (r.location || '').trim(), cases: parseFloat(r.cases) || 0, qtyInCase: parseFloat(r.units_per_case) || 0, }; }).filter(r => r.partNumber); } catch(e) { console.error('[D1] invReadMasterList failed:', e.message); } } // Sheets fallback const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Master_List!A1:J5000'); const rows = data.values || []; const result = []; for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) { const r = rows[i]; const partNumber = (r[6] || '').trim(); const location = (r[7] || '').trim(); const cases = parseFloat(r[8]) || 0; const qtyInCase = parseInt(r[9]) || 0; if (!partNumber) continue; const baseSku = partNumber.split('=')[0].split('-')[0].trim(); result.push({ rowIndex: i + 1, partNumber, location, cases, qtyInCase, baseSku }); } return result; } // GET /inventory/lookup?barcode=xxx // Returns part info + all Master_List locations for that part async function invLookup(url, env) { const barcode = url.searchParams.get('barcode') || ''; if (!barcode) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'barcode required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const part = await invLookupUPC(env, barcode); if (!part) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Barcode not found in UPC sheet' }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const masterRows = await invReadMasterList(env); const locations = masterRows .filter(r => r.partNumber === part.partNumber) .map(r => ({ rowIndex: r.rowIndex, location: r.location, cases: r.cases, qtyInCase: r.qtyInCase, isPlaceholder: /will be here/i.test(r.location) })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ...part, locations }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // POST /inventory/log // Body: { type, partNumber, location, cases, initials, notes, rowIndex, isPlaceholder, newLocation } // Appends to Inventory_Log sheet async function invLog(request, env) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { type, partNumber, location, cases, initials, notes, rowIndex, isPlaceholder, newLocation } = b; if (!type || !partNumber || !cases || !initials) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing required fields' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const ts = new Date().toISOString(); // Cols: A=Timestamp B=Type C=PartNumber D=Location E=Cases F=Initials G=Notes H=Status I=VerifiedBy J=VerifiedAt K=RowIndex L=IsPlaceholder M=NewLocation const row = [ts, type, partNumber, location, cases, initials, notes || '', 'Pending', '', '', rowIndex || '', isPlaceholder ? 'YES' : 'NO', newLocation || '']; await invSheetAppend(env, 'Inventory_Log!A:M', [row]); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // GET /inventory/pending — mgmt only async function invPending(env) { const data = await invSheetGet(env, 'Inventory_Log!A2:M2000'); const rows = (data.values || []).map((r, i) => ({ logRow: i + 2, // 1-based sheet row timestamp: r[0] || '', type: r[1] || '', partNumber: r[2] || '', location: r[3] || '', cases: parseFloat(r[4]) || 0, initials: r[5] || '', notes: r[6] || '', status: r[7] || '', verifiedBy: r[8] || '', verifiedAt: r[9] || '', rowIndex: parseInt(r[10]) || 0, isPlaceholder: r[11] === 'YES', newLocation: r[12] || '' })).filter(r => r.status === 'Pending'); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ rows }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // POST /inventory/verify — mgmt only // Body: { rowIndex, action (Approved|Rejected), item } async function invVerify(request, env) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); // Frontend sends rowIndex + action (capitalised) + item; normalise here const logRow = b.logRow || b.rowIndex; const action = (b.action || '').toLowerCase().replace('approved','approve').replace('rejected','reject'); const verifiedBy = b.verifiedBy || (b.item && b.item.initials) || 'mgmt'; if (!logRow || !action) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing fields' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Read the log row const data = await invSheetGet(env, `Inventory_Log!A${logRow}:M${logRow}`); const r = (data.values || [[]])[0]; if (!r || !r[0]) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Log row not found' }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const type = r[1]; const partNumber = r[2]; const location = r[3]; const cases = parseFloat(r[4]) || 0; const rowIndex = parseInt(r[10]) || 0; const isPlaceholder = r[11] === 'YES'; const newLocation = r[12] || ''; const ts = new Date().toISOString(); if (action === 'approve') { const masterRows = await invReadMasterList(env); if (type === 'IN') { if (rowIndex && isPlaceholder && newLocation) { // Overwrite placeholder row: update location (col H) and cases (col I) await invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!H${rowIndex}:I${rowIndex}`, [[newLocation, cases]]); } else if (rowIndex) { // Add to existing row cases const existing = masterRows.find(r => r.rowIndex === rowIndex); const newCases = (existing ? existing.cases : 0) + cases; await invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!I${rowIndex}`, [[newCases]]); } else { // Insert new row after last matching base SKU const baseSku = partNumber.split('=')[0].split('-')[0].trim(); const matching = masterRows.filter(r => r.baseSku === baseSku); if (matching.length > 0) { const lastRow = matching[matching.length - 1].rowIndex; // Insert a blank row after lastRow using batchUpdate const token = await getToken(env); await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHEET_ID}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests: [{ insertDimension: { range: { sheetId: 0, dimension: 'ROWS', startIndex: lastRow, endIndex: lastRow + 1 }, inheritFromBefore: true } }] }) }); // Write G/H/I/J into the new row (lastRow + 1 in 1-based = lastRow+1) await invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!G${lastRow+1}:J${lastRow+1}`, [[partNumber, location, cases, '']]); } else { // No matching SKU found — append to end await invSheetAppend(env, 'Master_List!G:J', [[partNumber, location, cases, '']]); } } } else if (type === 'OUT') { if (rowIndex) { const existing = masterRows.find(r => r.rowIndex === rowIndex); const newCases = Math.max(0, (existing ? existing.cases : 0) - cases); await invSheetUpdate(env, `Master_List!I${rowIndex}`, [[newCases]]); } } } // Update log row status — cols H/I/J const newStatus = action === 'approve' ? 'Approved' : 'Rejected'; await invSheetUpdate(env, `Inventory_Log!H${logRow}:J${logRow}`, [[newStatus, verifiedBy, ts]]); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, success: true, status: newStatus }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ▼▼▼ v8 ADDITIONS — PACK & SHIP — nothing above this line was changed ▼▼▼ // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── Ship Sheet helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function shipSheetGet(env, range) { const token = await getToken(env); const [tab, cells] = range.split('!'); const encoded = cells ? encodeURIComponent(tab) + '!' + cells : encodeURIComponent(tab); const r = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}/values/${encoded}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }); const data = await r.json(); if (data.error) throw new Error('Ship sheet read: ' + data.error.message); return data; } async function shipSheetAppend(env, range, values) { const token = await getToken(env); const r = await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:append?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values }) } ); const data = await r.json(); if (data.error) throw new Error('Ship sheet append: ' + data.error.message); return data; } async function shipSheetBatchUpdate(env, updates) { const token = await getToken(env); const r = await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}/values:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ valueInputOption: 'USER_ENTERED', data: updates }) } ); const data = await r.json(); if (data.error) throw new Error('Ship batch update: ' + data.error.message); return data; } // ── Tracking normalization ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── // USPS labels include a ZIP prefix: 420 + 5-digit ZIP + 22-digit tracking = 30 digits // Strip the prefix so only the real tracking number is stored. function normalizeShipTracking(raw) { if (!raw) return ''; const t = raw.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, ''); if (/^420\d{5}\d{22}$/.test(t)) return t.slice(8); if (/^420\d{5}\d{21}$/.test(t)) return t.slice(8); return t; } // ── Carrier detection ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function detectShipCarrier(tracking) { if (!tracking) return 'Unknown'; const t = normalizeShipTracking(tracking); if (/^1Z/i.test(t)) return 'UPS'; if (/^(94|93|92|95|9[0-9])\d{18,}$/.test(t)) return 'USPS'; if (/^\d{22}$/.test(t)) return 'USPS'; if (/^[A-Z]{2}\d{9}[A-Z]{2}$/.test(t)) return 'USPS'; return 'Unknown'; } // ── Date key ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function shipTodayKey() { const d = new Date(); return d.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0'); } // Google Sheets auto-converts YYYY-MM-DD strings to date values, returning // them as M/D/YYYY on read. This normalizes both formats to YYYY-MM-DD. function normalizeShipDate(raw) { if (!raw) return ''; const s = String(raw).trim(); if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s)) return s; const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/); if (m) return m[3] + '-' + m[1].padStart(2,'0') + '-' + m[2].padStart(2,'0'); return s; } // ── Route dispatcher ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ── POST /ship/print-log ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── const PRINT_LOG_TAB = 'Print_Log'; async function shipPrintLog(request, env) { try { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { tracking } = b; if (!tracking) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'tracking required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const clean = normalizeShipTracking(tracking); const isUPS = /^1Z/i.test(clean); const isUSPS = /^(94|93|92|95|9[0-9])\d{18,}$/.test(clean) || /^\d{22}$/.test(clean) || /^[A-Z]{2}\d{9}[A-Z]{2}$/.test(clean); if (!isUPS && !isUSPS) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, rejected: true, reason: 'not a tracking number' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const carrier = detectShipCarrier(clean); const date = shipTodayKey(); const ts = new Date().toISOString(); try { const check = await shipSheetGet(env, PRINT_LOG_TAB + '!A1:A1'); if (!check.values || !check.values[0] || !check.values[0][0]) { await shipSheetAppend(env, PRINT_LOG_TAB + '!A:E', [['Date','Timestamp','Tracking','Carrier','Source']]); } } catch(e) { const token = await getToken(env); await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests: [{ addSheet: { properties: { title: PRINT_LOG_TAB } } }] }) }); await shipSheetAppend(env, PRINT_LOG_TAB + '!A:E', [['Date','Timestamp','Tracking','Carrier','Source']]); } await shipSheetAppend(env, PRINT_LOG_TAB + '!A:E', [[date, ts, clean, carrier, 'auto']]); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, tracking: clean, carrier, date }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── GET /ship/print-log?date=YYYY-MM-DD ────────────────────────────────────── async function shipGetPrintLog(url, env) { try { const date = url.searchParams.get('date') || shipTodayKey(); const data = await shipSheetGet(env, PRINT_LOG_TAB + '!A2:E20000').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const rows = (data.values || []).filter(r => normalizeShipDate(r[0]) === date); const scans = rows.map(r => ({ date: r[0]||'', timestamp: r[1]||'', tracking: r[2]||'', carrier: r[3]||'', source: r[4]||'auto' })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, scans, count: scans.length, date }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── POST /ship/archive-logs ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function shipArchiveLogs(env) { try { const cutoff = new Date(); cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - 45); const cutoffKey = cutoff.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + String(cutoff.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0') + '-' + String(cutoff.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0'); async function archiveTab(sourceTab, archiveTab, colRange) { const data = await shipSheetGet(env, `${sourceTab}!A2:${colRange}50000`).catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const rows = data.values || []; const old = rows.filter(r => normalizeShipDate(r[0]) < cutoffKey && normalizeShipDate(r[0]) !== ''); if (!old.length) return 0; try { await shipSheetGet(env, `${archiveTab}!A1:A1`); } catch(e) { const token = await getToken(env); await fetch(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests: [{ addSheet: { properties: { title: archiveTab } } }] }) }); } await shipSheetAppend(env, `${archiveTab}!A:${colRange}`, old); return old.length; } const [scanMoved, manifestMoved] = await Promise.all([ archiveTab('Scan_Log', 'Scan_Archive', 'I'), archiveTab('Manifest_Log', 'Manifest_Archive', 'I') ]); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, scanMoved, manifestMoved, cutoff: cutoffKey }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── GET /ship/order-lookup?q= ───────────────────────────────────────────────── async function shipOrderLookup(url, env) { const raw = (url.searchParams.get('q') || '').trim(); const daysParam = url.searchParams.get('days') || '7'; if (!raw || raw.length < 2) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'q required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const data = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Manifest_Log!A2:O20000'); const rows = data.values || []; const qUpper = raw.toUpperCase(); const qStripped = qUpper.replace(/[\s-]/g, ''); // strip spaces AND dashes for tracking comparison const qLower = raw.toLowerCase(); const todayStr = shipTodayKey(); const todayOnly = daysParam === 'today'; let cutoff; if (todayOnly) { cutoff = todayStr; } else { const days = parseInt(daysParam) || 7; cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - days * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10); } const results = []; let skippedByDate = 0; let sampleDate = rows.length ? (rows[0][0] || '') : ''; let sampleNormalized = sampleDate ? normalizeShipDate(sampleDate) : ''; for (const r of rows) { const rowDate = normalizeShipDate(r[0] || ''); if (rowDate) { if (todayOnly ? rowDate !== todayStr : rowDate < cutoff) { skippedByDate++; continue; } } // BUGFIX: previously a fragile regex classified the query as EITHER a // tracking number OR a name/order search, never both — a tracking // number containing a dash, or certain name/order text, could get // misclassified and search the wrong column entirely, finding nothing // even though the row existed. Now checks tracking, name, AND order // number every time, regardless of what the query looks like. const rowTracking = (r[1]||'').trim().toUpperCase().replace(/[\s-]/g, ''); const name = (r[9]||'').toLowerCase(); const orderNum = (r[2]||'').toLowerCase(); const match = (rowTracking.length > 0 && (rowTracking === qStripped || rowTracking.includes(qStripped))) || (name.length > 0 && name.includes(qLower)) || (orderNum.length > 0 && orderNum.includes(qLower)); if (!match) continue; let lineItems = []; try { lineItems = JSON.parse(r[12] || '[]'); } catch(_) {} results.push({ date: r[0]||'', tracking: r[1]||'', orderNum: r[2]||'', channel: r[3]||'', carrier: r[4]||'', service: r[5]||'', shipToState: r[6]||'', reconcileStatus: r[8]||'', customerName: r[9]||'', city: r[10]||'', zip: r[11]||'', lineItems, address1: r[13]||'', address2: r[14]||'' }); } const debugInfo = { totalRows: rows.length, skippedByDate, cutoff: todayOnly ? todayStr : cutoff, sampleDate: sampleDate + (sampleNormalized && sampleNormalized !== sampleDate ? ' (normalized: ' + sampleNormalized + ')' : ''), }; results.sort((a,b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date)); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, q: raw, results: results.slice(0,50), debug: debugInfo }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } async function handleShipRoute(url, method, request, env, session) { const path = url.pathname; try { if (path === '/ship/scan' && method === 'POST') return await shipScan(request, env); if (path === '/ship/scans' && method === 'GET') return await shipGetScans(url, env); if (path === '/ship/batch-close' && method === 'POST') return await shipBatchClose(request, env); if (path === '/ship/lookup' && method === 'GET') return await shipLookup(url, env); if (path === '/ship/order-lookup' && method === 'GET') return await shipOrderLookup(url, env); if (path === '/ship/manifest' && method === 'GET') return await shipGetManifest(url, env); if (path === '/ship/status-upload'&& method === 'POST') return await shipStatusUpload(request, env); if (path === '/ship/print-log' && method === 'POST') return await shipPrintLog(request, env); if (path === '/ship/print-log' && method === 'GET') return await shipGetPrintLog(url, env); if (path === '/ship/archive-logs' && method === 'POST') return await shipArchiveLogs(env); if (path === '/ship/manifest' && method === 'POST') { return await shipUploadManifest(request, env); } if (path === '/ship/reconcile' && method === 'POST') { return await shipReconcile(request, env); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Ship route not found' }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── POST /ship/scan ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function shipScan(request, env) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { tracking, initials } = b; if (!tracking) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'tracking required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); if (!initials) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'initials required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const date = shipTodayKey(); const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const clean = normalizeShipTracking(tracking); const carrier = detectShipCarrier(clean); // Duplicate check const existing = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Scan_Log!A2:I50000'); const dupe = (existing.values || []).find(r => normalizeShipDate(r[0]) === date && (r[2] || '').toUpperCase() === clean); if (dupe) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, duplicate: true, message: 'Already scanned at ' + (dupe[1] || 'unknown time'), scannedAt: dupe[1] || '', carrier: dupe[3] || carrier, initials: dupe[4] || '', }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Cols: Date | Timestamp | Tracking | Carrier | Initials | BatchClosedAt | Status | ShipmentID | OrderNum // Write the scan row immediately — ShipmentID + OrderNum filled in async below await shipSheetAppend(env, 'Scan_Log!A:I', [[date, ts, clean, carrier, initials.toUpperCase(), '', 'Scanned', '', '']]); // Look up ShipmentID + OrderNum from Veeqo inline (1 API call ~200ms) // Done synchronously so ctx.waitUntil isn't needed and data is always written if (env.VEEQO_API_KEY) { try { const qs = `?query=${encodeURIComponent(clean)}&status=shipped&page_size=3&page=1`; const key = (env.VEEQO_API_KEY || '').trim(); const vRes = await fetch('https://api.veeqo.com/orders' + qs, { headers: { 'x-api-key': key, 'Accept': 'application/json' } }); if (vRes.ok) { const orders = await vRes.json().catch(() => []); if (Array.isArray(orders) && orders.length > 0) { // Find exact tracking match let matched = null; for (const o of orders) { for (const alloc of (o.allocations || [])) { const s = alloc.shipment; if (s && s.tracking_number) { const tn = typeof s.tracking_number === 'object' ? String(s.tracking_number.tracking_number || '') : String(s.tracking_number || ''); if (tn.trim().toUpperCase() === clean) { matched = { order: o, shipment: s }; break; } } } if (matched) break; } if (matched) { const shipmentId = String(matched.shipment.id || ''); const orderNum = String(matched.order.number || matched.order.id || ''); const orderChannel = (matched.order.channel && matched.order.channel.name) || ''; const orderService = ((matched.order.allocations || [])[0]?.delivery_method?.name || ''); const orderState = (matched.order.deliver_to?.state || ''); // Write ShipmentID + OrderNum to the scan row we just appended // The row is the last one for today with this tracking and no ShipmentID const scanRows = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Scan_Log!A2:I50000'); const sRows = scanRows.values || []; for (let i = sRows.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { if (sRows[i][0] === date && (sRows[i][2] || '').toUpperCase() === clean && !sRows[i][7]) { await shipSheetBatchUpdate(env, [ { range: `Scan_Log!H${i + 2}`, values: [[shipmentId]] }, { range: `Scan_Log!I${i + 2}`, values: [[orderNum]] }, ]); break; } } // Upsert into Manifest_Log — add row or mark existing as Scanned const orderAddr = veeqoExtractAddress(matched.order); await manifestUpsertScanned(env, date, clean, { orderNum, channel: orderChannel, carrier, service: orderService, shipToState: orderState, customerName: veeqoExtractCustomerName(matched.order), city: orderAddr.city, zip: orderAddr.zip, address1: orderAddr.address1, address2: orderAddr.address2, lineItems: await veeqoExtractLineItems(env, matched.order), }); } } } } catch (_) { /* non-critical — scan still recorded */ } } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, date, tracking: clean, carrier, initials: initials.toUpperCase(), timestamp: ts, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── GET /ship/scans?date= ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function shipGetScans(url, env) { const date = url.searchParams.get('date') || shipTodayKey(); const data = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Scan_Log!A2:I50000'); const rows = (data.values || []) .filter(r => normalizeShipDate(r[0]) === date) .map(r => ({ date: r[0]||'', timestamp: r[1]||'', tracking: r[2]||'', carrier: r[3]||'', initials: r[4]||'', batchClosedAt: r[5]||'', status: r[6]||'', shipmentId: r[7]||'', orderNum: r[8]||'', })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ date, scans: rows, count: rows.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── POST /ship/batch-close ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function shipBatchClose(request, env) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { carrier, initials, date: reqDate } = b; const date = reqDate || shipTodayKey(); const ts = new Date().toISOString(); if (!carrier) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'carrier required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const data = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Scan_Log!A2:I50000'); const rows = data.values || []; const updates = []; rows.forEach((r, i) => { if (normalizeShipDate(r[0]) === date && r[3] === carrier && !r[5]) { updates.push({ range: `Scan_Log!F${i + 2}`, values: [[ts]] }); } }); if (updates.length > 0) await shipSheetBatchUpdate(env, updates); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, carrier, date, closedAt: ts, updated: updates.length, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── POST /ship/manifest ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function shipUploadManifest(request, env) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { rows, uploadedBy, date: reqDate } = b; const date = reqDate || shipTodayKey(); if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'rows array required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Read existing rows — if today's rows exist, delete them first (overwrite behavior) const existing = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Manifest_Log!A2:I20000'); const existingAll = existing.values || []; const todayIndexes = existingAll .map((r, i) => (normalizeShipDate(r[0]) === date ? i + 2 : null)) // +2 = 1-based, skip header row .filter(n => n !== null); if (todayIndexes.length > 0) { // Delete today's rows by clearing them, then compact the sheet // Simplest reliable approach: read all rows, filter out today's, rewrite whole sheet const token = await getToken(env); // Clear entire Manifest_Log data range await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent('Manifest_Log!A2:I20000')}:clear`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } } ); // Rewrite all rows EXCEPT today's date const keepRows = existingAll.filter(r => normalizeShipDate(r[0]) !== date); if (keepRows.length > 0) { await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent('Manifest_Log!A2')}?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: keepRows }) } ); } } // Cols: Date | Tracking | OrderNum | Channel | Carrier | Service | ShipToState | UploadedBy | ReconcileStatus const sheetRows = rows.map(r => [ date, (r.tracking || '').trim().toUpperCase(), (r.orderNum || '').trim(), (r.channel || '').trim(), detectShipCarrier(r.tracking), (r.service || '').trim(), (r.shipToState || '').trim(), (uploadedBy || '').trim().toUpperCase(), 'Pending', ]); await shipSheetAppend(env, 'Manifest_Log!A:I', sheetRows); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, date, uploaded: sheetRows.length, replaced: todayIndexes.length > 0 }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── GET /ship/manifest?date= ────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function shipGetManifest(url, env) { const date = url.searchParams.get('date') || shipTodayKey(); const data = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Manifest_Log!A2:I20000'); const rows = (data.values || []) .filter(r => normalizeShipDate(r[0]) === date) .map(r => ({ date: r[0]||'', tracking: r[1]||'', orderNum: r[2]||'', channel: r[3]||'', carrier: r[4]||'', service: r[5]||'', shipToState: r[6]||'', uploadedBy: r[7]||'', reconcileStatus: r[8]||'', })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ date, manifest: rows, count: rows.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── POST /ship/reconcile ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function shipReconcile(request, env) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const date = b.date || shipTodayKey(); const [scanData, manifestData] = await Promise.all([ shipSheetGet(env, 'Scan_Log!A2:I50000'), shipSheetGet(env, 'Manifest_Log!A2:I20000'), ]); const scanRows = scanData.values || []; const manifestRows = manifestData.values || []; const scannedSet = new Set( scanRows.filter(r => normalizeShipDate(r[0]) === date && r[2]).map(r => r[2].trim().toUpperCase()) ); const results = { scanned: 0, gap: 0, total: 0 }; const updates = []; manifestRows.forEach((r, i) => { if (normalizeShipDate(r[0]) !== date) return; results.total++; const tracking = (r[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const isScanned = scannedSet.has(tracking); const newStatus = isScanned ? 'Scanned' : 'Gap'; if (isScanned) results.scanned++; else results.gap++; if ((r[8] || '') !== newStatus) { updates.push({ range: `Manifest_Log!I${i + 2}`, values: [[newStatus]] }); } }); if (updates.length > 0) await shipSheetBatchUpdate(env, updates); const gapList = manifestRows .filter(r => normalizeShipDate(r[0]) === date && !scannedSet.has((r[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase())) .map(r => ({ tracking: (r[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase(), orderNum: r[2] || '', channel: r[3] || '', carrier: r[4] || '', service: r[5] || '', shipToState: r[6] || '', })); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, date, results, gaps: gapList, updated: updates.length, }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── GET /ship/lookup?tracking= ──────────────────────────────────────────────── async function shipLookup(url, env) { const tracking = (url.searchParams.get('tracking') || '').trim().toUpperCase(); if (!tracking) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'tracking required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); const [scanData, manifestData] = await Promise.all([ shipSheetGet(env, 'Scan_Log!A2:I50000'), shipSheetGet(env, 'Manifest_Log!A2:I20000'), ]); const sm = (scanData.values || []).find(r => (r[2] || '').trim().toUpperCase() === tracking); const mm = (manifestData.values || []).find(r => (r[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase() === tracking); const scan = sm ? { date:sm[0]||'', timestamp:sm[1]||'', tracking:sm[2]||'', carrier:sm[3]||'', initials:sm[4]||'', batchClosedAt:sm[5]||'', status:sm[6]||'' } : null; const manifest = mm ? { date:mm[0]||'', tracking:mm[1]||'', orderNum:mm[2]||'', channel:mm[3]||'', carrier:mm[4]||'', service:mm[5]||'', shipToState:mm[6]||'', uploadedBy:mm[7]||'', reconcileStatus:mm[8]||'' } : null; const verdict = scan && manifest ? 'scanned_in_manifest' : scan ? 'scanned_not_in_manifest' : manifest ? 'gap' : 'not_found'; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ tracking, verdict, scan, manifest }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── POST /ship/status-upload ────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Replaces entire Tracking_Status tab with new rows from the app. // Body: { rows: [[header...], [data...], ...] } // Any valid session — mgmt signs in for this tab anyway. async function shipStatusUpload(request, env) { try { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { rows } = b; if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length < 2) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'rows array with header + data required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const token = await getToken(env); // 1. Clear the tab first await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent('Tracking_Status')}:clear`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } } ); // 2. Write all rows starting at A1 const writeRes = await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent('Tracking_Status!A1')}?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: rows }) } ); const writeData = await writeRes.json(); if (writeData.error) throw new Error('Sheet write: ' + writeData.error.message); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, rowsWritten: rows.length - 1 }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // WAREHOUSE MESSENGER — /msg/* // Sheet: MSG_SHEET_ID env var // Tab: Messages (cols: A=ID, B=Timestamp, C=Initials, D=Category, // E=Priority, F=Body, G=Status, H=Reply, I=ReplyAt) // Routes: // GET /msg/list — list messages (query: initials= for own; all=1 for mgmt) // POST /msg/send — send a new message (any session or no session) // POST /msg/reply — mgmt reply (mgmt session required) // POST /msg/status — mgmt set status (mgmt session required) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function handleMsgRoute(url, method, request, env, session) { const path = url.pathname; try { if (path === '/msg/send' && method === 'POST') return await msgSend(request, env); if (path === '/msg/list' && method === 'GET') return await msgList(url, env, session); if (path === '/msg/reply' && method === 'POST') return await msgReply(request, env, session); if (path === '/msg/status' && method === 'POST') return await msgSetStatus(request, env, session); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Msg route not found' }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── Sheet helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function msgSheetGet(env, range) { const token = await getToken(env); const r = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.MSG_SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }); const data = await r.json(); if (data.error) throw new Error('Msg sheet read: ' + data.error.message); return data; } async function msgSheetAppend(env, range, values) { const token = await getToken(env); const r = await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.MSG_SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:append?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values }) } ); const data = await r.json(); if (data.error) throw new Error('Msg sheet append: ' + data.error.message); return data; } async function msgSheetBatchUpdate(env, updates) { const token = await getToken(env); const r = await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.MSG_SHEET_ID}/values:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ valueInputOption: 'USER_ENTERED', data: updates }) } ); const data = await r.json(); if (data.error) throw new Error('Msg batch update: ' + data.error.message); return data; } // ── Ensure header row exists ────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function msgEnsureSheet(env) { // Try reading the header row. If the tab doesn't exist the Sheets API returns // an error like "Unable to parse range: Messages!A1:I1" — we catch that and // create the tab first, then write the header row. let headerExists = false; try { const check = await msgSheetGet(env, 'Messages!A1:I1'); const row = (check.values || [])[0] || []; if (row[0] === 'ID') headerExists = true; } catch(e) { // Tab missing — create it const token = await getToken(env); await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.MSG_SHEET_ID}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests: [{ addSheet: { properties: { title: 'Messages' } } }] }) }); // Ignore response — tab may already exist from a prior partial run } if (!headerExists) { await msgSheetAppend(env, 'Messages!A1', [ ['ID', 'Timestamp', 'Initials', 'Category', 'Priority', 'Body', 'Status', 'Reply', 'ReplyAt'] ]); } } // ── Row parser ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function msgParseRow(row, rowIndex) { if (!row || row.length < 2) return null; return { id: row[0] || '', timestamp: row[1] || '', initials: row[2] || '', category: row[3] || '', priority: row[4] || 'Normal', body: row[5] || '', status: row[6] || 'open', reply: row[7] || '', replyAt: row[8] || '', _row: rowIndex // 1-based sheet row (data starts at row 2) }; } // ── POST /msg/send ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Body: { initials, category, priority, body } // No auth required — any warehouse worker can send async function msgSend(request, env) { const body = await request.json(); const { initials, category, priority, body: msgBody } = body; if (!initials || !category || !msgBody) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'initials, category and body are required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const allowed_cats = ['Resources', 'Materials', 'Alert', 'Question', 'Safety', 'Other']; const allowed_pri = ['Normal', 'Urgent', 'Critical']; if (!allowed_cats.includes(category)) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid category' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } await msgEnsureSheet(env); const id = 'MSG-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7).toUpperCase(); const timestamp = new Date().toISOString(); const pri = allowed_pri.includes(priority) ? priority : 'Normal'; await msgSheetAppend(env, 'Messages!A:I', [[ id, timestamp, String(initials).toUpperCase().slice(0, 4), category, pri, String(msgBody).slice(0, 500), 'open', '', '' ]]); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, id }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── GET /msg/list ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ?initials=JD — returns only messages from that person (any session) // ?all=1 — returns all messages (any session — mgmt reads via X-Session but // badge poll also works unauthenticated; filter sensitive on frontend) // ?limit=N — max rows to return (default 200) async function msgList(url, env, session) { await msgEnsureSheet(env); const initials = (url.searchParams.get('initials') || '').toUpperCase().trim(); const all = url.searchParams.get('all') === '1'; const limit = Math.min(parseInt(url.searchParams.get('limit') || '200', 10), 500); const data = await msgSheetGet(env, 'Messages!A2:I1001'); const rows = data.values || []; let messages = rows .map((r, i) => msgParseRow(r, i + 2)) .filter(Boolean) .filter(m => m.id && m.body); // skip blank rows if (initials && !all) { messages = messages.filter(m => m.initials === initials); } // Sort newest first, cap at limit messages = messages.reverse().slice(0, limit); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, messages }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── POST /msg/reply ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Body: { id, reply } // Requires mgmt session async function msgReply(request, env, session) { if (!session || session.pin_level !== 'mgmt') { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Management access required' }), { status: 403, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const body = await request.json(); const { id, reply } = body; if (!id || !reply) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'id and reply required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const rowIndex = await msgFindRowById(env, id); if (!rowIndex) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Message not found' }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const replyAt = new Date().toISOString(); await msgSheetBatchUpdate(env, [ { range: `Messages!G${rowIndex}`, values: [['replied']] }, { range: `Messages!H${rowIndex}`, values: [[String(reply).slice(0, 400)]] }, { range: `Messages!I${rowIndex}`, values: [[replyAt]] } ]); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── POST /msg/status ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Body: { id, status } status: 'open' | 'archived' // Requires mgmt session async function msgSetStatus(request, env, session) { if (!session || session.pin_level !== 'mgmt') { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Management access required' }), { status: 403, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const body = await request.json(); const { id, status } = body; const allowed = ['open', 'archived', 'replied']; if (!id || !allowed.includes(status)) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'id and valid status required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const rowIndex = await msgFindRowById(env, id); if (!rowIndex) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Message not found' }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } await msgSheetBatchUpdate(env, [ { range: `Messages!G${rowIndex}`, values: [[status]] } ]); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── Find row number by message ID ───────────────────────────────────────────── async function msgFindRowById(env, id) { const data = await msgSheetGet(env, 'Messages!A2:A1001'); const rows = data.values || []; for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { if ((rows[i][0] || '') === id) return i + 2; // +2: 1-based + header row } return null; } // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // EBAY AI MESSAGES — v15 addition // Routes: GET /ebay-msg/list — fetch unanswered eBay buyer messages // POST /ebay-msg/ai-draft — generate AI draft replies for a batch // POST /ebay-msg/send — send a reply via Trading API AddMemberMessageRTQ // or AddMemberMessageAAQToPartner // GET /ebay-msg/log — fetch reply log from eBay_MsgLog sheet // All routes require mgmt session (enforced in router above). // eBay token: reuses getEbayToken(env) — same OAuth refresh token as repricer. // Anthropic: uses ANTHROPIC_KEY env var, model claude-haiku-4-5-20251001. // Google Sheet log: stored in repricer sheet (SHEET_ID_REPRICER) tab eBay_MsgLog // cols: A=ID, B=Timestamp, C=MessageID, D=ItemID, E=BuyerID, F=Subject, // G=Question (truncated), H=AIDraft, I=FinalReply, J=Status, K=SentAt // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── Route dispatcher ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function handleEbayMsgRoute(url, method, request, env) { const path = url.pathname; try { if (path === '/ebay-msg/list' && method === 'GET') return await ebayMsgList(url, env); if (path === '/ebay-msg/ai-draft' && method === 'POST') return await ebayMsgAiDraft(request, env); if (path === '/ebay-msg/send' && method === 'POST') return await ebayMsgSend(request, env); if (path === '/ebay-msg/log' && method === 'GET') return await ebayMsgLog(url, env); if (path === '/ebay-msg/analytics' && method === 'GET') return await ebayMsgAnalytics(url, env); if (path === '/ebay-msg/threads' && method === 'GET') return await ebayThreadsGet(url, env); if (path === '/ebay-msg/threads/save' && method === 'POST') return await ebayThreadsSave(request, env); if (path === '/ebay-msg/cleanup' && method === 'POST') return await ebayThreadsCleanup(env); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'eBay message route not found' }), { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } } // ── GET /ebay-msg/list ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Fetches ALL buyer messages by running two Trading API calls in parallel: // 1. GetMemberMessages — pre-sale listing questions (AskSellerQuestion) // 2. GetMyMessages — full inbox including post-purchase order messages // Results are merged, deduplicated by messageId, and sorted newest first. // Each message includes replyType: 'RTQ' (pre-sale) | 'AAQ' (post-order) // so the send route knows which eBay API call to use. // ?days=N — look back N days (default 7, max 30) // ?status=Unanswered|All — filter (default Unanswered) async function ebayMsgList(url, env) { const days = Math.min(parseInt(url.searchParams.get('days') || '7'), 30); const status = url.searchParams.get('status') === 'All' ? 'All' : 'Unanswered'; const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); const now = new Date(); const start = new Date(now.getTime() - days * 86400000); function toEbayDate(d) { // eBay Trading API requires ISO 8601 UTC: 2026-03-19T20:00:00.000Z return d.toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, '.000Z'); } // ── 1. GetMemberMessages — pre-sale listing questions ────────────────────── // eBay only accepts MessageStatus = 'Unanswered' or 'Answered' (not 'All'). // When status='All', we run both in parallel and merge. async function fetchMemberMessages() { async function fetchWithStatus(msgStatus) { const xmlBody = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?> <GetMemberMessagesRequest xmlns="urn:ebay:apis:eBLBaseComponents"> <RequesterCredentials><eBayAuthToken>${ebayToken}</eBayAuthToken></RequesterCredentials> <MailMessageType>AskSellerQuestion</MailMessageType> <MessageStatus>${msgStatus}</MessageStatus> <StartCreationTime>${toEbayDate(start)}</StartCreationTime> <EndCreationTime>${toEbayDate(now)}</EndCreationTime> <Pagination><EntriesPerPage>100</EntriesPerPage><PageNumber>1</PageNumber></Pagination> </GetMemberMessagesRequest>`; const r = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME': 'GetMemberMessages', 'X-EBAY-API-SITEID': '0', 'X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL': '967', 'X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN': ebayToken, 'Content-Type': 'text/xml', }, body: xmlBody }); const xml = await r.text(); if (xml.includes('<Ack>Failure</Ack>')) { const errMsg = parseXmlError(xml); const longMsg = xml.match(/<LongMessage>([^<]+)<\/LongMessage>/)?.[1] || ''; return { messages: [], error: errMsg + (longMsg ? ' | ' + longMsg : '') }; } const exchanges = xml.match(/<MemberMessageExchange>([\s\S]*?)<\/MemberMessageExchange>/g) || []; const messages = []; for (const ex of exchanges) { const m = parseMemberMessageBlock(ex); if (m) { m.replyType = 'RTQ'; m.source = 'listing'; messages.push(m); } } return { messages }; } if (status === 'All') { // Run both Unanswered + Answered in parallel, merge const [unans, ans] = await Promise.all([ fetchWithStatus('Unanswered').catch(e => ({ messages: [], error: e.message })), fetchWithStatus('Answered').catch(e => ({ messages: [], error: e.message })) ]); const combined = [...unans.messages, ...ans.messages]; const error = unans.error || ans.error || undefined; return { messages: combined, error }; } else { return fetchWithStatus('Unanswered'); } } // ── 2. GetMyMessages — full inbox including post-purchase order messages ─── // eBay requires two-step approach: // Step A: ReturnSummary to get unread count (optional but good practice) // Step B: ReturnHeaders with date range to get MessageIDs // Step C: ReturnMessages with explicit MessageIDs to get bodies // This avoids the "Missing required parameter" error from passing date range with ReturnMessages. async function fetchMyMessages() { // Step 1: Get message headers (IDs) for the date range const headersXml = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?> <GetMyMessagesRequest xmlns="urn:ebay:apis:eBLBaseComponents"> <RequesterCredentials><eBayAuthToken>${ebayToken}</eBayAuthToken></RequesterCredentials> <DetailLevel>ReturnHeaders</DetailLevel> <StartTime>${toEbayDate(start)}</StartTime> <EndTime>${toEbayDate(now)}</EndTime> <Pagination><EntriesPerPage>100</EntriesPerPage><PageNumber>1</PageNumber></Pagination> </GetMyMessagesRequest>`; const rh = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME': 'GetMyMessages', 'X-EBAY-API-SITEID': '0', 'X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL': '967', 'X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN': ebayToken, 'Content-Type': 'text/xml', }, body: headersXml }); const headXml = await rh.text(); if (headXml.includes('<Ack>Failure</Ack>')) return { messages: [], error: parseXmlError(headXml) }; // Extract MessageIDs from headers response const allMsgIds = (headXml.match(/<MessageID>([^<]+)<\/MessageID>/g) || []) .map(m => m.replace(/<\/?MessageID>/g, '').trim()) .filter(Boolean) .slice(0, 50); // cap at 50 to avoid oversized request // Surface how many IDs we found for diagnostics if (!allMsgIds.length) { // Try to get a snippet of what eBay returned so we can debug const summaryMatch = headXml.match(/<Summary>([\s\S]*?)<\/Summary>/); const totalCount = summaryMatch ? (summaryMatch[0].match(/<TotalMessageCount>(\d+)<\/TotalMessageCount>/) || [])[1] : null; const debugNote = totalCount !== null ? 'eBay reports ' + totalCount + ' total messages but 0 IDs in date range' : 'ReturnHeaders returned 0 MessageIDs'; return { messages: [], error: debugNote }; } // Step 2: Fetch full message bodies by explicit MessageIDs const msgIdTags = allMsgIds.map(id => ` <MessageIDs><MessageID>${escXml(id)}</MessageID></MessageIDs>`).join('\n'); const bodiesXml = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?> <GetMyMessagesRequest xmlns="urn:ebay:apis:eBLBaseComponents"> <RequesterCredentials><eBayAuthToken>${ebayToken}</eBayAuthToken></RequesterCredentials> <DetailLevel>ReturnMessages</DetailLevel> ${msgIdTags} </GetMyMessagesRequest>`; const rb = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME': 'GetMyMessages', 'X-EBAY-API-SITEID': '0', 'X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL': '967', 'X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN': ebayToken, 'Content-Type': 'text/xml', }, body: bodiesXml }); const bodyXml = await rb.text(); if (bodyXml.includes('<Ack>Failure</Ack>')) return { messages: [], error: parseXmlError(bodyXml) }; // Parse <Message> blocks from bodies response const msgBlocks = bodyXml.match(/<Message>([\s\S]*?)<\/Message>/g) || []; const messages = []; for (const blk of msgBlocks) { const m = parseMyMessageBlock(blk); if (!m) continue; // Skip eBay system/automated messages — only real buyer messages const sender = (m.senderId || '').toLowerCase(); if (sender === 'ebay' || sender.startsWith('cs-')) continue; if (!m.body && !m.subject) continue; // skip truly empty // Filter unread only if status=Unanswered if (status === 'Unanswered' && m.read === 'true') continue; m.replyType = 'AAQ'; m.source = 'inbox'; messages.push(m); } return { messages }; } // ── Run both in parallel ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── // NOTE: GetMyMessages (inbox) temporarily disabled pending OAuth refresh token fix // Only listing questions (GetMemberMessages) active until token is resolved const [memberResult, myMsgResult] = await Promise.all([ fetchMemberMessages().catch(e => ({ messages: [], error: e.message })), Promise.resolve({ messages: [], error: null }) // inbox disabled temporarily ]); // Merge and deduplicate by messageId const seen = new Set(); const combined = []; for (const m of [...memberResult.messages, ...myMsgResult.messages]) { const key = m.messageId || (m.senderId + '|' + m.body.slice(0, 40)); if (seen.has(key)) continue; seen.add(key); combined.push(m); } // Sort newest first combined.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.createdAt) - new Date(a.createdAt)); const errors = []; if (memberResult.error) errors.push('GetMemberMessages: ' + memberResult.error); if (myMsgResult.error) errors.push('GetMyMessages: ' + myMsgResult.error); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, messages: combined, count: combined.length, days, status, sourceBreakdown: { listing: memberResult.messages.length, inbox: myMsgResult.messages.length }, warnings: errors.length ? errors : undefined, debug: { memberError: memberResult.error || null, inboxError: myMsgResult.error || null, } }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── XML parsing helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── function parseXmlError(xml) { const m = xml.match(/<ShortMessage>([^<]+)<\/ShortMessage>/); return m ? m[1] : 'eBay API error'; } function parseMemberMessageBlock(ex) { const msgIdMatch = ex.match(/<MessageID>([^<]+)<\/MessageID>/); if (!msgIdMatch) return null; const itemIdMatch = ex.match(/<ItemID>([^<]+)<\/ItemID>/); const titleMatch = ex.match(/<Title>([^<]+)<\/Title>/); const senderMatch = ex.match(/<SenderID>([^<]+)<\/SenderID>/); const bodyMatch = ex.match(/<Body>([\s\S]*?)<\/Body>/); const subjectMatch= ex.match(/<Subject>([^<]+)<\/Subject>/); const createdMatch= ex.match(/<CreationDate>([^<]+)<\/CreationDate>/); const statusMatch = ex.match(/<MessageStatus>([^<]+)<\/MessageStatus>/); const typeMatch = ex.match(/<MessageType>([^<]+)<\/MessageType>/); const priceMatch = ex.match(/<CurrentPrice[^>]*>([\d.]+)<\/CurrentPrice>/); return { messageId: (msgIdMatch[1] || '').trim(), itemId: (itemIdMatch?.[1] || '').trim(), title: (titleMatch?.[1] || '').trim(), senderId: (senderMatch?.[1] || '').trim(), body: (bodyMatch?.[1] || '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim(), subject: (subjectMatch?.[1]|| '').trim(), createdAt: (createdMatch?.[1]|| '').trim(), status: (statusMatch?.[1] || '').trim(), type: (typeMatch?.[1] || '').trim(), currentPrice: priceMatch ? parseFloat(priceMatch[1]) : null, read: 'false', }; } function parseMyMessageBlock(blk) { const msgIdMatch = blk.match(/<MessageID>([^<]+)<\/MessageID>/); if (!msgIdMatch) return null; const senderMatch = blk.match(/<Sender>([^<]+)<\/Sender>/); const subjectMatch = blk.match(/<Subject>([^<]+)<\/Subject>/); const textMatch = blk.match(/<Text>([\s\S]*?)<\/Text>/); const createdMatch = blk.match(/<ReceiveDate>([^<]+)<\/ReceiveDate>/); const readMatch = blk.match(/<Read>(true|false)<\/Read>/); const itemIdMatch = blk.match(/<ItemID>([^<]+)<\/ItemID>/); const extIdMatch = blk.match(/<ExternalMessageID>([^<]+)<\/ExternalMessageID>/); // Strip HTML from body text const rawBody = (textMatch?.[1] || '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').replace(/ /g,' ').trim(); return { messageId: (msgIdMatch[1] || '').trim(), externalMsgId: (extIdMatch?.[1] || '').trim(), // thread ID for AAQ replies itemId: (itemIdMatch?.[1]|| '').trim(), title: '', // GetMyMessages doesn't include listing title senderId: (senderMatch?.[1]|| '').trim(), body: rawBody, subject: (subjectMatch?.[1]|| '').trim(), createdAt: (createdMatch?.[1]|| '').trim(), status: (readMatch?.[1] === 'true') ? 'Answered' : 'Unanswered', type: 'OrderInquiry', currentPrice: null, read: (readMatch?.[1] || 'false'), }; } // ── POST /ebay-msg/ai-draft ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Body: { messages: [{ messageId, itemId, title, senderId, body, currentPrice }] } // Returns same array with aiDraft added to each item. // Calls Claude Haiku once per message (parallel, max 5 at a time). async function ebayMsgAiDraft(request, env) { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const msgs = Array.isArray(body.messages) ? body.messages.slice(0, 20) : []; const agentName = (body.agentName || '').trim(); // e.g. "Danny" if (!msgs.length) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'No messages provided' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Sign-off line — personalised if agentName provided const signOff = agentName ? `Best,\n${agentName} | XFITTING Team Support` : `Best,\nXFITTING Team Support`; async function draftOne(msg) { const prompt = `You are a customer support assistant for XFITTING, an eCommerce brand that sells PEX plumbing fittings and valves on eBay. Your job is to respond to customer messages in a clear, professional, and efficient way, matching the tone and style of XFITTING support. ------------------------- TONE & STYLE GUIDELINES ------------------------- - Always start with: "Hi," - Always end with EXACTLY this sign-off on its own line: "${signOff}" - Keep responses concise, direct, and helpful (no fluff) - Use simple, clear language (assume DIY-level understanding unless customer shows expertise) - Be polite but not overly apologetic - Be confident and solution-oriented - Avoid long paragraphs; keep it easy to read ------------------------- CORE PRINCIPLES ------------------------- 1. Always guide the customer toward the correct next step 2. Protect the business from unnecessary loss (do not offer refunds/replacements unless justified) 3. Keep everything documented through eBay (returns, refunds, etc.) 4. Never blame the customer or sound defensive 5. Assume good intent, but stay cautious of scams or abuse 6.
