If something is unclear, ask a short clarifying question ------------------------- PRODUCT KNOWLEDGE ------------------------- - XFITTING sells PEX B (crimp/barb), PEX A (expansion), Push Fit, CPVC, and brass fittings - Compatibility rules: - PEX B = crimp/barb system - PEX A = expansion system (NOT interchangeable with PEX B) - Push Fit = works with PEX, copper, CPVC (no tools required) - Never guess compatibility — if unsure, ask for pipe type/system clarification ------------------------- RETURNS & REFUNDS POLICY ------------------------- - Always direct customers to open a return through eBay - Do NOT offer off-platform refunds - Wrong item / defective: apologize briefly, instruct to open return under "wrong item received" or "defective" - Buyer remorse: still direct to eBay return process - Do NOT promise prepaid labels unless policy clearly requires it - Emphasize eBay keeps everything documented and protects both sides ------------------------- COMMON SCENARIOS ------------------------- 1. Return request: "Please open a return request through eBay by going to your order and selecting 'Return this item.'" 2. Wrong item: Acknowledge, direct to open return under "wrong item received", do NOT send replacement without process 3. Late delivery: Acknowledge delay, reference carrier (USPS etc.), no compensation unless necessary 4. Technical/compatibility question: Answer clearly if known; if unclear, ask what pipe/type/system they are using 5. Suspicious behavior: Stay neutral, do not accuse, stick strictly to eBay process ------------------------- WHAT NOT TO DO ------------------------- - Do NOT give long explanations unless necessary - Do NOT go off-topic - Do NOT offer partial refunds casually - Do NOT bypass eBay processes - Do NOT sound robotic - Do NOT include a subject line - Do NOT repeat the buyer's question back to them ------------------------- LISTING CONTEXT ------------------------- Listing: "${msg.title || 'PEX Fitting'}"${msg.currentPrice ? ` — Price: $${msg.currentPrice.toFixed(2)}` : ''} Item ID: ${msg.itemId || 'N/A'} Buyer message from ${msg.senderId || 'buyer'}: "${(msg.body || '').slice(0, 800)}" ${msg._note ? ` ------------------------- AGENT NOTE (important context — factor this into your reply): ${msg._note.slice(0, 300)} -------------------------` : ''} Now write the reply. Start with "Hi," and end with "${signOff}". Keep it short and professional. After your sign-off, on a new line add ONLY: [CONFIDENCE:X.XX] where X.XX is 0.00-1.00. 1.00=clear question/certain answer | 0.70=standard | 0.50=some ambiguity | 0.30=unclear/complex`; try { const aiResp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-api-key': env.ANTHROPIC_KEY || env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' }, body: JSON.stringify({ model: 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001', max_tokens: 300, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }] }) }); const aiData = await aiResp.json(); // Surface API-level errors (wrong key, model not found, quota, etc.) if (aiData.error) { return { ...msg, aiDraft: '', draftOk: false, draftError: aiData.error.message || JSON.stringify(aiData.error) }; } const draft = (aiData.content?.[0]?.text || '').trim(); if (!draft) { return { ...msg, aiDraft: '', draftOk: false, draftError: 'Empty AI response — raw: ' + JSON.stringify(aiData).slice(0, 300) }; } // Parse confidence tag from end of draft let confidence = 0.8; let cleanDraft = draft; const confMatch = draft.match(/\[CONFIDENCE:([\d.]+)\]\s*$/); if (confMatch) { confidence = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, parseFloat(confMatch[1]) || 0.8)); cleanDraft = draft.replace(/\[CONFIDENCE:[\d.]+\]\s*$/, '').trim(); } return { ...msg, aiDraft: cleanDraft, draftOk: true, confidence }; } catch(e) { return { ...msg, aiDraft: '', draftOk: false, draftError: e.message }; } } // Process in batches of 5 parallel const results = []; for (let i = 0; i < msgs.length; i += 5) { const batch = msgs.slice(i, i + 5); const done = await Promise.all(batch.map(draftOne)); results.push(...done); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, messages: results }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── POST /ebay-msg/send ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Body: { messageId, itemId, senderId, reply, replyType, subject, originalBody, externalMsgId } // replyType: 'RTQ' → AddMemberMessageRTQ (pre-sale listing questions) // 'AAQ' → AddMemberMessageAAQToPartner (post-purchase order messages) // Logs the sent reply to eBay_MsgLog sheet. async function ebayMsgSend(request, env) { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { messageId, itemId, senderId, reply, replyType, subject, originalBody, externalMsgId } = body; if (!messageId || !reply) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'messageId and reply are required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); const useAAQ = replyType === 'AAQ'; // post-purchase order messages let xmlBody, callName; if (useAAQ) { // AddMemberMessageAAQToPartner — for post-purchase order messages // Requires ItemID + ParentMessageID (the original message ID) callName = 'AddMemberMessageAAQToPartner'; xmlBody = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?> <AddMemberMessageAAQToPartnerRequest xmlns="urn:ebay:apis:eBLBaseComponents"> <RequesterCredentials><eBayAuthToken>${ebayToken}</eBayAuthToken></RequesterCredentials> <ItemID>${escXml(itemId || '')}</ItemID> <MemberMessage> <Body>${escXml(reply)}</Body> <ParentMessageID>${escXml(messageId)}</ParentMessageID> <RecipientID>${escXml(senderId || '')}</RecipientID> </MemberMessage> </AddMemberMessageAAQToPartnerRequest>`; } else { // AddMemberMessageRTQ — for pre-sale Ask Seller a Question messages callName = 'AddMemberMessageRTQ'; xmlBody = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?> <AddMemberMessageRTQRequest xmlns="urn:ebay:apis:eBLBaseComponents"> <RequesterCredentials><eBayAuthToken>${ebayToken}</eBayAuthToken></RequesterCredentials> <ItemID>${escXml(itemId || '')}</ItemID> <MemberMessage> <Body>${escXml(reply)}</Body> <DisplayToPublic>false</DisplayToPublic> <EmailCopyToSender>false</EmailCopyToSender> <ParentMessageID>${escXml(messageId)}</ParentMessageID> <RecipientID>${escXml(senderId || '')}</RecipientID> </MemberMessage> </AddMemberMessageRTQRequest>`; } const r = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME': callName, 'X-EBAY-API-SITEID': '0', 'X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL': '967', 'X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN': ebayToken, 'Content-Type': 'text/xml', }, body: xmlBody }); const xml = await r.text(); const ok = xml.includes('<Ack>Success</Ack>') || xml.includes('<Ack>Warning</Ack>'); const errM = xml.match(/<ShortMessage>([^<]+)<\/ShortMessage>/); const errMsg = errM ? errM[1] : ''; // Log to sheet regardless of success/failure const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const logId = 'EMSG-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5).toUpperCase(); await ebayMsgLogAppend(env, [[ logId, ts, messageId || '', itemId || '', senderId || '', subject || '', (originalBody || '').slice(0, 300), '', // AIDraft col (not stored here — set in ai-draft) reply.slice(0, 500), ok ? 'Sent' : 'Failed', ok ? ts : '', ok ? '' : errMsg ]]); if (!ok) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: errMsg || 'eBay API error', xml: xml.slice(0, 800) }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Save seller reply to thread history (fire-and-forget — don't block response) if (ok) { const threadId = externalMsgId || messageId || ''; ebayThreadsSave({ json: async () => ({ messages: [{ threadId, messageId: 'REPLY-' + logId, timestamp: ts, itemId: itemId || '', buyerId: senderId || '', subject: subject || '', direction: 'SELLER', body: reply, replyType: useAAQ ? 'AAQ' : 'RTQ', sentAt: ts, }]}) }, env).catch(() => {}); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, logId }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── GET /ebay-msg/log ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Returns last 100 log entries from eBay_MsgLog sheet. async function ebayMsgLog(url, env) { await ebayMsgEnsureSheet(env); const limit = Math.min(parseInt(url.searchParams.get('limit') || '100'), 500); const data = await rSheetGet(env, 'eBay_MsgLog!A2:L1001').catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const rows = (data.values || []).map(r => ({ id: r[0] || '', timestamp: r[1] || '', messageId: r[2] || '', itemId: r[3] || '', buyerId: r[4] || '', subject: r[5] || '', question: r[6] || '', aiDraft: r[7] || '', finalReply: r[8] || '', status: r[9] || '', sentAt: r[10] || '', error: r[11] || '', })).reverse().slice(0, limit); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, log: rows }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── Sheet helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function ebayMsgEnsureSheet(env) { try { const check = await rSheetGet(env, 'eBay_MsgLog!A1:L1'); const row = (check.values || [])[0] || []; if (row[0] === 'ID') return; // already exists } catch(e) { // Tab missing — create it const token = await getToken(env); const sid = repricerSheetId(env); await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${sid}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests: [{ addSheet: { properties: { title: 'eBay_MsgLog' } } }] }) }).catch(() => {}); } // Write header row const token = await getToken(env); const sid = repricerSheetId(env); await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${sid}/values/${encodeURIComponent('eBay_MsgLog!A1:L1')}?valueInputOption=RAW`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: [['ID','Timestamp','MessageID','ItemID','BuyerID','Subject','Question','AIDraft','FinalReply','Status','SentAt','Error']] }) }).catch(() => {}); } async function ebayMsgLogAppend(env, rows) { await ebayMsgEnsureSheet(env); const token = await getToken(env); const sid = repricerSheetId(env); const url = `${SHEETS_URL}/${sid}/values/${encodeURIComponent('eBay_MsgLog!A:L')}:append?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`; await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: rows }) }); } // ── XML escape helper (reuse if already defined, else define here) ──────────── function escXml(s) { return String(s || '') .replace(/&/g, '&') .replace(/</g, '<') .replace(/>/g, '>') .replace(/"/g, '"') .replace(/'/g, '''); } // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // EBAY THREAD HISTORY — v15 addition (thread storage + 30-day cleanup) // Sheet tab: eBay_Threads (auto-created in SHEET_ID_REPRICER) // Cols A–J: ThreadID, MessageID, Timestamp, ItemID, BuyerID, Subject, // Direction, Body, ReplyType, SentAt // Routes added: // GET /ebay-msg/threads?threadId=X — fetch thread history for one conversation // POST /ebay-msg/threads/save — save one or more messages to sheet // POST /ebay-msg/cleanup — delete rows older than 30 days (called silently) // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── Sheet name ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── const THREAD_TAB = 'eBay_Threads'; const THREAD_HEADERS = ['ThreadID','MessageID','Timestamp','ItemID','BuyerID','Subject','Direction','Body','ReplyType','SentAt']; // ── Ensure eBay_Threads tab exists with correct headers ────────────────────── async function ebayThreadsEnsureSheet(env) { try { const check = await rSheetGet(env, `${THREAD_TAB}!A1:J1`); const row = (check.values || [])[0] || []; if (row[0] === 'ThreadID') return; // already set up } catch(e) { // Tab missing — create it const token = await getToken(env); const sid = repricerSheetId(env); await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${sid}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests: [{ addSheet: { properties: { title: THREAD_TAB } } }] }) }).catch(() => {}); } // Write header row const token = await getToken(env); const sid = repricerSheetId(env); await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${sid}/values/${encodeURIComponent(THREAD_TAB + '!A1:J1')}?valueInputOption=RAW`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: [THREAD_HEADERS] }) }).catch(() => {}); } // ── Append rows to eBay_Threads ─────────────────────────────────────────────── async function ebayThreadsAppend(env, rows) { await ebayThreadsEnsureSheet(env); const token = await getToken(env); const sid = repricerSheetId(env); const url = `${SHEETS_URL}/${sid}/values/${encodeURIComponent(THREAD_TAB + '!A:J')}:append?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`; await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: rows }) }); } // ── GET /ebay-msg/threads?threadId=X ───────────────────────────────────────── // Returns all messages for a given threadId, sorted oldest first. // threadId = ExternalMessageID for AAQ threads, or MessageID for RTQ threads. async function ebayThreadsGet(url, env) { const threadId = (url.searchParams.get('threadId') || '').trim(); if (!threadId) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'threadId required' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } await ebayThreadsEnsureSheet(env); const data = await rSheetGet(env, `${THREAD_TAB}!A2:J5000`).catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const rows = (data.values || []) .filter(r => (r[0] || '') === threadId) .map(r => ({ threadId: r[0] || '', messageId: r[1] || '', timestamp: r[2] || '', itemId: r[3] || '', buyerId: r[4] || '', subject: r[5] || '', direction: r[6] || '', // BUYER | SELLER body: r[7] || '', replyType: r[8] || '', sentAt: r[9] || '', })) .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.timestamp) - new Date(b.timestamp)); // oldest first return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, thread: rows, count: rows.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── POST /ebay-msg/threads/save ─────────────────────────────────────────────── // Body: { messages: [{ threadId, messageId, timestamp, itemId, buyerId, subject, direction, body, replyType, sentAt }] } // Saves one or more messages. Deduplicates by messageId to avoid double-writes. async function ebayThreadsSave(request, env) { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const msgs = Array.isArray(body.messages) ? body.messages : []; if (!msgs.length) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, saved: 0 }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } await ebayThreadsEnsureSheet(env); // Read existing messageIds to deduplicate const existing = await rSheetGet(env, `${THREAD_TAB}!B2:B5000`).catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const existingIds = new Set((existing.values || []).map(r => (r[0] || '').trim()).filter(Boolean)); const toWrite = msgs .filter(m => m.messageId && !existingIds.has(m.messageId)) .map(m => [ (m.threadId || '').slice(0, 200), (m.messageId || '').slice(0, 200), m.timestamp || new Date().toISOString(), (m.itemId || '').slice(0, 50), (m.buyerId || '').slice(0, 100), (m.subject || '').slice(0, 200), m.direction === 'SELLER' ? 'SELLER' : 'BUYER', (m.body || '').slice(0, 1000), (m.replyType || 'RTQ'), m.sentAt || '', ]); if (toWrite.length) { await ebayThreadsAppend(env, toWrite); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, saved: toWrite.length, skipped: msgs.length - toWrite.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ── POST /ebay-msg/cleanup ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Deletes rows from eBay_Threads where Timestamp < 30 days ago. // Called silently by the frontend on app open — no UI feedback. // Uses batchUpdate deleteRows (working bottom-up to preserve row indices). async function ebayThreadsCleanup(env) { await ebayThreadsEnsureSheet(env); const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); const data = await rSheetGet(env, `${THREAD_TAB}!A2:C5000`).catch(() => ({ values: [] })); const rows = data.values || []; // Collect 0-based sheet row indices (add 1 for header, add 1 for 0-based = +2) of rows to delete // We process bottom-up so deletions don't shift indices const toDelete = []; for (let i = rows.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const ts = rows[i][2] || rows[i][0] || ''; // col C = Timestamp, fallback col A if (ts && ts < cutoff) { toDelete.push(i + 1); // +1 for header row (sheet row index is 1-based data, but API uses 0-based) } } if (!toDelete.length) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, deleted: 0 }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Get sheetId for eBay_Threads tab const token = await getToken(env); const sid = repricerSheetId(env); const metaR = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${sid}?fields=sheets.properties`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }); const meta = await metaR.json(); const sheetId = ((meta.sheets || []).find(s => s.properties?.title === THREAD_TAB) || {}).properties?.sheetId; if (sheetId === undefined) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, deleted: 0, note: 'Sheet not found' }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // Build deleteRange requests (bottom-up, already sorted descending above) const requests = toDelete.map(rowIdx => ({ deleteDimension: { range: { sheetId, dimension: 'ROWS', startIndex: rowIdx + 1, // +1 = skip header (0-based: row 0=header, row 1=first data) endIndex: rowIdx + 2 } } })); // Batch in chunks of 100 to stay under Sheets API limits let deleted = 0; for (let i = 0; i < requests.length; i += 100) { const chunk = requests.slice(i, i + 100); await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${sid}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests: chunk }) }); deleted += chunk.length; } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, deleted }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // CRON AUTO-FETCH — runs every 30 min via scheduled handler // Also callable manually: GET /cron/fetch-messages (mgmt session required) // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function cronFetchEbayMessages(env) { try { const ebayToken = await getEbayToken(env); const now = new Date(); const start = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 86400000); const toEbayDate = d => d.toISOString().replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, '.000Z'); const messages = []; // GetMemberMessages — listing questions try { const r1 = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME':'GetMemberMessages','X-EBAY-API-SITEID':'0','X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL':'967','X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN':ebayToken,'Content-Type':'text/xml' }, body: `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?> <GetMemberMessagesRequest xmlns="urn:ebay:apis:eBLBaseComponents"> <RequesterCredentials><eBayAuthToken>${ebayToken}</eBayAuthToken></RequesterCredentials> <MailMessageType>AskSellerQuestion</MailMessageType> <MessageStatus>Unanswered</MessageStatus> <StartCreationTime>${toEbayDate(start)}</StartCreationTime> <EndCreationTime>${toEbayDate(now)}</EndCreationTime> <Pagination><EntriesPerPage>100</EntriesPerPage><PageNumber>1</PageNumber></Pagination> </GetMemberMessagesRequest>` }); const xml1 = await r1.text(); for (const ex of (xml1.match(/<MemberMessageExchange>([\s\S]*?)<\/MemberMessageExchange>/g) || [])) { const m = parseMemberMessageBlock(ex); if (m) { m.replyType='RTQ'; m.source='listing'; messages.push(m); } } } catch(e) { console.error('[cron-msg] GetMemberMessages:', e.message); } // GetMyMessages — inbox/order messages (two-step) try { const rh = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME':'GetMyMessages','X-EBAY-API-SITEID':'0','X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL':'967','X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN':ebayToken,'Content-Type':'text/xml' }, body: `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?> <GetMyMessagesRequest xmlns="urn:ebay:apis:eBLBaseComponents"> <RequesterCredentials><eBayAuthToken>${ebayToken}</eBayAuthToken></RequesterCredentials> <DetailLevel>ReturnHeaders</DetailLevel> <StartTime>${toEbayDate(start)}</StartTime> <EndTime>${toEbayDate(now)}</EndTime> <Pagination><EntriesPerPage>50</EntriesPerPage><PageNumber>1</PageNumber></Pagination> </GetMyMessagesRequest>` }); const headXml = await rh.text(); const msgIds = (headXml.match(/<MessageID>([^<]+)<\/MessageID>/g)||[]) .map(m=>m.replace(/<\/?MessageID>/g,'').trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0,30); if (msgIds.length) { const rb = await fetch('https://api.ebay.com/ws/api.dll', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'X-EBAY-API-CALL-NAME':'GetMyMessages','X-EBAY-API-SITEID':'0','X-EBAY-API-COMPATIBILITY-LEVEL':'967','X-EBAY-API-IAF-TOKEN':ebayToken,'Content-Type':'text/xml' }, body: `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?> <GetMyMessagesRequest xmlns="urn:ebay:apis:eBLBaseComponents"> <RequesterCredentials><eBayAuthToken>${ebayToken}</eBayAuthToken></RequesterCredentials> <DetailLevel>ReturnMessages</DetailLevel> ${msgIds.map(id=>` <MessageIDs><MessageID>${escXml(id)}</MessageID></MessageIDs>`).join('\n')} </GetMyMessagesRequest>` }); const bodyXml = await rb.text(); for (const blk of (bodyXml.match(/<Message>([\s\S]*?)<\/Message>/g)||[])) { const m = parseMyMessageBlock(blk); if (!m||!m.body) continue; const s=(m.senderId||'').toLowerCase(); if (s==='ebay'||s.startsWith('cs-')) continue; if (m.read==='true') continue; m.replyType='AAQ'; m.source='inbox'; messages.push(m); } } } catch(e) { console.error('[cron-msg] GetMyMessages:', e.message); } // Save to eBay_Threads (deduplication built in) if (messages.length) { const toSave = messages.map(m=>({ threadId: m.externalMsgId||m.messageId||'', messageId: m.messageId||'', timestamp: m.createdAt||new Date().toISOString(), itemId: m.itemId||'', buyerId: m.senderId||'', subject: m.subject||'', direction:'BUYER', body: m.body||'', replyType: m.replyType||'RTQ', sentAt:'', })).filter(m=>m.messageId||m.body); await ebayThreadsSave({ json: async ()=>({ messages: toSave }) }, env); } console.log(`[cron-msg] Fetched ${messages.length} messages`); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, fetched:messages.length }), { headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } catch(e) { console.error('[cron-msg]', e.message); return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } } // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ANALYTICS — GET /ebay-msg/analytics?days=30 // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function ebayMsgAnalytics(url, env) { await ebayThreadsEnsureSheet(env); const days = Math.min(parseInt(url.searchParams.get('days')||'30'), 90); const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - days*86400000).toISOString(); const data = await rSheetGet(env, `${THREAD_TAB}!A2:J5000`).catch(()=>({values:[]})); const rows = (data.values||[]).filter(r=>r[2]&&r[2]>=cutoff); const buyerRows = rows.filter(r=>r[6]==='BUYER'); const sellerRows = rows.filter(r=>r[6]==='SELLER'); // Messages by day (last 14) const byDay = {}; buyerRows.forEach(r=>{ const d=(r[2]||'').slice(0,10); if(d) byDay[d]=(byDay[d]||0)+1; }); const byDaySorted = Object.entries(byDay).sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0])).slice(-14); // Unique threads const threads = new Set(rows.map(r=>r[0]).filter(Boolean)); // Avg response time const threadMap = {}; rows.forEach(r=>{ const tid=r[0]; if(!tid) return; if(!threadMap[tid]) threadMap[tid]={buyer:null,seller:null}; if(r[6]==='BUYER'&&!threadMap[tid].buyer) threadMap[tid].buyer=r[2]; if(r[6]==='SELLER'&&!threadMap[tid].seller) threadMap[tid].seller=r[2]; }); const times = Object.values(threadMap).filter(t=>t.buyer&&t.seller&&t.seller>t.buyer).map(t=>(new Date(t.seller)-new Date(t.buyer))/60000); const avgMin = times.length ? Math.round(times.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/times.length) : null; const unanswered = Object.values(threadMap).filter(t=>t.buyer&&!t.seller).length; // Busiest day const dow=[0,0,0,0,0,0,0]; buyerRows.forEach(r=>{ if(r[2]) dow[new Date(r[2]).getUTCDay()]++; }); const dowNames=['Sun','Mon','Tue','Wed','Thu','Fri','Sat']; const busiestDay = dowNames[dow.indexOf(Math.max(...dow))]; return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, days, totalMessages:buyerRows.length, totalReplies:sellerRows.length, totalThreads:threads.size, unanswered, listingQuestions:buyerRows.filter(r=>r[8]==='RTQ').length, orderMessages:buyerRows.filter(r=>r[8]==='AAQ').length, avgResponseMin:avgMin, busiestDay, byDay:byDaySorted, }), { headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // CS TOOL — POST /cs-tool/draft // Body: { platform, orderNumber, productTitle, sku, buyerMessage, agentNote, agentName } // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function csToolDraft(request, env) { const body = await request.json().catch(()=>({})); const { platform='Unknown', orderNumber='', productTitle='', sku='', buyerMessage='', agentNote='', agentName='' } = body; if (!buyerMessage) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error:'buyerMessage is required' }), { status:400, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); const signOff = agentName ? `Best,\n${agentName} | XFITTING Team Support` : `Best,\nXFITTING Team Support`; const platformNotes = { Amazon: 'This is an Amazon order. Do NOT mention eBay. Returns go through Amazon A-to-Z guarantee. Follow Amazon communication policy — no external links.', Walmart: 'This is a Walmart Marketplace order. Direct customer to Walmart returns portal. Professional and solution-focused.', Shopify: 'This is a direct website order. More flexible — direct email communication is fine. Offer to resolve directly.', Email: 'This is a direct email inquiry. Warm and professional. Handle returns and questions directly without routing through a marketplace.', Other: 'Handle professionally and guide customer to the correct next step.', }; const platformNote = platformNotes[platform] || platformNotes['Other']; const prompt = `You are a customer support assistant for XFITTING, an eCommerce brand that sells PEX plumbing fittings and valves. TONE & STYLE: - Always start with: "Hi," - Always end with: "${signOff}" - Concise, direct, helpful — no fluff. Simple language (DIY-level). - Polite but not overly apologetic. Confident and solution-oriented. PRODUCT KNOWLEDGE: - XFITTING sells PEX B (crimp/barb), PEX A (expansion), Push Fit, CPVC, and brass fittings - PEX B = crimp/barb | PEX A = expansion (NOT interchangeable with PEX B) | Push Fit = works with PEX/copper/CPVC - Never guess compatibility — ask for pipe type/system if unclear RETURNS & REFUNDS: - Direct customers to open a return through the platform they purchased on - Do NOT offer off-platform refunds or promise prepaid labels unless required - Wrong item/defective: apologize briefly, guide to return process PLATFORM: ${platform} ${platformNote} ORDER DETAILS: ${orderNumber ? 'Order: ' + orderNumber : ''} ${productTitle ? 'Product: ' + productTitle : ''} ${sku ? 'SKU/ASIN: ' + sku : ''} CUSTOMER MESSAGE: "${buyerMessage.slice(0, 1000)}" ${agentNote ? '\nAGENT NOTE (factor this in): ' + agentNote.slice(0, 300) : ''} Write the reply. Start with "Hi," end with "${signOff}". After sign-off add ONLY on a new line: [CONFIDENCE:X.XX] (0.00-1.00) 1.00=clear | 0.70=standard | 0.50=ambiguous | 0.30=unclear/complex`; try { const aiResp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'x-api-key': env.ANTHROPIC_KEY||env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, 'anthropic-version':'2023-06-01' }, body: JSON.stringify({ model:'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001', max_tokens:400, messages:[{ role:'user', content:prompt }] }) }); const aiData = await aiResp.json(); if (aiData.error) return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:aiData.error.message }), { status:400, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); let draft = (aiData.content?.[0]?.text||'').trim(); let confidence = 0.8; const cm = draft.match(/\[CONFIDENCE:([\d.]+)\]\s*$/); if (cm) { confidence = Math.min(1,Math.max(0,parseFloat(cm[1])||0.8)); draft = draft.replace(/\[CONFIDENCE:[\d.]+\]\s*$/,'').trim(); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, draft, confidence }), { headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } catch(e) { return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, error:e.message }), { status:500, headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'} })); } } // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // CS MSG LOG — multi-platform message log (Amazon, Walmart, Shopify, Email) // Sheet tab: CS_MsgLog (auto-created in SHEET_ID_REPRICER) // Cols A–I: ID, Timestamp, Platform, OrderNumber, SKU, CustomerMessage, // AIDraft, AgentNote, AgentName // Routes: // POST /cs-tool/log-save — save a CS message + draft to CS_MsgLog // GET /cs-tool/log — fetch CS_MsgLog entries (last N rows) // POST /cs-tool/log-clean — delete rows older than 30 days // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const CS_LOG_TAB = 'CS_MsgLog'; const CS_LOG_HEADERS = ['ID','Timestamp','Platform','OrderNumber','SKU','ProductTitle','CustomerMessage','AIDraft','AgentNote','AgentName']; async function csMsgLogEnsureSheet(env) { try { const check = await rSheetGet(env, `${CS_LOG_TAB}!A1:J1`); } // POST /cs-tool/log-save async function csMsgLogSave(request, env) { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const { platform='', orderNumber='', sku='', productTitle='', customerMessage='', aiDraft='', agentNote='', agentName='' } = body; } // POST /cs-tool/log-clean — delete rows older than 30 days async function csMsgLogClean(env) { await csMsgLogEnsureSheet(env); const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 86400000).toISOString(); const data = await rSheetGet(env, `${CS_LOG_TAB}!A2:B2001`).catch(() => ({ values: [] })); } return cors(new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, deleted: toDelete.length }), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── VEEQO INTEGRATION (v16) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const VEEQO_BASE = 'https://api.veeqo.com'; const VEEQO_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 14; // 14 days covers real issues; Status Check handles longer audit const VEEQO_STUCK_DAYS = 7; const VEEQO_NOSCAND_DAYS = 2; // ── Veeqo API helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function veeqoFetch(env, path, options = {}) { const key = (env.VEEQO_API_KEY || '').trim(); if (!key) throw new Error('VEEQO_API_KEY not configured'); const res = await fetch(VEEQO_BASE + path, { ...options, headers: { 'x-api-key': key, 'Accept': 'application/json', 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...(options.headers || {}), }, }); if (!res.ok) { const txt = await res.text().catch(() => ''); throw new Error(`Veeqo ${path} → HTTP ${res.status}: ${txt.slice(0, 200)}`); } const data = await res.json().catch(() => null); if (!data) throw new Error(`Veeqo ${path} → invalid JSON response`); return data; } // ── FBA / excluded channel detection ───────────────────────────────────────── // Excludes Amazon FBA orders — these are fulfilled by Amazon, not us, // and have massive order volume that would blow Cloudflare subrequest limits. // Exclusion is by channel name match (case-insensitive) OR fulfillment_type. // Exclude Amazon FBA orders — fulfilled by Amazon, not our warehouse. // Uses the official 'fulfilled_by_amazon' field from Veeqo's order object, // plus channel name fallback for any edge cases. function veeqoIsFBA(order) { // Primary: official Veeqo field — most reliable if (order.fulfilled_by_amazon === true) return true; const ch = ((order.channel && order.channel.name) || '').toLowerCase(); // Channel name contains 'fba' — catches "Amazon Channel FBA" and variants if (ch.includes('fba')) return true; return false; } // ── Extract tracking / carrier / channel from a Veeqo order ────────────────── // Veeqo list response uses alloc.shipment (singular object), NOT alloc.shipments (array) function veeqoExtractTracking(order) { // tracking_number is an OBJECT: { tracking_number: "9334610...", status: "created", ... } for (const alloc of (order.allocations || [])) { const s = alloc.shipment; if (s && s.tracking_number) { // It's an object with a nested tracking_number string field const tn = s.tracking_number; const raw = typeof tn === 'object' ? (tn.tracking_number || '') : String(tn); const t = String(raw).trim().toUpperCase(); if (t && t.length > 5) return t; } } return null; } function veeqoExtractTrackingStatus(order) { // tracking_number object also contains status field: created, in_transit, delivered, etc. for (const alloc of (order.allocations || [])) { const s = alloc.shipment; if (s && s.tracking_number && typeof s.tracking_number === 'object') { return s.tracking_number.status || null; } } return null; } function veeqoExtractCarrier(order) { for (const alloc of (order.allocations || [])) { const s = alloc.shipment; if (s) { // carrier is an object: { name: "Buy Shipping", slug: "buy-shipping", ... } // sub_carrier_id and service_carrier_name are the actual carrier strings const sub = String(s.sub_carrier_id || '').toUpperCase(); if (sub.includes('UPS')) return 'UPS'; if (sub.includes('USPS')) return 'USPS'; const svc = String(s.service_carrier_name || '').toUpperCase(); if (svc.includes('UPS')) return 'UPS'; if (svc.includes('USPS')) return 'USPS'; const sn = String(s.service_name || s.short_service_name || '').toUpperCase(); if (sn.includes('UPS')) return 'UPS'; if (sn.includes('USPS')) return 'USPS'; } } // Fallback: delivery_method name on the order const dm = String((order.delivery_method && order.delivery_method.name) || '').toUpperCase(); if (dm.includes('UPS')) return 'UPS'; if (dm.includes('USPS') || dm.includes('FIRST CLASS') || dm.includes('PRIORITY MAIL')) return 'USPS'; return 'Unknown'; } function veeqoExtractChannel(order) { return (order.channel && order.channel.name) || 'Unknown'; } // ── Customer name / address / line items ────────────────────────────────────── // Based on Veeqo's standard order schema (deliver_to address object, // line_items array). Verify against a real response via GET /veeqo/debug // (now includes the raw deliver_to + line_items for exactly this purpose) // and adjust field names below if your Veeqo account's shape differs. // Verified against a prior working implementation — d.full_name and // d.zip_code were incorrect guesses; Veeqo's deliver_to object uses // first_name+last_name (no combined full_name field) and postcode as // the zip fallback, not zip_code. function veeqoExtractCustomerName(order) { const d = order.deliver_to || {}; return [d.first_name || '', d.last_name || ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim(); } function veeqoExtractAddress(order) { const d = order.deliver_to || {}; return { address1: (d.address1 || '').trim(), address2: (d.address2 || '').trim(), city: (d.city || '').trim(), zip: (d.zip || d.postcode || '').trim(), }; } // Returns a compact array of { s, q, b, i } — SHORT keys are intentional // and REQUIRED: packship.html's order-lookup card renderer reads exactly // these (li.s=sku, li.q=qty, li.b=bin/location, li.i=image), stored as a // JSON string in a single sheet cell, so longer key names silently render // as blank even though the data technically loaded. // s/q (sku, qty) — from li.sellable.sku_code / li.quantity — verified // against a prior working implementation. // b (bin/location) — verified working prior implementation sourced this // from Veeqo's li.sellable.stock_entries[0].location, but that only // reflects Veeqo's OWN stock records, which may be stale/absent for // this warehouse's actual setup. Kept the live D1 master_list lookup // here instead (by SKU, highest-cases row) since that's the same // source of truth the rest of this app already trusts for location — // more likely to be accurate than trusting Veeqo's copy. // i (image) — sellable.image_url IS a real Veeqo field (confirmed via // prior working implementation) — restored, was previously dropped // under the mistaken assumption no source existed. // Async because of the D1 bin lookup — every caller must await this now. async function veeqoExtractLineItems(env, order) { const items = order.line_items || []; const out = []; for (const li of items) { const sellable = li.sellable || li.product || {}; const sku = sellable.sku_code || sellable.sku || ''; const qty = parseInt(li.quantity) || li.quantity_shipped || 1; const img = (sellable.image_url || '').trim(); if (!sku) continue; let bin = ''; let binSrc = ''; // Priority 1: Veeqo's own bin location — sellable.stock_entries[0].location. // This is what the proven-working reference implementation used, and // it's reliable here since all orders fulfill from a single Veeqo // location ("My Location") — no ambiguity about which stock_entries // index is the right one. A prior version of this function replaced // this with a D1 master_list lookup instead, on the theory that D1 // would be more accurate — but that requires Veeqo's sku_code to // exactly match this app's part_num/base_sku convention, which isn't // guaranteed, and in practice returned blank far more often than // Veeqo's own field would have. Reverted to Veeqo-first. const stockEntries = sellable.stock_entries || []; if (stockEntries.length && stockEntries[0].location) { bin = String(stockEntries[0].location).trim(); binSrc = 'veeqo'; } // Priority 2 (fallback only): D1 master_list, in case a given item has // no stock_entries in Veeqo for some reason. Exact part_num, then // fuzzy base_sku. if (!bin && env && env.DB) { try { const row = await d1First(env, 'SELECT location FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(part_num)=? AND cases > 0 ORDER BY cases DESC LIMIT 1', [sku.toUpperCase()] ); if (row && row.location) { bin = row.location; binSrc = 'd1_exact_fallback'; } else { const baseSku = sku.split('=')[0].trim(); if (baseSku) { const fuzzyRow = await d1First(env, 'SELECT location FROM master_list WHERE UPPER(base_sku)=? AND cases > 0 ORDER BY cases DESC LIMIT 1', [baseSku.toUpperCase()] ); if (fuzzyRow && fuzzyRow.location) { bin = fuzzyRow.location; binSrc = 'd1_base_sku_fallback'; } } } } catch(e) { console.error('[Veeqo] D1 bin fallback failed for', sku, e.message); } } out.push({ s: sku, q: qty, b: bin, i: img, _binSrc: binSrc || 'none_found' }); } return out; } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── TRACKING_MONITOR SHEET HELPERS ─────────────────────────────────────────── // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Cols: A=Date B=Tracking C=Carrier D=OrderNum E=OrderID F=Channel // G=ScanDate H=DaysSince I=LastEvent J=LastEventDate K=LastLocation // L=AlertLevel M=Status const VEEQO_MONITOR_TAB = 'Tracking_Monitor'; let _veeqoTabChecked = false; async function veeqoEnsureMonitorTab(env) { if (_veeqoTabChecked) return; const token = await getToken(env); const metaR = await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}?fields=sheets.properties`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }); const meta = await metaR.json(); const exists = (meta.sheets || []).some(s => s.properties?.title === VEEQO_MONITOR_TAB); if (!exists) { await fetch(`${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}:batchUpdate`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ requests: [{ addSheet: { properties: { title: VEEQO_MONITOR_TAB } } }] }), }); await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent(VEEQO_MONITOR_TAB + '!A1:M1')}?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: [['Date','Tracking','Carrier','OrderNum','OrderID','Channel','ScanDate','DaysSince','LastEvent','LastEventDate','LastLocation','AlertLevel','Status']] }), } ); } _veeqoTabChecked = true; } async function veeqoMonitorWrite(env, rows) { const token = await getToken(env); await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent(VEEQO_MONITOR_TAB + '!A2:M10000')}:clear`, { method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: '{}' } ); if (!rows.length) return; await fetch( `${SHEETS_URL}/${env.SHIP_SHEET_ID}/values/${encodeURIComponent(VEEQO_MONITOR_TAB + '!A2')}?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ values: rows }), } ); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── VEEQO HELPER FUNCTIONS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Look up one order by tracking number via Veeqo free-text query async function veeqoLookupByTracking(env, tracking) { try { const qs = `?query=${encodeURIComponent(tracking)}&status=shipped&page_size=5&page=1`; const res = await veeqoFetch(env, `/orders${qs}`); if (!Array.isArray(res) || res.length === 0) return null; // Prefer exact tracking match for (const order of res) { const t = veeqoExtractTracking(order); if (t && t === tracking.trim().toUpperCase()) return order; } return res[0]; } catch (_) { return null; } } // Get shipment_id from order allocation (needed for tracking events endpoint) function veeqoExtractShipmentId(order) { for (const alloc of (order.allocations || [])) { const s = alloc.shipment; if (s && s.id) return s.id; } return null; } // Fetch real tracking events from Veeqo: GET /shipping/tracking_events/{shipment_id} // Returns array of { timestamp, description, status, location } newest first async function veeqoGetTrackingEvents(env, shipmentId) { try { const res = await veeqoFetch(env, `/shipping/tracking_events/${shipmentId}`); if (!Array.isArray(res)) return []; // Sort newest first return res.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.timestamp || 0) - new Date(a.timestamp || 0)); } catch (_) { return []; } } // Business days since a date (Mon-Fri only, skips weekends) function veeqoBusinessDaysSince(dateStr) { const start = new Date(dateStr); const now = new Date(); let days = 0; const cur = new Date(start); while (cur < now) { cur.setDate(cur.getDate() + 1); const dow = cur.getUTCDay(); if (dow !== 0 && dow !== 6) days++; } return days; } // For manifest sync — fetches page 1 only (today's orders are newest = first page) async function veeqoGetOrdersSince(env, sinceDate) { const since = sinceDate.toISOString().split('T')[0]; const batch = await veeqoFetch(env, `/orders?page_size=100&page=1&created_at_min=${since}&status=shipped`).catch(() => []); return Array.isArray(batch) ? batch : []; } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── TARGETED TRACKING SYNC ────────────────────────────────────────════════════ // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Reads Scan_Log for tracking numbers in two windows: // • 2-4 calendar days old → 2-day no-carrier-scan check // • 10-20 business days old → 10-day delivery check // For each: looks up order in Veeqo, then calls /shipping/tracking_events // for rich event history. Stores OrderNum + OrderID in sheet. async function veeqoTrackingSync(env) { if (!env.VEEQO_API_KEY) return { ok: false, error: 'VEEQO_API_KEY not set' }; await veeqoEnsureMonitorTab(env); const today = shipTodayKey(); const now = Date.now(); const DAY_MS = 86400000; // Read Scan_Log — A=Date B=Timestamp C=Tracking D=Carrier E=Initials // F=BatchClosedAt G=Status H=ShipmentID I=OrderNum const scanData = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Scan_Log!A2:I50000'); const scanRows = (scanData.values || []); const needNoScanCheck = []; const needDeliveryCheck = []; const seen = new Set(); for (const r of scanRows) { const dateStr = (r[0] || '').trim(); const tracking = (r[2] || '').trim().toUpperCase(); const carrier = (r[3] || '').trim(); const shipmentId = (r[7] || '').trim(); // col H — populated at scan time const orderNum = (r[8] || '').trim(); // col I — populated at scan time if (!tracking || !dateStr || seen.has(tracking)) continue; const scanTime = new Date(dateStr).getTime(); if (isNaN(scanTime)) continue; const calDays = Math.floor((now - scanTime) / DAY_MS); const bizDays = veeqoBusinessDaysSince(dateStr); if (calDays >= 2 && calDays <= 4) { needNoScanCheck.push({ tracking, carrier, scanDate: dateStr, calDays, shipmentId, orderNum }); seen.add(tracking); } else if (bizDays >= 10 && bizDays <= 20) { needDeliveryCheck.push({ tracking, carrier, scanDate: dateStr, bizDays, shipmentId, orderNum }); seen.add(tracking); } } const alertRows = []; // ── Helper: resolve shipment data for one item ──────────────────────────── // If ShipmentID is stored in Scan_Log (col H) → use tracking events directly (1 call). // If not (older rows) → order lookup to get shipment ID, then tracking events (2 calls). // Either way: one call per item once ShipmentID is populated from scanning forward. async function resolveItem(item) { let shipmentId = item.shipmentId; let orderNum = item.orderNum; let channel = ''; let events = []; if (!shipmentId) { // Fallback: order lookup to get shipment ID const order = await veeqoLookupByTracking(env, item.tracking); if (order) { shipmentId = veeqoExtractShipmentId(order); orderNum = orderNum || String(order.number || order.id || ''); channel = veeqoExtractChannel(order); } } if (shipmentId) { events = await veeqoGetTrackingEvents(env, shipmentId); } const latest = events[0] || null; const latestStatus = latest ? (latest.status || '').toLowerCase() : ''; const tnStatus = latestStatus || 'created'; return { shipmentId, orderNum, channel, events, latest, tnStatus }; } // Build alert row from resolved data // Cols: Date|Tracking|Carrier|OrderNum|OrderID|Channel|ScanDate|DaysSince| // LastEvent|LastEventDate|LastLocation|AlertLevel|Status function makeRow(item, resolved, alertLevel, statusLabel, daysSince) { const latest = resolved.latest; return [ today, item.tracking, item.carrier, resolved.orderNum || '', resolved.shipmentId || '', resolved.channel || '', item.scanDate, daysSince, latest ? (latest.description || latest.status || '') : '', latest ? (latest.timestamp || '').split('T')[0] : '', latest ? (latest.location || '') : '', alertLevel, statusLabel, ]; } // ── 2-day no-scan check ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── for (const item of needNoScanCheck) { const r = await resolveItem(item); if (/delivered|out_for_delivery|in_transit/.test(r.tnStatus)) continue; if (/returned_to_sender|recipient_refused/.test(r.tnStatus)) { alertRows.push(makeRow(item, r, 'RETURNED', 'Returned to Sender', item.calDays)); } else if (/contact_support|exception|failed|damage/.test(r.tnStatus)) { alertRows.push(makeRow(item, r, 'EXCEPTION', 'Exception / Problem', item.calDays)); } else { // created / awaiting_collection / no events = never scanned if (!r.latest) { const row = makeRow(item, r, 'NEVER_SCANNED_2D', 'Never Scanned by Carrier (2d+)', item.calDays); row[8] = 'Label created — no carrier scan after ' + item.calDays + ' days'; alertRows.push(row); } else { alertRows.push(makeRow(item, r, 'NEVER_SCANNED_2D', 'Never Scanned by Carrier (2d+)', item.calDays)); } } } // ── 10-day delivery check ───────────────────────────────────────────────── for (const item of needDeliveryCheck) { const r = await resolveItem(item); if (/delivered/.test(r.tnStatus)) continue; if (/returned_to_sender|recipient_refused/.test(r.tnStatus)) { alertRows.push(makeRow(item, r, 'RETURNED', 'Returned to Sender', item.bizDays)); } else if (/contact_support|exception|failed|damage/.test(r.tnStatus)) { alertRows.push(makeRow(item, r, 'EXCEPTION', 'Exception / Problem', item.bizDays)); } else { alertRows.push(makeRow(item, r, 'STUCK_10D', 'Not Delivered (10+ business days)', item.bizDays)); } } // Sort: EXCEPTION → RETURNED → STUCK_10D → NEVER_SCANNED_2D const priority = { EXCEPTION:1, RETURNED:2, STUCK_10D:3, NEVER_SCANNED_2D:4 }; alertRows.sort((a, b) => (priority[a[11]] || 9) - (priority[b[11]] || 9)); await veeqoMonitorWrite(env, alertRows); return { ok: true, checked_no_scan: needNoScanCheck.length, checked_delivery: needDeliveryCheck.length, flagged: alertRows.length, ts: new Date().toISOString(), }; } async function veeqoManifestSync(env) { if (!env.VEEQO_API_KEY) return { ok: false, error: 'VEEQO_API_KEY not set' }; const today = shipTodayKey(); const sinceDate = new Date(Date.now() - 1 * 86400000); const allOrders = await veeqoGetOrdersSince(env, sinceDate); const todayOrders = allOrders.filter(o => { if (veeqoIsFBA(o) || !veeqoExtractTracking(o)) return false; return (o.shipped_at || o.created_at || '').split('T')[0] === today; }); if (todayOrders.length === 0) return { ok: true, added: 0, date: today }; // Read existing manifest — build dedup set. Verified against a prior // working implementation: dedup on EITHER today's date OR already marked // 'Scanned' (regardless of date) — a scan-first upsert can create a row // dated slightly differently than this sync's "today" boundary right // around midnight, and without the Scanned check that edge case could // duplicate an order that a packer already scanned. const existing = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Manifest_Log!A2:I20000'); const existingRows = (existing.values || []); const existingToday = new Set( existingRows .filter(r => normalizeShipDate(r[0]) === today || (r[8] || '').trim() === 'Scanned') .map(r => (r[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase()) .filter(Boolean) ); // Only add orders not already in manifest // Cols A-I unchanged (Date/Tracking/OrderNum/Channel/Carrier/Service/ // ShipToState/UploadedBy/ReconcileStatus). Cols J-O are new — they were // already being READ by shipOrderLookup (customerName, city, zip, // lineItems, address1, address2) but never actually written here, so // lookups always came back blank for this info. Now populated. const newRows = []; for (const o of todayOrders) { const tracking = veeqoExtractTracking(o); if (existingToday.has(tracking)) continue; const addr = veeqoExtractAddress(o); newRows.push([ today, tracking, String(o.number || o.id || ''), veeqoExtractChannel(o), veeqoExtractCarrier(o), ((o.allocations || [])[0]?.delivery_method?.name || ''), (o.deliver_to?.state || ''), 'veeqo-sync', 'Pending', veeqoExtractCustomerName(o), addr.city, addr.zip, JSON.stringify(await veeqoExtractLineItems(env, o)), addr.address1, addr.address2, ]); } if (newRows.length > 0) { // Write headers on row 1 if sheet is empty (first ever sync) — matches // prior working implementation, self-documents the tab for anyone who // opens it directly in Sheets. const headerCheck = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Manifest_Log!A1:A1'); if (!headerCheck.values || !headerCheck.values[0] || !headerCheck.values[0][0]) { await shipSheetAppend(env, 'Manifest_Log!A:O', [[ 'Date','Tracking','OrderNum','Channel','Carrier','Service', 'ShipToState','UploadedBy','ReconcileStatus', 'CustomerName','City','Zip','LineItems','Address1','Address2' ]]); } await shipSheetAppend(env, 'Manifest_Log!A:O', newRows); } return { ok: true, added: newRows.length, total: todayOrders.length, date: today }; } // ── Upsert scan into Manifest_Log ───────────────────────────────────────────── // Called after a successful scan + Veeqo background lookup. // If tracking already in today's manifest → mark Scanned. // If not yet → add new row marked Scanned (packer scanned before hourly sync ran). async function manifestUpsertScanned(env, today, tracking, orderData) { try { const existing = await shipSheetGet(env, 'Manifest_Log!A2:O20000'); const existingRows = (existing.values || []); const idx = existingRows.findIndex( r => normalizeShipDate(r[0]) === today && (r[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase() === tracking ); if (idx >= 0) { // Row exists — update ReconcileStatus to Scanned if not already, and // backfill customer/address/items in case the earlier sync-manifest // pass ran before this fix (or this is the first time we have it). const updates = []; if ((existingRows[idx][8] || '') !== 'Scanned') { updates.push({ range: `Manifest_Log!I${idx + 2}`, values: [['Scanned']] }); } if (!existingRows[idx][9] && orderData.customerName) { updates.push({ range: `Manifest_Log!J${idx + 2}:O${idx + 2}`, values: [[ orderData.customerName || '', orderData.city || '', orderData.zip || '', JSON.stringify(orderData.lineItems || []), orderData.address1 || '', orderData.address2 || '', ]] }); } if (updates.length) await shipSheetBatchUpdate(env, updates); } else { // Not in manifest yet — add row marked Scanned, full data await shipSheetAppend(env, 'Manifest_Log!A:O', [[ today, tracking, orderData.orderNum || '', orderData.channel || '', orderData.carrier || '', orderData.service || '', orderData.shipToState || '', 'scan-first', 'Scanned', orderData.customerName || '', orderData.city || '', orderData.zip || '', JSON.stringify(orderData.lineItems || []), orderData.address1 || '', orderData.address2 || '', ]]); } } catch (_) { /* non-critical */ } } // ── Route dispatcher ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Every path returns hardcoded CORS headers — never depends on cors() helper // so a crash in cors() itself can never cause a no-CORS response. function veeqoResp(body, status) { return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status: status || 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': _currentOrigin || '*', 'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET,POST,PUT,OPTIONS', 'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type,X-API-Key,X-Session-Token,X-Session', } }); } async function handleVeeqoRoute(url, method, request, env, session) { const path = url.pathname; // Session check first — before any async work. // sync-manifest is opened to ANY valid session (ops/mobile included) — // it's called automatically by Pack & Ship's Close Batch flow, which // ordinary floor workers use via initials-only sign-in (a 'mobile'-level // session, not 'mgmt'). It's a read/pull operation (bringing today's // Veeqo orders into the Scan/Manifest log), not sensitive like pricing // or alerts, so this is safe to allow at ops level. Everything else // under /veeqo/* stays mgmt-only. if (!session) { return veeqoResp({ error: 'Session required' }, 401); } if (path !== '/veeqo/sync-manifest' && session.pin_level !== 'mgmt') { return veeqoResp({ error: 'Management session required' }, 401); } // Each route in its own try/catch — crash in one can't affect CORS headers if (path === '/veeqo/sync-manifest' && method === 'POST') { try { const result = await veeqoManifestSync(env); return veeqoResp(result); } catch(e) { return veeqoResp({ ok: false, error: e.message }, 500); } } if (path === '/veeqo/sync-tracking' && method === 'POST') { try { const result = await veeqoTrackingSync(env); return veeqoResp(result); } catch(e) { return veeqoResp({ ok: false, error: e.message }, 500); } } if (path === '/veeqo/labels-today' && method === 'GET') { try { const today = shipTodayKey(); const sinceDate = new Date(Date.now() - 1 * 86400000); const orders = await veeqoGetOrdersSince(env, sinceDate); const safe = Array.isArray(orders) ? orders : []; const labels = safe .filter(o => !veeqoIsFBA(o) && veeqoExtractTracking(o)) .filter(o => (o.shipped_at || o.created_at || '').split('T')[0] === today) .map(o => ({ tracking: veeqoExtractTracking(o), carrier: veeqoExtractCarrier(o), channel: veeqoExtractChannel(o), orderNum: String(o.number || o.id || ''), })); return veeqoResp({ date: today, total: labels.length, ups: labels.filter(l => l.carrier === 'UPS').length, usps: labels.filter(l => l.carrier === 'USPS').length, labels, }); } catch(e) { return veeqoResp({ ok: false, error: e.message }, 500); } } if (path === '/veeqo/alerts' && method === 'GET') { try { await veeqoEnsureMonitorTab(env); const data = await shipSheetGet(env, `${VEEQO_MONITOR_TAB}!A2:M10000`); const alerts = (data.values || []).map(r => ({ date: r[0] || '', tracking: r[1] || '', carrier: r[2] || '', orderNum: r[3] || '', orderId: r[4] || '', channel: r[5] || '', scanDate: r[6] || '', daysSince: r[7] || '', lastEvent: r[8] || '', lastEventDate: r[9] || '', lastLocation: r[10] || '', alertLevel: r[11] || '', status: r[12] || '', })); return veeqoResp({ alerts, count: alerts.length }); } catch(e) { return veeqoResp({ ok: false, error: e.message }, 500); } } // GET /veeqo/order-search?q=... — search by customer name, order // number, or tracking number (Veeqo's query= param, already confirmed // working elsewhere in this file for tracking-number lookups during // scanning — same general search, just exposed standalone here). // Built for the returns workflow: replaces manually searching Veeqo's // own UI by customer name to find which marketplace/order a return // belongs to. Reuses the same extraction helpers already proven // working (veeqoExtractCustomerName, veeqoExtractChannel, etc.) // rather than guessing at field names fresh. if (path === '/veeqo/order-search' && method === 'GET') { try { const q = url.searchParams.get('q'); if (!q) return veeqoResp({ ok: false, error: 'q parameter required' }, 400); const qs = `?query=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&page_size=10&page=1`; const raw = await veeqoFetch(env, `/orders${qs}`); const orders = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : []; const results = await Promise.all(orders.map(async o => ({ orderNum: String(o.number || o.id || ''), orderId: o.id, channel: veeqoExtractChannel(o), // CORRECTED: confirmed with the user Veeqo genuinely doesn't // track returns/refunds — that "refundStatus" guess was wrong // and has been removed. Replaced with something real and // useful instead: confirmed from Veeqo's own API docs that // manually-created orders use a "direct" channel type_code // (e.g. their own "Phone" order example) — this is exactly the // signal for spotting a possible replacement shipment sent to // the same customer outside the normal marketplace order flow. channelTypeCode: (o.channel && o.channel.type_code) || null, isManuallyCreated: !!(o.channel && o.channel.type_code === 'direct'), customerName: veeqoExtractCustomerName(o), address: veeqoExtractAddress(o), tracking: veeqoExtractTracking(o), trackingStatus: veeqoExtractTrackingStatus(o), carrier: veeqoExtractCarrier(o), status: o.status || '', isFBA: veeqoIsFBA(o), lineItems: await veeqoExtractLineItems(env, o), }))); return veeqoResp({ ok: true, query: q, count: results.length, results }); } catch(e) { return veeqoResp({ ok: false, error: e.message }, 500); } } // GET /veeqo/debug — returns raw structure of first 2 shipped orders if (path === '/veeqo/debug' && method === 'GET') { try { const qs = `?page_size=2&page=1&status=shipped`; const raw = await veeqoFetch(env, `/orders${qs}`); const orders = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : []; // BUGFIX: .map(o => ({...})) is a plain (non-async) arrow function — // using await directly inside it is a SyntaxError ("Unexpected reserved // word"), which broke the ENTIRE worker on deploy (a syntax error // anywhere fails the whole file's parse, not just this route). Fixed // by resolving the async line-items lookups with Promise.all BEFORE // building the sample array, so the .map() itself stays synchronous. const sampleOrders = orders.slice(0,2); const sampleLineItems = await Promise.all(sampleOrders.map(o => veeqoExtractLineItems(env, o))); return veeqoResp({ raw_count: orders.length, sample: sampleOrders.map((o, i) => ({ id: o.id, number: o.number, status: o.status, shipped_at: o.shipped_at, fulfilled_by_amazon: o.fulfilled_by_amazon, channel_name: o.channel && o.channel.name, delivery_method_name: o.delivery_method && o.delivery_method.name, allocations_count: o.allocations ? o.allocations.length : 0, // Full shipment object from first allocation first_shipment_full: o.allocations && o.allocations[0] ? o.allocations[0].shipment : 'no allocation', // Raw deliver_to and line_items — use these to verify/adjust // veeqoExtractCustomerName / veeqoExtractAddress / veeqoExtractLineItems // if the field names below don't match what your Veeqo account returns. deliver_to_full: o.deliver_to || 'no deliver_to', line_items_full: o.line_items || 'no line_items', // What our extract functions now return extracted_tracking: veeqoExtractTracking(o), extracted_carrier: veeqoExtractCarrier(o), extracted_customer_name: veeqoExtractCustomerName(o), extracted_address: veeqoExtractAddress(o), extracted_line_items: sampleLineItems[i], })) }); } catch(e) { return veeqoResp({ error: e.message }, 500); } } return veeqoResp({ error: 'Veeqo route not found' }, 404); } // ── END VEEQO INTEGRATION ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
